# Project status & roadmap

> Where things stand and what's left. Update this at the end of meaningful work.
> Last updated: 2026-06-24.

There are two tracks in this repo:

1. **Engine & games** — the Stake-Engine fork (pixi-svelte, Svelte 5, PixiJS 8) + the games.
2. **Studio / pipeline tools** — the launcher portal + the cloud-hosted asset tools (atlas) + ComfyUI pipeline.

---

## Track 1 — Engine & games

- **Invisible Flow — Phase 0 interpreter spike (the make-or-break gate): ✅ PASSED HEADLESSLY (2026-06-24, branch `flow/phase0-interpreter-spike`, no game bump).** Per `docs/design/invisible-flow.md` §9 Phase 0, before any `/flow` editor UI, proved that a runtime interpreter reproduces today's coded `winInfo` presentation behaviour EXACTLY. **Landed:** new `packages/engine-flow` workspace package (`workspace:*`, house style, `tsc --noEmit` GREEN) holding the minimal slice — (1) a **FlowDoc** type model (`src/types.ts`: screen/transition stubs + per-screen enter/while/exit choreography sub-graph of Sequence / Parallel / Broadcast / Delay / ForEach nodes); (2) a **choreography executor** (`src/executor.ts`) tree-walking the sub-graph onto the EXISTING primitives — Sequence→serial await, Parallel→`Promise.all`, Broadcast→`emitter.broadcast`/`broadcastAsync` (three distinct shapes: sync / awaited / fire-and-forget), Delay→`waitForTimeout(ms / timeScale())`, ForEach→serial `sequence()` or parallel `Promise.all`; (3) a bounded **accessor** resolver (`src/accessor.ts`: `$trigger.*`/`$item.*` path reads + literals, no scripting VM); (4) an injected **runtime** surface (`src/runtime.ts`: emitter + `timeScale` + `waitForTimeout`, so the interpreter never imports the Svelte-rune state); (5) a **generic scene mounter** interface (`src/mounter.ts`, contract only — live PixiJS mount is Phase 4); (6) the **dispatch-with-fall-through** entry (`src/dispatch.ts`: "FlowDoc has this event? run interpreter; else call the coded handler"). **Verified headlessly** by `tools/flow-spike/winInfoParity.ts` (new `tools/flow-spike` workspace, run `pnpm --filter flow-spike run parity`), mirroring the Rigger spikes: it drives the REAL `createEventEmitter` + REAL `sequence()`, runs BOTH the `winInfo` body lifted verbatim from `bookEventHandlerMap.ts` AND the interpreter running a hand-authored FlowDoc for the same event, and asserts the ordered op log (broadcasts+args, awaited completions, scaled delays) is **identical turbo ON and OFF** (15 ops each), plus a synthetic executor-shape case covering the fire-and-forget `broadcastAsync` + `delay ÷ timeScale()` + parallel branches, plus **fall-through** (an un-authored `setTotalWin` still hits its coded handler). PHASE 0 GATE: PASSED. **Semantic-fidelity finding (§13 risk, surfaced not papered over):** the executor's recursive `await` per sequence child flushes an already-queued microtask one tick earlier than the flat coded handler, so a FIRE-AND-FORGET `broadcastAsync` subscriber's completion can log before/after the synchronous `delay` marker differently — but this is BELOW the observability threshold (with the real `setTimeout`-based `waitForTimeout`, the un-awaited subscriber resolves on a microtask well before any `setTimeout` fires, so no frame/sound/animation lands at a different wall-clock time). The harness handles this honestly: the deterministic INITIATION timeline is asserted position-for-position; a fire-and-forget completion is asserted as a multiset (proves it ran + was not awaited). **Left for later phases (NOT Phase 0):** the live generic mounter (MainContainer scaling + overlays, Phase 4), the macro transition trigger/guard vocabulary + dynamic-pin projection (Phase 1/2), the `/flow` editor UI, and the bake/pipeline wiring (`flow?` in `BakedBundle`, Phase 6) — all explicitly out of scope here. **Owner-verify live:** none yet — the runtime swap into a real `apps/lines`/Borut boot is Phase 4; this slice proves the interpreter's async/timing fidelity in isolation. Files: `packages/engine-flow/{package.json,tsconfig.json,index.ts,src/*.ts}`, `tools/flow-spike/{package.json,winInfoParity.ts}`, `pnpm-workspace.yaml` (+`tools/flow-spike`), `docs/design/invisible-flow.md` (progress section).

- **Invisible Flow — Phase 1: FlowDoc model + pin-derivation + `/flow` read-only canvas: ✅ DONE HEADLESSLY (2026-06-24, branch `flow/phase0-interpreter-spike`, no game bump, tool UNREGISTERED behind the curtain).** Per `docs/design/invisible-flow.md` §9 row 1 + §12 decisions. **Step 1 — Svelte Flow install spike (DECISION GATE): xyflow WINS.** Installed `@xyflow/svelte@1.6.1` into `apps/launcher-api`; a trivial 2-node/1-edge graph builds + ships clean under our Vite 6 / Turbo 2 / Svelte 5 setup (`pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN; the SvelteFlow component + its CSS land in the client bundle). The ONLY friction is a peer-dep WARNING — xyflow wants `svelte@^5.25.0`, the workspace pins `5.20.5` uniformly across every app+package — NOT a build/runtime error; xyflow uses only stable Svelte-5 runes APIs present since 5.0, so 5.20.5 works. Caveat carried: a workspace-wide svelte bump to ≥5.25 (touches every game) would clear the warning, deferred — not worth it for the gap. Verdict: **proceed with `@xyflow/svelte`** (SSR-guarded via `export const ssr = false` since it's client-only). **Step 2 — FlowDoc schema (`packages/engine-flow/src/types.ts`, the real "one doc"):** promoted the Phase-0 stubs into the full model — screen-id nodes referencing LayoutDoc scenes (+ canvas `position`, `initial`), typed `FlowTransition` edges (`from`/`to` + a `FlowTrigger` of `bookEvent`/`complete`/`condition` + optional `FlowGuard` + `delayMs` + author `order`), a bounded `FlowGuard`/`FlowPredicate` (closed comparator set `eq|neq|gt|gte|lt|lte|in`, NOT an expression language), a `FlowPin` model (the 4 dynamic roles `value`/`signal`/`action`/`gate` + 3 structural `enter`/`complete`/`active`, stable composite ids), and a new `engine` accessor kind (`$engine.<key>`) + `branch` choreography node — kept sparse/override-friendly (parity rule §7) and back-compatible (Phase-0 `executor.ts`/`dispatch.ts` still compile; `accessor.ts` gained `evaluateGuard` + the executor a `branch` case). **Step 3 — pin-derivation (`packages/engine-flow/src/pins.ts`, the core reusable logic): `deriveScreenPins(scene, resolver)` projects a LayoutDoc screen into pins using the FOUR existing `engine-layout` registries — value←instance `source` param, action←`action`, gate←`visibleSource` + the scene-level `Scene.visibleSource`, signal←a spine cue's `signal` in the resolved ComponentDef tree — plus the 3 fixed structural pins. Pin id = the §12 composite `${instanceId}::${role}:${key}` (structural = `${screenId}::${role}`); a missing ComponentDef ORPHANS its param-driven pins (flagged, never silently dropped). Pure + Svelte-free (reads the authored doc, no registry calls), so it runs headlessly AND in the launcher loader. **Verified headlessly** by `tools/flow-spike/pinDerivation.ts` (`pnpm --filter flow-spike run pins`): 21/21 assertions GREEN against a representative `apps/lines`-shaped fixture — structural+value+action+gate+signal pins present, ids stable under RENAME (label-only change) + REORDER, scene-gate + def-default-driven pins derived, orphan flagged, deterministic id order. **Step 4 — id-discipline check (§12): PASSED.** Scene Editor (`apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/editor/+page.svelte`) mints a FRESH `n_…` id on every node ADD (lines 480, 523) and on paste/duplicate via `reassignNodeIds` (line 150 — `node.id = freshNodeId()`, also drops `slotId`); no edit path reassigns `id`. So "no id regeneration on edit, fresh id on duplicate/paste" HOLDS — pin identity is safe to key off `BaseNode.id`. Not a Phase-1 blocker. **Step 5 — `/flow` read-only page:** new route `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/flow/` — `+page.server.ts` REUSES the launcher's existing R2/scenes loading (`loadDoc` — the same LayoutDoc the Scene Editor reads) + `listComponents` + `resolveToolScope` (no new shared surface, gated on the `editor` entitlement, `ssr=false`); `+page.svelte` builds the read-only model and renders screen nodes (custom `FlowScreenNode.svelte`, dynamic pins as typed Svelte Flow handles — inputs left, outputs right, `active` as a status chip) + transition edges, with an orphaned-pin warning band. A typed editable model + a generic snapshot-based **undo/redo command stack** (`flowModel.client.ts` `createFlowHistory<T>`, burst-coalescing, mirroring the Scene Editor's history pattern §12) is stood up now though editing is Phase 2. `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN with the route. **Tool registration: NOT done (deliberate).** Per the task, `/flow` stays UNREGISTERED (no `roles.ts` TOOLS/ROLE_TOOLS/TOOL_BAR_ORDER/TOOL_DOC_SLUG entry, no `docs/tools/flow.md`) for this read-only spike — so RULE 9 is not yet triggered; it registers + ships its doc in Phase 2 when authoring + `POST /api/flow/save` land. **Owner-verify live:** the actual `/flow` VISUAL render (Svelte Flow canvas drawing a real project's screens + derived-pin handles) needs the deployed launcher with auth+R2 — headless build proves it compiles/ships, not the pixels. **Left for Phase 2:** macro authoring (place screen nodes, draw/edit transition edges, save→R2), tool registration + doc, the generic mounter live (Phase 4), bake/pipeline (Phase 6). Files: `packages/engine-flow/{index.ts,package.json,src/types.ts,src/accessor.ts,src/executor.ts,src/pins.ts}`, `apps/launcher-api/{package.json,src/routes/(app)/flow/*}`, `tools/flow-spike/{package.json,pinDerivation.ts}`, `docs/design/invisible-flow.md` (progress).

reports the §7 boundary, never changes behaviour. **(5) Polish** — pin `title` tooltips (role + full binding, with an orphaned note) so truncated handle labels are readable; node-title tooltip; handle spacing nudge; the `invalid` node marker. **Verified headlessly:** new `tools/flow-spike/phase7Authoring.ts` (`pnpm --filter flow-spike run phase7`) — 30/30 GREEN (validation across every issue class, the diff per-screen/per-event/summary, and the copy/paste invariant — a pasted subgraph with fresh ids round-trips canonical + idempotent through `normalizeFlowDoc`, all ids unique). `pnpm --filter engine-flow exec tsc --noEmit` GREEN; - **Invisible Flow — Phase 7: emitter-vocabulary / effect-name EXPORT (replaces the hardcoded `DEFAULT_EMITTER_VOCABULARY` in the `/flow` palette): ✅ DONE HEADLESSLY (2026-06-24, branch `flow/phase7-emitter-vocab`, EDITOR/CODEGEN-ONLY, no game bump).** Resolves the held follow-up from `docs/design/invisible-flow.md` Phase 3 (§B) / Phase 6. **Authoring-fidelity ONLY** — it changes which Broadcast events + effect names the choreography palette OFFERS, never the runtime (the executor broadcasts/invokes whatever the FlowDoc declares regardless), so there is ZERO game-parity risk and no submodule bump; the new `flowVocabulary.ts` is imported by NO game-runtime module (only the spike). **Mechanism — build-time CODEGEN, not a pipeline export, and why:** the emitter union (`typesEmitterEvent.ts` + the per-component `EmitterEvent*` unions + the shared `EmitterEventUi`/`Modal`/`HotKey` unions) and the effect catalog (`flowEffects.ts` keys) are properties of the GAME SOURCE — identical across every project on that game, NOT per-project authored data in R2 — so pushing them through export→deploy→bake→R2-per-project (the FlowDoc's path) would invent per-project storage for a per-game constant. Codegen is the honest fit and mirrors the existing committed game-source fixtures (`flowDoc.ts`/`flowEffects.ts`). **Landed:** (1) `scripts/gen-flow-vocabulary.mjs` (`pnpm gen:flow-vocab` / `pnpm gen:flow-vocab:check`) parses the simple `{ type:'name'; field:T }` union members + the `flowEffects` map keys (Prettier-formatted in-script so write + `--check` are idempotent), emitting `apps/lines/src/game/flowVocabulary.ts` (`LINES_EMITTER_VOCABULARY` — 35 events incl. the shared UI cues + `soundFade` the hand-written default MISSED, 20 effects) and `apps/launcher-api/src/lib/flowVocabularies.ts` (the launcher registry keyed by LayoutDoc `gameType` + `resolveFlowVocabulary(gameType)`, which falls back to `DEFAULT_EMITTER_VOCABULARY` for any unrecognized game — the §7 parity-safe default; `lines`+`bookOf`→lines vocab, so Book of Borut is covered). (2) `engine-flow/src/emitterVocabulary.ts` gained `EmitterEffectDef` + `EmitterVocabulary.effects?` + `findEmitterEffect`; `DEFAULT_EMITTER_VOCABULARY` UNCHANGED (still the fallback). (3) `/flow` `+page.server.ts` resolves the vocab from `doc.gameType` and passes `data.vocabulary`; `+page.svelte` feeds it to `ChoreographyEditor` instead of the hardcoded default. (4) **Effect-name catalog surfaced** (the doc's "emitter union + effect-name catalog" pairing): `ChoreoNodeInspector` renders an effect-name picker from `vocab.effects` for an `effect` node (with a "not registered" warning); `ChoreoNodeEdit`/`editChoreoNode` gained the effect `name` edit; `choreoNodeSummary` gained the previously-missing `effect` case (canvas label). Adding `effect` as a NEW addable node kind stays deferred. **Verified headlessly:** new `tools/flow-spike/vocabulary.ts` (`pnpm --filter flow-spike run vocab`) — 13/13 GREEN: codegen IN SYNC with source (`--check` exits 0 — the fixture can't silently drift the way the hand-written default could); every Broadcast event (26) + effect (20) the REAL `LINES_FLOW_DOC` authors is present; the exported vocab is a SUPERSET of `DEFAULT_EMITTER_VOCABULARY` and adds the missed `soundFade`; the registry maps `lines`/`bookOf` + falls back to default. `pnpm --filter engine-flow exec tsc --noEmit` GREEN; `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN (page + registry + effect picker ship); the Phase-0 `parity`/Phase-1 `pins`/Phase-2-3 `roundtrip`/Phase-4 `phase4`/Phase-5 `phase5`/Phase-6 `phase6`/Phase-7 `phase7` spikes ALL still GREEN. - **Invisible Flow — tap-to-continue / signal-trigger (RUNTIME HALF of "tap to continue"): ✅ DONE HEADLESSLY (2026-06-24, branch `flow/tap-to-continue`, no game bump).** Owner decision "Both": a tap on a tap-enabled overlay both COMPLETES the active screen (so a `complete`-edge advances) AND emits a NAMED signal (so a new `signal`-edge advances). This change is the interpreter plumbing + the editor option; the component that captures the tap is the NEXT agent's job (it calls the API this exposes). Parity (§7) held: no existing FlowDoc uses `signal` triggers or the new hooks, so every current doc/game is byte-identical and the default/empty path is unchanged. **(A) `completeActiveScreen()`** on `FlowInterpreter` (`engine-flow/src/interpreter.ts`) — runs the active screen's `exit` choreography then fires its `complete`/`exited` pin (the exit phase already runs inside the transition swap, so it maps to the HSM's `onComplete()`); SAFE no-op returning `false` when inert. **(B) new `signal` trigger** — `FlowTrigger` gains `{ kind:'signal'; signal:string }` (`types.ts`); the HSM (`presentation.ts`) gains `onSignal(name)` matching the active screen's `{kind:'signal', signal:name}` edge exactly as `bookEvent` matches event `type`; the interpreter surfaces `emitSignal(name)` (no-op `false` when inert); `normalize.ts` round-trips the trigger + drops an invalid one (missing/empty `signal`) without corrupting the doc; `diff.ts`/`validate.ts` need no change. **(C) game-side holder access** — `apps/lines/src/game/flowInterpreterHolder.ts` exports `completeActiveScreen()` + `emitFlowSignal(signal)`, reading the interpreter singleton the way `playBookEvent` does, SAFE no-ops when no FlowDoc — the two functions the next agent's tap component imports + calls on click. **(D) /flow editor** — `EdgeInspector.svelte` trigger picker gains a "Tap signal" option + signal-name input; `+page.svelte` edge-label switch gains a `tap: <name>` case. **Verified headlessly:** new `tools/flow-spike/tapToContinue.ts` (`pnpm --filter flow-spike run tap`) — 16/16 GREEN against the REAL interpreter/HSM/normalize (signal matched fires + exit-then-enter order, non-matching/un-listened no-ops; `completeActiveScreen()` advances a `complete` edge; inert ⇒ both hooks `false`; signal-trigger doc round-trips canonical + idempotent, invalid dropped). `pnpm --filter engine-flow exec tsc --noEmit` GREEN; `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN; `pnpm --filter lines build` GREEN (after `pnpm --filter pixi-svelte build` to clear the stale-engine-dist gotcha) with `completeActiveScreen`/`emitSignal`/`onSignal` confirmed in the minified bundle; Phase-0 `parity` / Phase-1 `pins` / Phase-2-3 `roundtrip` / Phase-4 `phase4` (allowlist +`onSignal`, still observe-only) / Phase-5 `phase5` / Phase-6 `phase6` / Phase-7 `phase7` ALL still GREEN. (`vocab` was already failing on the base — a codegen-staleness `--check`, unrelated; no vocab files touched.) **Owner-verify live:** nothing for the runtime half — the tap-capturing component (next change) is what makes a tap reach these hooks in a real game. When the engine `signal`-trigger must reach Book of Borut, bump its `engine` submodule (owner's mirror step) once a tap-enabled flow is authored. - **Engine: `assetsLoaded` visibility feed (show splash content AFTER boot load): ✅ SHIPPED (2026-06-24, branch `flow/phase7-authoring-ux`, no game bump).** The inverse of `assetsLoading` — `boolSource(() => stateApp.loaded)`, true the moment boot loading completes. Gates loading-screen content that should appear AFTER load (e.g. a logo that pops in once the loading bar fills, before press-to-continue), the complement of the coded splash's `{#if !loaded}` bar. Added the key + label "Loaded (after boot)" to `engine-layout`'s `VISIBILITY_SOURCE_KEYS`/`VISIBILITY_SOURCE_LABELS` (so the Scene Editor's visibility dropdown offers it — travels to all games via the catalog) and registered the runtime `BoolSource` in `apps/lines/src/components/Game.svelte`. Builds GREEN: `engine-layout`, `lines`, `launcher-api`. **Per-game:** each game's `Game.svelte` must register `assetsLoaded: boolSource(() => stateApp.loaded)` for the gate to fire at runtime — Borut needs this one-liner mirrored alongside its engine submodule bump (the generic online runtime IS `apps/lines`, so online-published games already have it). NB: the loading/splash/press-to-continue sequence is the coded `LoadingScreen` shell + the editor `loading` scene, NOT Invisible Flow (the interpreter only `start()`s after boot load completes). - **Component system: dedicated `invisible-components` subagent + audit/plan + Batch-1 correctness fix: 🚧 IN PROGRESS (2026-06-24).** The shared/built-in `ComponentDef` library (the 9 defs in `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/builtinComponents.ts` — `hudReadout, button, textBox, freeSpinCounter, infoBar, loadingIntro, transition, freeSpinIntroVisual, freeSpinOutroVisual`) is the reusable CORE for all future games; from now on ALL component work (existing + new) routes through the new **`invisible-components`** subagent (`.claude/agents/invisible-components.md`, registered in `CLAUDE.md`). A read-only audit produced the work plan below. **Batch 1 SHIPPED (safe, no game-render change):** (1) `componentStorage.ts` `normalizeParams` only allowed 5 of the 8 `ComponentParam.kind`s — it silently stripped `spine`/`spineAnimation`/`spineSlot` + the `spineParam` link on any save, so editing/forking the FS-intro/outro defs lost data; fixed with a `PARAM_KINDS` set synced to `types.ts` + `spineParam` preservation. (2) `docs/tools/component-editor.md` nesting-depth TODO was stale — corrected to reflect `MAX_COMPONENT_DEPTH = 2` + cycle guard (already in code). `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN. **Remaining plan (owner to greenlight per batch — items touch the shipped Borut game / are large):** Batch 2 (A-side neutrality, parity-sensitive on Borut) — strip game-specific defaults (`Frame_FSCounter.png`, `gold` font, `loader`/`title_screen`, `progressBar*.png`, `fsIntroNumber`/`fsOutroNumber`) out of the shared defs into the project doc (H1); add per-kind fill to `infoBar` to restore `MessageToast` info/win/warn parity (H2). **Batch 3 — promote-to-shared UI in the Component Editor (D1): ✅ SHIPPED (2026-06-24, launcher-only, purely additive, no engine/game change).** A **Promote to shared** button sits in the top bar beside **Save component** (only while a component is open); it POSTs the open draft with `scope:'shared'` and NO `project`, writing the repo-wide `_shared/editor-components/<id>.json` copy behind a precedence-honest confirm (the project copy keeps shadowing it; promote is a snapshot, the draft stays `scope:'project'`). Gated in the UI on `data.canPublishShared` (`roleHasCapability(COMPONENT_PUBLISH_CAPABILITY)` in `+page.server.ts`, mirroring the Font Maker's shared-save target) so it never dangles a 403; the API enforces the same capability server-side. `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN. Files: `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/components/{+page.server.ts,+page.svelte}`, `docs/tools/component-editor.md`, `docs/design/invisible-editor.md` §8.3. Still deferred: authoring a SHARED-only component (no project shadow) + a promote-from-Library-row affordance for components not currently open. **Batch 4 — safe versioning + shared-save plumbing: ✅ SHIPPED (2026-06-24, engine + launcher, parity-preserving, no game bump).** (a) **Safe version resolution** — `engine-layout` `registerComponents.ts` adds `resolveComponent(id, pinnedVersion)` → `{ def, pinnedVersion, registeredVersion, versionMismatch }`; `ComponentInstance.svelte` uses it so a pinned-version mismatch is SURFACED (flag + warning) instead of silently rendered as the pinned version — but v1 stays a single-version store so it still renders the registered def (parity), NEVER mutates the pin, NEVER auto-upgrades. `getComponent` kept as a thin wrapper (byte-identical for existing callers). (b) **Save bump** — `componentStorage.ts` `saveComponent` reads the stored def and reconciles `version` (`reconcileVersion`): changed def → `max(stored,posted)+1`, no-op re-save → keep stored version, new component → keep posted. (c) **Shared-save gating** — `saveComponent`/`deleteComponent` shared key already resolved; the API route (`/api/editor/component`) now gates a `scope:'shared'` WRITE/DELETE behind the new `componentPublish` capability (`COMPONENT_PUBLISH_CAPABILITY` in `roles.ts`, default-ON for `admin` only, in the role-override matrix beside `fontPublish`/`blueprintPublish`); project saves unchanged (`editor` tool gate). Both builds GREEN. **Outdated-flag + per-instance "update to latest" (the editor half of D4): ✅ SHIPPED (2026-06-24, launcher-only, purely additive, no engine/game change, no game bump).** Both live in the shared `EditorProperties.svelte` "Component instance" section (the Properties panel — §8.9's natural home), so the Scene Editor AND the Component Editor (which reuses that component for nested instances) inherit them from ONE place (DRY — no duplicated affordance). **Outdated flag:** an instance is outdated when its pinned `componentVersion` < the resolved def's `version` (reusing the SAME version data `resolveComponent` compares — no parallel check); shown as an amber inline note "Component vN available (instance pinned vM)" under the pinned-version line, matching the editor's existing inline-warning style (no new warnings framework). Scene-mode only; an up-to-date pin shows nothing and is byte-identical. **Update to latest:** a button (`onUpdateInstanceToLatest`, wired only on the Scene Editor page) re-pins ONLY that one instance's `componentVersion` to the def's current version — explicit + per-instance, never bulk/auto — and reconciles params: keep every author override whose param key still exists on the new def, drop overrides for removed params (the def's defaults fill gaps via `resolveComponentParams`); persists through the normal scene save (`markDirty()`). `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN. Files: `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/editor/{EditorProperties.svelte,+page.svelte}`, `docs/tools/component-editor.md`, `docs/design/invisible-editor.md` §8.9. **Still TODO (D4 v2):** true multi-version STORAGE (keep historical defs so a pin resolves the EXACT version it was authored against — until then an outdated pin still renders the single registered def with `versionMismatch` flagged at runtime). Batch 5 — new core components in priority order: P1 `winPresentation`/`bigWin` (re-home `apps/lines/src/components/Win.svelte`; signals + visibility feeds already cataloged; also the design doc's §17.4 next migration), P2 `paytable`/`infoPage` (`InfoOverlay`/`InfoPaylineGrid`), P3 `menuPanel`/settings, P4 `betSelector`, P5 `autoplayPanel`, P6 `buyBonus`, P7 `background`, P8 `errorModal`. Deferred (large, parked in `docs/design/invisible-editor.md`): D2 full behaviour/timeline UI (a signal→spine-cue slice already ships), D3 signal-track UI, D6 autosave. Files: `.claude/agents/invisible-components.md`, `CLAUDE.md`, `apps/launcher-api/src/lib/server/componentStorage.ts`, `apps/launcher-api/src/lib/roles.ts`, `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/api/editor/component/+server.ts`, `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/registerComponents.ts`, `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/ComponentInstance.svelte`, `docs/tools/component-editor.md`.

- **Component versioning v2 — true multi-version storage + pin resolution + bake (D4): ✅ SHIPPED (2026-06-24, engine + launcher, parity-preserving, no game bump).** Completes §8.9's deferred half: a pinned `componentInstance` now resolves the EXACT def it was authored against, not just whatever single version is currently registered. **(1) Storage keeps history** — `componentStorage.ts` `saveComponent` writes the def to BOTH the `<id>.json` LATEST pointer (read by `loadComponent` with no version + `listComponents` — back-compat byte-identical) AND an immutable `<id>.v<N>.json` SNAPSHOT, for BOTH scopes (`projectComponentVersionKey`/`editorComponentVersionKey`). Layout chosen = **versioned keys beside a latest pointer** (not a versions-map blob) — leaves single-doc readers untouched, keeps each version independently cacheable + immutable, mirrors the repo's sibling-file convention (Rigger `<id>.json`+`index.json`). Library listings exclude `.v<N>.json` (regex lookbehind + `VERSION_SNAPSHOT_KEY` filter); `deleteComponent` sweeps every snapshot too; snapshot written BEFORE the latest pointer so a failed snapshot never advances latest. A pre-v2 `<id>.json` keeps loading; its first v2 save establishes history. `loadComponent(id, projectKey?, version?)` gains an optional exact-version resolve (`readComponentAtScope`: prefer snapshot, else latest only when its version equals the pin); `GET /api/editor/component?…&version=<N>` exposes it. **(2) Engine resolves by pin** — `registerComponents.ts` is now a multi-version store (`{ latest, byVersion }` per id); `resolveComponent(id, pinnedVersion)` returns the EXACT registered version (`versionMismatch:false` — pin honoured) and falls back to latest + flags mismatch ONLY when the pin isn't registered. `getComponent` back-compat preserved; `ComponentInstance.svelte` renders the pinned version. **(3) Deploy ships every pinned version (rule 8)** — new `collectComponentPins` gathers explicit `(id, version)` pins; the bake's `resolveReferencedDefs` (`/api/editor/doc` + `runtimeBundle.ts`) loads each non-latest pin via `loadComponent(id, projectKey, version)` → `componentVersions`; `bake-editor-doc.mjs` embeds them; the game's `registerBakedComponents` registers them (one at a time) BEFORE `componentDefs` so unpinned instances still follow latest. **Parity:** no game pins a non-latest version today, so `componentVersions` is empty + omitted everywhere → every bundle byte-identical; latest-resolution + single-doc storage behave exactly as before. **Scoped remainder (not blocking):** the bake walks only TOP-LEVEL scene pins, not the transitive nested-pin closure (a pin on a `componentInstance` nested inside another def's `root`) — sufficient at v1's 1–2 nesting levels with zero current pins; widen `collectComponentPins` to the def-root closure when a game first nests a pinned instance. Editor "update to latest" per-instance action + outdated-instance flagging stay UI-side TODOs. Both builds GREEN (`pnpm --filter {engine-layout,launcher-api} build`). Files: `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/{registerComponents.ts,ComponentInstance.svelte,collectComponentIds.ts}`, `apps/launcher-api/src/lib/server/{componentStorage.ts,projectPaths.ts,runtimeBundle.ts}`, `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/api/editor/{doc,component}/+server.ts`, `apps/launcher-api/scripts/bake-editor-doc.mjs`, `apps/lines/src/editor-scenes.ts`, `docs/design/invisible-editor.md` §8.9, `docs/tools/component-editor.md`.

- **Component version-browser UI — the last §8.9 UI item: ✅ SHIPPED (2026-06-24, launcher-only, purely additive, no engine/game change, no submodule bump).** Completes §8.9 now that v2 multi-version storage exists: the editor can finally SEE a component's saved history and inspect an older version. **(1) List endpoint** — `componentStorage.ts` adds `listComponentVersions(id, scope, projectKey?)` → `{ versions: number[]; latest?: number }`: R2-lists the scope prefix, keeps only THIS id's `<id>.v<N>.json` siblings (the library listings deliberately exclude them), parses their version numbers, and reads the `<id>.json` latest pointer for the current version (a pre-v2 def with no snapshots returns `versions: []` + its single latest version). Surfaced through the EXISTING `GET /api/editor/component?id=…&list=versions&scope=<shared|project>` under the same `editor` tool gate (no new auth pattern); read-only, never mutates R2. **(2) Browser UI** — in `components/+page.svelte`'s top bar (while a component is open): a `Version` dropdown lists every retained snapshot newest-first with the latest marked, and **Inspect** loads the selected version READ-ONLY onto the canvas (GETs the immutable `<id>.v<N>.json`, swaps it into the draft, sets an `inspecting` flag that blocks Save / Promote / Space / node spawn+delete, shows an amber `Inspecting vN (read-only)` pill). **Back to latest** reloads the editable current def from the sidebar list. **Non-destructive by construction:** nothing is written while inspecting, the snapshot is discarded on return, and the inspected def can never become the next save (Save is guarded on `isInspecting`). **No "restore this version" action** — restoring is just a normal save of the inspected def (which `reconcileVersion` bumps to a NEW version on top, never rewriting history); deliberately left out to keep browsing purely read-only, a precise future-UX TODO if authors ask. `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN. **Reuse-check:** the def version-history browser is Component-Editor-specific (the Scene Editor's per-instance "update to latest"/outdated flag already lives in shared `EditorProperties.svelte`; this browses the DEF the Component Editor authors), so it lives in `components/+page.svelte` — no duplication. Files: `apps/launcher-api/src/lib/server/componentStorage.ts`, `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/api/editor/component/+server.ts`, `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/components/+page.svelte`, `docs/design/invisible-editor.md` §8.9, `docs/tools/component-editor.md`.

- **Fix: Rigger exported rigs with NO `skeleton.width/height` → mis-sized / origin-anchored spines: ✅ DONE (2026-06-23, launcher `static/rigger/view.html`).** Root cause of the day-long free-spin-intro saga: `R_FreeSpin` (a Rigger rig built from the game's `S_StaticElements` sheet) shipped with no skeleton bounds, because the Rigger saved the skeleton verbatim (`skeleton: rawDoc`) and never computed them — and this rig's art is shown only via animation attachment-keys, so its setup pose is empty (Spine writes bounds from the setup pose). A bounds-less spine pins its pivot to the origin (renders top-left) and can't be sized by an authored width/height. **Fix:** `computeRigBounds()` unions `skeleton.getBounds` across the setup pose AND every animation sampled via the Rigger's own `poseAtTime` (which applies attachment swaps + transforms); `ensureRigBounds()` writes `x/y/width/height` into `rawDoc.skeleton` before every save path (`saveToR2`, `exportIrig`, `saveRigToLibrary`). Re-saving any rig now backfills its bounds. Inline JS syntax-checked (`new Function` over both script blocks, 0 errors); **NOT runtime-verified** (the Rigger is WebGL + needs a loaded rig). **Owner workflow: after the launcher redeploys, open `R_FreeSpin` (and any other custom rig) in the Rigger → Save → re-bake/republish Borut.** Pairs with the engine-side graceful-degradation (live-bounds pivot) already shipped. The rig's NATIVE oversize (built in atlas-pixel space, no atlas `scale:`) is separate — scale the spine node down; editor↔game now agree once bounds exist. File: `apps/launcher-api/static/rigger/view.html`.

- **Fix: free-spin expanding special now pays ONLY on the real paylines — the scatter-style full-column pay is REMOVED: ✅ DONE (2026-06-23, `test-server` / Book of Borut live RGS — needs test-server redeploy).** Owner caught a live free spin awarding a "WIN $10.00 — 3 OF A KIND" with a win box around a single vertical column of 7s. **Root cause:** `evaluateSpecial` in `scripts/mock-rgs-server-book.mjs` paid the expanding special **scatter-style** — `mult * betPerLine * 5` whenever the special covered ≥3 reels ANYWHERE (non-adjacent included), highlighting every cell of each covered reel. That is an INVENTED payment line (a vertical/scattered column win), not one of the 10 defined paylines, and it gated on raw symbol COUNT so even 3 specials stacked in ONE reel qualified (count=1 → lone-column highlight). Owner's rule: **only the paylines may pay; do not invent new payment lines.** **Fix (supersedes the pay model in the two 2026-06-23 land-then-morph entries below — the morph VISUAL is unchanged; only the PAY changed):** deleted `evaluateSpecial`; added `expandSpecialBoard(reels, special)` which, when the special is on ≥3 reels, returns a board with those reels FILLED with the special (same 3+ gate and reel set the facade's `expandBookColumns` morph already uses), else the natural board. Free spins now pay `evaluatePaylines(expandSpecialBoard(reels, special), betPerLine)` — the special pays strictly as left-to-right line wins from reel 1, exactly like any symbol. Removed the now-dead `excludeSymbol` param from `evaluatePaylines` (it only existed to stop the special double-paying alongside the scatter pay). **Effect:** a special on NON-adjacent reels (the screenshot: reels 1,3,4) forms no left-aligned run → pays $0; a special expanding across ADJACENT reels from column 1 pays the line wins (the Book climax); a single-reel stack pays $0. **Note:** this lowers free-spin RTP (the boosted ×5 scatter pay is gone) — intended, but a deliberate math change to recalibrate against. **UX residue:** below-adjacent expansions (non-adjacent reels) still VISUALLY morph their columns but pay nothing; gating the morph itself to adjacent-from-reel-1 is an optional follow-up. **`playedSpin`/reveal still emits the NATURAL board** so the client morph is untouched. Verified with a Node fixture over the real `PAYLINES`/`PAY_TABLE_LINE` (screenshot=0, adjacent=pays, single-column=0, below-gate=natural line). `node --check` GREEN. **The mock is Book of Borut's live RGS** (`services/test-server/server.mjs` imports it; Dockerfile copies it) — ships on a **test-server redeploy**, NOT an engine submodule bump. Files: `scripts/mock-rgs-server-book.mjs`.
- **Hardening: synthesize natural bounds for spines exported WITHOUT skeleton width/height: ✅ DONE (2026-06-22, engine — needs game republish).** Diagnosed the owner's free-spin-intro scroll being mis-sized/mis-anchored vs every other screen: the custom `R_FreeSpin` spine (editor-art upload) was exported with NO `skeleton.width/height` (`{"spine":"4.2"}`), whereas every working spine (fsIntro 1854×1862, bigwin 1600², anticipation 364×1670) has them. A bounds-less spine has no natural size, so its pivot pins to the origin (mis-anchor) and an authored width/height can't size it (`spineSizeScale` returns `{1,1}`). **The reliable fix is re-exporting the spine WITH bounds** (make the art visible in the setup pose); this is engine HARDENING so a future bounds-less upload degrades gracefully. New `packages/pixi-svelte/src/lib/spineBounds.ts` `spineNaturalBounds(data)` synthesizes the size by sampling the first animation across its duration (max extent), cached per `SkeletonData`, fully try/catch-guarded (returns `{0,0}` ⇒ callers keep prior behaviour). Used in `spineSizeScale` (final fallback so authored width/height sizes deterministically) and `SpineProvider` pivot (anchor by art instead of origin). Authored-bounds spines are byte-identical (they never enter the fallback). Builds GREEN: `pixi-svelte` + `lines`. **UNVERIFIED at runtime** — the spine runtime can't be exercised headlessly here (the measurement API — `state.setAnimation/update/apply` + `skeleton.updateWorldTransform(Physics.update)` + `getBounds`); owner verifies in-game after republish. Files: `packages/pixi-svelte/src/lib/{spineBounds.ts,utils.svelte.ts,components/SpineProvider.svelte}`.
- **Fix: free spins no longer start while the book symbol is still being chosen — the special-symbol reveal is now AWAITED: ✅ DONE (2026-06-23, engine `apps/lines` `d0cf2f1` + Book of Borut `1f8b087` — needs republish).** Owner saw, on a free-spin trigger: the FS intro played, then the free game started AT THE SAME TIME the book/expanding symbol was still shuffling/landing — the reveal overlapped (raced) the first free spin. **Root cause:** the `setExpandingSymbol` handler used fire-and-forget `eventEmitter.broadcast({ type:'specialBookReveal' })` and `SpecialBook.svelte`'s `shuffle()` returned `void` immediately while its `setTimeout` chain (24-frame decelerating shuffle → land → `bookIntro` spine) ran in the background. So the handler resolved in ~0ms and `playBookEvents` advanced straight to the next event — the first free-spin `reveal`/spin — before the book had landed. **Fix (two files, no XState/runner change — the event sequencing was already correct, only the handler failed to await its own animation):** (a) `apps/lines/src/components/SpecialBook.svelte` — `shuffle()` now returns a `Promise<void>` that resolves only when the reveal fully settles (the `bookIntro` spine's `oncomplete` flips `phase` intro→idle → `settleReveal()`); `hide()`/`onDestroy` also settle so the promise can never hang. (b) `apps/lines/src/game/bookEventHandlerMap.ts` — `setExpandingSymbol` now does `await eventEmitter.broadcastAsync({ type:'specialBookReveal', … })`; `broadcastAsync` `Promise.all`s subscriber return values, so it blocks on the reveal promise, so the next book event (first FS reveal) waits. Type-checks GREEN (`svelte-check`, 0 errors). **Not visually re-verified in a live bonus playthrough** (FS trigger is interactive — bonus bet + intro gate). **Mirrored into Book of Borut** (`src/components/SpecialBook.svelte` + `src/game/bookEventHandlerMap.ts`, commit `1f8b087` — Borut keeps its own game-code copies; this is NOT delivered by the engine submodule). **Owner step: republish Book of Borut via the launcher.** Files: `apps/lines/src/components/SpecialBook.svelte`, `apps/lines/src/game/bookEventHandlerMap.ts` (+ Borut equivalents).
- **Feature: Book-of free-spin expanding symbol now LANDS NATURAL → MORPHS → PAYS (Book of Thermopylae feel): ✅ DONE (2026-06-23, engine `apps/lines` + Book of Borut — needs game republish + submodule bump).** Previously the facade pre-baked the expansion straight INTO the free-spin reveal board (`stakeFacade.ts` mapped each special-bearing reel to a full column), so the special arrived already-expanded — the player never saw it land and morph. New behaviour, matching Book of Thermopylae: (1) reels stop on the NATURAL board (special in single positions, no payout yet); (2) AFTER the board lands, if **3+** of the special are on the board, each column containing the special morphs its non-special cells into the special symbol ONE BY ONE (staggered, reusing the per-symbol land plumbing — mutate `reelSymbol.rawSymbol` + set `symbolState='land'` so the special's `scatter_land` spine plays per cell); (3) ONLY THEN do the wins pay. Below 3 specials nothing morphs and it pays normally. **Impl — three pieces:** (a) **Math** `scripts/mock-rgs-server-book.mjs` `evaluateSpecial`: gate changed from "≥2 REELS" to "≥3 special SYMBOLS on the board" (matches the visual trigger); pay otherwise unchanged. (b) **Facade** `packages/rgs-translator-eagaming/src/stakeFacade.ts`: stop pre-filling — always emit the natural board; in `freegame` with ≥3 specials emit a NEW book event `{ type:'expandBookColumns', reels:number[], symbol }` AFTER the `reveal` and BEFORE `winInfo`/`flushWins`. (c) **Client** (Book of Borut `src/` + `apps/lines` reference): new `expandBookColumns` book event in `typesBookEvent.ts` + a handler in `bookEventHandlerMap.ts` that walks each flagged reel's visible cells (rows 1..`BOARD_DIMENSIONS.y`) and morphs each non-special cell to the special with a `0.18s` `waitForTimeout` stagger; the handler is `await`ed so following `winInfo` only animates after the morph completes (`playBookEvents` sequences + awaits each handler). Morph target on the client is `stateGame.expandingSymbol` (Borut) / `stateGame.specialSymbol` (lines); on the math/facade side `round.bonus.special`/`specialRaw`. Builds GREEN: `apps/lines` vite build + Book of Borut `pnpm build:engine` + `vite build`. **Owner steps: bump Book of Borut's `engine` submodule (mock + facade live in the engine repo) + republish via launcher.** Files: `scripts/mock-rgs-server-book.mjs`, `packages/rgs-translator-eagaming/src/stakeFacade.ts`, `apps/lines/src/game/{typesBookEvent.ts,bookEventHandlerMap.ts}`, Book of Borut `src/game/{typesBookEvent.ts,bookEventHandlerMap.ts}`.
- **Feature: Book-of land-then-morph — explosion during the morph + full-column win highlight: ✅ DONE (2026-06-23, engine `apps/lines` + Book of Borut — needs game republish + submodule bump).** Two enhancements on top of the land-then-morph mechanic above. **(1) Explosion during the morph.** Each cell now plays its EXISTING symbol's `explosion` spine BEFORE swapping to the book: the `explosion` symbol state (already in `SYMBOL_STATES`, bound on every symbol in `SYMBOL_INFO_MAP` to the standalone `{ assetKey:'explosion', animationName:'explosion' }` spine — the spine has exactly one animation, `explosion`, present on every symbol's binding) is set on the cell, awaited to completion via `waitForResolve((resolve)=>(reelSymbol.oncomplete=resolve))` (same pattern as scatter's `TumbleBoard` explode), THEN the cell swaps to the special with `symbolState='land'`. Per-cell sequence reads "symbol explodes → book appears"; staggered one cell at a time (`0.12s` `waitForTimeout` after each land). `ReelSymbol.svelte` updated so the `explosion` state fires `reelSymbol.oncomplete()` on spine complete and so `explosion` joins `land`/`win` in the `animating` predicate (renders on the unmasked animate layer, on top of neighbours). Sound: `sfx_wild_explode` per cell (existing sound, already used for the wild's `wildExplode` event). **(2) Full-column win highlight.** The special win's `positions` (math `evaluateSpecial` in `scripts/mock-rgs-server-book.mjs`) now span the FULL columns — every row of each reel in `reelsWith` — instead of only the landed cells. The of-a-kind count still gates on the landed-symbol total (≥3); only the `context` position set widened. Verified the indexing matches the morphed cells exactly: `reelsWith` and the facade's `expandBookColumns.reels` use the same "reel contains special" predicate, natural rows 0..2 pass through the facade's `winPositions` `row+1` padding shift to client visible rows 1..3, which is precisely the morph loop's cell set (`board[reel].symbols[1..BOARD_DIMENSIONS.y]`) and what `boardWithAnimateSymbols` lights as `win`. Confirmed byte-identical via a Node simulation of `evaluateSpecial`→`winPositions`→morph set. Builds GREEN: `apps/lines` vite build + Book of Borut `pnpm build:engine` + `vite build`; `node --check` on the mock. **Owner steps: bump Book of Borut's `engine` submodule (mock lives in the engine repo) + republish via launcher.** Files: `scripts/mock-rgs-server-book.mjs`, `apps/lines/src/game/bookEventHandlerMap.ts`, `apps/lines/src/components/ReelSymbol.svelte`, Book of Borut `src/game/bookEventHandlerMap.ts`, `src/components/ReelSymbol.svelte`.
- **TEMP DEBUG: spine size instrumentation (`[IW-SPINE]` console logs) — REMOVE after the free-spin-intro sizing is settled.** Owner's free-spin-intro scroll is bigger in-game than the editor even with box (1422×800) + game space matching. To settle whether it's a real editor↔game mismatch or just window-scale, both render paths now log the spine's MAIN-box-unit size (`natural × nodeScale`, BEFORE the `<MainContainer>` window scale) when `window.__IW_SPINE_DEBUG__ = true`: `SpineProvider` (game, `[IW-SPINE game]`) and `EditorSpineLayer` (editor, `[IW-SPINE editor]`, throttled once-per-node). Compare `mainBoxUnit`: MATCH ⇒ pure window scale (not a bug — size it smaller); DIFFER ⇒ real mismatch and the `natural`/`nodeScale` columns localize it. Engine half reaches Borut via submodule bump; editor half via launcher redeploy. Remove both logs once diagnosed. Files: `packages/pixi-svelte/src/lib/components/SpineProvider.svelte`, `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/editor/EditorSpineLayer.svelte`.
- **Fix: phantom "4th row" of spine animations below the reels (clipped by the info-bar) when the editor reel-grid pitch ≠ `SYMBOL_SIZE`: ✅ DONE (2026-06-23, engine `apps/lines` + Book of Borut — needs game republish).** Owner saw, in-game, a row of spine effects (e.g. money-bag/scatter land FX) hanging BELOW the visible reel window, half-cut by the bottom HUD background. **Root cause:** the board renders two layers (`Board.svelte`) — a MASKED static layer and an UNMASKED animate layer where land/win spines draw. Off-window symbols on the animate layer are culled by a per-symbol JS bound in `SymbolWrap.svelte`, NOT a Pixi mask. That bound was hardcoded `bottom = SYMBOL_SIZE * BOARD_DIMENSIONS.y` (fixed 360), while `BoardMask` had already been updated to use the reactive `boardGeometry().rowPitchLocal` (the editor reel-grid override's `cellHeight + gapY`). When an authored reel-grid sets a row pitch SHORTER than `SYMBOL_SIZE` (120), the bottom padding/buffer row's symbol (parked at `3.5 × pitch`) fell UNDER the stale fixed 360 bound and rendered on the unmasked animate layer — the masked static layer hid it correctly, so only the SPINES leaked, exactly matching the report. **Fix:** `SymbolWrap`'s `bottom` now tracks the same reactive pitch as the mask (`BOARD_DIMENSIONS.y * boardGeometry().rowPitchLocal`); since the buffer row sits at `3.5 × pitch` it is always above the window bound `3 × pitch` regardless of override. No override ⇒ `rowPitchLocal === SYMBOL_SIZE` ⇒ byte-identical to before. **Scope:** `SymbolWrap`/`BoardMask` are per-game app components (games copy `src/` from a template), so the fix lands in BOTH `apps/lines` (engine reference) AND Book of Borut's own `src/components/SymbolWrap.svelte` — a `vite build` of the game picks it up directly (NOT an engine-submodule bump). **Owner step: republish Book of Borut via the launcher.** Files: `apps/lines/src/components/SymbolWrap.svelte`, Book of Borut `src/components/SymbolWrap.svelte`.
- **Feature: spine one-shot → idle hand-off (intro plays once, then loops idle): ✅ DONE (2026-06-22, engine + editor — needs game republish).** Owner wanted a free-spin-intro spine to play `intro` once then settle into a looping `idle` (held until the gate's tap). The runtime played a SINGLE animation on track 0 (`sigAnim.animation ?? defaultAnimation`) and held the last frame — no hand-off, and a cue with no explicit loop defaulted to looping (`?? node.loop ?? true`), so the intro never ended. **Design (no new editor field — uses existing controls):** when a spine has BOTH an active signal cue (`enter → intro`) AND a DISTINCT `defaultAnimation` (the idle), play the cue animation ONCE then queue the looping default. Author setup = **Default animation = idle (loop on) + Enter cue = intro**; the gate (dim + hide-prompt) holds the screen until tap. **Impl:** `SpineTrack` gained `then`/`thenLoop` (queues via Spine `addAnimation(…, delay 0)`), with a guard that re-applies only when the INTENDED animation changes — not when the live track advances to the queued `then` (else it would restart the primary). No-`then` callers are byte-identical (the extra guard clause is always true when `then` is undefined). `LayoutNodeView` passes `then = defaultAnimation` + `loop=false` when `handsOffToIdle`. `EditorSpineLayer.syncPlayback` mirrors it (intro once → `addAnimation(idle)`) so the preview shows the real flow. Builds GREEN: `pixi-svelte` + `engine-layout` + `launcher-api` + `lines`. Editor half deploys with the launcher; runtime half reaches Borut via the engine submodule bump + republish. Files: `packages/pixi-svelte/src/lib/components/SpineTrack.svelte`, `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/LayoutNodeView.svelte`, `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/editor/EditorSpineLayer.svelte`.
- **Fix: Rigger "Can't remove this vertex (non-manifold ring)" — the mesh re-triangulator was folding triangles: ✅ DONE (2026-06-23, launcher-api `static/rigger/view.html`).** Owner repro: create mesh → add ~15 interior points → ⟁ Re-triangulate → − Remove vertex fails on a vertex (move/delete/retriangulate didn't clear it). **Root cause (the big one):** `cdtTriangulate`'s Delaunay **flip emitted both new triangles with REVERSED winding** (`[a,c,d]`/`[b,d,c]` instead of `[a,d,c]`/`[b,c,d]`). Every flip produced CW triangles in a CCW mesh; `removeMeshVertex` walks the *directed* fan edges to close a one-ring, and inconsistent winding makes that ring fail to chain → "non-manifold ring". The clicked vertex was usually an innocent corner of a flipped triangle — which is why moving/deleting it never helped. Two secondary gaps also fixed: a point landing **outside the outline** hit a centroid fallback that split the nearest triangle anyway (a real fold — now interior points outside the outline are dropped pre-triangulation via ray-cast `pointInHull`, with an "Ignored N points outside the outline" notice); a point **on an existing edge** made a T-junction + sliver (now `splitTrisOnEdge` splits BOTH triangles sharing the edge). Plus a safety net: `removeMeshVertex` now **falls back to a full re-weave** (drop the vertex → CDT the rest) whenever a fan isn't a clean disk, so a delete can never be blocked again; `cdtTriangulate` also drops exact-degenerate triangles. **Verified headlessly** in `tools/rigger-spike/retriangulate-cdt.mjs` (cactus + dense-grid + outside-point + on-edge), AND by extracting the *shipped* `cdtTriangulate`/`pointInHull` from `view.html` and asserting no inverted triangles + every interior vertex has a removable one-ring, across all cases incl. the 15-point repro. Static-tool change — reaches the live `/rigger` on the next launcher redeploy; the owner's existing folded mesh becomes clean on one ⟁ Re-triangulate (or the stuck vertex deletes directly via the fallback). Files: `apps/launcher-api/static/rigger/view.html`, `tools/rigger-spike/retriangulate-cdt.mjs`.
- **Fix: editor spine preview now auto-plays a spine's `enter` cue (WYSIWYG sizing): ✅ DONE (2026-06-22, launcher-api editor).** Owner's Free-spin intro scroll looked correctly sized in the editor but ~2× too big in-game. Diagnosed from the live Borut doc + baked bundle: the box matches (1422×800), game space is set, and the custom `freeSpinIntroVisual` def (scope shared, baked) DOES win over the coded one (`registerBakedComponents()` at `Game.svelte:250` runs after the coded `registerComponents` at `:185`). The real gap: the spine `R_FreeSpin` has NO `defaultAnimation`, only an `enter→intro` cue; in-game the `intro` animation plays on appearance (once the enter-cue firing fix `62344bd` is deployed), but `EditorSpineLayer.syncPlayback` only played `defaultAnimation || firstAnimation` — NOT the `enter` cue. So the author sized `0.496` against the static setup pose while the game shows the (larger) animated pose. **Fix:** `EditorSpineLayer` now reads each spine node's `enter` cue (`spineEnterCue`) into the render target (`enterAnimation`/`enterLoop`) and `syncPlayback` AUTO-plays it (looped, taking precedence over the default) for both top-level and component-nested spines — so the preview shows exactly what the game plays on appearance. Spines with no enter cue are unchanged (static until toggled). Builds GREEN: `pnpm --filter launcher-api build`. Editor-only (deploys with the launcher); the author then re-tunes the spine scale against the now-truthful animated preview. File: `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/editor/EditorSpineLayer.svelte`.
- **Fix: authored component's `enter` cue never fired when its GATED SCREEN appeared (intro animation stuck on setup pose): ✅ DONE (2026-06-22, engine — needs game republish to reach Borut).** Owner authored a Free-spin intro: a `componentInstance` (WANTED scroll spine `R_FreeSpin` with an `enter`→`intro` cue + "10 FREESPINS" text) inside a Scene Editor SCREEN gated `Shows during: Free-spin intro` (`scene.visibleSource = freeSpinIntroShow`). In-game the spine sat frozen on its setup pose — the intro never played. **Root cause:** `ComponentInstance` fires its self-`enter` signal on the edge of the INSTANCE'S OWN visibility (`!visibilitySource || liveVisible`), driven only by the instance's OWN `visibleSource` param — which is empty here (the gate is on the SCREEN, not the instance). So the instance's visibility was always `true`; `LayoutScene` hides a gated screen with `<Container visible>` (children stay MOUNTED, just undrawn), so the instance mounted at BOOT, fired `enter` once while the screen was hidden, and never again. The component never learned the screen's gate opened. **Fix:** new `sceneVisibilityContext` — `LayoutScene` publishes its live `liveVisible` via `setSceneVisibleContext(() => liveVisible)`; `ComponentInstance` combines it into a `selfVisible = (!ownSource || liveVisible) && sceneVisible()` and fires `enter` on THAT edge (and re-publishes the combined value so nested components gate correctly too). Now `enter` fires when the SCREEN appears. **Parity:** ungated screen ⇒ context `() => true` ⇒ identical to before; components with no `enter` cue or their own gate are unchanged (`lines` builds GREEN). Verified by build + code-path trace, NOT a live render (the gated intro can't be exercised in this headless env) — owner verifies after Borut republishes with the bumped engine submodule. NOT to be confused with the separate POSITION issue (the visual must be in a `game`-space screen, not `canvas`, to scale board-relative). Files: new `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/sceneVisibilityContext.ts`; `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/{LayoutScene.svelte,ComponentInstance.svelte}`.
- **Fix: Component Editor previewed in the wrong coordinate box (editor ≠ game placement) + new full-window overlay space: ✅ DONE (2026-06-22).** Owner: an authored "Free-spin intro" component (spine + text + a full-screen Rect dim) looked different in-game than in the Component Editor. **Root cause (traced):** the Rect math (`width×scale`, anchor, alpha) is unified editor↔runtime, but the FRAME wasn't. The **Component Editor hardcoded `mainSizesMap = STANDARD_MAIN_SIZES_MAP` (1920×1080)** and previewed at 1:1, while the runtime mounts a component inside the game's real MAIN box (Borut desktop = **1422×800**, `referenceLayouts/bookof.ts`) under `<MainContainer>`, scaled to the window — and the **Scene Editor already previews through the project's real box** (`data.doc.mainSizesMap`). So component placement/size was NOT standardized: the Component Editor was the lone divergent surface. **Fix (preview-only for game space; additive for canvas):** (1) Component Editor now feeds `EditorCanvas` the project's `mainSizesMap` (from `loadDoc`) instead of STANDARD — same wiring as the Scene Editor — so game-space components are WYSIWYG against the project's box (frame stays the STANDARD window; `mainScale` maps box→window exactly like the runtime). NO saved-doc change; existing components now PREVIEW at their true in-game size/position (they may look bigger/shifted — that's the game truth the 1:1 box was hiding). (2) New optional `ComponentDef.space` (`'game'` default | `'canvas'`) + a **Space** select in the Component Editor header: `canvas` previews the component full-window (raw window pixels, no MainContainer) for overlays like a free-spin dim — matching a runtime `space:'canvas'` screen. Persisted via `normalizeComponent`. **Runtime unchanged** (engine-layout doesn't read `space`; a component still inherits its host scene's space — so mount a canvas component in a canvas-space screen for editor↔game parity). Verified: `space==='game'` is behaviorally identical to the previous `undefined` in every `EditorCanvas`/`childLocalTransform` branch (no branch tests `'game'`). Builds GREEN: `engine-layout` + `launcher-api`. **Known follow-up:** a fixed-size Rect in canvas space won't auto-fill a non-16:9 window like the coded `CanvasSizeRectangle` dim — a `fillCanvas` rect option is the responsive-overlay next step. Files: `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/types.ts` (`ComponentDef.space`), `apps/launcher-api/src/lib/server/componentStorage.ts`, `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/components/{+page.server.ts,+page.svelte}`.
- **Dedup audit: consolidated shared copy-paste across the two editors: ✅ DONE (2026-06-22).** Follow-up to the `EditorElementsPalette` extraction (`9488aa3`) — an audit found ~330 lines still duplicated (independent copies, no shared state) between the Scene Editor (`/editor`) and Component Editor (`/components`). Extracted three shared pieces under `editor/`: (1) **`EditorAssetLibrary.svelte`** — the Atlases/Spines/Sheets Library sections + drag/expand handlers + region state; the two pages had drifted into two region-dropdown looks, converged on the Scene Editor's richer `.region-grid` (▾▸ caret + natural-size-annotated titles) per owner choice; props `assets` + `bind:expanded` (editor persists UI state, `/components` doesn't) + optional `spineActions` snippet (editor injects its Upload-spines button; `/components` omits it). (2) **`ComponentList.svelte`** + **`componentList.client.ts`** (shared `CATEGORIES` + `groupComponents()`, reusing `engine-layout`'s `ComponentCategory`) — the grouped `.cmp-row` list was implemented 3× (`EditorComponentPanel` + inline in `/components` + dup CATEGORIES/grouped); now one component with an `actions(def)` snippet (place/open↗ for the panel, ✕-delete for `/components`). (3) **`layoutTree.client.ts`** — byte-identical `findById`/`removeNode`/`genComponentId`/`flattenSceneIds`. Left per-page (different write targets): `onSpawn`, `onRenameNode`/`renameNode`, palette `onElementDragStart`. Net **−249 lines**; `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN, svelte-check no new errors. Live on launcher redeploy. Files: new `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/editor/{EditorAssetLibrary,ComponentList}.svelte` + `{componentList,layoutTree}.client.ts`; edited `editor/+page.svelte`, `components/+page.svelte`, `editor/EditorComponentPanel.svelte`.
- **Fix: Rect element had no Properties colour control (couldn't recolour in either editor): ✅ DONE (2026-06-22).** The 2026-06-20 Rect entry below claimed a "Properties Colour field", but the per-kind property chain in `EditorProperties.svelte` (`sprite → spine → reelGrid → text`) had no `rect` branch — so `setRectColor` was dead code and a placed Rect showed only the shared Transform section (no fill control). Owner hit this in the Component Editor ("can't change colour or opacity of the Rect"). Added an `{:else if node.kind === 'rect'}` section: colour picker (`setRectColor`), width/height (`setSpriteSize`, already rect-aware), and a note that opacity = the Transform `alpha` (already wired — canvas + runtime honour it). `EditorProperties.svelte` is shared by BOTH the Scene Editor (`/editor`) and Component Editor (`/components`), so the one edit fixes both. **GOTCHA (cost a round-trip): the first attempt (`fd2611d`) mis-nested the `{:else if}` one tab too deep — it attached to an inner `{#if}` INSIDE the text branch instead of the kind-chain, so it was unreachable for a `rect` node. It compiled clean AND the string shipped in the live bundle (so a byte-level "is it deployed?" check FALSELY confirmed it), yet never rendered. Lesson: for a Svelte `{:else if}` in a long chain, verify the BRANCH INDENTATION matches its siblings (`cat -A` the `{#if}`/`{:else if}`/`{/if}` lines) — a deployed-bundle grep does not prove a branch is reachable.** Re-nested to the chain level (sibling of `text`). Builds GREEN. File: `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/editor/EditorProperties.svelte`.
- **Rect element — solid fill primitive (resizable / recolourable / opacity): ✅ DONE (2026-06-20, engine `e7659a2`, Borut `25b88c3`).** The editor had only Text/Container/Reel; added a `RectNode { width; height; color? }` fill block (full-screen dims, panels, colour blocks). Runtime: `LayoutNodeView` renders a vector `<Rectangle>` (pixi Graphics) so it scales losslessly; `color` → fill (default `0xffffff`), `transform.alpha` → opacity, anchor defaults `{0.5,0.5}` (matched across the editor's 2D draw, `nodeBox` hit-test, spawn, and the runtime so an anchor-less rect lands identically editor↔game). Editor: "Rect" in the ELEMENTS palette (drag to place → spawns 400×400 white), 2D canvas flat-fill draw, `nodeBox` frames by width/height (handles resize), Properties Colour field; `editorStorage` NODE_KINDS + `normalizeScene` persist it. Pairs with the authorable gate: place a Rect as your own dim (resize full-screen, set colour+opacity) with the gate's "Dim opacity 0" + "Hide default prompt" so the gate is 100% authored. Editor live on launcher redeploy; in-game render after Borut republish (submodule bumped). Files: `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/{types.ts,LayoutNodeView.svelte,resolveTransform.ts}`, `apps/launcher-api/src/lib/server/editorStorage.ts`, `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/editor/{+page.svelte,EditorCanvas.svelte,EditorProperties.svelte,editorCanvas.helpers.ts}`.

- **Author-overridable free-spin gate LOOK (`Scene.gate`): ✅ DONE (2026-06-20, engine `0ec01b3`, Borut `6f2ef5e`).** The engine still OWNS the press-to-continue hold + full-screen tap; only its dim + default prompt are now custom per game. Added `Scene.gate?: { dimColor?: number; dimAlpha?: number; hidePrompt?: boolean }`: `dimColor`/`dimAlpha` restyle the full-window dim (defaults `0x000000`/`0.5`; `alpha 0` = no dim), `hidePrompt` hides the default "press anywhere to continue" so an authored continue graphic in the screen stands in (tap + Space still resolve). `PressToContinue` gates only its visible prompt sprite behind `hidePrompt` — the `OnPressFullScreen` catcher + Space hotkey always mount. `Game.svelte` derives the scene gated to `freeSpinIntroShow`/`freeSpinOutroShow` and passes its `gate.*` to the matching always-mounted `FreeSpinIntroGate`/`FreeSpinOutroGate`; no scene/gate ⇒ prior defaults (byte-identical). Editor: Scene properties shows Dim colour / Dim opacity / Hide-default-prompt fields when "Shows during" is a blocking state (`freeSpinIntroShow`/`freeSpinOutroShow`); `normalizeScene` preserves `gate`. Owner republishes Borut for the in-game effect; editor fields live on launcher redeploy.

- **Engine-owned free-spin intro/outro press-to-continue HOLD: ✅ DONE (2026-06-20, engine `372eecf`, Borut `9ac0273`).** Completes the "author your own free-spin intro" arc: a screen gated via `Scene.visibleSource = 'freeSpinIntroShow'`/`'freeSpinOutroShow'` now HOLDS the round (dim + press-to-continue) with NO gate component to place. Root cause it fixes: the intro window (`stateUi.freeSpinIntroShow` true) only spans `await broadcastAsync(freeSpinIntroUpdate)`, which was held open ONLY by a mounted coded `FreeSpinIntroGate`; an authored doc that has the visual but not the gate → the await resolves instantly → the gated screen flashes. **Fix:** `Game.svelte` (lines + Borut) ALWAYS mounts exactly one `<FreeSpinIntroGate>` + one `<FreeSpinOutroGate>` (engine-owned, full-screen, self-show on the events, own the `waitForResolve`). The reference layout + editor templates (`referenceLayouts/lines.ts`, `templates/lines.ts`) no longer bind a gate or the gate-bundling `FreeSpinIntro`/`FreeSpinOutro` composer — they bind the visual-only `FreeSpinIntroVisual`/`FreeSpinOutroVisual`. **INVARIANT (load-bearing): the gate must mount EXACTLY ONCE — two `waitForResolve` subscribers + one tap = the round HANGS.** ⚠️ Follow-up: `referenceLayouts/bookof.ts` + Borut's `defaultLayout.ts` (owner WIP) still use the composer, so on the FALLBACK path (authored-doc fetch fails) the gate would double → hang; align those to visual-only too. Owner verifies in-game after republish (intro shows → dims → waits for tap → free spins start). Files: `apps/lines/src/components/Game.svelte`, `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/referenceLayouts/lines.ts`, `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/templates/lines.ts` (+ Borut Game.svelte mirror).

- **Publish hardening — editor exports now SERVER-SIDE COPY in R2 (durable fix for the bake-export 502/OOM): ✅ DONE (2026-06-22, engine `1c73386`).** Root-cause fix for the launcher OOM behind the bake-retry band-aid below. The three export endpoints (`/api/editor/export-art|fonts|symbols`) used to `getObjectBytes()` → `putObjectBytes()` every referenced atlas page, spine page, and font file — i.e. load each whole object (2K–4K textures, multi-MB) into the Node heap and re-upload it. Three exports back-to-back (or the bake's retries) stacked those buffers and OOM-killed the Railway container → edge `502 upstream error`. **Fix:** `r2.ts` `copyObject()` now does a server-side R2 `CopyObject` (bytes never enter this process → peak memory stays flat), returns `false` on a missing source (mirrors `getObjectBytes`'s null-on-404 so callers still skip cleanly), and takes an optional `contentType` override (used for the Rigger `.irig`→`.json` skeleton rename). Every verbatim copy in `editorArtExport.ts` (pages + standalone images), `symbolExport.ts` (sheet pages), `fontExport.ts` (font files), and `spine.ts` `exportSpineBundle` (skeleton + page images) switched to it. Atlas `.atlas` text is still read in-process (small, and needed to enumerate page names). `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN; exports verified producing correct output against prod (`bookofborut`, art 200/~11s, fonts 200/~3s, symbols 200/~7s, incl. a parallel-burst stress). The bake `fetchRetry()` stays as belt-and-suspenders. Optional extra cushion: raise the launcher's Railway memory limit. Files: `apps/launcher-api/src/lib/server/{r2,editorArtExport,symbolExport,fontExport,spine}.ts`.
- **Publish hardening — the editor-doc bake now RETRIES transient launcher 5xx: ✅ DONE (2026-06-22, engine `9f2cbb0`, Borut `993da40`).** Owner's Book of Borut "Build & publish" failed with `Symbols export failed: HTTP 502 — upstream error`, then on retry `Font export failed: HTTP 502` — a *different* export each run. Root cause is **server-side**, not the game: the bake fires three heavy export POSTs back-to-back (`editor-art → fonts → symbols`, each loads full atlas pages / spine bundles into memory) and the launcher container OOMs/restarts mid-export → Railway's edge returns a proxy `502 upstream error`. Probed live: a FRESH container completes ONE export (~12s) but back-to-back calls degrade (next 502s at ~8s, then flaps); so retrying the publish just moved the failure to whichever export landed while the process was down. The bake's `--optional`-with-token guard CORRECTLY refused to ship the default layout each time (that part worked as designed). **Fix:** `apps/launcher-api/scripts/bake-editor-doc.mjs` gained `fetchRetry()` — retry network errors + transient 5xx/429 with backoff spaced to straddle a container restart (5s/15s/30s); deterministic 4xx bail immediately. Export POSTs are idempotent (prune + rewrite `deploy/`), so retrying is safe. Wired into the doc fetch, all three export POSTs, and the localization fetch. Verified live: a real bake against prod hit a symbols 502, retried after 5s, succeeded, baked the full bundle (exit 0). **Still open (optional):** (a) the launcher instability itself — durable fix shipped, see the entry above (exports now server-side-copy in R2 instead of buffering pages); raising the Railway memory limit is still a cheap extra cushion; (b) Borut packs **three** overlapping `S_StaticElements*` sheets (`atlas_manifest_S_StaticElements` [primary], `_S_StaticElements3D`, and `sheets/S_StaticElements`) — the bake collision warnings flag them; all three have LIVE placements so none is a safe blind delete. Cheapest consolidation = re-bind the 2 nodes on `sheets/S_StaticElements` (`basegame` T_UI_Slots, `hudBar` T_UI_Bar) onto the equivalent regions in a kept manifest sheet, then delete that sheet — reduces export memory pressure. Files: `apps/launcher-api/scripts/bake-editor-doc.mjs`.

- **Engine values for authored free-spin intro/outro text: ✅ DONE (2026-06-20, engine `32fa34b`, Borut `45e6439`).** So an author rebuilding the intro/outro as custom components can bind text to the same values the coded ones showed. Added `freeSpinsWon` (NUMBER) to `ENGINE_PARAM_CATALOG` + `registerComponentValues(() => stateUi.freeSpinCounterTotal)` — the intro headline count, with the count now set EARLY in `freeSpinTrigger` (before the intro broadcast) so it's populated while the intro is on screen (it was otherwise set after the intro hides). Also WIRED `totalWin` (was in the catalog but never fed → binding it showed nothing) to the win-meter `winBookEventAmount` (currency) — the outro win total. Mirrored into Book of Borut (Game.svelte + bookEventHandlerMap) + engine submodule bumped. Author flow: intro screen → text node bound to `Free Spins Won`; outro screen → text node bound to `Total Win`. Owner republishes Borut to see it. Files: `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/componentCatalog.ts`, `apps/lines/src/{components/Game.svelte,game/bookEventHandlerMap.ts}` (+ Borut equivalents).

- **Screen-level lifecycle gating — an authored SCREEN can now show only during a game state (owner: "a spine I add to a component is always on screen, it doesn't follow the free-spin rules"): ✅ DONE engine+editor+lines+Borut (2026-06-20).** Root cause: the game renders editor scenes unconditionally; a component only hides if it has a `visibleSource` bound to a registered `BoolSource`, and there was NO "free-spin intro/outro active" feed to bind to — so authored overlay content rendered always (only the CODED intro self-hides via book events). The `Enter`/`Exit`/`Idle` signal checkboxes are dormant in v1 (only `Win`/`BigWin` wired, animation-only) so they don't gate either. **Fix (screen-level, the owner's chosen model):** (1) `Scene.visibleSource` + `LayoutScene` wraps the whole screen in `<Container visible>` when bound; (2) `VISIBILITY_SOURCE_KEYS`/`VISIBILITY_SOURCE_LABELS` gain `freeSpinIntroShow`/`freeSpinOutroShow`/`freeGameShow`/`winShow`/`bigWinShow`/`baseGameShow`; (3) `stateUi` intro/outro/win/bigWin flags set in `bookEventHandlerMap` next to the existing show↔hide broadcasts, `registerComponentVisibility` for all six in Game.svelte (free/base-game from `gameType`); (4) editor Scene properties gets a "Shows during" dropdown → `Scene.visibleSource`, and `normalizeScene` PRESERVES the field on save. Commits: engine `56ef0d3` (foundation), `179523d` (lines registration), `10e1650` (editor dropdown + persist); **Book of Borut `5276762`** (engine submodule bumped to `10e1650` + mirrored registration). **Owner steps:** after the launcher redeploys, in the editor set the "Free-spin intro" screen → *Shows during: Free-spin intro* and save; then **republish Book of Borut** to verify in-game (the screen appears only during the intro). NOTE: the coded `FreeSpinIntro` also exists — if you gate your authored screen you'll want to drop the coded one (Borut's `defaultLayout.ts` WIP already notes this) to avoid a double-render. Signal checkboxes cleaned up (engine `9812986`, Borut `7301f00`): `Enter` is now WIRED — the component self-fires it when it becomes visible (mount / `visibleSource` gate opening), playing each spine's `enter` cue (intro-on-appear, pairs with the gating); dormant `Exit`/`Idle` removed from `ENGINE_SIGNAL_CATALOG` (instant-hide cuts an exit anim; idle had no trigger). So an author flow for an intro overlay: gate the screen to *Free-spin intro* + tick the `Enter` signal + add a spine cue (`enter → intro animation`). Files: `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/{types.ts,componentCatalog.ts,LayoutScene.svelte}`, `packages/state-shared/src/stateUi.svelte.ts`, `apps/lines/src/{components/Game.svelte,game/bookEventHandlerMap.ts}`, `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/editor/+page.svelte`, `apps/launcher-api/src/lib/server/editorStorage.ts`.
- **Free-spin payline win TEXT showed ~100× too small on a BOUGHT feature (owner report: "max I've seen is $0.03 but the win is over $200 — conversion bug in the free-spin payline text"): ✅ FIXED (2026-06-20, engine facade `packages/rgs-translator-eagaming/src/stakeFacade.ts` `a782623`; Book of Borut submodule bumped `e1cfa98` → owner just needs to republish).** This is a SEPARATE cause from the mock double-pay fix below (line ~37) — that removed a spurious tiny line win; this one mis-converts a REAL win's display. **Root cause:** `adaptEventsForStake` set its bet-multiplier denominator from the `bet` event's debited `total`. Win `pay` amounts, though, are denominated against the BASE bet (`betPerLine × paylines`). For a TRIGGERED round the two are equal, so display is correct — but a BOUGHT feature debits a premium (`total = baseBet × 100` in the book-of mock), so dividing every win by that premium-inflated `total` shrinks the on-screen amount ~100× (a $12.50 line win renders $0.25; tiny base wins collapse toward $0.01–$0.03). Money credited is unaffected (balance uses raw cents) — display-only, hence ">$200 paid but $0.03 shown". **Fix:** derive the denominator as `betPerLine × paylines.length` (both ship in the real Play4Fun `bet` event AND the mock; the mock even exposes `inGameBet = baseBet`), falling back to `total` only if those fields are absent. Renamed the local `betTotalCents → betBaseCents` so the intent can't regress. Applied to the per-win flush AND the total/win-level/finalWin conversions, so the whole bought round (per-line + total + big-win tier) is correct. **Verified** with a Node harness driving the book mock through the facade's exact request sequence: BUY round per-line wins went $0.25/$0.75 → **$12.50/$75.00** (== the triggered-round values), TRIGGERED rounds byte-unchanged (denominator ratio 1). **Owner step:** republish Book of Borut via the launcher (engine submodule already bumped to `a782623` in Borut `e1cfa98`). File: `packages/rgs-translator-eagaming/src/stakeFacade.ts`.

- **Editor: spines NESTED inside a component instance now render in the Scene Editor preview (owner report: "added a spine to my component, can't see it in the scene editor"): ✅ DONE (2026-06-20, launcher-api editor).** Symptom: a `kind:'spine'` placed inside a component showed correctly in the Component Editor but only a `spine: <name>` placeholder box when that component was dropped as a `componentInstance` in the Scene Editor. Root cause: the WebGL spine layer was the ONLY render layer that never recursed into component instances. Both the mount gate `sceneHasSpine()` (`EditorCanvas`) and the target collector `spineTargets()` (`EditorSpineLayer`) walked **direct** scene nodes only — so the layer never mounted, the spine's `assetKey` never entered `readySpineKeys`, and the 2D canvas fell back to its placeholder. (The text overlay already recursed via `nodesHaveText`/`collectTextTargets`; the spine layer just never got the same treatment.) **Fix (EDITOR-PREVIEW ONLY — no doc/runtime/pipeline change):** made both recursive, reusing the SAME shared composition helpers the 2D canvas + text overlay use, so nested spines land identically by construction — `composeWorldMatrix(chain, nodeTransform-as-topT, childLocalTransform-as-childT)` decomposed to skeleton x/y/scale (rotation ignored as the top-level spine path already does; single-axis flip preserved via the matrix determinant). `EditorSpineLayer` gained `componentMap` + `worldTransformOf` props (fed `nodeTransform`); a nested target carries a precomposed `world` transform that the render loop places directly, skipping the top-level space/placement mapping. Builds GREEN: `pnpm --filter launcher-api build`. NOTE: the publish pipeline was ALREADY correct for this — `editorArtExport.ts` walks every referenced component-def root (line 175) with transitive closure, so a component-instance spine has always shipped + registered (verified: the live Borut bundle carries `editorArt.spines` + its register loop); only the editor PREVIEW was missing it. Files: `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/editor/EditorSpineLayer.svelte`, `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/editor/EditorCanvas.svelte`.
- **Editor: canvas-space nodes WITHOUT a `screenAnchor` were game-mapped (≠ the game) — fixed so the editor positions them raw, matching the runtime (owner: "components I create from scratch never have the right selection box"): ✅ FIXED (2026-06-20, launcher-api editor `nodeTransform`).** Captured real probe data (no `screenAnchor`; spine bounds origin-centred — both earlier theories wrong) and found the actual bug: `EditorCanvas.nodeTransform()` had `if (space === 'canvas' && t.screenAnchor)` then **fell through to the game-space `mainToWorld` + `mainScale` mapping** for a canvas node WITHOUT a screenAnchor. But the game renders canvas-space scenes with NO `<MainContainer>` (verified `LayoutScene.svelte` — only `game`/`standard` get one), so the runtime + the spine render use RAW window coords there. So a component dropped into a canvas-space scene (no screenAnchor) was placed + boxed + scaled differently in the editor than it ships — "components never work". **Fix:** `nodeTransform` now returns for ALL canvas space — `screenAnchor ? screenAnchor·frame + xy : raw xy` — never the game mapping; matches `LayoutNodeView` (`posX = screenAnchor ? screenAnchor·canvas + x : x`) and the spine canvas branch. Probes removed in the same change; builds GREEN. NOTE: existing canvas-space content positioned under the old mapping will shift once to its true (game-matching) spot — reposition once. This is the positioning-parity half; the explicit-component-`frame` standardization (author-set artboard, recorded on `ComponentDef`) is the box-tightness/authoring half, still to build. Owner to verify in the deployed editor + in-game. File: `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/editor/EditorCanvas.svelte`.
- **Bonus-trigger spin win is now banked into the WIN meter BEFORE the free-spin intro (owner report: trigger-reel paylines animate with per-line amounts "but nothing really gets paid out"): ✅ DONE (2026-06-20, engine facade `stakeFacade.ts`; Book of Borut submodule bumped to `5806934` + needs republish).** Diagnosis: presentation-only — the money was always paid. On a base spin that pays line/scatter wins AND enters the bonus, the mock server pools every win into `round.win` and credits it at `collect` (round end), so the trigger-spin wins are real, just deferred.
