But the Play4Fun→Stake facade only emitted `setTotalWin` (which sets `stateBet.winBookEventAmount`, the WIN meter) on `freegame` spins — never on the basegame trigger spin (it `break`s straight into the bonus with no `gameEnd`). So the trigger-spin wins lit up the paylines via `winInfo` (glow + sound only), then the free-spin intro took over and the meter caught up only at round end via `gameEnd`→`setTotalWin` — reading as "shown but never paid". **Option A fix (genre-faithful — keep paying the trigger-spin lines, just make the meter honest):** `adaptEventsForStake` now also banks the running total on the trigger spin — condition widened to `gameType === 'freegame' || totalFs > 0` (a `spinTrigger` seen while still basegame ⇒ trigger spin), pushing `setTotalWin` after the `winInfo` flush and before `freeSpinTrigger`. The running total only grows, so the final round total and RTP are untouched. Verified with a Node harness driving the book mock with `forceTrigger`: stream is now `reveal → winInfo×4 → setTotalWin(2150) → freeSpinTrigger → … → setTotalWin(9200) → finalWin` (the banked 2150 precedes the intro; final 9200 ≥ 2150). Files: `packages/rgs-translator-eagaming/src/stakeFacade.ts`.

- **Editor: full-screen overlays (Free-spin intro/outro) now PREVIEW their full-window dim behind the centred frame: ✅ DONE (2026-06-20, launcher-api editor + `engine-layout` catalog).** Follow-up to the spine-preview-offset fix below. In-game the intro/outro gate darkens the whole window (50% black `CanvasSizeRectangle`) behind a centred medallion frame; the editor drew ONLY the frame, so authors couldn't see the full-screen effect (owner: "in game they don't cover the whole screen as they should" — the cover is the gate's dim, which the preview omitted). **Change (EDITOR-PREVIEW ONLY — no doc/runtime change):** added `overlayDim?: number` to `BoundComponentDefault` (`boundComponentCatalog.ts`), set `0.5` on `FreeSpinIntro`/`FreeSpinOutro` only, + helper `boundComponentOverlayDim(node)`. `EditorSpineLayer` takes an `activeSceneId` prop and, when THIS spine layer is the active scene's, clears its WebGL canvas to translucent black (`gl.clearColor(0,0,0,scrim)`) instead of transparent — that scrim composites (by scene-order z-index) OVER the board + spine-background layers beneath it, while the overlay's own frame spine draws into the same canvas afterwards at full brightness. Scoped to the ACTIVE/selected scene only (gated on the dim anchor being visible) so the "see all screens" composite never blacks out from several dims stacking. Builds GREEN: `pnpm --filter launcher-api build`. Files: `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/boundComponentCatalog.ts`, `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/editor/EditorSpineLayer.svelte`, `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/editor/EditorCanvas.svelte`.

- **Loading splash percentage is now a built-in, optional part of the `LoadingBar` component (owner's ask: "add it to the bar, made optional"): ✅ DONE (2026-06-20, engine `LoadingBar.svelte` + `LOADING_INTRO_DEF`; Book of Borut needs an engine submodule bump + delete its `Percent` node + republish).** Follow-up to the load-complete-blank fix below. The owner pointed out the percentage was a SEPARATE editor node, not part of the bar — and the bar is a coded `bind` (LoadingBar) they can't open/edit, so the number couldn't live where it belongs. **Why the bar stays coded:** its fill is a live mask whose width tracks the load %, which the static node model can't express — so it must be a coded part; only the bar art/size (already params) and now the percentage are data. **Change:** `LoadingBar.svelte` now draws the percentage itself — reads the SAME live `stateApp.loadingProgress` as the mask, renders a `<Text>` centred on the bar INSIDE the same `{#if !loaded}`, so the number shares the bar's lifecycle and can't drift or linger at "100%". Optional + styled via params (`showPercent` default true, `percentFill`/`percentFontSize`/`percentFontFamily`/`percentOffsetY`) threaded through the param context; `LOADING_INTRO_DEF` drops the old `loadingIntro-percent` text node + its `source`/`fill`/`fontSize`/`fontFamily`/`value` params and gains a "Percentage" param group. Plain `<Text>` only (no bitmap-font catalog lookup yet — webfonts/system fonts render as authored; the owner's `proxima-nova` is a webfont so fine). Builds GREEN: `engine-layout`, `apps/lines`. **Owner steps:** (1) bump Book of Borut's engine submodule to ship the new `LoadingBar`; (2) in the Component Editor DELETE the now-redundant `Percent` node from the `loadingIntro` component (else the frozen node draws a SECOND number during loading — `showPercent` defaults on so the bar already shows its own); (3) republish. The percentage STYLE controls only appear on a FRESH/refreshed component (the owner's customised def is baked/frozen — see the baked-def-shadow note), but the default-styled number works without a refresh. Files: `packages/components-ui-pixi/src/components/LoadingBar.svelte`, `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/builtinComponents.ts`.

- **Editor: a free-spin-intro-style spine previewed OFFSET from its selection box (art landed bottom-left, box stayed centred): ✅ FIXED (2026-06-20, launcher-api editor — `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/editor/EditorSpineLayer.svelte`).** The owner saw the Free-spin intro preview's selection BOX centred in the frame but the SPINE art drawn far in the bottom-left — box and art disagreed. **Root cause (verified against `apps/lines/.../fsIntro/fs_screen.json`):** the two editor draw paths derive a spine's CENTRE from different measurements. The 2D selection box (`EditorCanvas.placedArtTransform`, contain/cover) sizes by the published natural size (`naturalSizeOf` → `skeleton.data.width/height`, the authored canvas) and centres that rect on the skeleton ORIGIN. The WebGL spine (`EditorSpineLayer.placeArt`) sized by the SAME natural size but re-centred on the live `getBounds()` SETUP-POSE bounds centre (`cx,cy`). For `fs_screen.json` the authored AABB is origin-centred (`skeleton.x=-927.29, width=1854.58` ⇒ centre ≈ 0) but the visible setup-pose attachments (number frame, minus the radial/particle canvas) sit OFFSET, so `getBounds` returned an asymmetric `cx,cy ≠ 0` and shifted the skeleton origin by `−s·cx / +s·cy` — dragging the art to a corner while the box stayed centred. Symmetric backgrounds/Win spines have `cx≈cy≈0`, which is why cover (Background) and positioned (Win/FS-counter) looked correct — the bug only surfaces on an asymmetric setup pose. **Fix:** in `placeArt`, when the natural size came from `nat` (the authored, origin-centred `skeleton.data` canvas) centre the contain/cover art on the ORIGIN (`cx=cy=0`) and anchor the positioned art over that origin-centred rect (`originX=−bw/2`), matching the box's assumption; only a pure-`getBounds` size (no `nat`) keeps the live bounds centre. Now box and art coincide for contain/cover/positioned; symmetric art is a no-op (its bounds centre ≈ origin). Builds GREEN: `pnpm --filter launcher-api build`. **Editor-only** (the game mounts its own coded overlay; this is purely the preview alignment). Files: `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/editor/EditorSpineLayer.svelte`.

- **Loading splash "100%" text lingered after the progress bar vanished at load-complete: ✅ FIXED (2026-06-20, game-side feed in `apps/lines` + Book of Borut; NO engine submodule bump needed).** Owner saw, on the Book of Borut start screen, the progress BAR disappear at 100% but the "100%" percentage text stay on (until press-to-continue faded the whole splash). **Root cause:** the `loadingIntro` splash bar is the one coded part (`LoadingBar.svelte`), which self-hides on `stateApp.loaded` via its own `{#if !loaded}`; the percentage is an editor-native `kind:'text'` node bound to the `loadingProgress` feed with NOTHING gating it on `loaded`. **Why the first attempt (a node-level `visibleBinding` on the CODED `LOADING_INTRO_DEF` percent node) would NOT have worked — the real lesson:** the owner CUSTOMISED the `loadingIntro` component in the Component Editor (replaced the `loader` spine with their `S_Title` art), so a BAKED copy of the def is frozen into the game's doc and SHADOWS the coded def (`registerBakedComponents()` registers baked defs last). Confirmed from the live bundle (`games.invisiblewall.org/bookofborut`): the baked `loadingIntro-percent` node has no gating and overrides the coded one — any coded-def change is invisible to it. **This matches the owner's own point ("why isn't it part of the bar — sounds half-made"): a fix on a frozen node can't reach a customised doc.** **Fix (drive it from the LIVE feed, which baking can't freeze):** the game's `loadingProgress` `valueSource` formatter now returns `''` when `stateApp.loaded` — so the readout BLANKS at load-complete, in lockstep with the bar, and because the percent node renders that feed through `ParamReadoutText` (whose `text` `$derived` re-runs when `loaded` flips, read inside the formatter), it reaches the owner's BAKED percent node with NO editor re-edit and NO engine change. **Parity:** during loading it renders "73%" exactly as before; editor preview (no game ⇒ no `loaded`) shows the static `0%`. Builds GREEN: `pnpm --filter lines build`; Borut `vite build`. **Scope:** pure game-side registration (`Game.svelte`) — `apps/lines` (engine reference) + Book of Borut each get the one-line formatter change; NO engine submodule bump. **Owner step: republish Book of Borut via the launcher** (ships the new `Game.svelte`); the "100%" will blank the instant loading completes. (The earlier `visibleBinding` engine experiment was REVERTED — superseded by this feed-driven fix, which is robust to the baked-def shadow.) Files: `apps/lines/src/components/Game.svelte`, Book of Borut `src/components/Game.svelte`.

- **Special Book `bookIntro`/`bookIdle` now INHERIT the symbol's effective win binding (were a frozen coded copy → showed stale template art): ✅ FIXED (2026-06-19, engine `19e6241` + Borut `eb5c9cc`).** Owner saw, in `/symbols` for Borut, the Book reveal/Book idle columns showing OLD fruit symbols (the `lines.json` default-base template), different from the authored Win art. **Root cause:** the original Special Book impl copied each symbol's *coded* `win` cell into `bookIntro`/`bookIdle` in `SYMBOL_INFO_MAP`; that literal copy ignored the win OVERRIDE authored in the Symbols State Machine, and the `/symbols` tool's `effectiveCell` fell the (un-overridden) book cells through to `symbolDefaultsFor('bookOf')` — which is the committed `lines.json` fruit template (no `bookOf` entry). **Fix (inherit, override-aware, still overridable):** dropped the literal book copies from `SYMBOL_INFO_MAP`; `getSymbolInfo` now aliases `bookIntro`/`bookIdle` → `'win'` unless the merged map has an explicit (baked) book cell, so it resolves the EFFECTIVE win (override > default); the tool's `effectiveCell` mirrors this (a non-overridden book cell returns the symbol's win override ?? win default). `bookIntro`/`bookIdle` stay in `SYMBOL_STATES` so a dedicated reveal/idle animation can still be bound per symbol. Builds GREEN (`lines` + `launcher-api`); Borut `pnpm build:engine && vite build` GREEN. **Owner step:** once the launcher redeploys, hard-refresh `/symbols` — book columns will mirror your Win art; republish Borut to ship in-game. Files: `apps/lines/src/game/{utils,constants}.ts`, `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/symbols/symbols.client.ts`, Book of Borut `src/game/{utils,constants}.ts`.

- **Reel opened OFFSET (a padding row leaked) and only "snapped" into place on the first bet, when the reel uses an editor reel-grid pitch ≠ `SYMBOL_SIZE`: ✅ FIXED + VERIFIED (2026-06-19, engine `packages/utils-slots/src/createReelForSpinning.svelte.ts`).** Owner saw, at game open, an extra row peeking above the top frame + below the bottom (Book of Borut), and the whole reel jump into alignment on the first spin click. **This is the residual of the re-home effect added for the reactive row-pitch override (entry below, "Reel spin regression").** That effect re-homes a stopped reel when the row pitch CHANGES, keyed off the height VALUE — but it captured its baseline `lastHeight = getSymbolHeight()` at effect-SETUP time, i.e. when `Board.svelte` mounts. `Board` only mounts AFTER the loading screen is dismissed (`Game.svelte` `{:else}`), while the editor doc (and thus `setBoardOverride` → the new pitch) resolves DURING the loading screen. So by the time the effect's baseline was captured the pitch had ALREADY changed → `height === lastHeight` → the effect never fired → the reel's `reelY` `Tween` stayed at its module-init home `-SYMBOL_SIZE` (created doc-less) while symbols were pitched at the override height, offsetting the whole reel by `(overridePitch − SYMBOL_SIZE)` and leaking a padding row. The first spin ends in `removePaddingAndBounceBack → placeY(homeY())`, which re-homes to the correct height → the "snap". **Fix:** seed the re-home baseline (`homedHeight`) at REEL-CREATION time (right where the `reelY` `Tween` is created, doc-less = `SYMBOL_SIZE`) instead of inside `readyToSpinEffect`. The effect's FIRST evaluation then sees `overridePitch !== homedHeight` and re-homes — regardless of whether the pitch changed before or after the board mounted. **Parity:** static-number height ⇒ `isReactiveHeight` guard early-returns (untouched); reactive-but-unchanged pitch (every app with no `reelGrid` override, incl. `apps/lines` whose reference layout has NO reelGrid node) ⇒ `homedHeight` stays `SYMBOL_SIZE`, effect inert ⇒ byte-identical. **Verified** in `apps/lines` dev: (1) at rest with no override `reelY = -120` (`-SYMBOL_SIZE`), symbols `[-60,60,180,300,420]` — parity; (2) an isolated harness against the LIVE compiled `createReelForSpinning` (Svelte `effect_root`) reproduced the exact bug timing — pitch 120→140 BEFORE `readyToSpinEffect` left the reel stale at `reelY=-120`; the fixed effect's first run re-homed it to `-140` (symbols `[-70,70,210,350,490]`). Under the old code the baseline would have been `140` ⇒ `140===140` ⇒ stayed stale at `-120`. **Scope:** `createReelForSpinning` is shared engine (in the submodule), so Book of Borut picks this up via an engine submodule bump (no per-game copy to mirror, unlike `BoardMask`/`stateGame`). Cascading reel is unaffected (no such effect). **Borut bump ✅ DONE + PUSHED** (Book of Borut `5c1bd02`: engine submodule `75358c4`→`84e2e62`; owner's `defaultLayout.ts` WIP left untouched). **Owner step: republish Book of Borut via the launcher** to ship. File: `packages/utils-slots/src/createReelForSpinning.svelte.ts`.

- **§17 Phase 3 — free-spin INTRO + OUTRO are now editor-positionable (gate/visual split): ✅ CODE DONE + PUSHED, parity-gated OFF, NOT runtime-verified (2026-06-19, engine `a369b9e`(intro)+`75358c4`(outro) + Book of Borut `1e86017`).** The owner's real goal after the Transition proof (Phase 1): make the free-spin intro/outro editable/positionable instead of coded overlays the game self-centres + the editor can't move. Unlike the Transition, these are **composites** with two coordinate needs — full-screen parts (dim + press-to-continue) and a board-relative visual (spine + count) — so a single positioned instance can't hold both. **Architecture (owner-chosen hybrid):** each overlay splits into a **GATE** (full-screen coded `canvas` bind: dim + press + the round-blocking await; outro gate also owns `WinCountUpProvider` + `WinCoins`) and a **VISUAL** (the `FreeSpinAnimation` block + count-in-slot, mounted as a `game`-space `componentInstance` `freeSpinIntroVisual`/`freeSpinOutroVisual` the owner positions). **Key engine change (R-A):** `FreeSpinAnimation.svelte` gained `boundToInstance` — when set it DROPS its own `<MainContainer>` + `boardLayout()` offset and renders at LOCAL origin, so the instance node (defaulted to board-centre) + the scene's game-space `<MainContainer>` place + scale it; default false = self-centres (parity). New coded sub-components `FreeSpin{Intro,Outro}{Gate,Visual}.svelte` + `OutroStatePublisher.svelte`; the existing `FreeSpinIntro`/`FreeSpinOutro` became OFF-path **composers** (gate+visual together, render-equivalent). The outro's count-up amount + win level bridge gate→visual via a shared rune `freeSpinOutroState.svelte.ts` (count stays IN the spine slot per owner decision — rides the animation, positioned as one unit). New shared defs `FREE_SPIN_{INTRO,OUTRO}_VISUAL_DEF` (each a `componentInstance` wrapping the coded visual with `boundToInstance:true`) + `BOUND_COMPONENT_DEFAULTS` previews (`game`/`boardCentre`). `apps/lines` gates the scene shapes on a new `FREE_SPIN_OVERLAY_INSTANCES` flag (default OFF) via `defaultLayout`; `Game.svelte` registers the defs + gate/visual components + mounts the `game`-space visual scenes. **Borut mirror (`1e86017`):** all components copied (game-agnostic) + Game.svelte register/mount + submodule→`75358c4`; Borut is editor-doc-driven (no flag) — the owner authors the visual componentInstance online. Builds GREEN: `engine-layout`, `apps/lines`, Borut `build:engine`+`vite build`. **NOT runtime-verified** (WebGPU render can't be auto-checked) — OFF=parity by construction; **owner verifies the ON path in-browser** (flip `FREE_SPIN_OVERLAY_INSTANCES=true` in `apps/lines`, or in the Borut editor place + position the "Free-spin intro"/"Free-spin outro" components, then republish). Known z-order nuance: the split renders the visual ON TOP of the gate (press behind the visual — passive, fine; outro coins behind the spine frame — common design). Known limit: the OFF composer is render-EQUIVALENT, not byte-identical (two FadeContainers/subscribers). Design: `docs/design/invisible-editor.md` §17. Files: `apps/lines/src/components/{FreeSpinAnimation,FreeSpinIntro,FreeSpinOutro,FreeSpin{Intro,Outro}{Gate,Visual},OutroStatePublisher,Game}.svelte`, `apps/lines/src/game/{editorFlags,freeSpinOutroState.svelte}.ts`, `apps/lines/src/editor-scenes.ts`, `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/{builtinComponents,boundComponentCatalog,referenceLayouts/lines}.ts` (+ Borut mirrors).

- **Special Book component (Book-of expanding-symbol reveal) — BUILT + MIRRORED + GATED-STATES SHIPPED: ✅ CODE DONE across engine reference + `/symbols` tool + Book of Borut, builds GREEN, NOT yet runtime-verified (2026-06-19, engine `699f826` + launcher `0c714a5` + Borut `9582ccd`).** Engine reference in `apps/lines`; `/symbols` tool now exposes `bookIntro`/`bookIdle` gated to `gameType==='bookOf'` (`launcher-api` `symbolsStorage.ts` schema + `symbols.client.ts#visibleStatesFor` + `+page.server.ts`/`+page.svelte`); Book of Borut mirrored (its own per-game copies; `setExpandingSymbol` already drove board-expansion via `expandingSymbol`, so the reveal `specialSymbol`+broadcasts were layered on top — note `specialSymbol` is redundant with Borut's `expandingSymbol`, kept for engine parity; `defaultLayout.ts` left untouched = owner WIP). **OWNER STEPS TO GO LIVE:** (1) in `/symbols` (Borut = bookOf) bind a spine animation for each symbol's `bookIntro`/`bookIdle` (defaults to the symbol's `win` spine until then); (2) in the online editor, PLACE the `SpecialBook` component (a `specialBook` scene — Borut mounts it by that id) where you want it on the board; (3) republish Borut via the launcher. **Still NOT runtime-verified** (WebGPU render can't be auto-checked + `apps/lines` dev doesn't drive the book mock) — the reveal animation is proven by build/types only.** Owner wants a bonus component that REVEALS which symbol is the free-spin special expanding symbol (the player previously had no way to know — the chosen symbol was emitted by the facade as `setExpandingSymbol` but the engine dropped it). **Owner design decisions:** (1) the reveal/idle animations come from the **Symbols State Machine** (new per-symbol states), NOT free-text params; (2) Idle renders the **chosen symbol's OWN spine** playing a state-named animation from that symbol's spine; (3) component states = **Reveal** (slot-machine shuffle through all symbols → land on the server-chosen one, plays its `bookIntro` one-shot) + **Idle** (plays its `bookIdle`, looping). **Built (apps/lines reference only):** modelled the dropped `setExpandingSymbol` book-event (`typesBookEvent.ts` union + `bookEventHandlerMap.ts` handler → stores `stateGame.specialSymbol` + broadcasts new emitter events `specialBookReveal {symbol}` / `specialBookHide`); added two symbol states **`bookIntro`/`bookIdle`** to `SYMBOL_STATES` (`game/types.ts`, now 8) with coded defaults reusing each symbol's `win` spine (`constants.ts` — `win` extracted to named `*Win` consts, byte-identical render); new coded bound component `SpecialBook.svelte` (shuffle decelerates over 24 frames 50→320ms via `t²`, lands → `bookIntro` → on spine-complete swaps to looping `bookIdle`; self-centres over the board via `MainContainer`+`boardLayout()`; timer cleared on hide/destroy); registered in `Game.svelte` + placed as a `specialBook` `canvas`-space scene in `referenceLayouts/{lines,bookof}.ts` + `BOUND_COMPONENT_DEFAULTS`/`PARAMS` (one `scale` knob). `getSymbolInfo` needed NO change (generic over state). The facade was NOT touched (it already emits the event). `pnpm --filter {engine-layout,lines} build` GREEN, `svelte-check` 0 errors. **Edge note:** if a symbol's `bookIntro` resolves to a *sprite* (not spine) its `oncomplete` never fires → stuck on intro; harmless with the spine defaults, revisit if a game authors a sprite bookIntro. **Done since:** gated `/symbols` states (launcher `0c714a5`) + Borut mirror (`9582ccd`) — see the OWNER STEPS above to go live. Engine files: `apps/lines/src/components/{SpecialBook,Game}.svelte`, `apps/lines/src/game/{typesBookEvent,bookEventHandlerMap,stateGame.svelte,types,constants,typesEmitterEvent}.ts`, `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/{boundComponentCatalog,referenceLayouts/{lines,bookof}}.ts`.

- **§17 editor-owned animated overlays — `Transition` migrated to an editor-positioned component (the proof, HYBRID approach): ✅ CODE DONE, parity-gated default-OFF (2026-06-19, engine `apps/lines`; not yet committed/owner-verified).** Goal: make the animated overlays (Transition first, then FS intro/outro — the owner's real target) EDITABLE in the scene editor — positioned/restyled — instead of coded `bind` components whose self-centering throws away the editor node position (the root of the "overlays look offset in the editor" complaint). **Investigation found §17 is partly built:** the signal-cue tier (`registerComponentSignals`, `ENGINE_SIGNAL_CATALOG`, the `<ComponentInstance>` cue interpreter, the editor "Plays on signal" UI) already ships. **But first contact showed the pure signal-cue path is the WRONG tool for these overlays** — they're stateful + round-blocking (replay on every trigger, auto-hide after, intro→idle sequence, press-to-continue, `await broadcastAsync` gating) and the v1 cue tier only does fire-and-play. **Owner chose the HYBRID** (over extending the interpreter): wrap each overlay as a `componentInstance` that POSITIONS the coded part, which keeps its proven lifecycle but renders at its LOCAL origin so the editor node's transform places it. This directly fixes the offset, is low-risk, and matches the counter/info-bar/loading-intro pattern. **Built for Transition:** `TRANSITION_DEF` (a componentInstance wrapping `bind:Transition`) in `builtinComponents.ts` + `BUILTIN_COMPONENTS`; `DefaultLayoutOptions.transition` gates the `basegameOverlays` node (`referenceLayouts/lines.ts`) — direct `bind:Transition` OFF (parity) vs `componentInstance(transition)` at `screenAnchor {0.5,0.5}` ON; `Game.svelte` registers the def; `TRANSITION_INSTANCE` flag (`editorFlags.ts`, default false, mirrors `HUD_BUTTON_INSTANCES`); `editor-scenes.ts` forwards it; `Transition.svelte` passes `x/y=0` (local origin) to `TransitionAnimation.svelte` only when ON, else `undefined` → canvas-centre (byte-identical). `TransitionAnimation` keeps reading `canvasSizes()` for height so sizing stays viewport-responsive (no baked size). The signal-completion-channel I first built for the pure-cue approach was REVERTED (unused under hybrid) — `registerComponentSignals`/`componentSignalContext`/`ComponentInstance`/`LayoutNodeView` are back to their pre-change state. Builds GREEN (`engine-layout` + `apps/lines`); flag-OFF parity verified by construction (the OFF branch emits the byte-identical `bound-transition` node). **Owner step: flip `TRANSITION_INSTANCE = true` locally and verify in-browser** (drag the transition instance in the editor, confirm the in-game wipe follows + the round still blocks) — WebGPU render can't be auto-verified (`reference_pixi_engine_verification`). Then it can default-on + mirror to Borut, and Phase 3 applies the same hybrid to the FS intro/outro (decomposing their count text into a sibling editor node). Design: `docs/design/invisible-editor.md` §17. Files: `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/{builtinComponents,referenceLayouts/lines}.ts`, `apps/lines/src/components/{Game,Transition,TransitionAnimation}.svelte`, `apps/lines/src/{editor-scenes.ts,game/editorFlags.ts}`.

- **Free-spin expanding special symbol DOUBLE-PAID (spurious tiny payline win on top of its expansion pay): ✅ FIXED (2026-06-19, mock `scripts/mock-rgs-server-book.mjs`).** Owner saw, in the bonus, "a line that pays that should not pay" + "pays very little / conversion looks wrong" — the `$0.03 – 3 OF A KIND` toast on the flooded-with-pears reels. **Root cause:** the chosen free-spin special is one of `PAY_SYMBOLS` and lives in `PAY_TABLE_LINE`, so during the bonus it was scored TWICE — once scatter-style by `evaluateSpecial` (the correct boosted `× 5` expansion pay) AND again as a regular left-aligned payline by `evaluatePaylines`. That second, redundant line win (`mult × betPerLine`, tiny) is the spurious toast; the facade then faithfully converts the tiny `pay` → the tiny on-screen amount, so it LOOKED like a conversion bug but the conversion is correct (the win itself was wrong). Base game has no special → unaffected, which is why it "differs from normal mode". **Fix:** `evaluatePaylines` takes an optional `excludeSymbol`; the free-spin path passes `round.bonus.special` so the expanding symbol pays via `evaluateSpecial` ONLY. **Verified** over 400 forced bonus rounds: pre-fix **222** special-paid-as-line violations → **0** post-fix, while special scatter-style/expansion wins (1023) and normal non-special line wins (2129) are untouched. Mock-only (the lines mock has no special path). **Owner step: redeploy/refresh `services/test-server`** (the baked mock is Borut's live RGS — no submodule bump). File: `scripts/mock-rgs-server-book.mjs`.

- **Play4Fun facade swallowed RGS bet rejections → engine crashed with the misleading `"Empty state in data.round"`: ✅ FIXED (2026-06-19, engine `packages/rgs-translator-eagaming/src/stakeFacade.ts`).** Owner saw the "Sorry, something went wrong / Empty state in data.round" modal mid-play. **Root cause (NOT the mock dedup edit, NOT empty events from a real spin):** the engine guard `handleRequestBet` (`utils-xstate/createPrimaryMachines.ts`) throws that generic message whenever `requestBet` returns a round with `state.length === 0`. The facade's `requestBet` produced exactly that on a **rejected bet**: the RGS returns a rejection error-shaped (`{result:0, error, errorCode, platform:{balance}}`) with **NO `events` array**, but the facade only ever read `.events`, spread `?? []`, and built a `SUCCESS` round with `state:[]` — dropping the real reason. Most likely trigger given the owner's balance ($4,859.50): a **buy-feature** bet (mode ≠ BASE) costs 100× the stake (`mock-rgs-server-book.mjs`), so a $50 buy needs $5,000 → `insufficient balance` (errorCode 200). **Fix:** after the first `bet` POST, `if (isPlay4FunError(first.response))` return an engine/modal-friendly error shape — `status.statusCode='ERR_<code>'`, scaled `balance`, plus top-level `error` + `message` so `handleRequestBet` (throws on truthy `data.error`) and `ModalError.svelte` (renders `error.error` + `error.message`) surface the actual reason. **Verified:** confirmed the book mock returns the `isPlay4FunError`-shaped rejection on the wire (insufficient-balance, no events array); `tsx` isn't installed so the full-facade smoke import wasn't run. Byte-parity for accepted bets (the new branch only fires on an error-shaped first response). File: `packages/rgs-translator-eagaming/src/stakeFacade.ts`.

- **Symbols State Machine showed dead symbols (e.g. `H5`) because `publish-symbol-defaults.mjs` silently failed to import the game's `constants.ts`: ✅ FIXED (2026-06-19, engine `apps/launcher-api/scripts/publish-symbol-defaults.mjs`; Borut needs a submodule bump + republish to clear the stale R2 doc).** Owner: the `/symbols` tool grid still listed symbols not in the game even after the 2026-06-18 config filter (`03882c2`). **Root cause (NOT the filter, NOT the wrong config file):** the publish script `import()`s the game's `SYMBOL_INFO_MAP` from `constants.ts`, but a real game's `constants.ts` pulls in siblings with NO extension (`import config from './config'`). Node (v24, type-stripping) does not rewrite those specifiers, so the import throws `ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND` **before** the config filter ever runs; under the build's `pnpm publish:symbols --optional` the failure is swallowed (`⚠ … skipping (--optional). Tool grid may be STALE.`). So Book of Borut has **never** successfully published `symbols/defaults.json` since `constants.ts` started importing `./config` — its R2 doc is frozen at the old 12-symbol version that still has `H5` (dropped in Borut `b188269`), and the tool serves that verbatim (it does not filter at read time). `apps/lines` dev never hit this — its `config.ts` is a self-contained default-export object, so its import (and the committed `lines.json` fallback) was always correct, masking the bug. **Fix:** the script registers an ESM resolve hook that, on a failed RELATIVE resolution, retries against the on-disk `.ts` sibling (append `.ts/.tsx/.mts/.cts/index.ts`, or swap `.js→.ts` etc.). Fires ONLY on failure → self-contained configs (apps/lines) are byte-unaffected (still drops `H5` via the filter). Verified by dry-run: Borut now imports `constants.ts`, applies the filter, resolves **11 symbols (no H5)**; apps/lines unchanged. **SECOND root cause (surfaced once the import was fixed):** the publish then reached the endpoint but got `HTTP 400 "Invalid symbol-defaults payload"` (and `--optional` swallowed THAT too) — a game's coded `SYMBOL_INFO_MAP` carries engine-only cell fields the tool schema doesn't model (Borut cells have a `winFrame`), and the server's `defaultCellSchema` was `.strict()` → it rejected the whole doc. Fixed BOTH ends: the script now `sanitizeCells()` strips each cell to the known keys (`type/assetKey/animationName/previewKey/sizeRatios`) before sending, and `symbolDefaults.ts#defaultCellSchema` is `.strip()` not `.strict()` (defence in depth, so no game-specific field ever 400s a build again). **Done for Borut:** ran the real publish from the local checkout (`.env` token + fixed script) → **"Published 11 symbols to borut/bookofborut/symbols/defaults.json"**; the tool now shows no H5 on reload. `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN. **Token was NOT the blocker** (owner confirmed `EDITOR_DOC_SECRET` is wired via `.env` in the build): the two code bugs above were what `--optional` swallowed — bug #1 killed the run before the PUT, bug #2 got a 400 at the server. Both fixed → launcher republishes now publish symbols correctly on their own. Files: `apps/launcher-api/scripts/publish-symbol-defaults.mjs`, `apps/launcher-api/src/lib/server/symbolDefaults.ts`.

- **Free-spin intro/outro reverted to STANDALONE board-centered overlays; outro brought to editor-param parity with the intro: ✅ DONE + PUSHED (2026-06-19, engine `ba04b1c` + Book of Borut `483f2f1`).** Owner decision: the free-spin intro AND outro live as their OWN full-screen, board-centered overlay components — NOT owned by the free-spin counter. This REVERTS the 2026-06-18 "counter owns the intro" experiment (entry below). **Removed:** the `ungated` escape (`BaseNode.ungated` in `types.ts` + the gated/ungated split in `ComponentInstance.svelte` — only the counter intro used it → dead code), the counter's `ungated` `FreeSpinIntro` bind child + its 5 intro params (`introSpine`/`introAnimation`/`idleAnimation`/`introSlot`/`introScale`) + the `FS_INTRO_BOUND_COMPONENT` const from `FREE_SPIN_COUNTER_DEF` (`builtinComponents.ts`), and the `boundToCounter` dual-mode branch from every `FreeSpinIntro.svelte` (5 apps) — the standalone full-screen branch (own `MainContainer`+dim via `FreeSpinAnimation`, board-centered) is the sole render again. **Kept** the legit counter features added alongside the experiment (the `frameImage` panel-art param, the `visibleSource` default, label/font/value). **Outro parity (the "intro AND outro option" the owner had lost track of — now as SEPARATE overlays, not on the counter):** added `BOUND_COMPONENT_PARAMS['FreeSpinOutro']` (`boundComponentCatalog.ts`) = `outroSpine`/`outroAnimation`/`idleAnimation`/`slotName`, and each `FreeSpinOutro.svelte` (5 apps) now reads them via `$props()` with the old hardcodes as defaults (`fsOutroNumber`/`intro`/`idle`/`slot_number`) — so the outro spine/animation/slot are editor-pickable exactly like the intro, byte-parity for unauthored games. Also fixed a pre-existing intro bug surfaced by review: the intro read `stringParam('introSlot')` but the editor param key is `slotName` → the "Number slot" field was silently ignored; now reads `slotName`. **Centering: NO code change** — the overlays self-center on the board via `FreeSpinAnimation`'s own `<MainContainer>`; the mis-centering the owner saw is the stale `space` tag (entry below, "Overlay screens … render offset"), which the owner re-tags to `canvas` in the editor. Builds GREEN: `pnpm --filter engine-layout build` + `apps/lines` `pnpm build`; code-reviewed clean (no dangling `ungated`/`boundToCounter` refs). **Owner steps:** (1) in the editor set each overlay scene's `space` → `canvas` + Save (fixes centering of the existing doc); (2) **republish Book of Borut via the launcher to ship it.** (The Borut-side mirror is DONE — `483f2f1`: its own `src/components/{FreeSpinIntro,FreeSpinOutro}.svelte` were edited to match + the engine submodule bumped `042e5fc`→`ba04b1c`; `build:engine` + `vite build` GREEN. Borut also had the owner's WIP debug-win-lines work in-tree at the time — left untouched/uncommitted.) Files: `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/{types,ComponentInstance.svelte,builtinComponents,boundComponentCatalog,referenceLayouts/{lines,bookof,engineSkeleton}}.ts`, `apps/{lines,cluster,scatter,ways,price}/src/components/{FreeSpinIntro,FreeSpinOutro}.svelte`.

- **Board mask leaked a 4th (padding) row when the reel uses a non-square cell pitch: ✅ FIXED + PUSHED (2026-06-19, engine `bbcc4a1` + Book of Borut `17f7d50`).** Owner saw 4 rows instead of 3 in Book of Borut (only visible once a free-spin overlay hid the menu bar that normally covers the bottom leak). **Root cause (NOT config — `numRows`/`BOARD_DIMENSIONS.y` are 3):** `BoardMask.svelte` sized the mask to `BOARD_DIMENSIONS.y * SYMBOL_SIZE` (= `boardLayout().height`, 360px), but once the editor reel-grid override sets a non-square `cellHeight`/`gapY` the symbols pitch at `boardGeometry().rowPitchLocal` (`stateGame.svelte.ts`). A shorter pitch (e.g. 90) makes `360 / pitch > 3`, so the bottom padding row falls inside the fixed-height mask window and shows. **Fix:** size the mask to `BOARD_DIMENSIONS.y * boardGeometry().rowPitchLocal` (the real visible window), exposing `boardGeometry` on `stateGameDerived`. Byte-parity when `rowPitchLocal === SYMBOL_SIZE` (no override / square cell) → `windowHeight === 360`, identical to before. **Scope:** only `apps/lines` (engine reference) and Book of Borut have the `rowPitchLocal` reel-grid-override mechanism — cluster/scatter/ways/price pitch at `SYMBOL_SIZE` always, so they're unaffected (no `boardGeometry` to read; left untouched). Borut's `BoardMask`/`stateGame` are its own per-game copies (not in the engine submodule) → no submodule bump needed; the engine commit keeps the reference honest. Builds GREEN: `pnpm --filter lines build`; Borut `pnpm build`. In-game render not auto-verified (PixiJS/WebGPU per `reference_pixi_engine_verification`). **Owner step: republish Book of Borut** to ship. Files: `apps/lines/src/{components/BoardMask.svelte,game/stateGame.svelte.ts}`, Book of Borut `src/{components/BoardMask.svelte,game/stateGame.svelte.ts}`.

- **Overlay screens (free-spin intro/outro, win/transition, info bar, counter) render offset + ignore the editor gizmo when the live R2 doc lacks the `space: 'canvas'` tag: ⚠️ DIAGNOSED, owner fixing by hand (2026-06-19).** Owner: the free-spin intro ("the old original one") renders shrunk into the top-left and the gizmo bounding box doesn't match; other overlays too. **Root cause (stale data, not code):** `<LayoutScene>` defaults a scene with no `space` to `game`, which wraps it in a `<MainContainer>`. The coded full-screen overlays (e.g. `FreeSpinIntro` standalone) already build their OWN full-screen `MainContainer` + dim, so `game` space = MainContainer-in-MainContainer → double-scale/offset, and the editor gizmo (drawn game-space) stops matching the full-screen content. The `space` tag was added 2026-06-05; overlay scenes created before then come back from R2 without it. The fallback `defaultLayout.ts` + shared `referenceLayouts/bookof.ts` already tag these `canvas`, so freshly-seeded docs are correct — only the owner's pre-existing doc is stale. **Manual fix (owner's choice, reusable):** in the editor set each overlay screen's `space` dropdown (Outline panel) to `canvas (window edges)` and Save — applies to `freeSpinIntro`/`freeSpinOutro`/`basegameOverlays`/`infoBar`/`freeSpinCounter`/`background`; keep `basegame` = `game`, `hudBar` = `standard`. **Rule:** full-screen overlays + self-positioning coded components must be `canvas` (no MainContainer wrap); the tell is a gizmo that doesn't sit where the screen renders. A systemic normalizer backfill (default-space per known scene-id) was offered but the owner opted to hand-fix to learn the pattern; revisit if more stale docs surface.

- **Info bar offset in-game vs editor — author HUD screens aren't mounted by the game (hard-coded scene-id lookup): ✅ FIXED (2026-06-19, Book of Borut `8f46cf1`; engine reference unaffected — apps/lines has no infoBar mount).** Owner: the win/info bar ("Win $100.00 — 2 of a kind") renders on the banner inside the reel cabinet in the editor but pinned to the WINDOW top in the live game, with no doc change that would explain it. **Root cause (NOT a render/anchor/space-math bug):** the info bar was authored as a **"New HUD screen"**, which the editor mints with a **`hud_…` id** (`editor/+page.svelte:1062`, so `isHudScene()` groups it under the HUD layer). But the game locates the scene by an EXACT id — `Game.svelte` `scenes.find((s) => s.id === 'infoBar')` — so the author's `hud_…` screen was never found; the lookup fell through to the bundled `fallbackInfoBar` (the seeded `canvas`-space, `screenAnchor {0.5,0.06}` = window-top 6% bar). Editor rendered the author's screen on the banner; game rendered a DIFFERENT (fallback) scene at the window top → the "offset." The game only mounts a fixed list of scene ids (`background`/`basegame`/`basegameOverlays`/`hudBar`/`hudCorners`/`freeSpinCounter`/`infoBar`/`freeSpinIntro`/`freeSpinOutro`/`loading`); **any author-created HUD screen (`hud_…`) is invisible to the runtime.** **Fix (Borut, targeted):** the `infoBarScene` lookup now prefers the seeded `infoBar` id, then ANY scene carrying an `infoBar` componentInstance (the author's HUD screen, wherever placed), then the fallback — so authoring the bar as a HUD screen reaches the game. No double-render (only the one mount draws it; `hud_…` isn't in the hard-coded id set). Borut `vite build` GREEN. **Owner step: republish Book of Borut** (the fix is in the game's deployed bundle/`Game.svelte`, not the live doc — the doc was already correct; the runtime just couldn't find it). **Broader gap (deferred, §20.1):** the engine should render author-created scenes GENERICALLY instead of hard-coded-id mounts, so a NEW HUD screen of any kind (custom banner, jackpot display) shows in-game; today only the info bar is found-by-content. File: Book of Borut `src/components/Game.svelte`.

- **Coinciding paylines double-paid (real-money payout bug): ✅ FIXED + VERIFIED (2026-06-19, mock RGS; both `book` + `lines` variants).** Owner spotted a low symbol (the cactus) "paying twice." Traced the full chain: Book of Borut runs `PUBLIC_RGS_TRANSPORT=play4fun`, the facade just translates, and the actual payout authority is the **mock RGS baked into `services/test-server`** (Railway, serves `games.invisiblewall.org/api/bookofborut`) — there is NO separate real RGS, so `scripts/mock-rgs-server-book.mjs` `evaluatePaylines` IS the live math. **Bug:** the config emitted `lineCoinciding: false` but NOTHING read it — the evaluator pushed a win for every payline, so two lines crossing the SAME leftmost symbol run (e.g. `[2,2,2,2,2]` and `[2,2,1,0,0]` both pay a 2-of-a-kind cactus on cells (0,2)+(1,2)) each paid → identical double toast + over-credit. Not a render bug; a genuine overpay. **Fix:** promoted `lineCoinciding` to a real `const LINE_COINCIDING = false` (wired into both config builders so flag↔behavior can't drift) and added `dedupeCoincidingWins` — collapses wins that land on identical cells (key = symbol + leftmost `occurs` reel:row positions), keeping the highest pay. Mirrored into the `lines` mock (`scripts/mock-rgs-server.mjs`). **Verified end-to-end** by driving the real mock over 4000 spins: before (`coinciding=true`) → 538 coinciding double-pays; after → **0**, with legitimate distinct-cell line wins untouched (1641 winning rounds preserved). Both files `node --check` GREEN. **Owner step: redeploy `services/test-server` (Railway) to ship — Book of Borut needs no submodule bump** (the mock lives in the engine repo's test-server image, not the pinned engine). Files: `scripts/mock-rgs-server-book.mjs`, `scripts/mock-rgs-server.mjs`.
  - **FOLLOW-UP — coincidence generalized to SUBSUMPTION + a Win-lines debug tool (2026-06-19): ✅ FIXED + VERIFIED.** Owner then hit "a line that pays for a 2 AND a 3 at the same time." Same `lineCoinciding=false` rule, but the first dedup only matched *exactly-equal* cell sets — it missed a shorter run whose cells are a SUBSET of a longer one (a 2-of-a-kind paid again inside an overlapping 3-of-a-kind = the two leftmost symbols paid twice). `dedupeCoincidingWins` (both mocks) now keeps a win only if its winning cells are NOT contained in another winning line (covers equal coincidence AND proper subset; identical cells ⇒ identical symbols, so distinct/non-overlapping lines never merge). Verified over 5000 spins: **0** subset/coincidence violations (the test caught 244 on the pre-fix code → sensitivity confirmed). **Win-lines debug tool** added to Book of Borut (`registerDebugTool` id `winlines`, group State) — per spin it lists each line/scatter win's `paylineId`, symbol×kind, amount, and the REAL board symbol on every winning cell, flagging cells whose symbol ≠ the win symbol (a wild/book bridge). Debug-gated (`__IE_DEBUG__`) capture in `bookEventHandlerMap` (`reveal`→board, `winInfo`→wins); new `src/game/debugWinLines.svelte.ts` + `src/components/debug/WinLinesDebugTool.svelte`. Borut `build:engine` + `vite build` GREEN. **STILL OPEN — owner decision (NOT changed; RTP impact):** the book/scatter `S` is configured as a WILD (`bookMapping.wild='S'`, `wildSymbols:['SCAT']`) and bridges line runs, so a line *containing the book* pays "patterns that shouldn't pay"; Borut's art also defines a SEPARATE dynamite wild `W` the mock never spins → likely the book should be scatter-ONLY and `W` the wild. Also during free spins the book neither re-triggers nor pays on its own (only the expanding special pays, on ≥2 reels) — confirm intended. Files: `scripts/mock-rgs-server-book.mjs`, `scripts/mock-rgs-server.mjs`, Borut `src/game/{debugWinLines.svelte.ts,bookEventHandlerMap.ts,debugTools.ts}`, `src/components/debug/WinLinesDebugTool.svelte`.

- **Frames — lock screens together so they move + resize as one: 📋 PLAN REGISTERED (2026-06-18, `docs/design/invisible-editor.md` §23; NOT built).** Owner asked to "anchor a screen to another screen so they move and resize the same way, to avoid offset in the game." Decided AGAINST a scene→scene `anchorTo` pointer (dangling refs / chains / source-of-truth ambiguity). The chosen model: **promote `Scene.space` (the hardcoded `game`/`standard`/`canvas`/`background` enum) to first-class named FRAMES** — a frame is a named layout box with its own per-`LayoutType` size map; a scene references a frame by name; **all scenes on one frame are reparented into ONE `<MainContainer>` reading that frame's single `mainLayout` → shared centre+scale+pivot on resize, by construction, with no new transform math** (rides the existing uniform-scale path `createMainLayout` `:53-69` + `MainContainer` `:16-47`). "Anchor A to B" == "put A and B on the same frame". Fixes the `[[bug_editor_scenes_outside_maincontainer]]` / `[[gotcha_hud_scenes_separate_canvas]]` drift class (a `standard`-vs-`game` size-map mismatch). Additive schema (`Frame`, `LayoutDoc.frames?`, `Scene.frame?`; legacy `space` maps 1:1 to built-in frames → every existing doc/`editor-scenes.ts` stays valid). Runtime change = a `<LayoutFrameGroup>` mounting one container per frame, landed **together with §20.1's deferred "render doc scenes generically" step** (same change — both group/order doc scenes instead of hardcoded-id mounts in `Game.svelte:675-711`). Editor must mirror the frame lookup in `editorCanvas.helpers.ts`/`EditorCanvas.svelte:262-300` or preview diverges; adds a visible frame boundary + per-scene frame picker + "locked-together" tags. **Build order + open decisions in §23** (default new scenes to `game` frame; opt-out = a custom frame with its own `sizes`; verify the `standard` HUD-bar migration before flipping). Next step when picked up: step 1 (types + `resolveFrame` back-compat shim, parity-gated).

- **Free-spin counter OWNS the intro (counter-local spine, opt-in): ⛔ REVERTED 2026-06-19 (see the top "Free-spin intro/outro reverted to STANDALONE …" entry — owner chose separate centered overlays instead). History kept for context.** Owner wants the free-spin intro as a pickable-spine PARAM on the free-spin COUNTER so they can delete the separate intro/outro screens. Built parity-gated. Mechanism: (1) new `BaseNode.ungated` (`types.ts`) — a direct child of a `componentInstance` def root flagged `ungated` renders OUTSIDE the instance's `visibleSource` `<Container visible>` wrapper (`ComponentInstance.svelte` splits gated vs ungated children). This is load-bearing because intro and counter are SEQUENTIAL on DIFFERENT gates (`freeSpinTrigger` plays the intro + awaits press-to-continue, hides it, THEN `freeSpinCounterShow`) — so the intro must escape the counter's gate. (2) `FREE_SPIN_COUNTER_DEF` gains an `ungated` `FreeSpinIntro` bind child + params `introSpine`/`introAnimation`/`idleAnimation`/`introSlot`/`introScale` (group "Free-spin intro"). (3) Per-game `FreeSpinIntro.svelte` (5 ref apps + Borut) is now DUAL-MODE: standalone (the existing full-screen `MainContainer`+dim render, byte-identical = parity) vs `boundToCounter` (reads params off `getComponentParams()`, renders the spine LOCALLY at the component origin scaled by `introScale` — NO full-screen dim, the COUNTER decides size/position per owner's "why full screen?" steer; full-screen `PressToContinue` kept so the round's `await broadcastAsync(freeSpinIntroUpdate)` resolves). Inert until `introSpine` set (an `if(!introSpine) return` guard in `freeSpinIntroUpdate` stops the inert child hanging the press-gate). Standalone intro scene + `FreeSpinIntro` untouched = parity. **Owner opt-in:** use the BUILT-IN `freeSpinCounter` (NOT the pre-existing `FreeSpinCounterNEW` project copy — it predates the intro child and won't have the params; re-create or switch), set `introSpine` (+ `introScale`), and DELETE the standalone "Free-spin intro" screen (else both subscribe to `freeSpinIntro*` and double-render). Editor shows the params after the launcher redeploys; runtime needs the Borut submodule bump. `pnpm --filter engine-layout build` + all 5 ref games + Borut `vite build` GREEN. Known limits (extend later): the count only shows if the chosen spine exposes the `introSlot` slot; the tap-catcher is still full-screen; a full-screen/dim mode is a later add. Files: `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/{types,ComponentInstance.svelte,builtinComponents,referenceLayouts/{bookof,lines,engineSkeleton}}.ts`, `apps/{lines,cluster,scatter,ways,price}/src/components/FreeSpinIntro.svelte` (+ Borut `src/components/FreeSpinIntro.svelte`).

- **Reel "Symbol size (× cell)" — REAL root cause: baked symbol-map `sizeRatios` shadowed the reel control: ✅ FIXED (2026-06-18, engine `apps/lines` + Book of Borut; per-game src, no engine-submodule change).** After the aspect/contain fix (entry below), the PUBLISHED Book of Borut STILL rendered symbols ~1.5× oversized while the editor looked right — at the identical reel `symbolSizeRatios = {1,1}`, even in **incognito** (cache ruled out). Diagnosed against the LIVE deployed bundle (`games.invisiblewall.org/bookofborut`, SvelteKit `_app/immutable/…`, index `no-store` so always fresh): the fix code WAS deployed (`n.width*e.cellWidth/e.cellSize` + the `contain` logic both present) → a runtime/data cause, not a stale deploy. **Root cause:** the Symbols State Machine bakes a per-cell `sizeRatios` for EVERY symbol (e.g. `{type:"sprite",assetKey:"h2.png",sizeRatios:{width:1,height:1}}`), and `resolveSymbolSizeRatios` checked that **baked per-cell override BEFORE the reel control** — so the reel's `symbolSizeRatios` was silently shadowed by the default `{1,1}` (taken as `stretch` = full `SYMBOL_SIZE` = overflow) for every published game. `apps/lines` dev has no baked map, so it tested clean and masked the bug the whole time. The reel control's label ("applied to EVERY symbol… Reset to fall back to per-symbol sizes") and the editor preview's `node.symbolSizeRatios ?? cell.sizeRatios` precedence both require the reel to win FIRST. **Fix:** reorder `resolveSymbolSizeRatios` → reel (contain) > baked per-cell (stretch) > coded (stretch), in BOTH `apps/lines/src/game/symbolMap.ts` and Book of Borut `src/game/symbolMap.ts`. No `pixi-svelte`/engine-submodule change. Builds GREEN (`pnpm --filter lines build`; Borut `vite build`). Verified deployed-bundle code via HTTP fetch+grep; inspected the resolver in the running `apps/lines` via `__PIXI_APP__` + dynamic module import (caveat: Vite dynamic-`import()` duplicates `.svelte.ts` singletons, so eval-injected `setBoardOverride` doesn't reach the resolver's binding — a test artifact; the real single-instance game path is correct). **Owner: republish Book of Borut to ship.** Behavior note: the reel control now also overrides per-symbol Symbols-tool sizes (matches the editor + the control's own docs); clear it to restore per-symbol sizes.

- **Free-spin INTRO — spine bundle + animations are now editor-pickable (quick win, behavior stays coded): ✅ DONE (2026-06-18, engine + all reference games + Borut).** Owner asked how to author the free-spin intro and whether it should be a "pick spine anim" param on the counter. Answer: NO — the intro is a separate one-shot, round-blocking, press-to-continue overlay (different lifecycle from the persistent counter), so it stays its own component; the full editor-owned version is the deferred §17 behavior-layer work. The quick win instead exposes the COODED intro's spine knobs as editor params without touching its behavior. New `BOUND_COMPONENT_PARAMS['FreeSpinIntro']` (`boundComponentCatalog.ts`) = 4 `string` params: `introSpine` (spine bundle), `introAnimation`, `idleAnimation`, `slotName` (the count-injection slot). The editor already auto-renders `getEditableParams(bind.component)` → `bind.props` for ANY bind anchor, so the `freeSpinIntro` scene's `FreeSpinIntro` node now shows these fields with no editor change. Each game's `FreeSpinIntro.svelte` (all 5 reference apps + Borut — structurally identical) now reads them via `$props()` with the OLD hardcodes as defaults (`fsIntroNumber`/`intro`/`idle`/`slot_number`), so an unauthored game is byte-identical (parity); the dim backdrop, `freespins_*.png` sprites, `PressToContinue`, the `freeSpinIntro*` event subscription, and the count `BitmapText` are untouched. CAVEAT (documented in the catalog comment): a custom `introSpine` bundle MUST expose a slot matching `slotName` or the count has nowhere to mount — hence `slotName` is also a param. `pnpm --filter engine-layout build` + `pnpm --filter scatter build` + Borut `vite build` GREEN. A visual spine/animation PICKER (dropdown of project spines + that spine's animations) is a follow-up — these are plain text fields for now. Files: `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/boundComponentCatalog.ts`, `apps/{lines,cluster,scatter,ways,price}/src/components/FreeSpinIntro.svelte` (+ Borut `src/components/FreeSpinIntro.svelte`).

- **Loading / Intro splash → editor-native `loadingIntro` component: ✅ ENGINE WIRED (2026-06-18, pushed: engine `f3a084a` + `5636eb3`).** Owner asked for a new Loading/Intro component pre-wired with the engine params, mirroring the free-spin-counter / info-bar conversions. Built `LOADING_INTRO_DEF` (`engine-layout/builtinComponents.ts`) as a HYBRID: most parts are editor-native plain nodes (a `kind:'spine'` Logo = the `loader` bundle / `title_screen` anim, and a `kind:'text'` Percent bound `text→value` showing "73%"), but the progress BAR can't be — its fill is a live mask whose width tracks the load, which the static node model can't express — so it's the ONE bound coded part: a `bind:{component:'LoadingBar'}` child (the §14.3 HUD-readout decomposition). New coded `LoadingBar.svelte` (`packages/components-ui-pixi`, exported) reproduces the proven `LoadingProgress` masked render (full-width fill sprite cropped by `<Rectangle isMask width={W*loadingProgress/100}>`), reading its `barWidth`/`barHeight` + `imageBackground`/`imageProgress`/`imageFrame` frame params off the component param context and `loadingProgress`/`loaded` straight off `stateApp`; it hides itself the moment loading completes (the coded splash's `{#if !loaded}`). Catalog (`componentCatalog.ts`): `loadingProgress` added to `ENGINE_PARAM_CATALOG` (number → value + text source); `assetsLoading` added to `VISIBILITY_SOURCE_KEYS` (true only while the boot load is in flight). `apps/lines` reference (`Game.svelte`): registers the def, the bound `LoadingBar`, the `loadingProgress` value source (`valueSource(() => stateApp.loadingProgress, n => Math.round(n)+'%')`), and the `assetsLoading` gate. Def params default to the coded splash (`1967*0.2 × 346*0.2`, the `progressBar*.png` frames) so a freshly placed instance reads like the original; a game with different bar art/size overrides them in the editor. **Parity-safe: it does NOT replace the coded `LoadingScreen` mount** (which owns the interactive press-to-continue → transition flow + the `onloaded` callback the component can't carry) — it's a placeable building block for composing the splash's static content in the editor. **NOTE — concurrent-commit hazard:** the def + catalog + `Game.svelte` edits were swept into the owner's `f3a084a` ("info bar…") `git commit -am`, but the untracked `LoadingBar.svelte` + its export weren't, so `f3a084a` briefly shipped a broken main (imported a symbol that didn't ship); fixed by `5636eb3`. Builds GREEN: `engine-layout`, `lines`, `launcher-api`. Files: `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/{builtinComponents.ts,componentCatalog.ts}`, `packages/components-ui-pixi/{index.ts,src/components/LoadingBar.svelte}`, `apps/lines/src/components/Game.svelte`.
  - **FOLLOW-UP — the component now actually RENDERS in-game (the editor-preview-only gap is closed): ✅ DONE + pushed (2026-06-18, engine `apps/lines` `98d6b4b`, Book of Borut `2f73dd0`).** Owner deployed with a `loadingIntro` placed and got NO splash, no press-to-continue, no logo. **Root cause (a "shows in editor ≠ ships" trap):** the in-game loading screen is the HARDCODED coded `LoadingScreen.svelte`; the editor's `loading` scene was NEVER rendered as nodes — `Game.svelte` only read it to find the `LoadingScreen` bind anchor and REPOSITION the coded splash. So a placed `loadingIntro`/logo rendered nothing in-game, and replacing the `LoadingScreen` anchor with it left the loading path with nothing to draw. The earlier "drop it in the loading scene and it shows" guidance was WRONG. **Fix (no engine change — `<LayoutScene>` already exists):** `Game.svelte` derives `authoredLoadingScene` = the loading scene MINUS the inert `LoadingScreen` anchor; when the author placed real content there it's handed to `<LoadingScreen authoredScene>` (new optional prop), which renders those nodes via `<LayoutScene>` as the `start`-phase splash visual and keeps ONLY the coded press-to-continue / transition / `onloaded` shell. No authored content ⇒ the coded splash renders unchanged (parity for un-authored docs / dev). **Book of Borut MIRRORED** (`2f73dd0`): same `Game.svelte` + `LoadingScreen.svelte` edits PLUS the registrations `apps/lines` already had but Borut lacked (`LOADING_INTRO_DEF` + `LoadingBar` + `loadingProgress` value source + `assetsLoading` gate); Borut's engine submodule (`7483183`) already carried the def/part/catalog so NO submodule bump. Builds GREEN: `lines` `vite build`; Borut `build:engine` + `vite build`. In-game render not auto-verified (PixiJS/WebGPU) — owner to confirm live. **Owner steps to SEE it now:** republish Borut (ships the registrations + render path), then in the editor place a "Loading / Intro" component (or a logo) in the `loading` scene, position/restyle, Save + rebake/publish → it becomes the splash, press-to-continue intact. Files: `apps/lines/src/components/{Game,LoadingScreen}.svelte`, Book of Borut `src/components/{Game,LoadingScreen}.svelte`.

- **Info bar (win/info toast) → editor-native component: ✅ ENGINE + BORUT WIRED (2026-06-18, pushed: engine `f3a084a`, Book of Borut `aade859`).** Borut now registers `INFO_BAR_DEF` + the `message`/`messageShow` sources, seeds an `infoBar` scene in `defaultLayout` (canvas, `screenAnchor {0.5,0.06}`), mounts it via `<LayoutScene>`, and drops `<MessageToast/>`; submodule bumped to `f3a084a`. Borut `build:engine` + `vite build` GREEN. The bar renders out of the box (fallback → text-only) and overrides from the live editor doc. **Owner to finish the look:** author a pill/plaque `background` frame (ships export→deploy→bake→pull→register), then place/position/restyle the InfoBar in the editor + rebake/publish — until then it's gold text, no pill. In-game render not auto-verified (PixiJS/WebGPU). History below kept for context. Owner wants the in-game info bar (the "Win $1.00 — 2 of a kind" banner) controllable in the Scene Editor (position, size, font, font size, colour). It was the coded HTML `MessageToast` (`components-ui-html`, a CSS pill fed by `showMessage`/`stateMessage`) — a DOM overlay the editor (PIXI-only) can't touch. Re-homing it as an editor-native `componentInstance`, mirroring the §14.3 free-spin-counter conversion. **Done (engine, this repo):** (1) `INFO_BAR_DEF` built-in (`engine-layout/builtinComponents.ts`) — container + background sprite (`region`/`tint` ← author-picked `background` image param) + text (`text` ← engine-fed `message` STRING source); params `source`/`visibleSource`/`background`/`tint`/`fill`(gold)/`fontSize`/`fontFamily`/`value`; empty `assetKey` + no picked frame ⇒ text-only (no crash). (2) `componentCatalog.ts` — `message` added to `ENGINE_PARAM_CATALOG` + `COMPOSED_STRING_SOURCE_KEYS` (Source dropdown), `messageShow` added to `VISIBILITY_SOURCE_KEYS`. (3) `apps/lines` reference — registers the def + `message: textSource(stateMessage.current?.text)` value source + `messageShow: boolSource(stateMessage.current !== null)` gate; new `InfoBarComponentInstance` story. `engine-layout` lib typechecks GREEN. **Next (Borut, after the engine lands on main + submodule bump):** register `INFO_BAR_DEF` + the `message`/`messageShow` sources in `Game.svelte`, seed an `infoBar` scene in `defaultLayout('bookOf')` (canvas space, `screenAnchor {0.5, 0.06}` = the toast's `top:6%` centre) + mount like `fsCounterScene`, REMOVE `<MessageToast />`; then author the pill background image (ships export→deploy→bake→pull→register) + reposition/restyle in the editor + rebake. `showMessage(...)` in Borut `bookEventHandlerMap.ts` stays — it now feeds the PIXI bar. Per-kind recolour (info/win/warn) is a later follow-on. Design: `docs/design/invisible-editor.md` §18.9. Files: `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/{builtinComponents.ts,componentCatalog.ts}`, `apps/lines/src/components/Game.svelte`, `apps/lines/src/stories/InfoBarComponentInstance.stories.svelte`.

- **Reel "Symbol size (× cell)" — editor↔game divergence + aspect distortion: ✅ FIXED (2026-06-18, engine + launcher + Book of Borut; not committed/pushed).** Owner set the reelGrid node's `symbolSizeRatios` (Scene Editor → reel "Symbol size (× cell)"); the editor preview looked right but the live game over-scaled the symbols (and stretched them). **Two stacked bugs.** (1) **Wrong reference size:** the editor measures the ratio against the AUTHORED `cellWidth/cellHeight`, but the game effectively measured against the SQUARE `cellSize`. `SymbolSprite` sizes art in board-local space as `SYMBOL_SIZE * sizeRatios`, and the board container is scaled by `cellSize/SYMBOL_SIZE` — so on screen the symbol is `cellSize * ratio`, not `cellWidth * ratio`. With cellSize 120 / cellWidth 80 → 1.5× oversize. (2) **No aspect lock:** BOTH paths stretched width/height independently (game = Pixi `width`+`height`; editor = `drawArtRegionSprite` independent `scaleX/scaleY`). Symbols normally look right only because the coded per-symbol `sizeRatios` in `constants.ts` are hand-tuned to each art's aspect; a single uniform reel ratio discards that → distortion. **Fix (owner's ask — "keep the region aspect ratio"):** when the reel `symbolSizeRatios` override is active, the W/H ratios define a BOUNDING BOX `cellWidth·ratioW × cellHeight·ratioH` and each symbol is scaled by ONE uniform factor `min(boxW/artW, boxH/artH)` to fit inside it (contain-fit, native aspect preserved). Non-reel paths (coded per-symbol / baked per-cell `sizeRatios`) are UNCHANGED → byte-parity for every existing game; square cells → correction 1 → identical to today. **Implementation (4 spots, one shared formula):** (a) `packages/pixi-svelte/src/lib/components/Sprite.svelte` — new optional `contain` prop: treats `width`/`height` as a box and uniform-fits the texture (reactive to the texture loading; falls back to the raw box until resolved). (b) per-game runtime, mirrored in BOTH `apps/lines/src/...` and Book of Borut `src/...`: `game/stateGame.svelte.ts` `boardSymbolSizeRatios()` folds the `cellWidth/cellSize` + `cellHeight/cellSize` correction (the node transform scale cancels); `game/symbolMap.ts` `resolveSymbolSizeRatios()` returns a `fit: 'contain' | 'stretch'` provenance (reel branch = contain); `game/utils.ts` `getSymbolInfo` adds a `symbolFit` field; `components/SymbolSprite.svelte` passes `contain={symbolInfo.symbolFit === 'contain'}`. (c) editor preview `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/editor/EditorCanvas.svelte` `drawReelGrid` contain-fits the region (via `regionNaturalSize`, = the orig/untrimmed size Pixi's `texture.width/height` also reports → identical aspect) when `node.symbolSizeRatios` is set. **Parity verified algebraically incl. the node's transform scale (cancels in the cellWidth/cellSize correction) and the trim model (both sides use orig size).** Builds GREEN: `pnpm --filter pixi-svelte build`, `pnpm --filter lines build`, `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` (the launcher's type-check), and Borut `build:engine` + `vite build`. **Ship path:** push this repo → launcher-api auto-deploys (editor preview fixed); the game fix needs the engine `pixi-svelte` change on `main`, then Book of Borut bumps its engine submodule pin (its `contain` change is currently applied to the submodule working tree, uncommitted) + a tokened republish. In-game render not auto-verified (PixiJS/WebGPU per `reference_pixi_engine_verification`) — owner to confirm live. Files: `packages/pixi-svelte/src/lib/components/Sprite.svelte`, `apps/lines/src/{game/stateGame.svelte.ts,game/symbolMap.ts,game/utils.ts,components/SymbolSprite.svelte}`, `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/editor/EditorCanvas.svelte`, + the Book of Borut mirror.

- **Free-Spin Counter — `visibleSource` is now a pickable dropdown + defaults to `freeSpinCounterShow`: ✅ DONE (2026-06-18, engine-layout; pushed).** Owner placed a counter and it showed ALWAYS instead of only during free spins. Root cause: a counter only gets a visibility gate when its `visibleSource` param resolves to a registered `BoolSource` (`ComponentInstance.svelte` wraps the subtree in `<Container visible>` only then; no source ⇒ rendered bare ⇒ always visible). The instance's `visibleSource` was empty, and the param had NO `options`, so the editor rendered it as a blank free-text box (vs the `source` dropdown) — the feed name (`freeSpinCounterShow`) was undiscoverable. Fix: new `VISIBILITY_SOURCE_KEYS = ['freeSpinCounterShow']` catalog (`componentCatalog.ts`); `FREE_SPIN_COUNTER_DEF.visibleSource` now carries `options: VISIBILITY_SOURCE_KEYS` (editor shows a dropdown) + `default: 'freeSpinCounterShow'` (`resolveComponentParams` applies def defaults as the base layer, so a blank instance now inherits the gate — a free-spin counter that's always visible is almost never wanted; clear it to render ungated). Reference games set `visibleSource` explicitly so they're unchanged (parity); the launcher `counter` starter clones the def so NEW counters get the dropdown+default. NOTE: an ALREADY-saved project copy of the counter keeps its old param (no default) — owner must set `visibleSource` on that instance (or re-create from the starter). Intro/outro need NO counter param: `freeSpinCounterShow` already flips true after the intro fires and false at the outro (the intro/outro overlays are separate self-animating components; making them editor-owned is the deferred "animated overlays" initiative). `pnpm --filter engine-layout --filter launcher-api build` GREEN. Files: `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/{componentCatalog,builtinComponents}.ts`.

- **Symbols State Machine — grid now mirrors the game's IN-PLAY symbol set (config-filtered publish): ✅ DONE (2026-06-18, launcher; not committed/pushed).** Owner-reported: the `/symbols` tool showed EVERY symbol, including ones the game doesn't use (e.g. `H5`). Root cause: the grid is scaffolded from each game's coded `SYMBOL_INFO_MAP`, which carries visual/animation defaults for every symbol the engine *can* render — but the authoritative in-play set is the game **config** (`apps/<game>/src/game/config.ts` default-export `symbols`), which the tool never consulted. The launcher is cloud and can't import a game's source, so the fix lives in the existing build→R2 bridge: `apps/launcher-api/scripts/publish-symbol-defaults.mjs` now ALSO imports the game config (`--config`, default `./src/game/config.ts`), and before publishing intersects `SYMBOL_INFO_MAP` with the config's `symbols` keys — keeping `SYMBOL_INFO_MAP`'s ordering, dropping only absent names (e.g. `H5` for lines: 12→11). Best-effort: a missing/odd config, empty `symbols`, or `--no-config-filter` publishes the full set (prior behaviour, build never loses symbols to an unreadable config); a config symbol with no map entry is warned (no silent gap). Verified the filter against the real `apps/lines` config (resolves H1–H4,L1–L5,W,S — no H5; drop confirmed, order preserved). Also filtered the committed offline fallback `apps/launcher-api/src/lib/data/symbolDefaults/lines.json` (removed `H5`) so dev/unpublished `apps/lines` matches. Script `node --check` GREEN. **Owner step:** a published project (e.g. Book of Borut) keeps showing its old full set until it **republishes** (a tokened build runs `publish:symbols`, re-writing `symbols/defaults.json` filtered). Files: `apps/launcher-api/scripts/publish-symbol-defaults.mjs`, `apps/launcher-api/src/lib/data/symbolDefaults/lines.json`, `docs/design/invisible-symbols-state-machine.md`.

- **Editor-placed SPINE nodes were dropped from the built game (key mismatch): ✅ FIXED (2026-06-18, launcher; not committed/pushed → pushed).** Owner-reported: a spine placed in the Scene Editor renders fine in the editor but is MISSING from the built/deployed game. Root cause was a key mismatch in the export→register→resolve chain, NOT a missing pipeline step. The game's doc has every `spine` node's `assetKey` **rewritten from the full R2 bundle prefix down to the plain bundle name** by `resolveSpineKeysForGame` (in `runtimeBundle.ts` AND `routes/api/editor/doc/+server.ts`), and `LayoutNodeView` does `<SpineProvider key={node.assetKey}>` — so the game looks the spine up by the BARE bundle name (e.g. `cauldron`). But `editorArtExport.ts` registered the exported bundle under the **full prefix** (`editorArt.spines[].key = <client>/<project>/spines/<bundle>/`, the un-rewritten `assetKey`), so `bakedEditorArtAssets()` keyed the asset by the prefix → the game's bundle-name lookup never matched → the spine silently failed to load. (The editor PREVIEW works because it renders the raw doc via the looser `resolveEditorSpine`, which accepts the full prefix.) Fix: `editorArtExport` now sets `entry.key = bundleFromAssetKey(clientKey, projectKey, assetKey)` (the SAME rewrite the doc gets) before pushing, so the registration key == the doc's lookup key. One-line change + import; `exportSpineBundle` and the symbol-spine path (symbols aren't `kind:'spine'` nodes, so they're not rewritten) are untouched. The fix covers BOTH the baked (`bake-editor-doc.mjs` → `/api/editor/export-art`) and live-runtime (`buildRuntimeBundle`) paths since both register via `editorArt.spines`. `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN. Export is idempotent → a **republish** rewrites `editor-art/index.json` with the corrected key. **Book of Borut (separate repo):** rebuild/republish so its bake re-fetches the corrected index; its `src/game/editor-scenes.ts` must already register `editorArt.spines` (mirrored 2026-06-14) — bump the engine submodule if it predates that. In-game render not auto-verified (server-side pipeline; PixiJS/WebGPU per `reference_pixi_engine_verification`) — owner to confirm live after republish. File: `apps/launcher-api/src/lib/server/editorArtExport.ts`.

- **Rigger — dedicated keyframable OPACITY control (+ slot colour/opacity easing): ✅ DONE + PUSHED (2026-06-18, launcher static tool, branch `feat/spin-button-states`).** Owner asked for "a new option to keyframe the opacity of my objects." Opacity *technically* already existed but was buried — the Animate-mode slot panel had a single combined "colour" row whose alpha slider auto-keyed the slot's `rgba` timeline, with no dedicated label, no key button, and no easing. Split it: a **colour** row (RGB tint only, preserves the current alpha) + a dedicated **opacity** row (0–100% slider + **◆ Key opacity @ t** button). Each commits through the existing `keySlotColor` by re-reading the *other* channel from `slotColorAt` at the playhead, so retinting never drops opacity and vice-versa — still ONE `rgba` timeline (no conflicting standalone Spine `alpha` channel; stays byte-valid Spine 4.2). Added per-key **easing** for the colour/opacity key — new `setSlotColorCurve` / `slotColorCurveType` mirror the bone `setKeyCurve` (Linear drops the curve, Stepped, or a per-channel bezier array baked from the segment), surfaced as Linear/Stepped/Ease In/Out/In-Out buttons when an rgba key sits at the playhead. **Verified headless:** an Ease-In-Out 100→0% fade samples 1.000/0.500/0.000 at t=0/0.5/1, monotonic, slow at both ends, 16-float curve; Stepped holds. Both inline `<script>` blocks pass `node --check` (vanilla JS, no build step). **Interactive drag not auto-verified** (rigger is auth-gated + needs a loaded rig per `reference_tool_deploy_verification`) — owner to confirm live. Docs: `docs/tools/rigger.md`, `docs/design/invisible-rigger.md` §5.4a. File: `apps/launcher-api/static/rigger/view.html` (`renderSlotAnimDetail`, `slotColorCurveType`, `setSlotColorCurve`).

- **Spin button — rotating per-game custom-art frame + autoplay-only stop + config-gated speed features: ✅ DONE (2026-06-18, engine + launcher; on branch `feat/spin-button-states`, not committed/pushed).** Owner wanted the spin button's icon to spin while reels roll (the universal slot convention) and asked for the full standard feature set. Findings: the engine already shipped turbo (`stateBet.isTurbo` + `ButtonTurbo` + space-hold), autoplay (`autoSpinsCounter` + loss/win limits + the `AutoSpins*` modal suite + `createIntermediateMachineAutoBet`), the BET→STOP morph, and Space-to-spin — so the work was the rotating icon + a config layer, not rebuilding. **Three pieces shipped:** (1) **Rotating custom-art frame** — new `imageSpinning` state-image on `BUTTON_DEF` (authored online via the region picker, rides the editor-art pipeline, no new R2 class) + a `spinning` `engineProvided` boolean; `ButtonFrame.svelte` swaps to that frame and rotates it off the Pixi ticker (1 rev/s, resets upright on stop) only when one is authored (parity otherwise). Cascade in `buttonStateImage.ts` checks `spinning` FIRST (a single roll sets both `spinning` and `disabled`). (2) **Autoplay-only stop model** (owner's refinement — "stop only if autoplay was selected"): a single bet's roll is now INERT (icon spins, button disabled — nothing to stop in ~1s); the STOP only exists to cancel an autoplay sequence (`hasAutoBetCounter()`). Applied in both the coded `ButtonBetProvider`/`ButtonBet` and the parametric `spin` action (+ Space hotkey) in `Game.svelte`. (3) **Config-gated speed features** ("defang via config, never gut"): `stateUi.config.features { turbo, autoplay, spaceHold }` + `UI_FEATURES_UK`/`setUiFeatures` (UK Gambling Commission bans the trio); `UIDefault.svelte` gates the turbo/autospin buttons + `EnableSpaceHold` on the flags. **Authorable in the editor:** added `GameSettings`/`settings?` to `LayoutDoc` (`engine-layout/types.ts`); a "Game Settings" panel in `/editor` (jurisdiction dropdown + 3 toggles, UK forces-off) persists via `editorStorage` `normalizeGameSettings` round-trip + `buildDocPayload`, rides `bake-editor-doc.mjs` verbatim into `baked-editor-bundle.json`, and `Game.svelte` `onMount` applies it via `setUiFeatures` (UK preset overrides). Builds GREEN: `lines` (engine, forced rebuild) + `launcher-api`. Code-reviewed clean (ticker effect no leak/double-add, cascade parity, no double-bet path). **Not visually auto-verified** — the rotation needs an authored `imageSpinning` frame + the instance-button path (off in `apps/lines`; coded stub is a rectangle), and `/editor` is auth-gated; owner to confirm live in Book of Borut once a spin icon is dropped in. Follow-up: layouts leave a gap where a gated turbo/autoplay button was (no reflow); the fully-parametric button-instance bound to `turbo`/`autoSpin` *actions* isn't snippet-gated (editor-side concern). Files (engine): `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/{buttonStateImage.ts,registerComponentActions.ts,builtinComponents.ts,ComponentInstance.svelte,types.ts}`, `packages/components-ui-pixi/src/components/{ButtonFrame,ButtonBetProvider,ButtonBet,UIDefault}.svelte`, `packages/state-shared/src/stateUi.svelte.ts`, `apps/lines/src/components/Game.svelte`; (launcher): `apps/launcher-api/src/lib/server/editorStorage.ts`, `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/editor/+page.svelte`, `apps/launcher-api/scripts/bake-editor-doc.mjs`. See `docs/design/invisible-editor.md` §22.

- **Scene Editor — top-banner declutter: floating canvas device bar + on-canvas undo/redo, real ToolTopBar icon-collapse, permanent Components tab: ✅ DONE (2026-06-18, launcher; not committed/pushed).** Owner-reported: the editor header was so crowded it stretched the whole layout, and the ToolTopBar's "collapse to icon-only when it doesn't fit" was a no-op. Five changes: (1) **`ToolTopBar` real fit-collapse** — replaced the blunt `@media (max-width:1100px)` label-hide with a **ResizeObserver** that drops the tool labels (icon-only) only when the labelled switcher actually overflows its track, remembering the labelled "natural" width so it re-expands with hysteresis (`apps/launcher-api/src/lib/ToolTopBar.svelte`). (2) **New universal `CanvasModeBar.svelte`** (`$lib`, generic over the value type) — a floating segmented control top-centre of a `position:relative` canvas, mirroring the Rigger's `#modeBar` look; **canonical for domain A** (see `docs/ui-inventory.md` §9). (3) **Editor device selector moved off the header onto the canvas** — the `DESKTOP/TABLET/LANDSCAPE/PORTRAIT` pills are now a `<CanvasModeBar>` floating over `.canvas-area` (bound to `currentLayoutType`; non-`desktop` flagged purple, matching the old override accent). (4) **Undo/redo moved onto the canvas** — `EditorCanvas` gained optional `canUndo/canRedo/onUndo/onRedo` props; the buttons render in the top-right `.canvas-actions` next to "Reload art" (only when wired, so the Component Editor's shared `EditorCanvas` shows none). The **Fit button was removed** (the `fitView()` initial framing stays). (5) **Components is now a permanent left tab** next to Library/Outline — the header "Components" toggle button is gone. Dead header CSS (`.layout-pills`/`.pill*`/`.history-btns`/`.hist-btn*`) removed. `z-index:2000` on the bar so it sits above the editor's HUD overlay layers (z-index 1001 in the same stacking context). NOT a tool-registry change → no `roles.ts`/tool-doc rule triggered. `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN. Interactive render not auto-verified (the `/editor` page is auth-gated + needs a project — per `reference_tool_deploy_verification`) — owner to confirm live after deploy. Files: `apps/launcher-api/src/lib/{ToolTopBar,CanvasModeBar}.svelte`, `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/editor/{+page,EditorCanvas}.svelte`, `docs/ui-inventory.md`.

- **Component Editor — "Free-Spin Counter" starter in the New-component dropdown: ✅ DONE (2026-06-18, launcher; not committed/pushed → pushed).** Owner expected to author a free-spin counter from the Component Editor, but the "New component" `<select>` only offered `Blank`/`Button`/`HUD readout` — so there was no way to spin up a fresh counter (the built-in `FREE_SPIN_COUNTER_DEF` was list-editable + scene-placeable, but not a *starter*).
