- **Symbols State Machine — GLOBAL symbol size (`defaultSizeRatios`) with per-cell override: ✅ DONE (2026-06-18, launcher/engine repo; lines-only render-side, Borut mirror pending).** Owner wanted one knob to resize every symbol at once. Symbol render size is `SYMBOL_SIZE * sizeRatios`, set per symbol AND per state with no global default — so resizing all symbols meant editing every cell (resizing reel cells / `engine-layout` `cellSize` scales symbols + grid together and can't change a symbol's size *within* its cell; only `sizeRatios` can). Added an optional doc-level `defaultSizeRatios: { width, height }` modelled on the `winLine`/`highlight` global-config pattern; per-cell `sizeRatios` is now OPTIONAL (absent = inherit the global). **Resolution order at render:** author per-cell override > global `defaultSizeRatios` > coded `SYMBOL_INFO_MAP` size > `{1,1}`. The global wins for ALL symbols incl. special ones (scatter/book/wild); keep one bespoke by setting a per-cell size on it. **Editor UI** (`apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/symbols/+page.svelte`): a new "Symbol size" panel at the top of the grid (Width/Height ratio inputs bound to `doc.defaultSizeRatios`, "Reset to default" + "on" badge); the per-cell panel now shows either W/H inputs + a "↩ Use global size" link (custom size) OR an "Inherits the global symbol size (W×H)" hint + a "Set a custom size for this cell" button (inheriting). **Files (grouped):** _client/UI_ `symbols/+page.svelte` + `symbols/symbols.client.ts` (`SizeRatios`, optional cell `sizeRatios`, `defaultSizeRatios`, `resolveCellSize`, `setDefaultSizeRatios`/`clearDefaultSizeRatios`, `docSignature`, save body); _schema/export/bake_ `src/lib/server/symbolsStorage.ts` (`symbolsDocSchema.defaultSizeRatios` + optional cell `sizeRatios`), `src/lib/server/symbolExport.ts` (`SymbolExportResult.defaultSizeRatios`, forwarded verbatim, `map` stays sparse), `src/routes/api/editor/export-symbols/+server.ts`, `scripts/bake-editor-doc.mjs` (validate + embed `bundle.symbols.defaultSizeRatios`); _engine render-side (lines)_ `apps/lines/src/editor-scenes.ts` (`bakedSymbolDefaultSize()` + `BakedBundle.symbols.defaultSizeRatios`), `apps/lines/src/game/symbolMap.ts` (`resolveSymbolSizeRatios(name,state)` reads the override + coded layers SEPARATELY, not the merged map), overlaid in `apps/lines/src/game/utils.ts#getSymbolInfo`. Runtime (Game Maker) bundle carries it automatically via `SymbolExportResult`. **Scope finding (verified):** the Symbols baked-doc stack is **lines-only** in this repo — cluster/scatter/ways/price have self-contained `SYMBOL_INFO_MAP`s and do NOT use the baked symbols pipeline, so they're unaffected. **Book of Borut render-side mirror: ✅ DONE + PUSHED (2026-06-18, Book-of-Borut `5e320a3`).** Mirrored all four render files into Borut's own stack — `src/game/types.ts` (optional per-cell `sizeRatios`), `src/game/editor-scenes.ts` (`bakedSymbolDefaultSize()` + `BakedBundle.symbols.defaultSizeRatios`, matching Borut's no-runtime-bundle accessor shape), `src/game/symbolMap.ts` (`resolveSymbolSizeRatios`, reads layers separately), `src/game/utils.ts#getSymbolInfo` (overlay, preserving Borut's fallback-symbol logic) — and **bumped the engine submodule** `bfd7e85 → e2a25f5` so `bake:doc` embeds `defaultSizeRatios` (owner's uncommitted in-submodule `bookof.json` edit verified untouched by that range). Borut `vite build` GREEN (note: `vite build` strips types without full TS check; the mirror is verbatim from the type-checked lines impl). Owner step: set a global size in `/symbols` for the bookofborut project + republish (the tokened build bakes it). `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN. In-game render not auto-verified (PixiJS/WebGPU per `reference_pixi_engine_verification`) — owner to confirm live. Docs: `docs/tools/symbols-state-machine.md`, `docs/design/invisible-symbols-state-machine.md`.

- **Win line — configurable from the Symbols State Machine (style + glow + animated draw + amount-text): ✅ DONE (2026-06-18, launcher engine repo + Book of Borut; not committed/pushed).** Owner wanted to style the in-game win line from the `/symbols` tool instead of it being hardcoded. **Widened the existing `winLine` contract** from `{ enabled }` to `{ enabled?, line?, text? }` (all sparse): `line` = colour, thickness (`×SYMBOL_SIZE`), glow on/off + glow colour, animated draw on/off + speed; `text` = font (dropdown of project bitmap fonts + builtins gold/goldblur/silver/purple), size (`×SYMBOL_SIZE`), colour (tint). Colours are CSS hex strings (Pixi 8 `ColorSource`). **Engine/launcher repo:** schema in `symbolsStorage.ts` (`winLineSchema` + prune); client types/sparse setters (`setWinLineLine`/`setWinLineText`/`clearWinLineStyle`) + `docSignature` in `symbols.client.ts`; the `/symbols` `.winline` section expanded into a config panel (`+page.svelte`) with the font list loaded via `resolveEditorFonts` (`+page.server.ts`); verbatim passthrough in `symbolExport.ts` + `bake-editor-doc.mjs`; contract-type-only widen in `apps/lines/src/editor-scenes.ts` (apps/lines has no win line — symbol-glow model). `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN (the `(app)/symbols` page compiled in the bundle). **Book of Borut (renderer):** `editor-scenes.ts#bakedWinLineConfig()` resolves the config with coded defaults (mirrors the old literals → byte-identical when unauthored); `WinLine.svelte` consumes it — stroke colour/thickness, an optional **layered-stroke glow** (no `pixi-filters`/`pixi.js` dep — Borut only depends on `pixi-svelte`), an optional **`svelte/motion` `Tween`** draw (first→last tile, *then* the amount via a `revealed` gate), bitmap amount text via `style.fill` tint; `bookEventHandlerMap.ts` `winInfo` now `await`s `broadcastAsync({type:'winLineShow'})` so an animated line finishes before the symbol glow (non-animated resolves instantly = original timing). Borut `vite build` GREEN. Docs: `docs/tools/symbols-state-machine.md`, `docs/design/invisible-symbols-state-machine.md`. **Not yet shipped end-to-end:** needs a tokened Borut build (bake embeds `bundle.symbols.winLine`) + the engine submodule bump + republish; in-game render not auto-verified (PixiJS/WebGPU per `reference_pixi_engine_verification`) — owner to confirm live by forcing a line win.

- **Free-spin counter — reusable `freeSpinCounter` componentInstance is the LIVE render across ALL reference games (lines + cluster/scatter/ways/price) + Borut code-ready: ✅ DONE (2026-06-18, not committed/pushed).** Finished wiring the existing `FREE_SPIN_COUNTER_DEF` (no new component built). **Live-render path traced (lines):** `apps/lines/src/components/Game.svelte` resolves the `freeSpinCounter` scene from the fetched R2 editor doc, falling back to `fallbackEditorScenes` — which is `defaultLayout('lines')` (= `engine-layout/referenceLayouts/lines.ts`, re-exported via `apps/lines/src/game/defaultLayout.ts`; the "two files" are the SAME module). That scene was ALREADY a `kind:'componentInstance'` (`componentId:'freeSpinCounter'`, `params:{source:'freeSpins',visibleSource:'freeSpinCounterShow',label:'FREE SPIN'}`) with the def + `freeSpins` text source + `freeSpinCounterShow` bool source registered. So lines was *already* live-rendering the componentInstance; only the comments were stale (fixed). **Parity confirmed:** `LayoutNodeView`'s text path emits `<BitmapText>` for any `style.fontFamily` the boot-registered font catalog marks bitmap (§9.4 landed), and each game registers `gold` as `kind:'bitmap'` — so the gold counter renders through the BitmapFont blitter exactly as the old coded `<BitmapText fontFamily='gold'>`. The coded `FreeSpinCounter.svelte` stays registered/in-repo as a fallback (no longer mounted). **cluster/scatter/ways/price — migrated (surgical, NOT a full editor-doc migration):** these 4 are NOT on the editor-doc/`<LayoutScene>`-everywhere path — each mounted `<FreeSpinCounter />` directly via a coded `{#if desktop/landscape}`. Rather than port the whole game onto the R2 doc system, each now registers the def + the two feeds + the bitmap font catalog at boot and renders a single locally-built `freeSpinCounter` componentInstance scene (`space:'game'`) in place of the coded mount. The feeds read LOCAL `$state` driven off the SAME `freeSpinCounter{Show,Hide,Update}` emitter events the coded counter consumed (`current`/`total` fields — `bookEventHandlerMap` untouched), NOT `stateUi` (these games never wrote `stateUi.freeSpinCounter*`). Exact parity: the coded counters were byte-identical across the 4 (frame `Frame_FSCounter.png`, `gold` font, panel `SYMBOL_SIZE*2`, font `SYMBOL_SIZE*0.275`, same `boardLayout()` placement); the instance reproduces the def's SYMBOL_SIZE-120 sizing via `scale = SYMBOL_SIZE/120`. Per-app helper modules `textSource.svelte.ts`/`boolSource.svelte.ts` added (mirror `apps/lines`); `engine-layout` added as a workspace dep to each. **No def change** was needed (all games share the same frame + font, so the hardcoded `Frame_FSCounter.png` default sufficed — frame-image parameterization deferred until a game actually needs a different graphic). **Borut:** code is already ready — `Book of Borut/src/components/Game.svelte` registers `FREE_SPIN_COUNTER_DEF` + the feeds and renders `fsCounterScene` via `<LayoutScene>`. But its checked-in fallback `defaultLayout('bookOf')` still uses `bind:{component:'FreeSpinCounter'}` and its LIVE composition lives in its R2 editor doc. Owner to-do (online editor, no hand-edit of the doc): in Borut's `freeSpinCounter` scene replace the coded `FreeSpinCounter` bind anchor with a `freeSpinCounter` componentInstance, `params: source='freeSpins', visibleSource='freeSpinCounterShow', label='FREE SPIN'`; republish; then bump Borut's engine submodule. Files: `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/builtinComponents.ts` (comments only), `apps/{lines,cluster,scatter,ways,price}/src/components/Game.svelte`, `apps/{cluster,scatter,ways,price}/src/game/{textSource,boolSource}.svelte.ts` (new), `apps/{cluster,scatter,ways,price}/package.json` (+`engine-layout` dep). **Interactive render not auto-verified** (PixiJS/WebGPU screenshot unreliable per `reference_pixi_engine_verification`); parity rests on byte-identical coded specs + green builds — owner to confirm live in free spins.

In `EditorProperties.svelte` (component mode, non-readout) a **"Show button params"** button reveals a checkbox list of those five — tick only the states you need; `onToggleStateParam` (impl `toggleButtonStateParam` in `components/+page.svelte`) adds/removes the full param object (removal prunes orphan bindings). Auto-opens when a re-opened button already has state params. Also added a `RegionPicker` branch to the Defaults editor so an exposed `image`-kind param's default region is settable in the Component Editor (was a plain text box). Runtime is unchanged — ticking any of the 4 state keys enables the existing `resolveButtonStateImage` cascade; author binds their bg sprite's `region`→`image`. Builds GREEN: `engine-layout`, `launcher-api`. **Interactive picker not auto-verified** (the `/components` page is auth-gated) — owner to confirm live. Files: `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/componentCatalog.ts`, `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/editor/EditorProperties.svelte`, `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/components/+page.svelte`.

- **Rigger — Alt-drag to duplicate keyframes (dopesheet + graph), with a ghost preview: ✅ SHIPPED (2026-06-17, launcher static tool).** Owner-requested: select keys (Shift/Ctrl-click or marquee), then **Alt-drag** to duplicate — a faded **ghost** previews the drop while dragging (originals never move), copies land where released, the new keys end up selected. `apps/launcher-api/static/rigger/view.html`. **Dopesheet:** dot `onmousedown` flags `dopeDrag.duplicate = e.altKey`; during the drag, instead of moving the real dot, a `cloneNode` **ghost** (`.tl-key.ghost`, opacity .5, `pointer-events:none`) is appended to the track and tracks the cursor; on mouseup the same furthest-first ordering applies `copyTrackKey` instead of `retimeTrackKey`. New copy dispatcher `copyTrackKey(tr,src,dest)` + per-kind `copyBoneKey`/`copySlotKey`/`copyChannelKey`/`copyDrawOrderKey`/`copyEventKey`, all built on `dupKeyInArr()` (deep-clones the source key via JSON, overwrites any clash at dest, in-place sort so the array reference is kept). **Graph:** `startGraphMove` flags `graphDrag.duplicate`; the duplicate drag does NOT mutate the originals — it stores `graphDrag.dT/dV` and `renderGraphBody` draws **ghost circles** at `start+offset`; on mouseup `finalizeGraphDuplicate(g)` clones each selected key at `start+offset` (one clone per key object even when it backs x+y curves) and selects the clones. Both views: an overlapping duplicate overwrites the clashing key (same semantics as retime). Hint bar shows "⧉ Alt-drag: duplicating N key(s)". Static tool (no build step); inline-script syntax verified clean. **Interactive drag not auto-verified** (auth-gated, needs a loaded rig) — owner to confirm live. Docs: `docs/tools/rigger.md`.

- **Rigger — dopesheet/graph multi-select + multi-move + multi-line select: ✅ SHIPPED (2026-06-17, launcher static tool).** Owner-requested: select & move multiple keys in the dopesheet AND the graph, plus select multiple lines. The **graph** view already had shift-click multi-select + marquee + group-move + box-scale; added a fat transparent **hit-line over each curve** so clicking a curve selects ALL its keys (Shift adds another curve) — "select multiple lines" in the graph. The **dopesheet** was single-key-drag only; rebuilt it with a real selection model (`dopeSel = [{tid,time}]`, `tid = kind:name:ch` matching the `retime*` granularity): click a dot to select, **Shift/Ctrl-click** to toggle, **drag a marquee box across rows** to select keys on multiple lines (Shift = additive; hit-tested by each `.tl-key`'s client rect so it spans the separate row DOM elements), drag any selected key to **retime the whole group** (DOM-only during the drag, applied furthest-first on mouseup so keys sliding past each other don't merge; snaps the grabbed key via new `dopeSnap`), and **Shift/Ctrl-click a track's gutter name** to select every key on that line. **Delete/Backspace** removes the selection in both views (dedup by channel@time in the graph); **Esc** clears it. Marquee is **Shift/Ctrl-drag** (additive) so a plain track drag keeps scrubbing the playhead (owner preference); graph marquee likewise honours Shift/Ctrl as additive. Removed the dead single-key `tlDrag` path. New state (`dopeSel`/`dopeDrag`/`dopeMarquee`/`dopeTrackById`/`dopeDotEls`) + helpers (`trackId`, `dopeSelHas`, `toggleDopeSel`, `selectLineKeys`, `retimeTrackKey`, `dopeSnap`, `startDopeDrag`, `startDopeMarquee`, `positionDopeMarquee`). Vanilla JS/HTML static tool (no build step); inline-script syntax verified clean. **Interactive drag/marquee not auto-verified** (rigger is auth-gated + needs a loaded rig) — owner to confirm live. Docs: `docs/tools/rigger.md`. File: `apps/launcher-api/static/rigger/view.html`.

- **Rigger — per-channel "Key" buttons + toolbar "Key all": ✅ SHIPPED (2026-06-17, launcher static tool).** Owner-requested animation-keying convenience in the Invisible Rigger (`apps/launcher-api/static/rigger/view.html`). (1) In the animate-mode **bone Properties** (`renderBoneAnimDetail`), fields are now grouped, each with a per-channel **Key** button: one beside `rotation` (keys `rotate`), one beside `x`/`y` (keys `translate`), one beside `scaleX`/`scaleY` (keys `scale`) — each calls the existing `keyBone(name, {channel}, true)`. The old full-width "◆ Key this bone @ Ns" button was relabelled **"◆ Key all transforms @ Ns"** (unchanged behaviour: keys rotate+translate+scale of the selected bone). (2) New toolbar button **"◆ Key all"** beside the existing **◆ Key**, wired to a new `keyAll()` that keys *every* bone's pose (rotate+translate+scale) PLUS *every* slot's attachment + colour at the playhead (loops `skeleton.bones` + `rawDoc.slots`, single dopesheet refresh, reports `N bones + M slots` in the hint bar). Pure vanilla JS/HTML in the static tool (no build step); inline-script syntax verified clean. Docs updated (`docs/tools/rigger.md`). File: `apps/launcher-api/static/rigger/view.html` (`#keyAllBtn`, `renderBoneAnimDetail`, `keyBone`/`keyAll`).

- **Editor — image-param frame picks are now ATLAS-SCOPED (a picked frame carries its source atlas): ✅ FIXED + PUSHED (2026-06-17, engine + editor).** Follow-up to the Reload-art fix below: an `image`-kind component param (e.g. a button's "State images") stored only a BARE atlas frame NAME (`T_Button_Plus`), so when two atlases packed the same name it was ambiguous in THREE places — the region picker highlighted the frame in *both* atlases, the runtime's flat texture map let the last-loaded sheet win, and the art exporter guessed "the first project atlas that packs the name." Editing one atlas looked ignored. Fix: encode the source atlas into the picked value as `<assetKey>::<region>` (the SAME format `editorArtTextureKey` already produces) and split it at each resolve point. New `scopedFrameRef()`/`parseScopedFrameRef()` helpers in `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/editorArtKey.ts` (parse only treats the prefix as an atlas when it `isManifestAssetKey` — so a legacy bare name, or a name merely containing `::`, is untouched). `RegionPicker.svelte` gains an **opt-in `scoped` prop**: when set (the editor's image-param picker only — `EditorProperties.svelte`) it emits `<sheetKey>::<region>`, highlights only the matching atlas's frame, and shows the bare name; OFF by default so the Symbols tool (which stores a bare name in `draft.assetKey`) is byte-identical. Runtime `LayoutNodeView.svelte` (`spriteRef`) + editor preview `EditorCanvas.svelte` split the bound region so the scoped atlas overrides the node's. The exporter `editorArtExport.ts` (`addImageRef`) ships the EXACT manifest from a scoped value instead of name-guessing. **Backward compatible** — a legacy bare name takes the identical pre-fix path at every site (runtime, preview, picker highlight, export). Reviewed (`code-reviewer`): manifest-key alignment confirmed (picker `sheet.key` = exporter `sheets[].key` = runtime `editorArtNamespace(sheet.key)`), no other `RegionPicker`/image-param consumer broken. Builds GREEN: `engine-layout`, `launcher-api`, `lines`. **Book of Borut vendors the engine as a submodule — owner must bump it + republish to pick up the runtime (`LayoutNodeView`) + `editorArtKey` change** (editor/launcher half deploys from `main`). Files: `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/{editorArtKey.ts,LayoutNodeView.svelte}`, `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/editor/{RegionPicker,EditorProperties,EditorCanvas}.svelte`, `apps/launcher-api/src/lib/server/editorArtExport.ts`.

- **Editor — edited atlas kept showing the OLD image on name-resolved sprites (Reload art did nothing): ✅ FIXED + PUSHED (2026-06-17, branch `game-maker` `3925293`).** Owner-reported: changed/removed an image in an atlas, but a button component's sprite kept drawing the old art even after "Reload art" and a full hard reload. Root cause was NOT caching — it was the cross-atlas name resolver. A sprite that resolves a frame by NAME (catalog/bound sprite, or a sprite whose own atlas no longer packs the frame) goes through `spriteRegionIndex` (the project-wide `region-name → manifest` map) in `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/editor/EditorCanvas.svelte`. Two bugs there: (1) **`refreshAssets()` (Reload art) never reset `spriteRegionIndex`/`regionScanStarted`** — it cleared images + region-sets but left the name→manifest map intact, so a name-resolved sprite kept pointing at whatever manifest the prior scan locked onto; editing the atlas (or re-exporting) changed nothing for it. Now the map is cleared + flagged for rebuild on the next draw. (2) **The one-time index scan folded manifests in promise-RESOLUTION order**, so when two atlases packed the same region name the "winner" was nondeterministic (whichever `/api/editor/regions` fetch resolved first) — and a *different* atlas that still carried the old frame could silently shadow the edited one. Now fetched in parallel but **folded in listing order** (`regionContainerKeys()` order — first container wins, deterministic), and a `console.warn` fires when two atlases pack the same region name instead of silently shadowing the loser. Same class of issue as the per-manifest texture scoping the game already does (`<assetKey>::<region>`). Type-check: `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN. **Note for authors:** duplicate region names ACROSS atlases are inherently ambiguous — to control which art a name-resolved sprite draws, keep the name unique (rename/remove the dup) or bind the sprite to a specific atlas key; the new console warning now surfaces these collisions. File: `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/editor/EditorCanvas.svelte` (`refreshAssets`, `ensureRegionIndex`).

- **Storybook ENGINE-LAYOUT stories crashed ("addToParent" undefined) + new "Authored Components" gallery: ✅ FIXED + ADDED (2026-06-16).** (1) **Crash fix (pushed `6b92b3a`):** every `ENGINE-LAYOUT/*` story (button, HUD, freespin counter, componentInstance parity, value feed) rendered `<LayoutScene>`/`<UI>`/`<Text>` directly inside `<StoryGameTemplate>` — which sets the pixi _app_ context (via the game's `setContext()`) but never renders an `<App>`. The `@@pixi_parent` context is created ONLY by `<App>` (`InitialiseParent`), so `getContextParent()` was `undefined` and `Container.svelte`'s `parentContext.addToParent(node)` threw, blanking the whole story iframe. Wrapped each story's pixi content in the game's `<App>` (provides the parent context + loads the full game asset manifest). `ComponentsGame`/`Mode*` stories were unaffected (they render `<Game>`, which has its own `<App>`). Verified live in Storybook (button + parity render, no errors). Files: `apps/lines/src/stories/{ButtonComponentInstance,HudComponentInstance,EngineLayoutComponentInstance,FreeSpinCounterComponentInstance,EngineLayoutValueFeed}.stories.svelte`. (2) **Authored Components gallery (NEW):** the hardcoded Button stories only registered SAMPLE defs — no Storybook surface showed the author's REAL components saved in the Component Editor (owner-reported: "they show in editor and in game, but not in storybook"). Added `COMPONENTS/Authored (live)` (`apps/lines/src/stories/AuthoredComponents.stories.svelte`): on mount it fetches `/api/editor/components/all` (new launcher endpoint), registers every def, and renders each as a live `ComponentInstance` in a grid, each isolated in a `<svelte:boundary>` (so a component referencing a missing spine/bitmap-font degrades to a "render failed" tile, never blanks the gallery). The published engine Storybook is served **same-origin** from the launcher (`/storybook/view/...`, auth-gated), so the fetch carries the session cookie; in local dev (no session/cross-origin) it shows a clear fallback message instead of crashing (verified against the static build). **CAVEAT:** a component's project-specific ART only resolves if that asset is in the lines bundle, so cross-project buttons render structure + text + engine-asset parts but may show empty art (full-fidelity art per project = that project's own published Storybook). **Backend:** `listAllComponents()` in `apps/launcher-api/src/lib/server/componentStorage.ts` enumerates the `editor/<projectKey>/components/*.json` prefix (all projects) + `_shared/editor-components/` (built-ins excluded); served by `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/api/editor/components/all/+server.ts` (session-gated on the `editor` tool, like the sibling routes). `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` + `apps/lines build-storybook` both GREEN. **Owner step:** republish the engine Storybook (and the launcher auto-deploys the endpoint) to see the gallery populate live.

- **Editor-layout (doc) spines now ship to the game: ✅ DONE code-only (2026-06-14, engine pushed; Borut needs submodule bump + republish).** Closed the gap noted below: an editor-placed `spine` node rendered in the editor but was MISSING in the built game (its bundle was never exported/registered — `editorArtExport` only handled `sprite` nodes). Now mirrors the symbol-spine path. **Refactor:** factored the spine bundle copy into one shared helper `exportSpineBundle()` in `apps/launcher-api/src/lib/server/spine.ts` (copies atlas + skeleton + pages; renames a Rigger `.irig` skeleton → `.json` so `PIXI.Assets.load` can parse it; returns `{entry,written}` or null for a non-bundle/coded key). `symbolExport.ts` now calls it (no behaviour change). **New wiring:** `editorArtExport.ts` collects `node.kind === 'spine'` assetKeys, copies each via the helper, and adds `index.spines` (`EditorArtSpine = ExportedSpineEntry`); `bake-editor-doc.mjs` embeds `editorArt.spines`; the game's `bakedEditorArtAssets()` (apps/lines `editor-scenes.ts`) registers each as a `type:'spine'` asset under the node's `assetKey` — the exact key `LayoutNodeView` hands `<SpineProvider>`. The doc renderer already guards the animation (`{#if anim}`) so a no-animation spine won't crash. `editor-art/` is already in `pull-project-assets.mjs` `GENERATED_SUBTREES`, so the files travel + prune automatically. Verified: `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` + `pnpm --filter lines build` GREEN. **Book of Borut:** its own `src/game/editor-scenes.ts` got the identical mirror (committed in the Borut repo); it still needs an **engine-submodule bump + republish** to pick up the launcher-side export/bake changes.

- **Rigger spine in the Symbols State Machine — crashed the game (skeleton load + animation): ✅ FIXED (2026-06-14).** Owner bound a brand-new Rigger spine (`tes1`) to a symbol's `win` state; the built Book of Borut game crashed loading it. **Two distinct bugs, two fixes:** (1) **launcher (committed `c1f67f4`, deployed):** the symbol export (`apps/launcher-api/src/lib/server/symbolExport.ts`) copied the bound bundle's skeleton VERBATIM, so a Rigger rig shipped its native `<name>.irig` extension. The game loads spines via `PIXI.Assets.load`, which resolves the skeleton parser by FILE EXTENSION — `.irig` is unknown to it → skeleton loaded `null` → `readSkeletonData(null)` threw "Cannot read properties of null (reading 'skeleton')" → spine never registered ("key … not found in loadedAssets"). The bytes ARE valid Spine JSON, so the export now ships the skeleton under a `.json` name (the `.atlas` never references the skeleton, so renaming is safe) and points the deploy index at it. Verified live: `editor-symbols/tes1/index.json` now lists `skeleton: editor-symbols/tes1/tes1.json` (200). (2) **engine (`packages/pixi-svelte/src/lib/components/SpineTrack.svelte`):** after the skeleton loaded, the symbol cell had no `animationName` (the Symbols tool's "(first animation)" default leaves it empty), so `setAnimation(_, null)` threw "animationName cannot be null". The tool's PREVIEW already falls back to the skeleton's first animation (`SymbolSpinePreview` `animationName || firstAnimation`) but the engine's `SpineTrack` passed the empty name straight through. `SpineTrack` now resolves `props.animationName || skeletonData.animations[0]?.name || null` and skips `setAnimation` when there's nothing to play — so "(first animation)" works in-game (matching the tool) and a missing name never hard-crashes. Verified: `pnpm --filter pixi-svelte build` GREEN. **Immediate owner workaround without an engine bump:** in the Symbols tool set the cell's animation dropdown to the explicit name (`animation`) instead of "(first animation)", save, republish. **Book of Borut vendors the engine as a submodule — owner must bump it + republish to pick up the `SpineTrack` hardening** (the launcher `.irig→.json` fix already ships via a plain republish). Note: spines placed via the Invisible EDITOR (not the Symbols tool) are still not exported into the game bundle at all — separate known gap.

- **Background sprite cover-fit — game stretched the background vertically (editor was correct): ✅ FIXED + PUSHED (2026-06-14).** Owner-reported: a doc-driven `background`-space SPRITE rendered stretched/overflowing downward in the built game at any art ratio, while the Invisible Editor preview looked right. Root cause: the editor sizes background art with the shared `coverTransform()` (a TRUE cover from the art's natural width AND height), but the game runtime's sprite path (`packages/engine-layout/src/lib/LayoutNodeView.svelte`) routed through `utils-layout`'s `normalBackgroundLayout`/`createBackgroundLayout`, which sets only ONE of width/height (chosen by the configured `backgroundRatio`). For a PIXI sprite, `sprite.width = X` only touches `scale.x` — `scale.y` stays `1`, so the other axis renders at natural pixel size (e.g. a 3413×1920 bg in a 1920×1080 window → width squeezed to 1920 but height left at 1920px → overflow = "stretched down"). The single-axis trick only ever worked for SPINE backgrounds (whose `spineSizeScale` applies a _uniform_ scale). Fix (fork-addition, not an upstream bug): the runtime sprite background now reads the loaded texture's natural size from pixi-svelte's app context (`getContextApp().stateApp.loadedAssets`) and computes the same `coverTransform()` the editor uses, applying the per-axis cover scale via `scale` and dropping the `width`/`height` props (so `propsSyncEffect` can't clobber `scale`). Editor == game for any aspect/`coverScale`/`stretch`/`fit`; non-background sprites + the spine background path (`<SpineProvider fit>`) are untouched (parity). Verified: `pnpm --filter engine-layout build` GREEN; svelte-check adds 0 new errors; code-reviewer pass (no TDZ — `$derived` lazy getters; safe transient before texture load → centred fallback). **Book of Borut vendors the engine as a submodule — owner must bump it (republish) to pick this up.** File: `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/LayoutNodeView.svelte`. Shared cover helper: `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/coverTransform.ts`.

- **Invisible Debug — publish-time debug toggle + in-game debug menu: ✅ D1–D7 SHIPPED + PUSHED (2026-06-14).** Engine D1–D5 on `main` (`9a3708e` D1–D4, `f49278f` D5); Book of Borut D6 in its repo (`e7df1e1`, engine submodule bumped to `9a3708e`); launcher D7 in `invisible-launcher` (`d7050d4`). New framework so published games can ship debug tooling on/off (chosen at publish time) with an on-screen menu — replacing the fiddly per-game `localStorage.IE_DEBUG` + `d`-hotkey gates. Design + build plan (D1–D7): `docs/design/invisible-debug-framework.md`. **Three layers:** (1) **`__IE_DEBUG__`** — a Vite `define` compile-time global (`mode !== 'production' || PUBLIC_IE_DEBUG === '1'`), added to all `apps/*/vite.config.js` + `scripts/new-game.mjs`; statically `false` in a normal prod build so ALL debug code tree-shakes out (proven both directions on `apps/lines`: 0 files contain `SYMBOL DEBUG`/`Invisible Debug`/`registerDebugTool` when off; present when `PUBLIC_IE_DEBUG=1`). Helper `packages/utils-shared/src/debug.ts#DEBUG_BUILD` + ambient `debug.d.ts` (one per package whose tsconfig scopes its own dir: utils-shared, components-pixi, components-ui-html, apps/lines). (2) **Registry** — `packages/state-shared/src/stateDebug.svelte.ts` (`stateDebug` runes store: `enabled`/`tools`/`active`/`register`(idempotent by id)/`toggle`) + `registerDebugTool({id,label,group?,surface,component})`, `DebugSurface='pixi'|'html'`. (3) **Surfaces (two mount points, Pixi≠DOM)** — `<DebugMenu>` (components-ui-html; floating bottom-right button + panel listing tools with on/off, hosts `surface:'html'` tools; inline styles so its CSS also tree-shakes) and `<DebugStage>` (components-pixi; mounts inside `<App>`, renders active `surface:'pixi'` tools); both `{#if __IE_DEBUG__}`-gated. **apps/lines migrated (D4):** `SymbolDebugOverlay`→registered pixi tool (`components/debug/SymbolDebugTool.svelte`, resolves via merged-map `getSymbolInfo`), new HTML `WinStateProbe`, registered in `game/debugTools.ts` loaded via a `__IE_DEBUG__`-gated dynamic `import`; old `SymbolDebug.svelte` + `d`-hotkey/`$effect` deleted; `<DebugStage/>` in `<App>`, `<DebugMenu/>` after `<Modals/>`. Verified `pnpm --filter lines build` GREEN. **D5 ✅ (2026-06-14, this commit):** mirrored onto all five other reference games — cluster/scatter/ways/price each get a `surface:'pixi'` symbol-overlay tool + `<DebugStage/>`/`<DebugMenu/>`; number-picker (pure-HTML, no Pixi App) gets `<DebugMenu/>` + a placeholder `debugTools.ts` (framework present for future tools, no `<DebugStage/>`). All five `pnpm --filter <game> build` GREEN. **D7 ✅ (2026-06-14, pushed to the `invisible-launcher` repo `d7050d4`):** a "🐞 Debug tools" checkbox on the Projects toolbar → `build_game` injects `PUBLIC_IE_DEBUG=1` (default off = clean player builds; the desktop launcher exe must be republished for OTHER users to get the checkbox). **D6 ✅ (2026-06-14, Book-of-Borut `e7df1e1`):** engine submodule bumped cffb230→`9a3708e`; `SymbolDebugOverlay.svelte`→`components/debug/SymbolDebugTool.svelte`, new `WinStateProbe`, `<DebugStage/>`/`<DebugMenu/>` mounted, old `localStorage.IE_DEBUG`+`d`-hotkey/`$effect` probe removed; `vite.config.js` wrapped in `defineConfig(({mode})=>…)` with the `__IE_DEBUG__` define; production `vite build --mode production` confirmed to tree-shake the debug tools out. Interim debug build without the launcher checkbox: run the build with `PUBLIC_IE_DEBUG=1`. **Owner step to see it live in Borut: republish with the new "🐞 Debug tools" checkbox ON** (the deployed bundle predates this until republished).

- **Editable game HUD — ROTATION now applied in-game (editor↔game parity): ✅ LANDED code-only (2026-06-14, not committed/deployed — owner review pending).** Bug (owner-reported): rotating a HUD element in the Invisible Editor showed the rotation in the editor preview but it was DROPPED in the built/deployed game. Root cause: the HUD bottom-bar/corners have a bespoke renderer (`LayoutEditable.svelte`) that re-implements node placement instead of going through `<LayoutNodeView>` (which already applies rotation). The resolver `hudPos()` (`packages/components-ui-pixi/src/hudPositions.ts`) returned only `{x, y, scaleX, scaleY, visible}` — it never surfaced `t.rotation` from `resolveTransform()` — and every HUD `<Container>` forwarded only `x`/`y`/`scale`. So scene sprites (via `<LayoutNodeView>`) rotated fine, but the reserved HUD elements (balance/win/bet readouts + the menu/buyBonus/autoSpin/spin/turbo/decrease/increase buttons), the corner logo/game-name, the placed-`componentInstance` HUD nodes, and the portrait drawer toggle all silently ignored rotation. Fix (fork-addition, not an upstream engine bug): (1) `HudPos` gains a required `rotation` field; `hudPos()` returns `t.rotation ?? 0` (fallback branch → 0). (2) `LayoutEditable.svelte` — every `pos.*`/`instance.pos` wrapping `<Container>` (desktop + portrait branches, corners, `instanceView` snippet, drawer toggle) now forwards `rotation={…}`. **Parity:** unrotated docs resolve rotation 0 (identity) → byte-identical to before. Verified: `pnpm --filter lines build` GREEN. Files: `packages/components-ui-pixi/src/hudPositions.ts`, `packages/components-ui-pixi/src/components/LayoutEditable.svelte`. **Latent sibling gap (NOT fixed — separate from the rotation report):** the corner logo/game-name containers still ignore `scale` (only x/y/rotation now), so scaling those two corner texts in the editor won't ship either; revisit if reported. **Book of Borut vendors the engine as a submodule — owner must bump it to pick this up.** Design: `docs/design/invisible-editor.md` (HUD section).

- **Invisible Editor — undo/redo + copy/paste/duplicate + multi-select: ✅ LANDED code-only (2026-06-13, not committed/deployed).** Three editor-wide capabilities, all in `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/editor/` (+ the shared `EditorCanvas`/`EditorOutline`, and the `/components` Component Editor adapted to the new selection shape):

  - **Undo/redo** — snapshot history hung off the single `markDirty()` choke point (so it covers every edit automatically, incl. the screen-panel ops). Bursts within 350 ms coalesce into one step; canvas drags already commit once on mouse-up = one step. `Ctrl/⌘+Z` / `Shift+Z` / `Ctrl+Y` + toolbar ↶↷ buttons. `resetHistory()` on a wholesale layout load. Snapshots use `$state.snapshot` (reactive proxies aren't `structuredClone`-able). Cap 80 steps.
  - **Copy / cut / paste / duplicate** — `Ctrl/⌘+C/X/V/D`. Operates on the whole selection; clones get fresh ids (recursive, `slotId` dropped) + a 24px nudge; paste drops into the ACTIVE scene (so it also moves nodes between screens). **Screen-level duplicate** = a row button (fresh scene + node ids, "… copy"). In-memory clipboard (cross-tab not wired).
  - **Multi-select** — `selectedIds[]` replaces the single `selectedId` (kept as a derived "primary" for the Properties panel + transform handles). Canvas: **Shift+click toggles** a node (Shift+drag on empty space still pans; Shift mid-drag still constrains); plain-drag a member moves the whole group; outline-only on the set, handles only when one is selected; Delete removes all (one undo step via new `onDeleteMany`). Outliner: **Shift = range** (pre-order flatten), **Ctrl/⌘ = toggle**. Properties shows an "N selected" pill.
  - Verified: `vite build` clean + `svelte-check` adds **0 new errors** (20 pre-existing, build-tolerated). Interactive feel is unverified (auth-gated editor) — owner to confirm live.

- **Invisible Editor — Screens panel management (reorder / delete / rename / create): ✅ LANDED code-only (2026-06-12, not committed/deployed).** The `/editor` SCREENS list is now fully editable: **drag-to-reorder** (HTML5 DnD, constrained within a group — game↔game, HUD↔HUD — since the editor composites game scenes in doc-array order and HUD always draws on top; a blue line shows the drop slot), **delete** (✕ button, confirm + active-scene re-resolve), **inline rename** (double-click the name → focused input, Enter/blur commit, Esc cancel), and two new create buttons — **"＋ New empty screen"** (`addEmptyScreen`, auto-named `Screen 001`…) and **"＋ New HUD screen"** (`addHudScreen`, blank `standard`-space scene minted with a `hud_` id so it groups under the top-layer HUD section). All live in `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/editor/+page.svelte` (`moveScene`/`deleteScene`/`startRenameScene`/`commitRenameScene`/`addEmptyScreen`/`addHudScreen` + drag state + sceneRow snippet + CSS). **Engine touch (recorded):** `packages/engine-layout/.../referenceLayouts/hud.ts#isHudScene` broadened to also match `hud_`-prefixed ids (editor-only consumer; runtime unaffected). **DEFERRED follow-up (owner asked to keep for later):** the reference games (`apps/lines` `Game.svelte`, etc.) mount scenes by hardcoded **id** in a fixed code order, so a brand-new empty/HUD screen and a reorder change the EDITOR preview but do **not** yet drive the shipped game — only `background`-space scenes render generically today. Wiring the runtime to render doc scenes generically by doc order (so new screens + reorder ship automatically) is the next engine step for this feature.

- **Invisible Symbols State Machine — S1 engine contract (symbol map made data-driven): ✅ LANDED code-only (2026-06-12, not committed/deployed).** New tool (owner-named) to author the symbol→state→asset map — the editable twin of the in-game Symbol Debug grid (`SymbolDebugOverlay.svelte`, gated on `localStorage.IE_DEBUG=1` + `d`). v1 = **bindings only** (no payline geometry), **standalone `/symbols` page**, **spine included** (spine "export" = mirror already-made bundles, not authoring). Design + ordered build plan (S1–S5): `docs/design/invisible-symbols-state-machine.md`. **S1 done (this entry) — the foundation everything hangs off:** `apps/lines/src/game/constants.ts#SYMBOL_INFO_MAP` is now overridable by a baked symbols doc. New `apps/lines/src/game/symbolMap.ts` (`getActiveSymbolInfoMap()` = coded map ⊕ baked overrides, cell-level sparse merge, lazily memoised so module init never races the bundle import); `game/utils.ts#getSymbolInfo` reads it; shared `SymbolCellInfo`/`SymbolInfoMap` types in `game/types.ts`; `editor-scenes.ts` gains `BakedBundle.symbols?:{map,index}` + `bakedSymbolMap()` (overrides, `undefined` when un-baked) + `bakedSymbolAssets()` (registers any sprite-sheet/image/**spine** the bindings introduce, mirroring `bakedEditorArtAssets`); `stateApp.ts` spreads `bakedSymbolAssets()` into `createApp({assets})`. **Pure parity** — no `symbols` field in the baked bundle yet ⇒ game renders byte-identical (un-baked repos + `apps/lines` dev get the coded map). Verified: `svelte-check` GREEN (0 errors; the 2 warnings are pre-existing tsconfig deprecations). **S2 done (2026-06-12, not committed):** doc schema + R2 read/write endpoints in the launcher. New `apps/launcher-api/src/lib/server/symbolsStorage.ts` (Zod `symbolsDocSchema` + `loadSymbolsDoc`/`saveSymbolsDoc`/`normalizeSymbolsDoc`/`emptySymbolsDoc`; sparse, `.strict()` cell, 6 fixed states — matches the S1 `SymbolInfoMap`); `symbolsDocKey()` in `projectPaths.ts` → R2 `<client>/<project>/symbols/symbols.json`; `GET`/`PUT /api/editor/symbols?project=<key>` (session-gated on the `editor` tool grant, mirrors the `component`/`doc` routes; 400 on Zod-invalid). Introduced `zod` to the launcher for the first time (already in the monorepo lockfile via `game-spec`) — schema is the shared source of truth for S3+S4. `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN. **S3 done (2026-06-12):** the `/symbols` grid tool page (launcher). Route `src/routes/(app)/symbols/+page.svelte` (+ `+page.server.ts`, `symbols.client.ts`, `SymbolSpritePreview.svelte`, `SymbolSpinePreview.svelte`); `symbolDefaults.ts` + generated `src/lib/data/symbolDefaults/lines.json` (the coded `SYMBOL_INFO_MAP` exported from the engine via `node --experimental-strip-types` — the grid's symbol list + per-cell DEFAULT bindings). Grid = symbols × the 6 states; each cell shows the effective binding (override ?? coded default) with a live preview (sprite via reused `RegionThumb`/`fetchRegions`; spine via reused spine primitives — only the FOCUSED cell mounts a live WebGL skeleton, so ~72 cells don't jank), an "edited" badge + Reset-to-default; click a cell → editor panel (type toggle, `RegionPicker` for sprite frame / spine bundle+animation picker, numeric sizeRatios); Save → `PUT /api/editor/symbols` with dirty tracking. Registered the `symbols` tool in `roles.ts` (TOOLS/ROLE_TOOLS/TOOL_BAR_ORDER/TOOL_DOC_SLUG) and switched the `/api/editor/symbols` gate from `editor` → `symbols`. `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN. **Two v1 follow-ups:** (a) the preview endpoints `/api/editor/regions`+`/api/editor/spine` are still `editor`-gated, so a `symbols`-only user 403s on previews (admin/developer/artist hold both — widen in S4); (b) default-art previews are placeholder chips until a project's symbol assets are in R2 (seeding — resolved by S4). **S4 done (2026-06-12):** the export → deploy → bake → pull chain (spine-aware) — a rebound symbol now actually ships. New `symbolExport.ts#exportEditorSymbols` (mirrors `editorArtExport`/`fontExport`): sprite cells → resolve the frame name to its containing project sheet, export TexturePacker JSON (frames keyed by name, NO namespace) + page into `deploy/editor-symbols/<stem>/`; spine cells → copy the bundle's own atlas+skeleton+pages VERBATIM (names preserved so atlas page refs resolve); dedup, prune, idempotent. Returns `{ map: doc.symbols (verbatim — no rewrite), index:{sheets,images,spines,collisions} }` whose keys match the cells' assetKeys exactly, so S1's `bakedSymbolMap()`/`bakedSymbolAssets()` line up with zero rewriting (offline fixture confirmed). New `POST /api/editor/export-symbols` (deploy-token gated, mirrors export-art). `bake-editor-doc.mjs` fetches it + embeds `bundle.symbols={map,index}` + warns on un-namespaced frame collisions; `pull-project-assets.mjs` adds `editor-symbols` to `GENERATED_SUBTREES` (mirror+prune). Refactored `spine.ts` to export `resolveBundlePrefix`/`atlasPageNames`/`SkeletonIndexEntry` + new `loadSkeletonIndex` (the verbatim-copy primitives; `resolveEditorSpine` now reuses it, no behaviour change). **Fixed an S3 bug:** the spine `<select>` stored `b.name` but resolution needs the full R2 prefix — now `value={b.key}` (display shows a basename). `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN. **Remaining: S5** prove end-to-end on Book of Borut — OWNER-INVOLVED (separate repo, engine submodule): (1) mirror S1's `symbolMap.ts`/`getSymbolInfo` into Book of Borut (record-every-engine-change rule); (2) keep BoB symbol frame names unique across bound sheets (the new bake collision warning surfaces violations); (3) verify the `_shared/spines/` fallback in the smoke test (BoB symbols likely share bundles); (4) rebind a symbol online → `pnpm build` → republish → new asset/animation shows in-game. **Must mirror the `getSymbolInfo`/`symbolMap.ts` change into Book of Borut + the other reference games when S1 ships.** **Post-S4 follow-ups landed (2026-06-12, committed):** (a) **responsive grid** — `/symbols` was a tiny fixed table; now full-width (`table-layout:fixed`) with a ResizeObserver-driven `--cell` size so previews scale with the window (width-driven, clamped 72–168px, vertical scroll); also repaint `RegionThumb` on size change (its canvas clears on resize). (b) **per-project defaults AUTO-PUBLISHED** (closes "Borut shows lines symbols"): each game publishes its coded `SYMBOL_INFO_MAP` to R2 `symbols/defaults.json` at build via new `apps/launcher-api/scripts/publish-symbol-defaults.mjs` (`node --experimental-strip-types`, imports the game's TS map) → deploy-token-gated `PUT /api/editor/symbol-defaults` → `savePublishedSymbolDefaults`; tool `+page.server.ts` reads `loadPublishedSymbolDefaults` first, falls back to committed `lines.json`. `scripts/new-game.mjs` scaffolds a `publish:symbols` step into `build`, so new games publish automatically. **Book of Borut still needs:** the S1 contract mirror in its own `src/game/*` + ONE tokened build (or a manual `publish:symbols` run) to populate its defaults — then the tool shows Borut's real symbols. See design doc "Per-project defaults — automatic publish".
- **Reel spin regression — board jumped to the new symbols before spinning: ✅ FIXED code-only (2026-06-12, not committed/deployed).** After `7834892` ("reelGrid gaps … drive the live board") `apps/lines` started passing `symbolHeight` as a getter (`() => boardGeometry().rowPitchLocal`) instead of the static `SYMBOL_SIZE`. That flipped `isReactiveHeight` to `true`, which ACTIVATED the new re-home `$effect` added in the same commit (`createReelForSpinning.svelte.ts`). The effect depended on `reelY.current`, so it re-ran on every reel-Y change and snapped the reel back to `homeY()` whenever it was parked off-home while `motion === 'stopped'`. During **pre-spin setup** (`preSpin` → `preSpinPadding`) `isPreSpinning` is already `true` but `motion` is still `'stopped'` and the reel is deliberately placed at `topY` (old symbols at the bottom, ready to slide in) — so the effect "corrected" it to home, landing the window straight on the new top-of-stack symbols. Result: click → symbols swap instantly → THEN the spin loop runs. Fix: re-key the effect off the height VALUE alone (track `lastHeight`, fire `placeY(homeY())` only when `getSymbolHeight()` actually changes — its documented intent: the editor's reactive row-pitch override loading after init), never off `reelY.current`. Inert for a static-number height (other games) and for any spin/pre-spin parking. Cascading reel was unaffected (no such effect). Verified: clean dev boot, no console errors; Prettier clean. File: `packages/utils-slots/src/createReelForSpinning.svelte.ts`. **Book of Borut vendors the engine as a submodule — owner must bump it to pick this up.**
- **Editable game HUD — PORTRAIT now data-driven (drawer preserved): ✅ LANDED code-only (2026-06-12, not committed/deployed — owner review pending).** Closes the Phase 3 gap below ("Portrait stays coded"). Authors who positioned the HUD in the editor's portrait view still saw the OLD coded `LayoutPortrait` in-game, because `UIDefault.svelte` hard-excluded portrait from the editable path (`&& layoutType() !== 'portrait'`). Fork-addition (our framework work, not an engine bug): (1) **`UIDefault.svelte`** — replaced the blanket portrait exclusion with a **parity gate** `hudHasPortrait(props.hud)` (new helper in `hudPositions.ts`): portrait uses `LayoutEditable` ONLY when at least one bar/corners node carries an `overrides.portrait` entry; otherwise it keeps the coded `LayoutPortrait` (parity for `apps/lines` + un-refreshed docs). The other three layoutTypes are unchanged. (2) **`LayoutEditable.svelte`** — added a `{#if layoutType === 'portrait'}` branch that REPRODUCES the fold-out drawer: same `DRAWER_Y`/`DRAWER_BUTTON_Y` constants, `drawerTween`/`drawerButtonTween` (`cubicInOut`), and `subscribeOnMount({drawerButtonShow/Hide, drawerUnfold/Fold})` wiring as `LayoutPortrait`. The reserved bar ids are grouped exactly as the coded layout (DRAWER group = menu/buyBonus/autoSpin/bet/turbo/balance under `y={drawerTween.current}`; win follows `y={Math.min(tween,350)}`; always-visible = decrease/increase + bet↔`LabelFreeSpinCounter` on `stateUi.freeSpinCounterShow`; `ButtonDrawer` in a `FadeContainer` on `stateUi.drawerButtonShow`, `y={drawerButtonTween.current}`). The tween y-offsets ride ON TOP of the AUTHORED portrait positions (author authors the UNFOLDED resting layout). `mounted()` double-render suppression, free-author-art (`LayoutNodeView`), `componentInstance` expansion, and label/tint overrides all stay live in portrait. Added a portrait branch to the menu overlay `{#if}` chain (mirrors `LayoutPortrait`'s menu cluster). Desktop/landscape/tablet markup is byte-identical (moved into the `{:else}`). The drawer toggle button (no reserved desktop id) resolves an optional `hud-btn-drawer` node, falling back to the coded portrait coord `(W*0.5+440, H-105)` from `STANDARD_MAIN_SIZES_MAP.portrait`. (3) **`referenceLayouts/hud.ts`** — each bar node now seeds a `portrait` override (the UNFOLDED resting positions, scale 1), COMPUTED from `LayoutPortrait`'s constants over the 1080×1920 box (`PW`/`PH`): balance `(540,1650)`, win `(540,1250)`, bet `(540,1790)`, menu `(100,1520)`, buyBonus `(980,1520)`, autoSpin `(360,1520)`, bet-btn `(540,1520)`, turbo `(720,1520)`, decrease `(150,1835)`, increase `(930,1835)`. So a freshly-seeded / "Refresh HUD layer" doc opts into the data-driven portrait and renders byte-for-byte like the coded `LayoutPortrait`; corners (canvas/screenAnchor) are layoutType-agnostic, unchanged. **Parity caveat:** the data-driven portrait is byte-identical to coded ONLY against a doc carrying the seeded portrait overrides; a doc with portrait authoring but a DIFFERENT (author-moved) layout renders that layout (intended). The drawer-button slot is the one element with no `hud-btn-*` reserved id — it's positioned from the computed fallback unless the author adds a `hud-btn-drawer` node. Verified: `pnpm --filter engine-layout build` GREEN (regenerated `scenes/bookof.json` — portrait overrides confirmed: spin `(540,1520)`, balance `(540,1650)`); `pnpm --filter lines build` GREEN (full Svelte+TS compile of `UIDefault`/`LayoutEditable`/`hud.ts`); Prettier clean on all four files. **Book of Borut vendors the engine as a submodule — owner must bump it to pick this up.** Files: `packages/components-ui-pixi/src/components/{UIDefault,LayoutEditable}.svelte`, `packages/components-ui-pixi/src/hudPositions.ts`, `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/referenceLayouts/hud.ts`. Design: `docs/design/invisible-editor.md` (HUD/portrait section).
  - **Code-review follow-up — 4 defects fixed code-only (2026-06-12, not committed):** the first landing rendered readout DOCS (bookof seeds `hud-balance/win/bet` as `componentInstance(hudReadout)` nodes via `hudScenes({readouts:true})`, NOT coded `bind` labels) incorrectly and broke non-portrait editable games. Fixes in `LayoutEditable.svelte`: (1) **BLOCKING — readouts now fold with the drawer.** The `componentInstance` loop sat OUTSIDE both fold wrappers, and `mounted('hud-balance'/'win'/'bet')` (covered via `coveredSources`) suppressed the coded fold-wrapped branches, so for bookof balance/win/bet rendered STATICALLY. Added a `FoldGroup` classifier (`RESERVED_ID_GROUP`/`ACTION_GROUP`/`SOURCE_GROUP` → `drawer`/`win`/`betRow`/`flat`) + `instancesInGroup()`; in portrait, drawer-group instances render inside `y={drawerTween.current}`, the win readout inside `y={Math.min(tween,350)}`, bet-row instances in the always-visible bet row — so a readout rides the SAME wrapper as the coded label it replaces. Off-portrait every instance still renders in the one flat loop (`flatInstances`), byte-identical. New shared `instanceView` snippet renders both the grouped and flat instances. (2) **BLOCKING — non-portrait deadlock.** The new `subscribeOnMount({drawerButtonShow/Hide,…})` runs on EVERY layoutType (the editable path renders ONE `<LayoutEditable>` for all layouts; `apps/lines` uses it everywhere), and the show/hide handlers `await` the portrait-only `FadeContainer.oncomplete` resolver, so `bookEventHandlerMap`'s `await broadcastAsync({type:'drawerButtonShow'})` hung freeSpin-enter on desktop/landscape/tablet. Each handler now short-circuits (`if (!isPortrait) return`) — matching the coded off-portrait layouts (no-ops). Kept the subscription unconditional (NOT gated by an init-time `if`) because the component is NOT remounted across a desktop→portrait resize; reading `isPortrait` at call time preserves the real fold on resize-into-portrait. (3) **SHOULD-FIX — free-spin counter swap for readout docs.** The `LabelFreeSpinCounter` branch was gated `!mounted('hud-bet')` (false for bookof), so the counter never showed and the bet readout was never hidden during free spins. Now: on `freeSpinCounterShow` the counter renders at `pos.bet` whenever the slot is visible (coded label OR readout) and the bet-row instances are NOT rendered (hidden, like the coded `LabelBet`). (4) **NIT — stale comment** claiming the live bar has no `componentInstance` nodes (false for any `readouts:true`/`buttons:true` consumer) corrected. Verified: `pnpm --filter engine-layout build` GREEN (deterministic `scenes/bookof.json` regen — no new diff); `pnpm --filter lines build` GREEN; `svelte-check` clean on `LayoutEditable.svelte` (the 53 errors are pre-existing dual-`Snippet`-type / `UiGameName`/`InfoOverlay`/`HudTicker` noise, none in the edited file). Desktop freeSpin-enter no longer hangs (handler returns an already-resolved promise off-portrait → `Promise.all` resolves). **Book of Borut vendors the engine as a submodule — owner must bump it to pick this up.**
- **Reel z-order — split the basegame `<LayoutScene>` mount so editor layers can stack above the reel: ✅ LANDED (2026-06-12).** The editor lets you order the `reelGrid` placeholder among the basegame layers, but the real `<Board/>` mounts in its OWN trailing `<MainContainer>` (a separate Pixi container) AFTER the basegame `<LayoutScene>`, so it always painted over the whole scene — no editor ordering could put a layer above the reel. Fix (`apps/lines/src/components/Game.svelte`, our fork-addition, proven first in Book of Borut): split `basegameScene` at the top-level `reelGrid` node index into `basegameBelowReel` / `basegameAboveReel` `$derived`s; render the UNCHANGED board block (`BoardFrame`/`Board`/`Anticipations`) BETWEEN them, so layers authored after the reel now stack above it (matching the editor preview). No top-level reelGrid → single pass, byte-identical to before (parity for doc-less boot / nested reelGrid). The board subtree is untouched. Only `apps/lines` carries this — it's the sole reference game on the `engine-layout` `<LayoutScene>` + separate-Board pattern; cluster/scatter/ways/price are vanilla upstream (Board, no engine-layout basegame `<LayoutScene>`), number-picker has no Board. New games inherit it by copying `src/` from Book of Borut / `apps/lines` (`scripts/new-game.mjs` writes only a placeholder route — no Game.svelte template); the reference Game.svelte already pairs this split with `setBoardOverride(findReelGridNode(doc))` on doc load. Verified: `pnpm --filter lines build` green. **Committed:** lines in engine `25c188e`; Book of Borut in `e8d42fb` (pushed).
- **Editor — insertable "Reel" element (build a reel from scratch): ✅ LANDED (2026-06-12).** The reel `reelGrid` node used to exist only via template seeding or the "Convert to reel grid" anchor path — a brand-new game's layout had no way to add a board placeholder. Added a click-to-insert **Reel** item to the editor's Library → Elements palette (`apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/editor/+page.svelte`): `insertReelGrid()` spawns a centred `reelGrid` seeded from the game's real board shape (`data.template?.board`, default 5×3 @ 120; `reelPadding`/`rowPadding` 0.5, gaps 0) and selects it. Single-reel guard (`existingReelGrid` `$derived.by`, same first-across-all-scenes scan as the engine's `findReelGridNode`): if a reel already exists the item highlights + its click jumps to the existing one instead of creating a second (which the engine would ignore). The inserted node renders/selects/moves via the existing `drawReelGrid` canvas path. Interactive element is a real `<button display:contents>` (native keyboard, no a11y warning). Pairs with the reel z-order split above so a from-scratch reel in a new game positions the live board + stacks correctly. Verified: `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` green.
- **i18n — silence "Uncompiled message detected" warnings: ✅ LANDED code-only (2026-06-12, not committed/deployed).** Every HUD label (BET, STOP, …) logged `Uncompiled message detected! … Please compile your catalog first.` and ICU interpolation/plurals were skipped. Root cause: we deliberately ship **uncompiled** catalogs (plain string maps merged from code + the Invisible Localization tool's baked strings), but never registered a runtime message compiler — so Lingui `i18n._()` hit its `else` warn-branch (gated solely on `this._messageCompiler` being unset). Fix (`packages/state-shared/src/stateI18n.svelte.ts`): `i18n.setMessagesCompiler(compileMessage)` at module init (Lingui's documented remedy for runtime-uncompiled catalogs); added `@lingui/message-utils@5.2.0` as a direct dep of `state-shared`. Verified: API exists in `@lingui/core@5.2.0` d.ts, import resolves, and the warn-branch is gated only on the field the setter writes. **Book of Borut vendors the engine as a submodule — picked up: bumped to engine `5cff45c` in Borut commit `fcc496c` (pushed).** Two other cosmetic warnings seen alongside it (cache-key dupes + Spine deprecations) were owner-deferred — see the "Deferred — bump PixiJS + Spine" entry below.
- **Deferred — bump PixiJS + Spine to clear two cosmetic runtime warnings (owner deferred 2026-06-12).** Both are console-only (no functional/visual impact); revisit together when we next do an engine render-stack bump. Current pins: `pixi.js@8.8.1`, `@esotericsoftware/spine-pixi-v8@4.2.74` (in `packages/pixi-svelte/package.json`).
  - **(a) `[Cache] already has key: T_UI_Slots / T_Background / totalwin.png …`** — a sheet loaded as BOTH a base asset and as editor-art (e.g. Borut's `S_StaticElements`) makes PixiJS's _global_ Cache register the same bare frame names twice; it warns and keeps the first (base wins). Harmless because our components look textures up via the processed map (`getProcessed` → the sheet's own `.textures`, scoped `<assetKey>::` per `a7e0f2d`), NOT the global Cache. **Clean fix needs a pixi bump:** 8.8.1's `Spritesheet` ctor is `(texture, data)` with no prefix; later 8.x adds a `cachePrefix`, so editor-art (which already carries a `namespace`) could be loaded with a per-manifest cache prefix and never collide. Alternative without a pixi bump: prefix editor-art frame names per-manifest at bake time (atlas-tool/bake change) — only fixes newly-baked art.
  - **(b) PixiJS deprecation `addChild`/`collectRenderables` at `Spine.addSlotObject`** — purely inside `spine-pixi-v8@4.2.74` (`Spine.js:615 this.addChild(container)` + its custom `collectRenderablesSimple`); PixiJS 8.7+ deprecated-but-still-honors these. Not our code (we just call `spine.addSlotObject` in `SpineSlot.svelte`). **Fix:** bump `spine-pixi-v8` — a 4.2.x patch (→ up to 4.2.119) is skeleton-data-safe (same Spine 4.2 format); 4.3.x bumps the data version and would require re-exporting all skeletons from the Spine 4.3 editor. Coordinate with the pixi bump.
  - **Risk to weigh:** both are render-critical and engine-wide (all six dev games + shipped games like Book of Borut). Requires a real visual pass — spine rendering especially, since WebGPU screenshot capture is unreliable here and needs human eyes.
- **Invisible Editor — `reelGrid` gap-aware board centring fix: ✅ LANDED code-only (2026-06-12, not committed/deployed — owner review pending).** Bug: with the §11.3 gap/non-square consumption live, any non-zero `gapX`/`gapY` (or `cellWidth/cellHeight ≠ cellSize`) shoved the live board off-centre + lopsided. The editor's `drawReelGrid` centres the full footprint symmetrically on the node origin, but in-game `getSymbolX` grows the gap **cumulatively rightward** and the reel pitch grows it **cumulatively downward**, while `boardLayout()`'s pivot stayed at the flush, gap-less centre (`BOARD_SIZES/2`) — that half was never updated when the gap term was added. Fix (`apps/lines/src/game/stateGame.svelte.ts`, `boardLayout()`): recentre the pivot on the gap-extended cluster — `pivot.x = BOARD_SIZES.width/2 + ((BOARD_DIMENSIONS.x−1)/2)·columnExtraLocal`, `pivot.y = BOARD_SIZES.height/2 + (BOARD_DIMENSIONS.y/2)·(rowPitchLocal−SYMBOL_SIZE)` (both locals from `boardGeometry()`). **Y factor is `rows/2`, NOT `(rows−1)/2`** — it does NOT literally mirror X: visible rows are `symbolIndex 1..rows` with a `−0.5` pitch lead (`symbolY=(symbolIndex−0.5)·pitch`), so their mean centre is `(rows/2)·pitch`, whereas X reel indices run `0..reels−1` (mean `(reels−1)/2`). **Parity:** no override ⇒ `boardGeometry()` = `{0, SYMBOL_SIZE}` ⇒ both terms = 0 ⇒ pivot byte-identical to today; `REEL_PADDING` (0.53) flush lead untouched. **Scope:** only `apps/lines` carries the override (cluster/ways/scatter/price/number-picker do not). `BoardMask` (a `BoardContainer` child) now centres on the cluster too (better than today's drift; its flush-`SYMBOL_SIZE` height is a separate pre-existing limit). `Anticipation` renders OUTSIDE `BoardContainer` and already ignores gap+scale (pre-existing, unchanged). `pnpm --filter lines build` GREEN. **Book of Borut's own repo has a separate copy of `stateGame.svelte.ts` (engine vendored as a submodule) that needs the identical `boardLayout()` pivot edit.** Design: `docs/design/invisible-editor.md` §11.4.
- **RGS translator (Play4Fun):** ✅ done. `packages/rgs-translator-eagaming` maps the Stake internal request shape to the Play4Fun `/rgs/engine` protocol (verified from real Hot Fruits captures). Stake-shaped facade lets `apps/lines` run unmodified against a local mock via `PUBLIC_RGS_TRANSPORT=play4fun`. See root `CLAUDE.md` for protocol details.
- **Games:** `apps/{lines,cluster,scatter,ways,number-picker,price}` are **dev/reference** games (engine dev only). **Shipped games get their own repo** vendoring this engine as a submodule pinned to a `main` commit, deploying on their own cadence — e.g. **Book of Borut** (evolution of Hot Fruits, Book-of slot type; port 3002). Scaffold a new one with `node scripts/new-game.mjs --name "…"`. Full model: `docs/design/games-deploy.md`.
- **Repo strategy (decided 2026-06-03):** engine + tools + launcher stay in this ONE repo (shared `workspace:*` packages); games are the only split. The single `main` is kept filterable per area by **scoped commit subjects** (`commit-msg` hook), **squash-merge** (PR title = the one commit), and **CODEOWNERS** — not by re-splitting repos. See `docs/design/games-deploy.md`.
- **Rule:** engine changes go on `main` — never per-game engine branches. Don't dismantle the Turborepo/pnpm-workspace structure.
- **Invisible Editor — template/import engine contract (started 2026-06-02, branch `feat/editor-templates`):** design addendum in `docs/design/invisible-editor.md` §7 covers (1) per-game-type **templates** with named slots (`sprite`/`spine`/`text` = artist-owned, `mount` = engine-owned anchor), (2) **import** an existing game by deriving its layout from engine helpers, (3) in-editor template authoring, (4) the **symbol naming convention** (`docs/conventions/symbol-naming.md`). Engine-side landed so far: `packages/engine-layout` template types + `slotId` on nodes; `apps/lines/src/game/{template.ts,defaultLayout.ts}` (the `lines` template + the engine-truth generator that now produces `editor-scenes.ts`'s fallback doc — verified byte-for-byte parity with the prior fixture); `packages/game-spec/src/symbols.ts` (`classifySymbol`/`isKindConsistentWithId`, compatibility not equality since `S`=scatter|wildScatter); `engine-layout` template registry (`getTemplate`/`listTemplateGameTypes`) + `findUnfilledRequiredSlots` validation. **Launcher-side landed:** `projectScaffold` seeds the editor doc's scenes from the game type's template; the editor `save` action returns non-blocking unfilled-required-slot warnings; the editor UI (`EditorOutline` + header) shows each scene's slots with filled/empty status and an "N slots empty" pill. **§7.5 LANDED (PRs #10/#11, on `main`):** R2-data template override (`_shared/editor-templates/<gameType>.json`, built-in code fallback) via `templateStorage.loadTemplate`/`saveTemplate`; `POST /api/editor/template` authoring endpoint; in-editor **Template mode** (tag nodes as slots in Properties → "Save template" exports a `GameTemplate` to R2; required flags round-trip) + a **game-type selector** (lines/ways/cluster/scatter/bookOf) so you can author any game type's template online. **Editor-only — no game-rendering change.** Also: the type selector **loads + displays** the chosen template (built-in `lines`+`bookOf`, R2 override), and the canvas now renders **live spine preview** (real skeleton, static by default, per-node Play/Pause) via a transparent WebGL overlay reusing the vendored spine-webgl runtime (no new deps; new editor-gated `GET /api/editor/spine`). **Remaining:** (a) load an existing game scene + scene switcher — ✅ DONE (2026-06-03): the editor had no way to LOAD a populated layout and no visible scene list; owner's ask was simply "a Load game scene button + a scene list, template is a separate system." Delivered in `apps/launcher-api/src/routes/(app)/editor/*` + `packages/engine-layout`: (1) **scene-bar in the left panel** — a "Load a game scene…" picker (built-in PLACED layouts via `ref:<type>` + blank-from-template via `skel:<type>`) + a **Screens list** (click a screen → canvas switches; `selectScene`). (2) **Placed reference layouts** moved into `engine-layout/referenceLayouts/` (the `lines` `defaultLayout` relocated there; `apps/lines/src/game/defaultLayout.ts` now re-exports it, parity preserved) with `listReferenceLayouts()`/`getReferenceLayout()`, so the launcher can load a game's real placed+layered scenes without a cross-app import. `loadChosen`→`adoptScenes` swaps scenes + `mainSizesMap` (now a `$state`, canvas resizes) + `gameType`. (3) **Template authoring is now a fully SEPARATE, opt-in mode** — a single "Template editor" toggle (no more Fill|Template segmented control/banner); default view has zero template UI. The slot dropdown + type selector + Save-template + the Template tab only appear inside template mode. Drag-to-slot (canvas `fillRequest`→`fillSlotFromPayload`, panel/outline slot drop targets) is retained but secondary. (4) **game type persisted per-project** (no DB migration) — optional `gameType` on `LayoutDoc` (kept by `editorStorage.normalizeDoc`, written by autosave); `+page.server.ts` resolves the template from `doc.gameType ?? projectGameType()`; `seedScenesFromTemplate()` is shared by `projectScaffold`. `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN; svelte-check = the same 7 PRE-EXISTING baseline errors only (none in touched files). **Rotated/trimmed atlas frames now render upright** (2026-06-03): `drawRegionSprite` (`EditorCanvas.svelte`) + `RegionThumb.svelte` previously drew packed `rotated` frames sideways. Fixed to mirror the slicer (`services/atlas-tool/slice_atlas.py`): on-page packed rect is `(h × w)` for a rotated frame (swap), then draw under a +90° (canvas-clockwise, = PIL `rotate(-90)`) rotation into a swapped local box so it lands in the upright `(w × h)` content box; trim (`offX/offY/origW/origH`) math stays in upright space. Non-rotated path unchanged. So any project atlas (incl. HotFruits Spine atlases with rotated frames) draws correctly when a region is placed. **⚠️ Rendering caveat (still true):** the editor draws a sprite only if its art is in the PROJECT's R2 (`/api/editor/asset?key=`), so loading the `lines` reference into a different project shows correctly-PLACED placeholder boxes (+ the text node), not textures — its frames live in the `lines` game, not a launcher project. To see real art, place regions from the project's OWN atlas (renders from R2 today) or author+save a layout for a project whose assets are in R2. (b) `game_type` column on `projects` + admin field — SUPERSEDED for the editor by `doc.gameType`; DB column is a future cleanup (`projectGameType()` still stubbed `'lines'` as the no-`gameType` fallback); (c) **Reskin-layout rollout STARTED (2026-06-03)** — owner direction: the editor is the **reskin tool** — load each game's CURRENT layout placed, tweak sprite/spine sizes+positions per screen, save, and the game reads it as **new inputs at boot via `<LayoutScene>` + `loadEditorScenes()` without code changes** (parity fallback = the generator, so no-doc renders byte-for-byte as today). Rollout target = ALL games, one parity-gated element at a time (recipe + sequencing in `~/.claude/plans/prancy-roaming-cocke.md`). **Landed:** Phase 0 — documented the bound-component transform contract in `engine-layout/LayoutNodeView.svelte` (Container applies position; bound component renders at local origin using `transform.anchor/width/height` — avoids double-transform). Phase 1.1 — **Book of Borut** (`Invisible-Wall-SL/Book-of-Borut`, `33647ed`) now derives its editor fallback from a `src/game/defaultLayout.ts` generator (parity refactor of `editor-scenes.ts`; `pnpm build` GREEN). Engine (`3d76191`) added a **`bookOf` placed reference layout** so the editor's "Load a game scene" picker offers **"Book of Borut — base game"**. **Finding:** Borut's board frame is ALREADY editor-driven (`frame_bg/frame_edge` are frames in its `reelsFrame` atlas; `BoardFrame.svelte` is only the animated glow, like `lines`) — so remaining migration is **background, FS counter, loading, FS intro, logo** (Phase 1.2+). **Deliberately NOT done yet (SUPERSEDED — see Phase 4 below; submodule since bumped to `8ad8d0e` cleanly):** ~~Borut engine submodule stays at `24aa308` (a bump jumps it ~hundreds of commits — risky)~~ — STALE: it was actually at `5184d24` and the bump to current main was an 11-commit fast-forward, GREEN. **Owner-side to SEE real art / live edits:** sync Borut's spine+atlas assets to the project R2 (else placed placeholders) + set `EDITOR_DOC_SECRET` (else game uses the in-code generator, not live edits). **Phase 1.2 LANDED (Book-of-Borut `2cd618c`):** the **background** is now editor-repositionable — rendered via a `background` LayoutScene bind node (defaults to x:0/y:0/scale:1 = no-op, so the responsive `Background` keeps self-positioning; dragging/scaling the node offsets it). `Background.svelte` unchanged → render parity by construction; **background verified pixel-identical in a Chrome harness** (Borut dev :3002 + `mock-rgs-server-book` :7788/:7799 + click-through splash). **Verification-harness gotcha (DISCOVERED):** on REPEATED reloads the game stays on the press-to-continue splash / reaches only a heartbeat (`actions=[]`) to the mock — RGS **authenticate never completes**, so the populated basegame (board frame, reels, UI) + free-spin scenery don't render. This is **non-deterministic + independent of the layout changes** (an A/B with the background change disabled reproduced it; the very first fresh run DID show the full board). So verifying the remaining Phase 1.3 scenery (FS counter, FS intro = free-spin-only; logo/name = basegame) is **blocked until the local RGS-auth flow is made reliable** (or verified by the owner in-game). The Borut dev server + mocks were left running this session. **Phase 1.3 PARTIAL (Book-of-Borut `4642f46`):** **FS counter + FS intro + FS outro** now editor-repositionable via the same zero-risk no-op-bind-node pattern (components UNCHANGED → parity by construction; `pnpm build` GREEN). FS counter keeps its desktop/landscape gate; intro/outro are separate scenes to preserve tree order. Engine `bookof.ts` reference layout expanded to mirror all 6 scenes (background/basegame/overlays/fsCounter/fsIntro/fsOutro) so the editor's "Load a game scene → Book of Borut" lists them as positionable anchors. **⏳ Verify in a free-spin round** (counter panel + intro/outro overlays unchanged). **NOT migrated (deliberately, awkward fit):** **LoadingScreen** (has a required `onloaded` callback prop → doesn't fit a no-op bind node) and **logo / game-name** (live in the HUD `UI` snippet layer, not a MainContainer scene → different coordinate space). **✅ MILESTONE (2026-06-04): the editor now renders Book of Borut's real game art per screen, fully from R2** — Background (foregroundAnimation spine = the kitchen), Base game (board frame region sprites), Free-spin intro (fsIntro spine = the golden burst), etc. Reached via a chain of fixes, all live: (1) **`ssr=false`** on the editor route — it's a client-only canvas/WebGL app; SSR-rendering certain docs 500'd. (2) **TexturePacker read directly** — `editorRegions.loadRegionSet` normalizes a TP json-hash/array manifest into the Invisible region shape, so a game's own atlas (`reels_frame.json`) renders once its `.json`+page are in R2 (no convert step). (3) **camelCase spine bundles** — `projectPaths.BUNDLE_SEG_RE` was lowercase-only, so `assertBundle` threw on `foregroundAnimation`/`fsIntro`/… → spine endpoint 500; now allows `A-Z`. (4) spine endpoint wrapped → `{found:false}` not 500 on a bad bundle. (5) **bookOf removed from the "Load a game scene" picker** — its standalone-key layout couldn't render + autosaved over the seeded region-sprite doc (the picker-clobber behind the grey frame). (6) **`scripts/seed-game-editor.mjs`** converts+uploads the game atlas and writes a doc that **auto-places each screen's real art** (lists project spines from R2 + FS-counter region + label); editor-only (game uses its coded fallback). **Reskin workflow now: open a screen → real art shows → drag to reposition → autosave.** ⚠️ Cloud-native follow-up owed: the seed still reads the atlas from the local game repo + runs locally — with TP-reading, the future path is upload the atlas via `/files` (browser) + drag in the editor (no PowerShell). `EDITOR_DOC_SECRET` still unset, so the doc is an editor sandbox until wired. **Still pending:** Borut submodule bump (1.4), the cloud-native upload path, then Phase 2 (all other games). (d) spine preview's camera y-flip/positioning + node rotation are reasoned-not-eye-checked (fixes localized to `EditorSpineLayer.svelte`'s camera block). **Asset-loading feedback (2026-06-04):** opening a game/heavy template no longer shows a blank canvas while R2 assets stream in — `EditorCanvas` now tracks in-flight loads across all three sources (images + atlas/sheet regions locally, spine bundles reported up from `EditorSpineLayer` via a new `onLoadingChange` callback) with monotonic started/settled counters, and shows a centered "Loading assets… N / M" overlay with a spinner + progress bar. A per-burst high-water mark drives the fraction (resets to 0 once everything settles, so totals don't drift across doc opens) and a 250 ms defer keeps quick single drag-drops from flashing it. `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN. **Visual verification:** test online at `app.invisiblewall.org/editor` (no local run needed). **Editable game HUD (in progress 2026-06-04):** making the `<UI>` HUD layer (logo/game-name + balance/bet/win labels + the spin/menu/bet button cluster) editable in the editor. Plan: `~/.claude/plans/luminous-dazzling-metcalfe.md`. **Phase 1 (DONE, `c2d224f`):** contract — `Scene.space` (`game`/`standard`/`canvas`) + `BaseNode.screenAnchor` (window-edge pinning for HUD corners); `STANDARD_MAIN_SIZES_MAP` moved to `constants-shared` (one source, shared by `utils-layout` runtime + `engine-layout` editor) + re-exported; `LayoutNodeView` honors screenAnchor; editor draws labeled placeholders for bind nodes (were invisible), frames standard/canvas scenes from the standard box, drag writes back the edge offset, `editorStorage` preserves `space`. **Phase 3a (DONE, `9505862`):** `referenceLayouts/hud.ts` — the HUD as two reusable editor scenes (`hudBar` standard + `hudCorners` canvas) with bind nodes at the flattened absolute `LayoutDesktop` coords; wired into the `lines` reference layout, so editor "Load a game scene → Lines — base game" lists draggable HUD screens. **Phase 2 (DONE, `b87654e`):** opt-in render path — `<UI>`/`<UIDefault>` gain an optional `hud={bar,corners}` prop; when present a new `LayoutEditable` positions the game's HUD snippets from the scenes (bar in `<MainContainer standard>`, corners via screenAnchor), else the coded `Layout*` render unchanged (parity). Only placement is data-driven; menu drawer + dynamic values stay coded; `apps/lines` opts in. All builds GREEN. **Phase 3 (DONE, `0d8ab28`):** per-layoutType overrides for **landscape + tablet** added to the `hudBar` nodes (computed from the same constants as `LayoutLandscape`/`LayoutTablet`; tablet scale 1, others 0.8); `LayoutEditable` renders the matching per-layout menu drawer. **Portrait stays coded** (its bar is an animated fold-out drawer — behaviour, not static placement; `UIDefault` keeps portrait on `LayoutPortrait` even when opted in). So in the editor, switching the layoutType pills on the HUD screens shows the correct desktop/landscape/tablet arrangement. **Phase 4 (DONE, Borut `493b4a9`):** Borut's engine submodule was actually at `5184d24` (NOT the `24aa308` older notes claimed) — only **11 commits behind main + a clean fast-forward**, so the bump was low-risk (the "hundreds of commits/risky" warning was stale). Bumped to `8ad8d0e`, `pnpm build` GREEN (no breakage). `Game.svelte` now wires `<UI hud={{bar,corners}}>` reading `hudBar`/`hudCorners` from the LIVE editor doc only (no fallback) → coded HUD renders unchanged until the HUD scenes exist, then data-driven. **Enabler (`8ad8d0e`):** new non-destructive **"+ Add HUD layer"** editor action appends `hudScenes()` to a project's doc (vs the clobber-prone "Load a game scene"); `hudScenes` exported from `engine-layout`. Reference-load picker also hardened to non-destructive (confirm + preview, no autosave-clobber) — `6c567e3`. **Remaining OWNER step to light it up:** in the editor on `borut/book_of_borut` → "+ Add HUD layer" → Save (writes the HUD scenes into the live doc) → reload the game (`?k=`) → HUD renders from the doc and is editable (logo/name + balance/win/bet + button cluster; desktop/landscape/tablet). (`EDITOR_DOC_SECRET` is set, so the loop is live.) (Known v1 limits: portrait HUD not editor-driven; tablet in-bar FS-counter + dynamic values stay coded; HUD elements render as labeled placeholder boxes in the 2D editor.)
