- **Invisible Editor — parametric Button `action` dropdown: ✅ LANDED (2026-06-09).** The `action` param (which selects a HUD button's BEHAVIOUR — spin / decrease / increase / autoSpin / turbo / menu / buyBonus — resolved at runtime via `getComponentAction` → the game's `registerComponentActions`) was a free-text box (silent typos). New `ENGINE_ACTION_CATALOG` (`componentCatalog.ts`) lists the canonical action keys; the editor now renders ANY param keyed `action` as a **dropdown** of the catalog in both the per-instance Params panel and the component Defaults panel (recognised by key, NOT by `options`, so it works on existing project Button defs without re-creation — and avoids tripping the `sourceParam` options-detection in the component-variables picker). A custom/out-of-catalog value is preserved as an extra option. Adding a NEW behaviour is still a game-side step (`registerComponentActions` in the shipped game's `Game.svelte`). **Decision (owner, 2026-06-09):** a shared `registerStandardHudActions(...)` helper to remove the per-game registration boilerplate was considered and **deliberately deferred** — if games end up diverging a lot, a shared standard-actions helper becomes a leaky abstraction; keep per-game registration explicit until a stable common core proves itself. (A full no-code custom-action system — visual scripting / composable primitives — is the bigger alternative, not planned.) `engine-layout` + `launcher-api` builds GREEN. ⏳ Owner-verify live.
- **Invisible Editor — resizable panels + persisted workspace layout: ✅ LANDED (2026-06-09).** The left + right (Properties) panels are now **drag-resizable** (handles on each inner edge; `beginResize` tracks via `window` pointer listeners so the drag follows even over the canvas, which gets `pointer-events: none` while dragging; widths clamped). The `.layout` grid columns are now driven by `leftWidth`/`rightWidth` state, and the existing canvas `ResizeObserver` re-fits the view automatically. **Persisted per-project** in `localStorage` (`iw-editor-ui:<projectKey>`, view-state only — never the doc): panel widths, the active left tab (library/outline), and **layer visibility** (`hiddenScenes` eye toggles) all restore on reopen. Loaded in `onMount`, re-serialised by a guarded `$effect`. **Collapsible state persisted too (follow-up, same day):** the Library atlas/sheet expansions (`libExpanded` in the same per-project store; restored-open atlases lazily re-hydrate their regions) AND the param-group `<details>` open/closed state (new `editor/groupCollapse.client.ts` helper, keyed `<componentId>:<group>`, used by both the instance Params panel and the component Defaults panel via controlled `open` + `ontoggle`). `launcher-api` build GREEN. ⏳ Owner-verify live.
- **Invisible Editor — "Expose text as params" (one-click, grouped per text node): ✅ LANDED (2026-06-09).** A text node in the Component Editor now has an **✨ Expose text as params** button that auto-creates + binds author params for its `text` / `font` / `size` / `colour` in one click (`exposeTextParams` in `components/+page.svelte` — keys namespaced `<slug><Field>` + deduped, defaults seeded from the node's current values, sets `node.paramBindings` for `text`/`style.fontFamily`/`style.fontSize`/`style.fill`; idempotent, skips already-bound fields). New `ComponentParam.group` + `label` (editor-only display hints, persisted by `normalizeParams`): the per-instance Params panel AND the component Defaults panel now render params **grouped into collapsible `<details>`** titled by the text node's name (plain field labels inside), so a component with several text objects edits each one independently. Both panels refactored onto a shared `{#snippet}` (`paramField` / `defRow`). Engine resolves these via the existing `LayoutNodeView` text/style bindings (+ `EditorCanvas` preview) — not cosmetic. Builds GREEN (`engine-layout` + `launcher-api`); code-reviewed (no blockers). ⏳ Owner-verify live.
- **Invisible Editor — `image` param kind (atlas-region picker) + cross-window component refresh: ✅ LANDED (2026-06-09).** Follow-ups to the custom-params work. **(a) Refresh bug:** a param added in the Component Editor didn't appear on the placed instance in the scene editor — the scene editor only re-pulled defs on `visibilitychange`, which never fires when the Component Editor is popped into its own WINDOW (both tabs stay `visible`). Fixed by also binding the refetch to window `focus` (`editor/+page.svelte`). **(b) Image picker:** new `ComponentParam.kind` `'image'` — a STRING value (atlas frame name) the editor renders with a **region picker** (thumbnails) instead of a free-text box, so authors pick art visually. New reusable `editor/RegionPicker.svelte` (lazy `fetchRegions` + `RegionThumb`); `image` option in "Your params"; `image` branch in the per-instance Params override (scene editor) AND the component Defaults panel (component editor); `image` added to the atlas-frame bind dropdown (`paramsForKinds(['string','image'])`); `pickSheets` (atlas-manifests + sheets) derived from `data.assets` in both pages; `normalizeParams` persists `'image'`. Engine needs NO branch — an image value resolves through the existing `region` sprite binding. **(c) Canvas preview (follow-up, same day):** the scene-editor canvas now reflects per-instance bindings. `EditorCanvas`'s `boundString`/`boundNumber`/`boundTextValue` read the OPEN-COMPONENT params (empty in the scene editor) and ignored the per-instance params `drawComponentInstance` already threads as `instanceParams` — so a placed instance's overridden image/text/fill didn't show on the canvas. Now they prefer `instanceParams ?? componentParams` (helpers take the params explicitly), so the canvas redraws the sprite with the picked frame (and text/fill overrides) live. Parity: both empty ⇒ static fallback. Builds GREEN; code-reviewed (no blockers). ⏳ Owner-verify live. **(c2) Cross-atlas preview (same day):** removed the "frame must be in the sprite's static atlas" limitation — when a BOUND region isn't in the sprite's own atlas, the canvas now consults the existing one-time `spriteRegionIndex` (frame name → atlas, the same lazy scan bind-anchor previews use) and `ensureRegionIndex()`, matching the game's by-name cross-atlas resolution. Scoped to `boundRegion && !boundAssetKey` on a MISS only, so normal sprites + same-atlas binds skip it entirely — no per-draw scan, no eager loading (the full scan fires once per session, only when a bound frame truly lives elsewhere). **(c3) Cross-atlas centralised in `findRegion` (same day):** the (c2) resolution only covered BOUND regions, so a recurring "image not showing — gray placeholder with the frame name" hit any sprite whose STATIC `assetKey` doesn't pack the frame (e.g. a widget background dragged from a different atlas, or a def whose sprite points at a stale atlas). Moved the cross-atlas fallback INTO `findRegion` (the single chokepoint used by every region draw + `naturalSize`): on a miss in the named atlas it resolves the frame by name via `spriteRegionIndex` (kicking `ensureRegionIndex()` if unbuilt). So bound OR static, the canvas resolves a frame exactly like the game does. Removed the now-redundant per-sprite-branch block. Still miss-only ⇒ same-atlas sprites pay nothing. **(d) Native color pickers (same day):** the remaining `#rrggbb` hash-string text inputs are now native `<input type="color">` pickers — the per-instance color-param override (NEW `color` branch in the `authorParams` loop, with a × to inherit the default), the bound-node color param (`editableParams`), and the static sprite **tint** + text **fill/stroke/dropShadow** fields. Stored values stay numeric (parsed via the existing `parseHex`/`setTintHex`/`hexFrom`). The component Defaults panel already used a native picker. **Data note for image params:** the picker only renders for params of kind `image` — a `string` param shows a text box. To convert, remove the string param in "Your params" and re-add it with the SAME key + kind `image` (the sprite's `region` binding is by key, so it survives).
- **Invisible Editor — custom (author-defined) component params: ✅ LANDED (2026-06-09).** The Component Editor's "Component variables" panel now has a **Your params** block (`EditorProperties.svelte`): an add form (key + kind string/number/color/boolean) + a removable list, wired through new `onAddParam`/`onRemoveParam` props to `addCustomParam`/`removeComponentParam` in `components/+page.svelte`. **Author flag:** added params carry `ComponentParam.author = true` (new optional field, persisted by `normalizeParams`) so the removable "Your params" list shows ONLY user-added params — a component's built-in/coded params (Button's `action`/`icon`/`variant`/`tint`…) can't be deleted by mistake. **UX cleanup (same day):** the node's "Bind to param" controls were cramped into one flex row (clipped labels); now stacked full-width (`.bind-grid`) with the common **Image — atlas frame** binding first, **whole texture** + **Tint** below, each with a one-line hint, and a 3-step explainer (add param → bind a node field → set per instance). Unlocks **per-instance graphic swaps**: declare e.g. `bgAtlas`/`bgFrame` (string), then on the component's background **sprite** node use Properties → "Bind to param" → _Texture ← param_ (`assetKey`) / _Atlas frame ← param_ (`region`) — the existing engine plumbing (`LayoutNodeView` sprite bindings + `ComponentInstance` param threading) already resolves it, so each placed instance gets a text field to swap its background with no per-button scripting. `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` green; not browser-verified (auth-gated tool).
- **Invisible Editor — complete + reconcile the scene set + "Add missing screens" button (2026-06-08):** owner report — opening Book of Borut in the editor, not all game screens appeared (the **logo/loading splash was missing**) and free-spin intro/outro looked like duplicates. **Root cause:** the editor renders `doc.scenes` from the saved R2 doc, and the FOUR scene-set sources had drifted — none defined a `loading`/logo scene (it lived only in coded `LoadingScreen.svelte`). **Fix (`docs/design/invisible-editor.md` §15, code-only, parity-safe, additive):** (1) `boundComponentCatalog.ts` — new `LoadingScreen` default (preview spine `loader` = the `title_screen` logo) so the editor draws the splash; (2) `templates/{bookof,lines}.ts` now enumerate the **full screen set** (loading/background/basegame/[freegame]/overlays/freeSpin intro+counter+outro), new mount slots NON-required (no validation noise; HUD stays the universal `hudScenes()` layer); (3) `referenceLayouts/bookof.ts` += loading scene + `...hudScenes()`; (4) `referenceLayouts/lines.ts` += inert loading + freeSpin scenes (deliberately NO `background` scene — keeps `apps/lines` on its §10.6 exact-cover default); (5) `seed-game-editor.mjs` `buildDoc()` += loading scene. Parity: every new scene is a `bind` anchor to an unregistered coded component + the game looks scenes up by id (never iterates all) → inert in-game. **No-console owner path:** new editor scene-bar button **"＋ Add missing screens (N)"** (next to "Add HUD layer") diffs the doc vs `engine-layout` `getFullSceneSet(gameType)` (covers lines+bookOf) and appends only ABSENT scenes by id, non-destructively (autosave persists) — mirrors `addHudLayer()`; shows only when something's missing. So picking up the logo scene is one click, no re-seed. `pnpm --filter {engine-layout,launcher-api} build` + `apps/lines` `vite build` GREEN. **Deliberately deferred/explained:** `freegame` is template-declared but given no placeholder content (no distinct art yet → would be a fake duplicate); intro/outro look identical because Borut ships no `fsOutro` spine so the preview falls back to `fsIntro` (faithful — the game reuses the frame too). **Move 2 LANDED (2026-06-08): single source via generated JSON + automation.** The `.mjs` seed and the TS `bookofReferenceLayout()` were two scene-set generators (the seed even inlined a ~180-line `hudScenes()` copy) that drifted. Now: `packages/engine-layout/scripts/gen-scene-sets.mjs` (uses **esbuild** — in-tree via Vite; no runtime TS loader exists — to bundle the pure-data reference graph, no `.svelte` in it) emits `packages/engine-layout/scenes/bookof.json` from `bookofReferenceLayout()`, deterministic, with a `--check` drift guard. First reconciled the reference's silent drift (`background`/`overlays`/`freeSpin*` were missing `space:'canvas'`; added slotIds) and verified its non-basegame scenes are byte-identical to the seed's prior literals. The seed now `import …'engine-layout/scenes/bookof.json' with {type:'json'}` (new `./scenes/*` export) and composes from it, overriding ONLY `basegame` (manifest region sprites) — deleted ~260 lines (inlined HUD + scene literals); HUD now comes from authoritative `referenceLayouts/hud.ts`. **Automation so it can't be forgotten:** `gen:scenes` runs in engine-layout's `build`, AND the pre-commit hook runs `--check` when a reference/template/seed/scenes file is staged (stale JSON → commit blocked with the fix command). Verified: stale→exit 1, fresh→pass; engine-layout + launcher builds GREEN; seed JSON import resolves from its dir. **Next (Move 3):** drive each screen's show/hide from the doc (route B, §8.7) so the editor OWNS, not just previews, every screen.
- **Symbol-debug overlay + atlas-manifest validator (2026-06-05):** shipped a reusable, game-agnostic **`SymbolDebugOverlay`** in `packages/components-pixi` — a symbols×states grid that magenta-marks any cell whose asset key isn't in the live `loadedAssets`, so a game can surface missing-symbol "holes" without per-game debug code. `IE_DEBUG`-gated + `d` to toggle; `apps/lines` is the reference consumer and **Book of Borut** consumes it via a thin wrapper. The whole grid sits under one transform root reserved for a **future zoom/pan + paylines view** (TODO). The Borut "missing symbols / holes" hunt's root cause was an **atlas-tool deploy bug** (fixed + shipped earlier same day): `_deployatlas` built the TexturePacker frame-map from `regions` only and **dropped every `rotated_regions` symbol** → 8 of 17 `symbolsStatic` frames had no frame entry → invisible cells. To stop that whole class of drift, added (`launcher`) **`scripts/derive-atlas-manifest.mjs`** (derive a project's atlas manifest from the game's committed sheet) + **`atlasManifestCheck.ts` / `GET /api/atlas/manifest-check`** (flags missing/extra/rotation-mismatched frame keys vs the game config). Also hardened the game's `getSymbolInfo` to return a silent fallback instead of throwing on an undefined symbol/state. **Deploy-path automation (same day):** the Atlas Maker's manual `deploy_path` was error-prone (empty → files dumped to the `deploy/` root, where the game can't find them; sticky field → wrong folder). Now `derive-atlas-manifest --all --game-root <repo>` scans the game's **git-tracked** `static/assets/` tree → publishes a per-project **`asset-map.json`** (stem → `deploy_path`/`deploy_basename` for every TexturePacker sheet + Spine page) to R2 + backfills empty `deploy_path` on existing manifests; the atlas-tool Deploy reads that map to **auto-resolve** an empty `deploy_path` (explicit field still overrides), and now **warns loudly** only on a genuine no-match root-dump. Validator flags `deployPathMissing`. Published for `borut/bookofborut` (23 assets indexed; `symbols/symbols2/symbols3/transition` backfilled). **Re-run `--all` when the game's asset layout changes.** Known leftovers: 3 manifests hold stale Windows-absolute `deploy_path`s (need `--force-deploy-path` or a manual fix); `mmBG` + scratch sheets are unmatched — `mmBG` is the **background Spine** (`mm_bg` → `spines/foregroundAnimation`), so a sprite-sheet deploy there only helps if it replaces the spine page. **Spine page-only deploy (same day):** the background reskin exposed that Deploy's TexturePacker `<base>.json` write **clobbers a Spine skeleton** (`mm_bg.json` is the 88 KB skeleton, not a frame-map). Fixed: asset-map entries now carry `kind` (`spine`|`sprite`), and `_deployatlas` does a **page-only deploy** (writes only `.png`/`.webp`, skips the `.json`/`.atlas`) when the target is a Spine — detected via the manifest `deploy_page_only` flag, asset-map `kind=="spine"`, or an existing skeleton at the target. So spine-page reskins (`mm_bg`) are now one-click safe. (Background reskin: confirmed page is shape-compatible 2028×1439; the stray TP `mm_bg.json` + `mmBG.*` leftovers were purged from R2 deploy/.)

- **Invisible Editor — fonts PUBLISH bridge (per-project Font Maker fonts now SHIP to the game): ✅ LANDED code-only (2026-06-12, not committed/deployed).** Owner report: a bitmap (BMFont) made in the Font Maker shows in the font editor + scene editor but is MISSING when the game is published. **Root cause:** the editor reads fonts by streaming straight from R2 (`<client>/<project>/fonts/`), but the build pipeline only mirrored `deploy/` → `static/assets/`; nothing exported fonts into `deploy/`, and the game registered only its HARDCODED built-in catalog (`gold/goldblur/silver/purple` from `assets.ts`). So per-project fonts were stranded at their R2 source prefix — the exact `editor-art` gap, for fonts. **Fix (mirrors `editorArtExport.ts` end-to-end):** (1) **Export** `apps/launcher-api/src/lib/server/fontExport.ts#exportEditorFonts` reads the project `fonts.json` (per-project + `_shared/fonts/` fallback), copies each font's descriptor+pages (bitmap) / files (web) into `deploy/editor-fonts/<folder>/` (names preserved so a bitmap descriptor's relative page refs resolve), writes `index.json` (the exported `FontCatalog`), prunes stale, idempotent. (2) **Trigger** `POST /api/editor/export-fonts` (same `EDITOR_DOC_SECRET` gate); `bake-editor-doc.mjs` calls it after the art export + embeds `bundle.fonts.catalog`. (3) **Transport** `pull-project-assets.mjs` now mirrors+prunes `editor-fonts/` alongside `editor-art/`. (4) **Game** `apps/lines/src/editor-scenes.ts` `bakedFontAssets()` (bitmap → `{type:'font'}` preload asset; pixi installs the `BitmapFont` under its face) + `bakedFontCatalog()` (merged into boot `registerFontCatalog` in `Game.svelte`, baked family overrides a built-in of the same name) + `registerBakedWebFonts()` (FontFace for web fonts); `stateApp.ts` spreads `bakedFontAssets()` into `createApp`. **Build-order bug fixed:** `scripts/new-game.mjs` ran `pull:assets` BEFORE `bake:doc` — but bake triggers the export that populates `deploy/`, so generated assets (art AND fonts) lagged a build. Reordered to `build:engine && bake:doc && pull:assets && vite build` (matches the §"Build order" rule in live-assets.md). **Strict rule recorded** (CLAUDE.md hard rule #8 + `docs/design/live-assets.md` new "Font export" section): any R2 asset class a game needs MUST travel the full export→deploy→bake→pull→register chain — "shows in the editor" ≠ "ships". `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN (full typecheck); `apps/lines` `vite build` GREEN. **⚠ Per-game follow-up (the shipped game, e.g. Book of Borut):** its repo has its OWN copies of `editor-scenes.ts`/`stateApp.ts`/`Game.svelte` (engine vendored as a submodule) that need the identical 3 game-side edits mirrored, its `package.json` build order fixed (bake before pull), the engine submodule bumped, and `EDITOR_DOC_SECRET` present in the build env (else the export is skipped — the "token trap"). Then rebuild → the Font Maker font ships.
- **Invisible Editor — fonts (edit text + see/ship real game fonts): 🚧 Phases 0+1+2 + Phase-4 HUD-text LANDED (2026-06-06), code-only/not deployed/not browser-verified.** Owner ask: from a text node's Properties panel, (1) see the same fonts in the editor the game renders, (2) edit the text → appears in-game, (3) change the font/bitmap → appearance changes in-game. **Render path DECIDED (owner, §9.5): WebGL/PIXI overlay** (pixel-exact PIXI parity over a 2D blitter). **Phase 4 HUD-text override (owner-chosen next step) done:** the coded HUD **logo / game-name** corners now take an editor-authored **font+size+fill+label** override (Book of Borut/all games have these as `container` bind anchors, not `kind:'text'` nodes — so the Phase 2 text picker didn't reach them). Contract: `engine-layout` `HudTextOverride` + `getHudTextOverride` (stored on `bind.props`, persisted by `normalizeNode` pass-through); engine `hudPositions.hudTextOverride()` → `<LayoutEditable>` hands it to the `gameName`/`logo` snippets (`Snippet<[HudTextOverride?]>`, ignore-arg = parity), forwarded through `<UI>`/`<UIDefault>`, merged in `<UiGameName>` + `apps/lines` snippets. Editor: a **"HUD text"** Properties section (font dropdown + size + fill/tint + label) for a `preview.style:'text'` bind anchor; the PIXI overlay renders the anchor in the chosen font (2D chip steps aside). `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` + `lines` svelte-check GREEN. **To SHIP in-game** a game bumps its engine submodule + applies the `gameName`/`logo` snippet merge (lines = reference) + rebuilds; the **editor preview needs no game change**. **Phase 2 (Properties: edit text + pick font) done:** `EditorProperties.svelte` text node — freeform `fontFamily` input → a `<select>` from `/api/editor/fonts` (`(game default)` + each catalog font tagged `[bitmap]`/`[web]`, off-catalog family kept as `(custom)`, empty-catalog hint); `text` textarea kept; for a bitmap font the `fill` field relabels to **tint**, a note explains tint+size-only, and Stroke + Drop-shadow are gated off (`{#if !isBitmapSelected}`). `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN. Tradeoff: arbitrary non-cataloged web families can no longer be typed (sync into the catalog first). **Only Phase 3 remains** (engine `LayoutNodeView` `<BitmapText>` + game boot-load + parity gate — the single engine-contract change; needed for the fonts to actually SHIP in-game). **Phase 1 (editor shows the real fonts) done:** `EditorTextLayer.svelte` — a transparent raw-PIXI `Application` over the 2D canvas (mirrors `EditorSpineLayer`) rendering top-level text nodes as `PIXI.BitmapText`/`PIXI.Text`, positioned via the 2D canvas's `nodeTransform` → `world*zoom+pan` (passed as `worldTransformOf`, one coord source). `fonts.client.ts` loads bitmap fonts via PIXI `Assets.load` (descriptor `<page>` refs rewritten to absolute editor-gated URLs by `/api/editor/asset?font=1` — the relativity gotcha) + web fonts via `FontFace`. Loads fold into the "Loading assets…" overlay; overlay reports owned node ids so the 2D `fillText` is skipped (no double-draw); "↻ Reload art" re-fetches the catalog. `pixi.js@8.8.1` added to the launcher. Built mostly by the engine-pixi-svelte agent; `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN. **Caveats:** top-level text only (nested-in-container → 2D fallback); matches the game's pixi anchor not the old `fillText` baseline (intended); a changed font PAGE needs a full reload. **Next: Phase 2** (Properties font dropdown + text edit) → Phase 3 (engine `LayoutNodeView` `<BitmapText>`, the only contract change). **Phase 0 (contract + fonts into R2, the spine analogue) done:** `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/fontCatalog.ts` — `FontEntry`/`FontCatalog` types (`kind: 'bitmap' | 'web'`, files relative to a `folder`) + `findFont`/`isBitmapFont`, exported from the barrel. Launcher: `SUB.fonts` + `fontCatalogKey`/`fontBundlePath`/`sharedFontsPrefix` (`projectPaths.ts`); `includeSharedFonts` scope opt (`toolScope.ts`) wired into `/api/editor/asset` (+ xml/fnt/woff2/woff/ttf/otf content types); `lib/server/fonts.ts` `resolveEditorFonts` (project `fonts.json` → `_shared/fonts/` fallback, each file → editor-gated `/api/editor/asset` URL); `GET /api/editor/fonts` (gated, `{ fonts: [] }` on no-catalog, never 500). Sync: `scripts/r2-sync-fonts.mjs` (clone of `r2-sync-spines.mjs`; parses BMFont `<info face>`+pages, web fonts by ext; `--dry-run` verified against the `lines` fonts → gold/silver/purple/goldblur). `pnpm --filter {launcher-api,engine-layout} build` GREEN. **Owner step to seed:** run `node scripts/r2-sync-fonts.mjs <fontsDir> <client> <project>` with R2 creds (defaults to the `lines` fonts → `borut/bookofborut/fonts/`). **Next: Phase 1** (2D BMFont blitter in `EditorCanvas` so the editor shows the real fonts) → Phase 2 (Properties font dropdown + text edit) → Phase 3 (engine `LayoutNodeView` `<BitmapText>`, the only contract change). Two gaps to close: the editor draws text with Canvas2D `fillText` (system-font fallback, loads none of the game's fonts) AND the engine's layout text path (`LayoutNodeView` `<Text>`) can't render a bitmap font even in-game — so the game's bitmap fonts (`gold`/`silver`/… from BMFont `.xml`+page, rendered via `<BitmapText>`) never show in a layout text node. Full plan: **`docs/design/invisible-editor.md` §9** — font catalog (`fonts.json` in R2, single source of truth) + `r2-sync-fonts.mjs` + `/api/editor/fonts` (Phase 0); editor 2D-canvas BMFont blitter for fidelity (Phase 1); Properties font dropdown + text editing (Phase 2); `LayoutNodeView` `<BitmapText>` + game boot-load + parity gate (Phase 3, the only engine-contract change). **Scope boundary:** static/semi-static layout text nodes only; dynamic coded values (FS counter, balance/win) stay coded. **Open decisions (§9.5):** editor render path (2D blitter recommended vs WebGL overlay), whether dynamic-text fonts are in v1, bitmap "change appearance" = tint/scale/swap not arbitrary recolor.

- **Invisible Editor → Atlas Maker deep-link: ✅ SHIPPED (2026-06-05).** A selected SPRITE node's Properties now has an **"🧩 Open in Atlas Maker"** button (`EditorProperties.svelte`). It links to `/atlas?atlas=<sheet>&region=<key>` (new tab); the launcher `/atlas` redirect forwards `atlas`+`region` to the tool; the atlas-tool (`ui_server.py`: `resolve_atlas_to_manifest`/`_manifest_deploy_stem`/`_handle_deeplink`) maps the deployed sheet → its **generation manifest** (direct name match, else by `deploy_basename`/`deploy_path` stem), sets it active, and scrolls+flashes the region card. **When no Atlas Maker recipe owns the sheet → AUTO-IMPORT (`88f9da5`, 2026-06-05, built in a worktree + code-reviewed):** the tool reads the existing TexturePacker/editor sheet from `manifests/<sheet>` (already hydrated), converts frames → snake*case regions (mirrors `tpRegions.mjs`), slices the page into per-region `output_override` art (mirrors `slice_atlas.py` rotation/trim; each slice `_mirror`ed to R2) so cards show the current art and Create Atlas re-packs it, sets `deploy_path`/`deploy_basename` from the project `asset-map.json`, activates the new `atlas_manifest*<stem>.json`, and opens it. **So "Open in Atlas Maker" now ALWAYS lands in the Atlas Maker** (silently creates the recipe the first time). The earlier Sheet Maker fallback redirect was REMOVED; an import failure shows the `ATLAS_IMPORT_FAILED`diag (never a 500). Guards an existing same-stem recipe from being clobbered (activates it instead).`import_sheet_to_manifest`+ helpers in`ui_server.py`; new diag in `diag_catalog.py`. Self-contained in the atlas-tool (the editor button + launcher forward from `a8500ba`are unchanged; the sheet-tool`?import=`handler is now dead code for this flow but harmless). **End-user loop:** Open in Atlas Maker → replace a frame via "Choose file" override → Create Atlas → Deploy → next game deploy pulls the new sheet. ⏳ Not browser-tested live. **Provenance note:** the original resolver half landed in`d4e6795`(a concurrent session's`git add -A`swept it in); the launcher/editor/diag half is`a8500ba`; the auto-import is `88f9da5`. Deploy: atlas-tool auto-deploys from `main`. **Round-trip caveat (reviewer):** re-pack is re-fit from the sliced art (alpha-crop + LANCZOS via `fit_to_region` spine-slot path), not byte-identical for trimmed/transparent frames — visually identical for opaque art.

- **Editor — "↻ Reload art" + asset cache-bust: ✅ SHIPPED (`795ed90`, 2026-06-05).** The editor cached each atlas/spine page in memory by a stable URL (only re-pulled on a full reload), so replacing a PNG in R2 didn't show. Added a **"↻ Reload art"** button next to Fit (`EditorCanvas.svelte`) that clears the per-session image + region caches and bumps a cache-bust token; the spine layer (`EditorSpineLayer.svelte` + `editorSpine.client.ts`) drops its bundle cache on the forwarded token and the always-on RAF re-fetches; asset URLs now carry `?v=` (endpoint is already `no-store`). **Caveat:** re-fetches the SAME R2 key the doc references — doesn't help if the update wrote a different key (e.g. an Atlas Maker Deploy lands in `deploy/`, not the manifest's `source_image_path`).

- **Editor — visualize + position empty MOUNT slots: ✅ SHIPPED (`1d443e5`, 2026-06-05).** An empty `mount` slot (e.g. "Reel grid") had no node → invisible + un-placeable. Now the Slots list shows **"+ add"** on an empty mount; clicking materialises the engine anchor as a labeled, draggable box on the canvas (defaulted to the board frame's rect via slot `boardFrame`, else a centred box) and selects it. Universal across game types (driven by the active template's mount slot via the now-exported `mountAnchor`, with an optional position/size init). `ContainerNode` gained optional `width`/`height` and `resolveTransform` surfaces them for containers — editor draws/sizes a real box; a PLAIN container ignores them in-game, a `bind` container forwards them to the mounted component (LayoutNodeView contract). Existing docs unchanged (no container had a size) → zero game-render change. Files: `engine-layout/{types,resolveTransform,seedScenes}.ts`, `EditorOutline.svelte`, editor `+page.svelte`. **Note:** the anchor's position drives the in-game component only for games that consume it via the layout; bookOf's `ReelGrid` still self-positions in code (wiring it to read the anchor is a separate reskin step).

- **Invisible Editor — Components (prefabs) v1: 🚧 LANDED code-only (2026-06-06, branch `editor-component-maker`), not deployed/not browser-verified.** Builds §8 of `docs/design/invisible-editor.md` — the reusable composite tier between node and scene — scoped to **compose + reuse + declare engine variables, NO authored behavior/timeline** (the §8.5/§8.6 GSAP layer is deferred). Owner scope decisions: one `ComponentDef` + a `category` tag (`ui`/`overlay`/`scenery`; a button = `category:'ui'`); the "provided list" of engine-set variables is a **curated code-owned catalog** in `engine-layout`. **Landed (4 commits):** (1) **schema** — `ComponentDef`/`ComponentParam`/`ComponentSignal`/`ComponentInstanceNode` + `ComponentCategory` in `engine-layout/types.ts`; `componentInstance` added to the `LayoutNode` union + `editorStorage` normalize set; new `componentCatalog.ts` (`ENGINE_PARAM_CATALOG` = bet/win/balance/totalWin/playerName/projectName + `ENGINE_SIGNAL_CATALOG` = enter/exit/idle/win/bigWin). (2) **engine render** — `ComponentInstance.svelte` resolves a def via a new `registerComponents` registry (mirrors `registerBoundComponents`) and renders `def.root` through the existing `LayoutNodeView` walk (so a component composes identically to an inlined container); `LayoutNodeView` gains a `componentInstance` branch; cycle guard + 2-level depth cap (`MAX_COMPONENT_DEPTH`, shared) via Svelte context; parity Storybook story in `apps/lines`. (3) **launcher storage/API** — `componentStorage.ts` (mirrors `templateStorage`: project `editor/<p>/components/<id>.json` shadows shared `_shared/editor-components/<id>.json`; load/save/list + normalize) + `GET/POST /api/editor/component` + `GET /api/editor/components`, gated to the `editor` tool. (4) **editor UI** — a third **component mode** on the existing canvas (scene/template/component): `EditorComponentPanel` (list by category, create, open-to-edit, save, picker that drops a `componentInstance`); **"Edit as component"** dissects a selected container into a `ComponentDef` draft via a synthetic scene (reuses canvas/outline/properties); a **variable-declaration UI** picks params/signals from the catalog (metadata only); the editor canvas resolves+draws an instance from a `componentMap` prop (its own renderer). **Invariant (review-fixed):** a component `root` is normalised to LOCAL space (identity transform) on create / "edit as component" / authoritatively on save, so the engine (applies root transform) and editor canvas (skips it) agree and a dropped instance isn't offset. `pnpm --filter {engine-layout,launcher-api} build` GREEN; code-reviewed. **Remaining:** deploy + browser-verify the create→declare→save→place loop online; extract a first real static composite (logo+frame) as the first shipped component; THEN the deferred §8.5/§8.6 behavior layer (signals→GSAP timeline, `registerComponentSignals`, per-signal track editor) + migrating a coded overlay (`Transition`).
- **Component authoring extracted into its own tool — "Invisible Component Editor" at `/components`: 🚧 LANDED code-only (2026-06-06, branch `components-tool`), not deployed/not browser-verified.** Owner re-scope: component AUTHORING is no longer a mode inside `/editor` — it's a standalone full-page launcher tool. **New tool** `componentEditor` registered in `roles.ts` (`name:'Invisible Component Editor'`, `url:'/components'`, nested-squares icon; granted to the same roles as `editor`). **New route** `src/routes/(app)/components/` (`+page.server.ts` + `+page.svelte`, `ssr=false`): left = components sidebar (create by name+category, open) + asset library; centre = the REUSED `EditorCanvas` fed a synthetic single `Scene` from the open component's `root.children`; right = REUSED `EditorOutline` + `EditorProperties` (with `componentMode` variable-declaration UI). **Reuse approach:** cross-route imports of the editor's child components (`../editor/EditorCanvas.svelte`, `EditorOutline`, `EditorProperties`, `RegionThumb`, `editorRegions.client`) — NOT copied, NOT moved — so `/editor` keeps owning them unchanged. **Gate:** the page + the `/api/editor/component[s]` routes both gate on the **`editor`** tool (the `componentEditor` entry only controls the home-grid card; granted to identical roles so they never diverge). **Deep-link contract:** `/components?id=<id>[&project=<key>]` opens that component on mount (project resolved from session scope; the `project` param is accepted but unused). **`/editor` slimmed:** `mode` reverted to `'scene'|'template'` (component mode removed); `EditorComponentPanel` is now a lean PICKER (list + place a `componentInstance` + "Open Component Editor ↗"); **"Edit as component"** now MATERIALISES the selected container into a project `ComponentDef`, POSTs it, and opens `/components?id=…&project=…` in a **new tab** (Atlas-Maker deep-link pattern) — the scene is NOT mutated in v1. Editor canvas still resolves/draws placed instances from `componentMap`. `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN (svelte-check: 0 new errors, 1 pre-existing removed). **Remaining:** deploy + browser-verify (a) /components create→edit→declare→save→reload, (b) ?id= deep-link, (c) /editor place + "Edit as component" → new tab, (d) /editor scene+template still work.
- **Editor↔game rendering parity + background layout pipeline (2026-06-06/07, owner-verified live):** a run of fixes making the editor preview match the running game, ending in a fully pipeline-ized background. **(1) Rotated atlas frames** render upright in the editor (`EditorCanvas` `drawArtRegionSprite` matches the slicer's −90 pack). **(2) Editor edits reach the game** — `LayoutNodeView` folded `scale` into `width/height` so a pixi-svelte `width`-setter no longer clobbered the editor's scale (`engine` `016c3f8`); `/api/editor/doc` → `no-store`; `loadEditorScenes` logs loudly when it falls back to the bundled layout. **(3) Per-screen z-ordering** — the editor composites one DOM group per screen, z-ordered by screen order, so a background spine sits BELOW the base-game 2D (`e65b595`). **(4) Spine sizing from authored data** — `skeleton.getBounds()` returns 0 for animation-driven art at the setup pose, so cover-fit stranded at scale-1 (raw 2× art); now sizes from `skeleton.data.width/height` in BOTH the editor (`EditorSpineLayer`, `7f6277c`) and the game (pixi-svelte `BaseSpineProvider`/`spineSizeScale`, `a804bfa`). **(5) Background pipeline = `docs/design/invisible-editor.md` §10 (steps 1–5 DONE, owner-verified):** ONE shared `coverTransform` helper (`engine-layout`, true cover from art dims) used by editor + game; the editor composites EVERY scene against ONE fixed window per `layoutType` (`STANDARD_MAIN_SIZES_MAP`) — switching screens no longer rescales, background previews full-bleed (`0f9304a`, the big one); background cover **scale + fit** are doc-driven + editable in Properties (`backgroundCoverScale`/`backgroundFit` readers + `BaseNode.fit`, `605af7f`); the GAME runtime honors them via `LayoutNodeView` → a `cover={{scale,fit}}` prop on background bind anchors + pixi-svelte `SpineProvider fit:'cover'|'contain'` (`1e043dc`). **Book of Borut** `Background.svelte` reads the `cover` prop (`93b8291`, submodule → `1e043dc`); its background is now full-bleed and editor-cover-driven. All additive/parity-safe (no `cover`/`fit` = renders as before). **Gotcha surfaced:** game deploy is NOT automatic for engine code — the game's `pnpm build` runs `build:engine` (rebuilds engine dists) then publishes via `publish-game-bundle.mjs`; the desktop launcher's Build&Deploy needs the project's **Portal project key** set (`bookofborut`) or it won't publish. The shared `main` ↔ game-submodule loop: bump submodule → `pnpm build:engine` → rebuild → publish.

- **Invisible Editor — configurable grid primitive (`reelGrid`): 🚧 Phase 1 LANDED code-only (2026-06-08), not deployed/not browser-verified.** Owner re-scope of the Component Editor: stop authoring empty shells — instead **open the real game's parts and expose their layout PARAMETERS** (board position, rows, columns, cell size, padding) for visual editing, with **static placeholders** standing in for the dynamic symbols. This is route (B)/§8.7's "configurable grid primitive"; the coded spin/symbol logic stays code. Investigation: board layout is fully derived from per-game `constants.ts` (`SYMBOL_SIZE`/`BOARD_DIMENSIONS`/`REEL_PADDING`) via `boardLayout()`+`getSymbolX/Y`; the engine already declares a `reelGrid` mount slot but emits no node. **Phase 1 (editor-only, parity-safe in-game):** new `ReelGridNode` (`kind:'reelGrid'` + `reels`/`rows`/`cellSize`/`reelPadding`) in `engine-layout/types.ts` (added to `LayoutNode` union); the engine's `LayoutNodeView` `if/else` chain has no branch → **renders nothing in-game** (coded `Board.svelte` still draws symbols, byte-parity); `editorStorage.normalizeDoc` `NODE_KINDS += 'reelGrid'` (round-trips params); seeded with the REAL board values into `referenceLayouts/bookof.ts` "Base game" scene (centred + per-layoutType overrides); editor draws a `reels×rows` placeholder grid (`EditorCanvas.drawReelGrid` + `editorCanvas.helpers.nodeBox` footprint), a "Reel grid" Properties section (reels/rows/cell size/reel padding number fields), `EditorOutline` glyph `⊞`. `pnpm --filter {engine-layout,launcher-api} build` GREEN. Plan + caveats: `docs/design/invisible-editor.md` §11. **Owner verify:** `/editor` on Borut → Load the bookof game scene → select **Reel grid** → confirm it draws over the board, moves/scales, and the param fields reshape it. **Phase 2 LANDED for engine + `apps/lines` + Borut mirror (2026-06-08, code-only) — LAYOUT-ONLY (owner-chosen):** the board is built at module load + the RGS book is a fixed N×M, so `reels`/`rows` are data-coupled (out of scope, kept descriptive + warned); Phase 2 drives **position + cell size + reel padding** into the live board via the board-container transform (no per-symbol math change). Shared readers `engine-layout/reelGrid.ts` (`findReelGridNode`/`resolveReelGridFromNode`/`resolveReelGridLayout` + `ReelGridNode` export); `boardLayout()` applies position + `scale=cellSize/SYMBOL_SIZE` (on `BoardContainer` + coded `BoardFrame` glow) + reelPadding x-offset; `Game.svelte` sets the override from the loaded doc; parity = no node ⇒ identical to today. Save-time mismatch **warning** via `GameTemplate.board {reels,rows}` (5×3) + `reelGridWarnings`, folded into the editor's existing `warnings` channel. `pnpm --filter {engine-layout,launcher-api}` + `apps/lines` builds GREEN. **Borut mirror ✅ PUSHED (Book of Borut `e53012b` on origin/main, build-green; republish pending):** engine submodule `1e043dc→9a37cf0`, 4 game-file edits mirrored, `pnpm build:engine`+`vite build` GREEN; parity-safe (no `reelGrid` node in Borut's live doc yet). Only remaining owner step: republish (Build&Deploy w/ Portal key). Also: an editor **"⊞ Convert to parametric grid"** Properties button turns the old `container` reelGrid mount-anchor into a parametric `reelGrid` node (seeded 5×3@120 at board centre = parity), closing the "existing doc has no reelGrid node" gap. **Phase 3 (later):** reels/rows runtime-driving as a separate RGS/math effort; win-lines; per-layoutType grid overrides. **Also (same session):** Component Editor (`/components`) UX cleanup — opening a component now stays on the Outline (was dumping into the asset Library) and the desktop/tablet/landscape/portrait pills were removed (a component is one design; the scene editor owns responsive overrides).

- **Invisible Editor — reel grid: cell spacing (gaps) + non-square cells + row padding (owner direction 2026-06-12, pushed `main`).** Extends the `reelGrid` primitive past §11's deferred boundary, owner chose the largest scope (real in-game). **Phase 1 (editor, `8be4547`):** `ReelGridNode` += optional `cellWidth`/`cellHeight` (non-square), `gapX`/`gapY` (inter-cell spacing), `rowPadding` (vertical inset) — all optional ⇒ absent = today's square/flush grid (parity); Properties fields + `EditorCanvas.drawReelGrid`/`nodeBox` honour `pitch=cell+gap` and non-square; + a **warm inner symbol marker** per cell (owner ask) showing padding/spacing/position. Stale "editor preview today" copy fixed. **Phase 2 (engine, `7834892`):** drives them into the LIVE board. `resolveReelGridFromNode`→`ReelGridLayout` exposes the new fields; `utils-slots` `symbolHeight` widened to `number | (() => number)` (lazy-resolved in both reel kinds — a number stays byte-identical; spinning reel's resting Y `homeY()` tracks the reactive pitch with a gated re-home effect inert for numbers); `apps/lines` `getSymbolX` moved to `stateGame` as a reactive export (per-index lattice slope = non-square width + gapX), `boardGeometry()` feeds the reel's `symbolHeight` (rowPitchLocal), `boardLayout()` adds a rowPadding y-offset; dead `getSymbolY` removed. **Owner decision:** symbol art keeps STAKE sizing (no stretch/fit) — gaps/non-square move the lattice only, square art never distorts. **Parity browser-verified** (`apps/lines` play4fun mock): clean boot, no errors, board at exactly 120px column+row pitch, first column at `REEL_PADDING` = byte-identical to baseline (read off the Pixi scene graph; WebGPU screenshots time out). Builds GREEN: engine-layout, lines, launcher. Plan: `docs/design/invisible-editor.md` §11.3. **Borut mirror ✅ DONE + PUSHED (Book of Borut `913f425`):** engine submodule `652a4fb→3709aa7` + the 3 game-file edits mirrored, `build:engine`+`vite build` GREEN, committed surgically (owner's static/assets untouched); parity-safe (no reelGrid node in Borut's live doc). Owner step: republish (Build & Deploy w/ Portal key). **Phase 3 (spin-feel params) ✅ LANDED + PUSHED (engine `1550099`, Borut mirror `ade1d24`):** `ReelGridNode.spin` (normal/fast profiles overriding `SPIN_OPTIONS_*`) + `resolveReelSpinProfile`; the game's `spinOptions` getter merges the authored profile over the coded base (no node ⇒ coded constants, parity). Editor: collapsible "Spin tuning (advanced)" section (Normal + Turbo, 8 fields each, blank = default). Motion blur NOT exposed (no game consumer). Runtime-verified in `apps/lines` (base 3/145 → injected 99/0). Owner step: Borut republish. **Whole reel-grid feature (gaps/non-square/row-padding + spin tuning) is now complete across editor + engine + lines + Borut.**
- **Invisible Editor — reel grid: anticipation overlay params (owner ask 2026-06-23, engine side LANDED).** Mirrors the spin-feel chain for the free-spin anticipation "hold" overlay. **engine-layout:** `ReelGridNode.anticipation` (`AnticipationProfile` = `spineKey` + `width/height/yOffsetRatio` + `intro/loop/outAnimation` + `sound`, all optional) + `resolveAnticipationProfile(node)` (returns only set fields; absent/empty ⇒ the game's coded `ANTICIPATION` config, parity). **Editor:** an "Anticipation (advanced)" `<details>` in the reelGrid Properties section (3 number + 5 text fields, blank = coded default), reusing the spin-tuning markup/CSS + `readAnticipation`/`writeAnticipation` (drops an empty object so an untouched node carries no `anticipation`). Travels to the game inside the `LayoutDoc` verbatim — no bake/seed change. `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN. **Runtime consumption (game reads the authored profile over the coded `ANTICIPATION` const) is the remaining step in Book of Borut** (`Anticipation.svelte`/`Anticipations.svelte` + a `stateGame` merge + engine submodule bump). Background: this overlay was first made editor-resize-aware (rendered inside the scaled `BoardContainer`, position derived via `getSymbolX`) in Book of Borut `cce950b`.
- **Book of Borut — free-spin RETRIGGER (owner ask 2026-06-23, LANDED + PUSHED).** During free spins, 3+ SCAT (the Book) landing awards **+10** more free spins, added to the remaining count (UNLIMITED chaining; only the scatter retriggers, never the special expanding symbol). **Math** (`scripts/mock-rgs-server-book.mjs`, engine `ae1171f`): free-spin branch re-checks scatters after each spin via `evaluateScatterTrigger`, bumps `bonus.left`/`total` by `RETRIGGER_FS=10`, emits a `retrigger` wire event before `playedBonusSpin` (so `total = played+left` is already bumped). Ships to live Borut via the **test-server auto-deploy** (test-server imports this mock; no submodule bump for the math). **Translator** (`stakeFacade.ts`, same commit): maps `retrigger` → a `freeSpinRetrigger` book event `{ extraFs, total }`. **Client** (Book of Borut `a8c7e85`, submodule → `ae1171f`): new `freeSpinRetrigger` book-event + handler (bumps the FS counter total, plays a brief self-dismissing celebration, no blocking gate) + new `FreeSpinRetrigger.svelte` overlay reusing the `fsIntro` panel + `fsIntroNumber` count (shows "+10"). Plays the existing `intro` spine track as a **placeholder** — author a dedicated `retrigger` track on `fs_screen` later and swap `animationName`. Borut `vite build` GREEN. Owner step: republish Borut. ⚠️ **RTP CAVEAT:** a 10M-bet Monte-Carlo of the mock showed it is NOT balanced money math — **~426% RTP before this feature** (5%-per-cell scatter → features trigger 3.6% of spins paying ~59×), pushed to **~549%** by the retrigger (retrigger fires 3.6% of free spins, ~31% of features retrigger, avg FS 10→15.6). The retrigger behaves correctly *as a feature*; the underlying mock needs real calibration before any real-money launch (separate task). NOT mirrored into `apps/lines` (reference uses the lines mock, never sees `freeSpinRetrigger`).

- **Invisible Editor — universal bound-component params: 🚧 LANDED code-only (2026-06-08), not browser-verified.** Generalized the bespoke per-component appearance editing (reelGrid params + HUD logo/game-name text override) into ONE mechanism: a declarative param schema per coded component (`EditableParam` + `BOUND_COMPONENT_PARAMS` + `getEditableParams` in `engine-layout/boundComponentCatalog.ts`), auto-rendered by `EditorProperties` (font/number/colour/checkbox/text controls), persisted to `bind.props` (top-level or nested `style`), flowing to the component at runtime. **Finding:** placement (x/y/**scale**) + **visibility** were already universal for HUD anchors (LayoutEditable applies scale + `{#if visible}`), so only author-set appearance params were new. **Seeded for HUD:** logo/game-name (text+font/size/fill), balance/win/bet labels (font/size/fill; live value stays coded), buttons (tint). **Game consumption** (parity-gated) in shared `components-ui-pixi`: `hudPositions` `hudStyle`/`hudText`/`hudTint` readers; `LayoutEditable` forwards per-element props; `UiLabel` merges style; `UiButton`/`ButtonBet`/`ButtonBuyBonus` wrap in `<Container tint={tint ?? 0xffffff}>`. `EditorCanvas.drawHudChip` reflects tint/fill so edits show in-editor. Parity: no `bind.props` ⇒ byte-identical. Engine `3ae6d6d`+`3b20be6`; Borut submodule→`3b20be6` (`b958ba7`); lines+launcher+Borut builds GREEN. Plan: design `docs/design/invisible-editor.md` §12. **Owner step:** republish Borut to see label/button params live. **Extend to any component:** add a `BOUND_COMPONENT_PARAMS` entry + make the component read the prop.

- **Invisible Editor — background cover scale ↔ stretch decoupling + coded-Background goes doc-driven: ✅ LANDED (2026-06-08, engine + editor + `apps/lines`; owner-verified full-bleed live in `apps/lines`).** Two owner-reported bugs (`docs/design/invisible-editor.md` §10.3 step 6): (1) editing **cover scale** also moved the Transform **SCALE.X/SCALE.Y** to the same number — they were the SAME field (`backgroundCoverScale = node.scale.x`, `setCoverScale` wrote both axes), with no vertical-vs-horizontal stretch; (2) the in-game background was **half-size / wrong-ratio** because the coded `Background.svelte` still used `normalBackgroundLayout({ scale: 0.5 })` (ratio-driven, ignores the doc) while the editor used the true `coverTransform`. **Fix:** cover scale is now a dedicated uniform **`BaseNode.coverScale`** (default 1); stretch is the free per-axis `node.scale` (default `{1,1}`) via new reader **`backgroundCoverStretch`**; `coverTransform` returns `{scaleX,scaleY,x,y}` = `fit·coverScale·stretch`. All cover paths consume it — editor (`EditorCanvas` background + placedArt, `EditorSpineLayer`), engine `LayoutNodeView` (bound `cover` carries `stretch`; sprite/spine bg fold stretch onto width/height + `scale`), and pixi-svelte composes stretch with `fit` (`spine.scale.set(baseX·sizeScale.x, …)`). **Real in-game fix:** `apps/lines/src/components/Background.svelte` dropped the `0.5`/ratio path for a true full-bleed cover via pixi-svelte `fit`, taking a `cover={{scale,fit,stretch}}` prop (default = exact edge-to-edge); `Game.svelte` passes the canonical readers from the doc's `background` scene. `coverScale` persists verbatim through `editorStorage.normalizeNode`. Builds GREEN (`engine-layout`, `launcher-api`, `lines`); verified live in `apps/lines` (background fills the viewport edge-to-edge, was half-size; no distortion at default stretch). **Borut mirror PENDING (separate repo):** apply the same `Background.svelte`/`Game.svelte` edits + bump the engine submodule + rebuild engine dist + republish. Frame sprites left as-is this pass (owner-deferred).

- **Invisible Editor — B6: HUD button cluster as one parametric `Button` component (owner direction 2026-06-09, branch `feat/editor-button-component`):** the button analogue of B5 (the HudReadout split, `65a5917`). Owner decisions: build it as **separate coded parts** (each movable/restylable, matching B5), and cover the **Borut HUD cluster only** first (spin/bet, menu, buy-bonus, auto-spin, turbo, bet +/−). Full scope = `docs/design/invisible-editor.md` §16. **Phase B6.1 LANDED code-only (`bf21aff`, not deployed/not browser-verified):** one `BUTTON_DEF` (built-in beside `HUD_READOUT_DEF`, instanced per button by an `action` param) built from two coded `bind` parts in `components-ui-pixi` — `ButtonFrame` (the `UiSprite` tile + the hit area: variant bg / disabled-grey / active border / tint, `onpointerup`→the action `onpress`) and `ButtonLabel` (the localized icon/label `Text`, `UiButton`'s style merge). Both read params off the `<ComponentInstance>` param context (`getComponentParams`); registered in `apps/lines` `registerBoundComponents`; `button` added to `BUILTIN_COMPONENTS`. **Pure parity** — no HUD node references the def yet. **Phase B6.2 LANDED (`2977a79`):** `registerComponentActions` registry (sibling to `registerComponentValues`; `ActionSource = { onpress, disabled?: BoolSource, active?: BoolSource }`, keyed by action name) + `ComponentInstance.svelte` resolves the `action` param and threads `onpress`/`disabled`/`active` onto the param context (reactive getters, only when an action is registered → parity); `apps/lines` `boolSource()` helper + a `registerComponentActions({ menu, buyBonus, autoSpin, turbo, increase, decrease })` call (full) + `spin` (onpress + disabled; the dynamic bet↔stop LABEL flip, `stopDisabled` state machine, and `OnHotkey` Space DEFERRED to B6.4). **Phase B6.3 LANDED (`440cbfa`):** **finding** — the generic `componentInstance` mount loop `LayoutEditable` gained in B4.3 (the readout work) is component-agnostic, so a `button` instance ALREADY mounts through it (no new branch). B6.3 just (a) registered `BUTTON_DEF` in `registerComponents` and (b) extended the `mounted()` double-render guard to the 7 coded button slots (`hud-btn-*`) so a converted button won't double-render with its coded twin — parity-safe (no button instances in `hudBarScene()` → guard false → coded buttons render byte-identically); + a proof story `apps/lines/.../ButtonComponentInstance.stories.svelte` (def expands → frame+label → press fires `onpress` → disabled greys). All builds GREEN; pushed. **Phase B6.4 LANDED (`05f680b`) — the parity flip + finished `spin`, flag-gated:** mirrors the readout's B4.4 flip. Mechanism: `registerComponentActions` gained `TextSource` + optional `ActionSource.label` (dynamic caption); `ComponentInstance` threads the live label onto the param context (reactive getter, only when the action provides one → overrides the static `label`; `ButtonLabel` already reads `label` when `icon` is absent). `hud.ts` += `HudBarOptions.buttons` + `buttonInstanceNode()` builder + a `btn()` picker that emits `componentInstance(button)` when `buttons` is set, else the coded `UiButton*` `bind` node (default = parity); `spin` gets `{action:'spin'}` with NO icon (dynamic label), the other six `{action, icon}`. `spin` completed in `apps/lines/Game.svelte`: replicates `ButtonBetProvider` verbatim (`stopDisabled` latch via `subscribeOnMount`, `getKey`, `onpress`) → `disabled` (key∈spin_disabled/stop_disabled), `label` (`bet()`/`stop()`), + a replacement `<OnHotkey Space>` mounted ONLY when the flip is on (`textSource.ts` = string sibling of `boolSource`). **Flag DEFAULTS OFF** (`apps/lines/src/game/editorFlags.ts` `HUD_BUTTON_INSTANCES = false`), threaded via `defaultLayout('lines',{buttons})` into the fallback doc + the hotkey gate; editor picker + Borut get OFF (`scenes/bookof.json` unchanged). All builds GREEN. **⏳ VERIFICATION GATE (owner/browser):** flip `HUD_BUTTON_INSTANCES = true`, run `apps/lines`, confirm the 7-button cluster renders + positions identically AND spin behaves (bet↔stop label, disabled greying, Space hotkey, turbo/auto-bet stop nuances). Until verified the flag stays OFF. **Editor "Convert to parametric button" affordance LANDED (`872cfb5`):** the author-facing flip (mirrors reelGrid's "Convert to parametric grid"). New `engine-layout/buttonConvert.ts` = `HUD_BUTTON_ACTION_MAP` (single source for `bind.component`→`{action,icon?}`; `hud.ts` `buttonInstanceNode` now consumes it so seed + convert can't drift — `gen:scenes --check` confirms `bookof.json` byte-identical) + `isHudButtonBind()` + `buttonBindToInstance(node)` (preserves transform byte-for-byte). Editor: `onConvertToParametricButton` prop + `⊞ Convert to parametric button` Properties button (shown for a container whose `bind.component` is a known HUD button) + the `+page.svelte` in-place handler. This is how a live doc (Borut's) adopts parametric buttons without a re-seed. Builds GREEN. **Local in-game verification ATTEMPTED, blocked by harness (2026-06-09):** flipped `HUD_BUTTON_INSTANCES=true`, ran `apps/lines` — the componentInstance→HUD-bar mount path is PROVEN (the balance readout, same `<ComponentInstance>`+`LayoutEditable` path, mounts correctly — confirmed via PixiJS scene-graph introspection; `button` def resolves, no engine errors; the B6.3 story compiles + lists in Storybook). But the full button cluster + win/bet never initialize locally because they're gated on bet-config from RGS **authenticate**, which **stalls at a heartbeat** (the documented non-deterministic play4fun auth gotcha, mock on :7788 only ever saw `actions=[]`); and the **WebGPU** renderer makes preview screenshots/evals time out (also documented). So pixel-level verification needs a stable browser env or the owner in-game. **Next:** open PR `feat/editor-button-component`→`main` (carries B6.1–B6.4 + the convert affordance; flag default OFF); after merge → owner browser-verify (flip flag in `apps/lines`, or use the editor convert affordance on a deployed project) → make the flag default → **B6.5** (Borut: submodule bump + enable buttons + `build:engine` + republish + convert its live HUD doc). **Known gap (pre-existing, out of B6.4 scope):** the B6.2 `turbo` action doesn't replicate `ButtonTurbo`'s `subscribeOnMount` stop-toggle nuance. **PR [#32](https://github.com/Invisible-Wall-SL/Invisible-Engine/pull/32) MERGED to `main` (`5161827`, 2026-06-09):** the whole B6 batch is on `main` (launcher auto-redeploys → the editor's Convert button + the `button` component go live). Flag stays OFF in `apps/lines`. **B6.5 LANDED + PUSHED (Book of Borut `5f383c2`):** bumped Borut's engine submodule `65a5917`→`5161827`; mirrored the button wiring into Borut's `Game.svelte` (register `ButtonFrame`/`ButtonLabel` + `BUTTON_DEF` + `registerComponentActions` for all 7 actions; `spin` replicates `ButtonBetProvider` verbatim; new `src/game/{boolSource,textSource}.ts`); the spin Space hotkey is **doc-gated** via `spinIsInstance` (Borut reads its HUD from the LIVE doc, no static flag — true once `hud-btn-bet` is a `componentInstance`). `build:engine` + `vite build` GREEN; committed surgically (engine pointer + 3 `src/` files only — the owner's concurrent `static/assets/**` edits left untouched, NOT staged). **Parity-inert:** Borut's live doc still has coded `UiButton*` `bind` nodes, so the game renders byte-identically until converted. **Remaining OWNER steps for Borut:** (1) **republish** Borut (Build & Deploy w/ Portal key — NOT done here; the owner has in-progress `static/assets` changes, so republish is theirs); (2) in the editor on `borut/book_of_borut`, select each HUD button → **⊞ Convert to parametric button** → Save → the parametric cluster goes live; (3) browser-verify the flip (the local RGS/WebGPU harness couldn't).

- **Invisible Editor — Component Editor UX + reusable styling sweep (2026-06-09, on `main`, auto-deployed):** a run of editor changes building on B6, all shipped to `main` (commits `d447f9c`→`64e3ba6`); launcher-api + `apps/lines` builds GREEN throughout. **Layout:** the Component Editor (`/components`) now tabs the **Outline with the asset Library on the LEFT** (`Library | Outline`, reusing `EditorOutline`); the right panel shows the selected node's **Properties alone** (the right-side Outline/Properties tab toggle removed). **Defaults scope (owner decision):** the right-panel **"Defaults (all projects)"** now edits the component def's OWN param defaults (`def.params[i].default`, saved WITH the component via Save component) instead of a per-project shadow; engine-provided params hidden (fed at runtime). **`ComponentParam.options` (string enum, new field):** the editor renders a **dropdown** wherever a param is set (component default + placed-instance override). Used for the readout `source` (balance/win/bet/totalWin via `VALUE_SOURCE_CATALOG`, derived from the numeric `ENGINE_PARAM_CATALOG`) and the button `variant` (dark/light). For engine-fed components the inert literal engine-param checklist is replaced by a note pointing to the source binding. **Decomposed-readout double-text FIX:** `HUD_READOUT_DEF` Background → new `preview.style:'tile'` (tile-only chip, no caption / stray `0.00`); Caption/Value carry `preview.textParam` (`label`/`value`) so the preview reads "BALANCE"/a number, not the part names (`EditorTextLayer` + `EditorCanvas.drawHudChip` resolve textParam vs the open component's resolved params). **Reusable tile styling (`TILE_PARAMS`):** texture / tint / outline-colour / outline-width / corner-radius as ONE shared `EditableParam` group in `boundComponentCatalog`, attached to BOTH `HudTicker` (readout bg) AND `ButtonFrame` (button frame). Each coded component reads+forwards what it supports — `HudTicker`→`UiSprite` (key/tint/backgroundColor/border\*), `ButtonFrame` layers them as the **resting** look UNDER its engine states (disabled grey + active border still WIN; bind.props tint overrides the instance tint). Editor `tile`/`button` chip previews reflect radius+border live. **This is the reuse proof the owner asked for: a 2nd consumer dropped in via one catalog line + reading the props — no new vocabulary, no duplicated UI/preview code.** **Blank Elements palette:** the Library in BOTH `/components` and `/editor` gains an **"Elements"** section with draggable **Text** + **Container** chips → `EditorCanvas.spawnNode` makes a default text node (proxima-nova/45/white) / empty container through the same drop pipeline (previously nodes could ONLY spawn from asset drags; a bare text node had no entry point). **Normalizer fix (`dc27bb7`):** `componentStorage.normalizeParams` now preserves `options` (was silently dropped on save/read). **⚠️ GOTCHA (cost the owner real confusion):** a SAVED R2 component (`_shared/editor-components/<id>.json` or a project shadow) **shadows the built-in def** (`loadComponent` precedence project ◁ shared ◁ built-in), so def/preview changes do NOT reach an already-saved copy — the stale copy must be **deleted (✕ in the Component Editor list)** to fall back to the updated built-in. Same for any baked node `preview` metadata. **Button-states finding (no code, owner asked):** the Stake button declares states `hovered`/`pressed`/`disabled`/`active`, BUT hover/pressed have NO visual in `UiButton` and disabled/active looks are HARDCODED constants — there are **no editable per-state style fields**; `disabled`/`active` are fed at runtime by `registerComponentActions` (game XState/bet state, keyed by the instance's `action`). Per-state styling = net-new fields + wiring hovered/pressed → DEFERRED per owner "nothing new".

- **Invisible Editor — B6 follow-up: parametric Button STATE IMAGES (owner request 2026-06-10): ✅ LANDED.** Un-defers the per-state styling above, scoped to image swaps: `BUTTON_DEF` gains five `kind: 'image'` instance params grouped under **State images** — `image` (resting), `imageHover`, `imagePressed`, `imageSelected` (engine `active` flag), `imageDisabled` (the **downstate**, engine `disabled` flag). The editor already renders `image` params with the RegionPicker (instance overrides + component defaults), so NO launcher change was needed. `ButtonFrame.svelte` now tracks `hovered`/`pressed` locally (mirroring the coded `Button.svelte`, incl. the disabled reset) and, when the current state's image is authored, renders a real `<Sprite key=…>` instead of the variant tile; cascade disabled → pressed (→hover→selected) → hovered (→selected) → selected → resting; empty-string params normalize to unset. **Parity:** with no image params the tile path is byte-identical (painted disabled-grey / active-border apply only where no image covers them); the active BORDER is tile-only, so an image-skinned button should author `imageSelected`. Side fix: a disabled `ButtonFrame` no longer fires `onpress` (matches the coded base button + `ComponentInstance`'s wrapper). **Verified live** in the lines Storybook — new story `state images swap the bg` mounts via `StoryPixiApp` + a `canvas`-space scene; all five swaps screenshot-proven. **Harness finding (pre-existing, open nit):** the older engine-layout proof stories (incl. B6.3's) mount NO `<App>` and `ButtonLabel` translates before `StoryLocale`'s mount-time `i18n.activate`, so they render blank in dev Storybook; the new story works around both (own `StoryPixiApp` + eager `i18n.activate` in the story script). ⚠️ Rollout caveat (same gotcha as above): an R2 `button.json` (shared or per-project) shadows the built-in def wholesale, hiding the new params until that saved copy is deleted (✕ in the Component Editor) or re-saved from the updated built-in.
- **Invisible Editor — state images for AUTHORED art buttons (2026-06-11, follow-up to the above after owner couldn't see the options):** root cause was TWO-fold: (1) bookofborut has a saved **project `button.json`** (plus the custom `Button_Square` `c_r3vqsa2q`) shadowing the built-in def — so the new params never listed; (2) those defs are **authored art** (bg sprite bound `region ← bg_image` + text, NO coded `ButtonFrame`), so the ButtonFrame-side state code never runs for them. Engine fix: new shared cascade `engine-layout/buttonStateImage.ts` (`resolveButtonStateImage` + `BUTTON_STATE_IMAGE_KEYS`, consumed by BOTH ButtonFrame and the authored path); `<ComponentInstance>`'s §18.4 interactive wrapper now tracks `hovered`/`pressed` (disabled-reset like the coded button) and — when the def declares state-image params — overrides the **`image` param with a reactive getter** resolving the cascade, so a bg sprite bound `region ← image` swaps per state. Parity: getter only defined for interactive instances of defs declaring the four state keys; everything else keeps the exact static param map. Proof story 3 (`authored art button…`) verified live (hover/pressed screenshot-proven; selected/downstate share the proven cascade + live flags). **Data migration RAN (owner-approved 2026-06-11) + verified:** the one-off `apps/launcher-api/migrate-button-states.mjs` (kept untracked beside its local backups `button.json`/`c_r3vqsa2q.json`/`scenes.backup.json` — delete all four once the owner confirms the editor looks right) renamed `bg_image`→`image` in BOTH saved defs (param + bg-sprite binding, the param now grouped under **State images** with the four new state params) and in the 6 placed button instances (autospin/spin/increase/decrease/turbo, incl. folding an orphaned `Image` key); hudReadout untouched; re-read from R2 confirmed clean (no stale `bg_image` on buttons). **Owner action: reload the editor tab** — an open tab still holds the pre-migration doc, and saving from it would resurrect `bg_image`. **Borut bumped + pushed (`2c990c1`):** engine submodule `2c9e6ac`→`32e6b6f` (carries both state-image commits), `build:engine` + `vite build` GREEN, committed surgically (pointer only — the owner's in-progress `static/assets` + baked-bundle edits left unstaged); **republish is the owner's** (Build & Deploy) — until then the deployed game keeps resting images only. **Follow-up (2026-06-11, RESOLVED): the swaps didn't ship — root cause was the TOKEN TRAP, not the engine.** Borut's turbo skin (`image=T_Button_Turbo_Off`, `imageSelected=imageDisabled=T_Button_Turbo`) + the migrated def + `Game.svelte`'s turbo `active`/`disabled` feed are all correct, but `pnpm build` ran `bake:doc`/`pull:assets` WITHOUT `EDITOR_DOC_SECRET` → shipped the `doc: null` placeholder → `registerBakedComponents()` + `bakedEditorArtAssets()` both no-op → the editor-art sheet (the ONLY source of the no-`.png` frame names the skin uses) never loaded. Fixed: Borut's `bake:doc`/`pull:assets` now run via `node --env-file-if-exists=.env …`, so `pnpm build` auto-loads the token from a gitignored `Borut/.env` (owner pastes it once; `.env.example` documents it). Owner confirmed working. See `project_component_art_to_game` memory (token-trap mitigation).
- **Invisible Editor — behavior layer (animated overlays `Win`/`Transition`/FS `intro`/`outro`): SCOPED, not started (owner direction 2026-06-09) → `docs/design/invisible-editor.md` §17.** Owner reframe: these overlays are _animation_, not logic, so the authoring surface is a **timeline/track editor, NOT a node/flow graph** (route A node-graph stays DEFERRED, re-confirmed). The §8.5 `BehaviorTrack`/`TweenStep` schema already exists; remaining = a shared **engine GSAP interpreter** in `<ComponentInstance>` + `registerComponentSignals` (book event → signal) + a **staged editor surface**. Altitude chosen: **Tier 0 (signal-preset dropdowns in Properties) → Tier 1 (full timeline panel)**, additive on one interpreter. Build order (parity-gated): interpreter → signal registry → Tier-0 UI → migrate `Transition` (proof) → `Win` + FS intros → Tier-1 timeline → Borut mirror. **v1 ceiling = tweens + spine playback + one count-up binding** (anything branching/stateful stays a coded `mount`) — the scope-creep line. **Parked behind the static-mount work (B6 + placement-only sweep); pick up at §17.4 step 1.**
- **Invisible Editor — spine pickers + signal-driven spine playback, wired end-to-end (2026-06-11, on `main`): PRs #44 + #45.** First real cut of the behavior layer, narrowed to the owner's "no spine-timeline editing" steer (tool split confirmed: scene editor = placement + properties; component editor = composition). **(1) Animation + skin pickers (#44):** a `kind:'spine'` node's animation is a dropdown (was free-text) + a new **skin** dropdown — both from the loaded skeleton's own lists, plumbed `EditorSpineLayer`→`EditorCanvas`→page→`EditorProperties` (`onSpineMetaChange`/`onSpineMeta`/`spineMeta`); engine honors via new `SpineNode.skin`→`<SpineProvider skin>` (`BaseSpineProvider.setSkinByName`, additive); live skin in the preview overlay. **(2) Signal→spine playback (#44, narrowed §8.5/§17, spine-only):** new `registerComponentSignals` registry (event sibling of the value/action feeds) + `componentSignalContext` + `SpineNode.cues:{signal,animation,loop}[]`; `<ComponentInstance>` walks its def for spine cues, subscribes each registered signal, and on fire writes the cue's animation into a reactive nodeId→anim map that `LayoutNodeView`'s spine block prefers over `defaultAnimation`. Editor: a **"Plays on signal"** cue editor in the spine Properties (component mode only). **(3) Game wiring (#45):** `utils-event-emitter` gained a lifecycle-free `subscribe` (the `onMount`-free sibling, needed because `<ComponentInstance>` subscribes from an `$effect`); `apps/lines` `signalSource.ts` `eventSignal()` + `Game.svelte` `registerComponentSignals({ win, bigWin })` — `win` fires on the `winShow` presentation event, `bigWin` on `winUpdate` gated to the `'big'` win-level tier. **All additive + parity-safe** (no skin/cues, unregistered signal, or scene-level spine = byte-identical). **End-to-end now works in `apps/lines`:** author a component with a spine cue → place it → it plays on the real win. **REMAINING:** Book of Borut activation (same `registerComponentSignals` call in its `Game.svelte` + submodule bump — its own repo, separate step); more signals (`idle`/`enter`/`exit`) are easy follow-ons on the same mechanism. See design §17.
- **Invisible Editor — Free-Spin Counter decomposed into an editable component (2026-06-11, on `main`): PRs #46/#47/#48/#49 + Book-of-Borut `2339e8c`.** Turned the opaque coded `FreeSpinCounter` (one `bind` anchor) into editor-visible/editable nodes, using the HUD-readout decomposition pattern. **(1) `FREE_SPIN_COUNTER_DEF` (#46):** built-in ComponentDef = Frame `sprite` + "FREE SPIN" `text` + dynamic "X OF Y" `text`, each a real node with `paramBindings` (text/font/size/fill editable); value engine-fed via a new `freeSpins` composed-string source (`stateUi.freeSpinCounterCurrent/Total`). Caption/value are plain `text` nodes (not reused `HudValue` — it's a numeric currency formatter). **(2) Bitmap text in the engine (#47, §9.4 Phase 3):** new `registerFontCatalog` registry; `LayoutNodeView` renders `<BitmapText>` when the catalog marks the family bitmap, else `<Text>` (parity). Fixes `gold` (and all bitmap fonts) for EVERY layout text node, not just this one. **(3) Component visibility feed (#48):** new `registerComponentVisibility` (`BoolSource`) registry; `<ComponentInstance>` reads a `visibleSource` param and gates the whole instance behind `<Container visible>` — but ONLY when a source resolved (render branches extracted to a snippet so the ungated path is byte-identical). This preserves the counter's free-spins-only gating. **(4) Live switch (#49):** both reference layouts' `freeSpinCounter` scene flipped from `bind: FreeSpinCounter` to a `componentInstance` (board-left placement per layoutType — desktop 87,220 — replicating the coded formula; params `source:'freeSpins'`, `visibleSource:'freeSpinCounterShow'`, `label:'FREE SPIN'`). Coded `FreeSpinCounter` stays registered as fallback (no double-mount). `apps/lines` (dev) now renders the decomposed counter. **(5) Borut (`2339e8c`):** engine submodule cf1a206→652a4fb + `Game.svelte` registers `freeSpins`/`freeSpinCounterShow`/`FREE_SPIN_COUNTER_DEF`/`registerFontCatalog`(gold/goldblur/silver/purple); `build:engine` GREEN, compiles. **OWNER STEPS to go live in Borut (order matters):** (a) **republish** the Borut bundle first (ships the registrations), then (b) **re-seed** `bookofborut`'s R2 doc so the live scene uses the componentInstance (the regenerated `scenes/bookof.json` seed emits it; needs R2 creds) — OR compose it in the editor. Until both, the live game keeps the coded anchor (safe). Couldn't browser-verify free spins here (needs funded RGS mock; svelte-check OOMs). See design §8/§9.4.

## Track 2 — Studio / pipeline tools

### Current live state

_(One line each; full done-work narrative in `docs/history.md`.)_

- **Launcher DB — prod `app_settings` missing fixed + migrate baseline-overshoot hardened: ✅ DONE (2026-06-20).** Prod logged `relation "app_settings" does not exist` on every `getDeployToken()` (fail-soft to `EDITOR_DOC_SECRET`, so non-breaking). **Cause:** migration `0009` (creates `app_settings`) was never applied — the 2026-06-13 manual baseline row was stamped at `0010`'s `when`, and the migrator's threshold check (applies entries with `when` > max recorded `created_at`) put `0009` BELOW the threshold → permanently skipped. **Fix:** created the table via the additive part of `db:push` (it ran the `CREATE TABLE` before erroring), then recorded a `baseline-0012-manual` row so `__drizzle_migrations` is consistent through `0012`. Verified via introspection that the live schema **exactly matches** `schema.ts` (all PKs/columns/defaults) — the `db:push` "`column "id" is in a primary key`" error is a **`push` false-positive** (spurious PK recreate), NOT real drift. **Rule: do NOT run `db:push` on this DB — use `pnpm db:generate` + the on-boot migrator.** **Hardening:** `migrate.ts` `baselineIfPushProvisioned` (stamped the newest journal `when` → skipped everything the push predated) replaced with `reconcilePushProvisioned`, which **replays every journal migration's SQL with duplicate-object errors tolerated** and records each — so a push-provisioned DB is reconciled with no overshoot and no spurious replay failures, whatever schema version the push matched. `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN.
- **Atlas Maker — blueprint "Resolved workflow" export (debugging): ✅ SHIPPED (2026-06-17, committed + pushed; auto-deploys to Railway — not yet verified live behind the auth/tunnel gate).** A faithful repro of the EXACT ComfyUI API/prompt graph the blueprint pipeline POSTs to `/prompt` for a real run, so users can see what the generic runner injects (copying their blueprint into ComfyUI by hand does NOT reproduce a run). **Backend (`services/atlas-tool/batch_atlas.py`):** new `resolve_blueprint_workflow(manifest, region_name, blueprint_id)` REUSES `build_workflow_blueprint` (single source of truth — byte-identical to a real run) and returns `(wf, output_node_id, changes)`; the baked→injected diff is built by deep-comparing `blueprint['graph'][node].inputs` (baked) vs the resolved `wf[node].inputs`, one entry per differing/new SCALAR field (wired array links skipped) `{node,title,field,baked,resolved,source}` with `source` derived from bindings/params (role name, `param:<key>`, `filename_prefix`, or `new`); `title` = `_meta.title` else `class_type` (matches the loader). **Thread-safety:** the in-process export shares module globals (`GEN_WIDTH`/`GEN_HEIGHT`/`MOCKUP_IMAGE`) that `apply_manifest_settings` mutates with concurrent request threads, so the apply→build→diff window snapshots EVERY `_SETTINGS_GLOBALS` target + `ATLAS_META` and restores them in `finally`, all under a new module `_RESOLVE_GLOBALS_LOCK`; overrides are passed EXPLICITLY (never via the `BP_PARAM_OVERRIDES` global). **Gen-size faithfulness:** because it replicates `apply_manifest_settings(manifest)` inside the lock, width/height reflect THIS manifest's `settings.gen_*`, not the process default. **Endpoint (`ui_server.py`):** `GET /blueprintresolved?manifest=<name>&region=<name?>&blueprint=<id?>` → `{ok,workflow,output_node,seed,changes,regions,notes}` (regions for the picker; `notes` warns when the region is unpinned = random seed each run); errors return `{ok:false,error}` (never a 500). **UI:** the Settings → 🎛 Blueprint settings panel gains a region dropdown + **⤓ Resolved workflow (as the pipeline sends it)** button → renders the changes table (Node / Field / Baked → Pipeline value / Source) + seed + notes + a **Download workflow.json** (Blob). Offline-verified: gen-size override applied (1536×2048), globals restored after success AND after errors, seed/param/prompt/prefix injection diffed correctly, source labels right, built-in-pipeline + bad-region errors readable. `py -m py_compile *.py` GREEN. Doc updated (`docs/tools/atlas-maker.md`). Code-reviewed (atlas-python-tools build + code-reviewer pass; faithfulness fixes applied: manifest lazy-pull reads the returned path, overrides keyed by the manifest's global active pipeline to mirror `main()`). **NOT verified live** (Railway + ComfyUI tunnel gate) — needs an in-app smoke test after deploy.
- **Invisible Game Maker — Phase 0 (generic runtime boot) live (2026-06-16).** First rung of "author a full game deploy entirely online" (design: `docs/design/invisible-game-maker.md`). A prebuilt generic engine bundle can now boot ANY project purely from a live fetch — no per-game repo, no build. New **`GET /api/editor/runtime?project=&k=`** (`runtimeBundle.ts`) serves a project's full `BakedBundle` live (doc + componentDefs/defaults + editor-art/symbols/fonts/localization indices) + an absolute `assetBase`; `apps/lines` gains opt-in **`?runtime=1`** boot (`editor-scenes.ts`/`+layout.ts`/`Game.svelte`) that registers the project's assets from live `/api/deploy` URLs instead of the build-baked bundle — **parity-guaranteed byte-identical when off** (`srcBase()`→`'assets/'`). **Path-form asset serving** (`/api/deploy/f/<token>/<client>/<project>/<...rel>`, shared via `deployServe.ts`; query form kept for build CI) fixes the exact relative-resolution bug flagged in open-item #6: a Spine page (and any spritesheet/bitmap-font sub-page) named inside its parent now resolves correctly when loaded relative to the parent URL — the query form dropped the rel/token → 404. **Headless gate vs `test1` PASS:** runtime 200, `basegame` present, every asset incl. the spine page `onboardSheet.webp` loads cross-origin. Commits `426ea1b`/`9210931`/`04960bb` (deployed). ⏳ On-screen Pixi render (boot-order timing) = owner visual confirm; localization-merge in runtime mode = known deferred gap. Phases 1–3 (server-side Publish + Game Maker page; config-from-R2; more game-types) next; Phase 4 (behavior-track DSL) is its own large engine project.
