- **Tool docs + onboarding back in sync (2026-06-14, code-only, not committed/deployed).** Owner flagged that many tools shipped doc-less and the launcher onboarding links rotted. Root cause: the "keep docs up to date" intent was a one-time pass (`B7`, 2026-05-30), never institutionalized — so every tool added since had no `docs/tools/` page, and the `/onboarding` "Read the guide" links 404'd (they pointed at `/docs/tools/<slug>.md` with no route serving it). Fixes: (1) **6 missing docs written**, grounded in route source + design docs — `rigger.md`, `invisible-editor.md`, `component-editor.md`, `font-maker.md`, `symbols-state-machine.md`, `spine-editor.md`; (2) `docs/tools/README.md` index rebuilt to the full registry; (3) **launcher now SERVES the docs** — new authed route `(app)/docs/[slug]` renders `docs/tools/*.md` via `marked` (bundled at build by `scripts/copy-tool-docs.mjs` → `src/lib/tool-docs/` + `import.meta.glob('?raw')`, so present/future docs Just Work); `toolDocPath()` → `/docs/<slug>`; (4) **institutionalized** so it can't decay again — new `.claude/agents/docs-keeper.md` subagent + **CLAUDE.md hard rule #9** ("a new/renamed tool isn't done until its doc ships in the SAME change"). `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN (docs route chunk 100.9 kB = markdown inlined). ⏳ Not browser-verified live (auth + Postgres gate); commit + deploy pending.
- **Launcher** at `app.invisiblewall.org` — invite-only email+password auth (scrypt) + Postgres sessions, three-layer role/user tool matrix, `/admin` panel (users/roles/tools/projects/clients/games/sessions), project + client layer (R2 isolated per **`<client>/<project>/`** — unified single-project repo, see below). Game cards on the home page are scoped to the active project (`games.project_key`; null = global). (history: see `docs/history.md`)
- **Desktop launcher — pull projects from the cloud** (2026-06-11, server live `17677fb`) — `C:\Invisible Wall SL\ComfyUI\Invisible_Launcher.py` now pulls the projects a signed-in account may access (scoped by the same `accessibleProjects` rules, grouped by a top **client selector**) instead of forcing a local-folder add. New `GET/POST /api/launcher/projects`: session-bearer GET returns accessible projects + their opaque `launcher_profile` + the distinct clients; admin-bearer POST publishes a project's profile (mirrors `register-game`). Schema: nullable `projects.launcher_profile` jsonb (migration `0008`; **column applied to prod** via idempotent `ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS` — listProjects selects it, so it had to land before deploy). Desktop side: a **Projects root** setting derives each project folder as `<root>\<client>\<project>` (no stored absolute paths / game root; ComfyUI stays the shared service), **↻ Sync projects** git-clones the project repo from its profile `repoUrl` + runs `pull:assets` (R2 assets) and auto-syncs _definitions_ after sign-in, **Load from folder** is now optional (reworded empty states), and owner **Publish setup** pushes a project's profile up. `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` + `py -m py_compile` GREEN. **⏳ Owner-verify:** release the desktop launcher (its own `invisible-launcher` repo + new exe per the B31 process — the `.py` edits are in the live working copy, not yet committed/released) and smoke-test on a fresh `comfyui_manager_config.json` (sign in → projects appear grouped by client, no manual Add → Sync clones a repo into `<Projects root>\<client>\<project>`).
- **Desktop launcher — deploy token auto-injected into every build (2026-06-12, server live `0aa2625`, launcher v1.0.17).** Fixes "after the launcher Sync update, a game deploy reverts ALL graphics to default (even the reels)." Root cause: the new Sync clones each project to the canonical `<root>\<client>\<key>` path (e.g. `borut\bookofborut`), and the launcher now builds THAT synced entry — but its `game.publish.env` had no `EDITOR_DOC_SECRET`, so `pull:assets`/`bake:doc` ran `--optional`, skipped, and shipped the committed placeholder art. (The old hand-made `iGaming\Borut\Book of Borut` entry DID carry the token in publish.env, so it had always worked — the duplicate folder masked the gap.) Fix kills the per-project `.env`/publish.env token footgun entirely: new **`GET /api/launcher/deploy-token`** (bearer-auth, no role gate, mirrors `git-credentials`) serves `EDITOR_DOC_SECRET`; the desktop launcher's `fetch_deploy_token()` pulls it in `_build_publish_game` and `build_game` injects it into the build env for EVERY project (node's `--env-file` won't override an already-set var, so it wins over a blank `.env`; an explicit publish.env value still overrides). Verified: a real `pull:assets` from R2 returns the live art (`symbolsStatic.png` 468 KB, all editor-art sheets) — R2 is the complete source of truth, nothing was lost. **Rescue commit** Book-of-Borut `e79a9a7` carried the editor-art namespacing (`editor-scenes.ts` + engine pin `5dbe66c` providing `editorArtNamespace`) + the `--env-file-if-exists=.env` build wiring from the old folder into the repo. **⏳ Owner steps:** (1) Build & publish Book of Borut from launcher v1.0.17 → confirm real reels/graphics; (2) delete the legacy `iGaming\Borut\` tree once verified; (3) ROTATE `EDITOR_DOC_SECRET` (it's plaintext in the local config + was screenshot-exposed). **Sync now auto-updates existing clones (launcher v1.0.18):** Sync no longer skips an already-cloned repo — it `git pull --ff-only`s it (+ `submodule update --init --recursive`) so no synced project lags behind `main`, and injects the deploy token into the sync-time `pull:assets` so R2 art hydrates too. `--ff-only` never clobbers local commits / a dirty tree (left as-is with a note; the canonical clones are normally clean since R2 is the asset source).
- **Deploy token — admin-managed + role-gated (2026-06-12, code-only, not committed/deployed).** The shared build/deploy token (`EDITOR_DOC_SECRET`) is no longer env-only. New generic `app_settings` key/value table (migration `0009_faulty_unicorn.sql`, **NOT applied to prod yet**) + `$lib/server/appSettings.ts#getDeployToken()` resolves the admin-managed DB value (`app_settings.deployToken`) first, else the `EDITOR_DOC_SECRET` env bootstrap. All 7 call-sites (`/api/deploy`, `/api/editor/doc`, `/api/editor/export-art`, `/api/editor/export-fonts`, `/api/launcher/deploy-token`, `/api/localization/strings`, `(app)/+page.server.ts`) read `getDeployToken()` — behaviour is identical when no DB row exists (today's prod keeps working). **Manage it in `/admin` → Settings → Deploy token:** view masked, reveal once, set a typed value, or rotate to a strong random token (admin-only; secret never logged, served no-store, reveals re-mask on reload). **`GET /api/launcher/deploy-token` is now capability-gated** by the new `gamePublish` ("Build & publish games") managed capability (default-ON for `admin`, grantable per role/user in `/admin` → Roles) — a deliberate change from the old "every signed-in user gets the token". `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN. **⏳ Owner steps:** apply migration `0009` to prod (`db:migrate`), grant `gamePublish` to any non-admin publishers, optionally move the token into the DB + rotate.
- **Online tools** — `/atlas` (redirect to atlas-tool), `/spine` (Spine Viewer), `/sheet` (redirect to sheet-tool), `/localization` (in-launcher), `/editor` (Invisible Editor, in-launcher), `/files` (FTP Browser: project-scoped R2 manager for non-admins; **admins get a two-tab full-server view — whole R2 bucket + a read-only Railway/Postgres inspector** at `/api/db/*`, secrets redacted). (history: see `docs/history.md`)
- **Invisible Font Maker** (`/fonts`, NEW 2026-06-12 — Phase 0+1 landed, code-only/not browser-verified) — a launcher-native tool to **create, view, and save bitmap fonts** per client/project into the existing R2 fonts contract (`<client>/<project>/fonts/fonts.json` + `_shared/fonts/`), so games consume them by family name. Closes the authoring gap: until now fonts only entered the contract via the PowerShell `r2-sync-fonts.mjs` script. **Decided:** host = launcher Svelte page (no new service); baking = browser-side `opentype.js` + Canvas 2D (gradient/outline/shadow effects in v1); output = BMFont **XML** + page PNG (byte-compatible with what `apps/lines` loads). **Landed:** registered in `roles.ts` (`fontMaker` tool, granted to admin/developer/artist); route `(app)/fonts/` (`ssr=false`, `fontMaker`-gated); **View mode** lists + live-previews a project's fonts via a self-contained, `fontMaker`-gated `GET /api/fonts/catalog` + `GET /api/fonts/asset` (so the tool does NOT need the editor grant) — the byte-streamer + BMFont `?font=1` descriptor page-rewrite were **extracted to `lib/server/assetStream.ts`** and shared with `/api/editor/asset` (editor path parity-preserved); `resolveEditorFonts` parametrized with an `assetUrl` builder. **Phase 2 — Import + Save ✅ LANDED 2026-06-12** (code-only): upload a BMFont (`.xml`/`.fnt`/`.json` + page image[s]) → client parses + live-previews → save into the project's `fonts/`. Uploads use **presigned PUT** (page PNGs exceed adapter-node's 512 KB body limit): `POST /api/fonts/upload-urls` (presign descriptor+pages) → browser PUTs to R2 → `POST /api/fonts/save` is authoritative (re-reads the descriptor from R2, re-derives `name`/`pageFiles` via `lib/server/bmfont.ts`, verifies pages exist, upserts `fonts.json` by `id===folder`; per-project writes only). New client `FontImport.svelte` + `parseDescriptorClient`/`loadLocalBitmapFont`. **Phase 3 — Generate ✅ LANDED 2026-06-12** (code-only): `FontGenerate.svelte` + `fontBake.client.ts` bake a BMFont (XML + page PNG) from a TTF/OTF entirely in the browser — `opentype.js` for metrics/glyph geometry, Canvas 2D `Path2D` for rasterization + **effects** (solid/gradient fill, outline, drop-shadow, each toggleable), shelf packer → single page; charset presets in `charsets.client.ts`; live PIXI `BitmapText` preview (= the metrics self-check) + atlas thumbnail; saves via the **same** Phase-2 path (the upload→save sequence was extracted to `fonts.client.ts` `saveBitmapFont` and is now shared by Import + Generate). Deps added: `opentype.js` + `@types/opentype.js`. Deferred to Phase 4: kerning, multi-page atlases, shared-library save target. All three modes (View / Import / Generate) functional. **Phase 4 (polish) ✅ LANDED 2026-06-12** (code-only): R1 — Generate **kerning** (`<kernings>` from opentype, toggle) + **multi-page atlases** (page-max-height control, one PNG per page). R2 — **delete** a font (`POST /api/fonts/delete` + View confirm button), **web-font import** (Import accepts woff2/woff/ttf/otf → `kind:'web'`; `/api/fonts/save` web branch), and a **shared-library save target** (`_shared/fonts/`) behind a new admin-gated capability **`fontPublish`** — the shared write/delete gate lives in `resolveFontTarget` (`lib/server/fonts.ts`), NOT `assertAllowed` (which only widens the shared READ allow-list); target selector shows only to publishers. Deferred: rename a font (moves R2 objects → own task). `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN. Design: `docs/design/invisible-font-maker.md`. **⏳ Owner-verify live**: View/Import (BMFont `mm_gold.xml`+`.webp`)/Generate (TTF + effects + kerning/multi-page)/web-font import/delete; admin → save to the shared library + confirm a non-`fontPublish` user gets 403. Confirm a game loads a saved font by family name.
- **Invisible Storybook** (2026-06-11, built — nothing published yet) — `/storybook` lists + serves published `storybook build` outputs from R2 behind launcher auth: the engine reference at `_shared/storybook/engine/` (any user with the tool) and per-project builds at `<client>/<project>/storybook/` (project-access gated, same model as spines/files). Serving is path-shaped (`/storybook/view/engine/…`, `/storybook/view/p/<projectKey>/…`) so storybook's relative links resolve; html/json `no-store`, hashed `assets/` immutable. Publish with `apps/launcher-api/scripts/publish-storybook.mjs` (`--shared` or `--project <c>/<p>`; builds via `pnpm exec storybook build` unless `--dir` given; prunes stale hashed keys; `--dry-run`). `new-game.mjs` scaffolds `publish:storybook`; `apps/lines` gained `build-storybook`. Doc: `docs/tools/storybook.md`. TODO: publish the engine reference + Borut's storybook (needs R2 creds), then smoke-test live.
- **atlas-tool + sheet-tool** on Railway — Python Atlas/Sheet Makers re-hosted; now **repo-root Docker build** sharing `services/_shared/iw_common` + thread-local per-request context. Sheet Maker: Refresh-from-R2, destructive-Save confirm, shared-manifest browsing, **packs Atlas generation manifests** (loose sprites from `batch/`), and an **R2-backed Import browser** (open any packed `.atlas` anywhere in the project). Atlas Maker: deploy now lands in the project's R2 `deploy/` (the "deploy to C:/" bug is fixed). **Fixes 2026-06-05 (`1ffa20f`, shipped):** (1) user images loaded from disk (`_setoutput`/`_userefimg`) now `_mirror` to R2 (+ `_clearoutput` `_unmirror`s on revert) — they were local-staging-only, so a hydrate/refresh/redeploy reverted the card to the old generated output ("as if not saved"); same class as B42's FX-mirror fix. (2) the R2 folder picker's "✓ Use this folder" wrote the breadcrumb label (`R2 repo: <path>`) into the field — `_fsbrowse` now returns a clean `rel` and the client selects that (browsing was already project-root-confined). (history: see `docs/history.md`)
- **Thumbnail/asset caching — random 502s fixed (2026-06-15, shipped `pipeline` + `launcher` commits).** Owner hit random `502 Bad Gateway` on `atlas-tool` `/thumb`,`/outthumb`,`/ref` when opening a Symbols manifest (different images each reload). Root cause: the tools re-decoded + re-resized every full-size image with PIL on EVERY hit (no cache), responses were `no-store`, and the client appended `?t=Date.now()` busters — so one many-thumbnail page fired a burst of full-res decodes that saturated the single `ThreadingHTTPServer` process; Railway's proxy 502'd whichever requests lost the race (load-dependent, hence random). Fix is cross-tool: new shared **`services/_shared/iw_common/imgcache.py`** = disk thumbnail cache keyed on (source path, mtime, size, box) + bounded PIL concurrency (semaphore) + atomic writes + per-key herd lock (evicted after write), plus ETag/304 helpers. Wired into atlas-tool (`thumb_bytes`/`_serve_img`/`/thumb`,`/outthumb`,`/ref`,`/vthumb`) and sheet-tool (`_serve_sprite`); cache-bust tokens are now mtime-derived (re-rendered image still refreshes in place, plain reload revalidates cheaply via 304). Launcher mirror: `getObjectBytes` surfaces R2's ETag, `assetStream.ts` serves the verbatim image branch `private, max-age=300, must-revalidate` + ETag/304 (auth gate still runs first; BMFont descriptor branch stays `no-store`). `py_compile` + `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN; code-reviewed (fixed a `thumb_token(None)` crash on ref-less regions + the lock-map leak). **⏳ Owner-verify live** after Railway rebuilds: reload a Symbols manifest in Atlas Maker — no more 502s, thumbnails revalidate (304) instead of regenerating; a re-rendered symbol still updates in place.

A single canonical `r2_slug` (lowercase, non-alnum→`_`) in `iw_common.context` + launcher `r2Slug` fixes the old hyphen/underscore divergence (`book-of-borut`→`book_of_borut`). Migrated via `scripts/migrate-r2-unified-repo.py` (copy→verify→retire); cutover complete — old prefixes deleted (4902 objects), the stale `book_of_borut` dupe + `cloud/batch`/`cloudtest/batch` strays purged. Design: `docs/design/unified-project-repo.md`. R2 fully clean — all old per-tool namespaces are 0 objects (the last `localization/cloud/strings.json` orphan was deleted too).

- **Atlas Maker — new project slice/settings targeted a phantom `symbolsStatic` manifest: ✅ FIXED + SHIPPED to `main` (2026-06-24, `atlas-tool`, `f138feb`; Railway auto-deploy restarts the container, so no manual restart needed).** Owner report: a brand-new project (`lines_flowtest`) with a fresh sheet (`atlas_manifest_newsheet.json`) failed "Slice source → refs" with `FileNotFoundError … manifests/atlas_manifest_symbolsStatic.json`, and the per-atlas settings/style panel headers showed `symbolsStatic` while the Session dropdown showed `newsheet`. **Root cause:** `creative_manifest_path()` (`services/atlas-tool/ui_server.py`) defaulted a blank/stale `manifest_path` config to a HARDCODED `"atlas_manifest_symbolsStatic.json"` — a fabricated name no project owns (never a seeded file). The slice + panel header routed through it; the dropdown used a different empty-fallback resolution → the two desynced. **Fix:** one shared `active_manifest_name()` resolver (owned configured value → project's FIRST real manifest, matching the dropdown → `""` for a brand-new project; NEVER fabricates a name); the dropdown `selected`, the panel header, and `_sliceatlas` all route through it so they can't disagree; `_sliceatlas` now shows a clear "compose/pick a manifest first" message instead of slicing a phantom path; phantom `symbolsStatic`/`symbols` defaults blanked in `DEFAULT_CONFIG` + `batch_atlas.py _DEFAULTS`. **Self-healing for existing projects** — a stale unowned `manifest_path` is now ignored on boot (falls through to the first real manifest), so no R2 data migration is needed. `py_compile` GREEN + a 7-case resolver fixture (incl. the exact bug case) passes. Owner-verify in-app after the deploy lands (the tool 403s every path, so no HTTP check): reopen `lines_flowtest`, the slice targets `newsheet` + the panel header matches the dropdown.
- **Atlas Maker — region generation on a cold project failed: `sheet_src/` shape ref never uploaded to ComfyUI: ✅ FIXED + SHIPPED to `main` (2026-06-24, `atlas-tool`, `fix/atlas-ref-upload-lazy`).** Owner report (`lines_flowtest`, fresh sheet): "Create Atlas → generate" crashed — ComfyUI `HTTP 400 LoadImage: Invalid image file: …/sheet_src/newsheet/hotfruitBG.png`, preceded by `[upload] ref not in staging, leaving as-is`. **Root cause (the lazy-hydrate gap):** a region's painted `shape_ref` silhouette lives under `sheet_src/` — a LAZY-hydrated subtree (`cloud_paths._LAZY_SUBTREES`) the cold project never pulled. `batch_atlas.py`'s generation ref path only knew INPUT_DIR-relative refs: `_locate_ref_in_staging` (and `normalize_shape_ref`) resolved `INPUT_DIR/<relpath>`, which a `sheet_src/` key never matches, so `_upload_workflow_refs` printed "leaving as-is" and shipped the raw R2 key into the `LoadImage` node — ComfyUI (which can't see our disk) rejected it. **Fix:** `_locate_ref_in_staging` now resolves `sheets/`/`sheet_src/` keys via `_staging_rel` (no prefix-doubling) + `ensure_lazy` + an exact by-key `_hydrate_from_r2_by_name` fallback — mirroring `ui_server._resolve_region_ref`, so generation pulls the ref from R2 on demand; `normalize_shape_ref` routes through the same locator; and `_upload_workflow_refs` now raises a clear, named-ref `RuntimeError` (surfaced by the GENERATION STOPPED banner) instead of silently shipping a bad path. **No data migration** — refs hydrate on demand mid-request, so cold/existing projects generate without pre-warming and no restart is needed for new generations. `py_compile` GREEN + an 8-case fake-R2 fixture (cold-project pull, no prefix-doubling, normalize, upload-rewrite, clear-error-on-missing, legacy-ref regression). Files: `services/atlas-tool/batch_atlas.py`. Owner-verify in-app: regenerate the `Lines_BG` region — the `hotfruitBG.png` ref now uploads + the prompt runs.

Built: (a) **`GET /api/deploy`** (`apps/launcher-api/src/routes/api/deploy/+server.ts`) — mirrors the `/api/editor/doc` posture (gated by `EDITOR_DOC_SECRET`, open CORS); listing mode returns `{files:[{rel,size}]}`, single-file mode (`&rel=`) streams bytes with inferred content-type, path-traversal guarded; added `listAllObjects(prefix)` to `r2.ts`. (b) **HTTP puller** `apps/launcher-api/scripts/pull-project-assets.mjs` (rewritten from S3→`fetch`, NO aws-sdk/R2 creds, so it runs in any game CI) — lists `/api/deploy`, mirrors each `rel` into `--dest`, `--optional` mode degrades to a loud warning + keeps checked-in assets (vs hard-fail) so it can't brick a build. (c) **Book of Borut prebuild hook** (separate repo): `prebuild: pnpm pull:assets --optional` → pulls `borut/book_of_borut` `deploy/` into `static/assets/` before `vite build`. `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN. **Step 4 (cache-bust) ✅ DONE (`6435c35`, 2026-06-06)** — solved at the SERVING layer, not per-file hashing: the test server now caches content-hashed `_app/immutable/` forever but sends `no-store, must-revalidate` on `index.html` + the game's stable-named `assets/…` (covers spine page images too, which an `.atlas`/JSON `?v=` query can't reach via relative resolution), so a re-publish always serves fresh. **Cleanup ✅ DONE (2026-06-06):** the superseded `r2-sync-deploy.mjs` (S3-based 2026-06-02 half) was deleted — the HTTP puller is the one path. **Step 5 (runtime override `PUBLIC_LIVE_ASSETS=1`) — NOT PURSUING (owner, 2026-06-06).** The feature goal (edit atlas → see new art in-game) is already met by producer + puller + dist-rebuild + fresh-serving (`6435c35`); the runtime override only saved a rebuild for previews, at the cost of the token-gated page-ref-resolution risk (a spine `.atlas`/sprite JSON fetched from `/api/deploy?...&rel=` can't resolve its sibling page — query is dropped on relative resolution — so it'd need per-type page-ref rewriting like the editor's loaders). Judged not worth it. **So live-assets is functionally COMPLETE.** Only step 6 remains = prove end-to-end live (`EDITOR_DOC_SECRET` is set on the launcher → needs a real Atlas Maker deploy with `deploy_path`, then republish Borut and confirm the new art shows).
   - **Dist-rebuild gap FIXED (2026-06-04).** Root-caused a class of "engine fix doesn't reach the game" bugs: standalone games consume engine packages (`pixi-svelte`, `engine-layout`) via their gitignored pre-built `dist/`, which bare `vite build` never rebuilt — so engine SOURCE changes silently never shipped. Fix: the game's `build` now runs `build:engine` (`pnpm --filter "<slug>^..." run build` — topological, only the ~2 packages that emit a dist) before `vite build`. Applied to Book of Borut (`build: pnpm build:engine && pnpm pull:assets --optional && vite build`, replacing the lifecycle `prebuild`) and baked into `scripts/new-game.mjs` so every future game gets it. Verified end-to-end: the SpineProvider spine-key fix now compiles into `build/_app/immutable`. Also fixed the live spine crash (editor stores spine assetKeys as R2 prefixes, games want plain bundle keys) two ways: engine-side `SpineProvider` fallback+fail-soft (`5184d24`) AND launcher-side `/api/editor/doc` rewrite via `resolveSpineKeysForGame` (`5196cc0`, fixes already-published games with no rebuild). Memo: `gotcha_game_build_stale_engine_dist`.

**NEEDS OWNER env vars (unset → consequence):**

- `EDITOR_DOC_SECRET` on the launcher — **SET (confirmed by owner 2026-06-04)**, so `GET /api/editor/doc` serves docs and a game launched with `?k=<secret>` fetches its live editor doc at boot (edits flow to the game). (Older notes above/below that say "unset / editor sandbox" are stale — disregard.)
- `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` on the launcher — until set, the Localization tool loads but Translate returns a clear error.
- `GAMES_BASE_URL` — set to the current test server, else the home Games section shows a muted hint. (Now superseded by the admin-managed games DB, but the env still gates the legacy quick-bridge.)
- `COMFY_ORG_API_KEY` (+ comfy.org credits) — required to run gpt_image generation.
- `SHEET_TOOL_URL` (+ matching `SHEET_TOOL_SECRET`) on the launcher — until set, `/sheet` shows the "not configured" page. Also still owed: create the Railway service from `services/sheet-tool/` (envs `R2_*`, optional `SHEET_PROJECT`/`SHEET_TOOL_SECRET`).
- `ATLAS_BLUEPRINT_SECRET` — set the SAME value on the launcher + atlas-tool Railway services to enable blueprint PUBLISHING (upload to the shared `_shared/blueprints/` library). Unset → publishing stays OFF in the deployed tool (fail safe); reading/using blueprints is unaffected. The launcher only hands it to users with the `blueprintPublish` capability (admin by default).

Verdict: format/serializer risk retired → Phases 1–3 clear; Phase 4 (weights) stays gated on auto-weights. - **Invisible Symbols State Machine — "↻ Reload from R2" (2026-06-18, on `main` `510fce1`):** the spine previews loaded each bundle ONCE per key with no cache-bust, and the bundle list came from the server `load`, so a spine re-exported into R2 under the same key (e.g. re-rigged in the Rigger) kept showing the cached skeleton/animation (`/api/editor/asset` skeleton+pages are HTTP-cached on purpose). Added a header **Reload from R2** button mirroring the editor's "Reload art": bumps a `reloadToken` threaded into `SymbolSpinePreview`/`SymbolSpineStage` → `loadSpineInstance(…, reloadToken)` (drops the in-session bundle cache + re-fetches with fresh `?v=`) AND calls `invalidateAll()` so a brand-new bundle + refreshed animation-name lists also appear. Local doc edits survive (`doc` is its own `$state`). Build GREEN. ⏳ owner-verify live: re-export a spine → Reload from R2 → new animation shows.
- **Invisible Editor end-to-end:** expand→thumbnail→drag→render→overlay→save (B18) + non-fallback doc fetch (seed a real `editor/<client>/lines/scenes.json` in R2; port consumer into Book of Borut, "step 9b/10b").
- **Invisible Editor — layout contract (coord-space + text + validation)** 🚧 code done (2026-06-05, branch `feat/editor-layout-contract`). Closes the "saved positions render offset / editor doesn't emit everything the game needs" gap. **(a) Coordinate-space self-wrapping:** `<LayoutScene>` (`packages/engine-layout`) now reads `scene.space` (`game|standard|canvas|background`) and **self-wraps** in the right container (`<MainContainer>` / `<MainContainer standard align…>` / none / cover-fit) so a scene renders identically regardless of mount site — fixes the bug where a `game`-space scene mounted outside `MainContainer` drew main-coords as raw canvas pixels (offset background). New `Scene.align` + `'background'` space; `LayoutNodeView` cover-fits bg sprites/spine via `normalBackgroundLayout`. `apps/lines/Game.svelte` migrated to drop its manual `<MainContainer>` wrappers; existing scenes tagged with their space (`basegameOverlays`→`canvas`, `hudBar`→`standard`+`align bottom`) to keep `lines` byte-identical. HUD path unaffected (it consumes scenes as position data via `hudPositions.ts`, not `<LayoutScene>`). **(b) Rich text:** `TextNode.style` → full `TextStyle` (wordWrap/wordWrapWidth/breakWords/align/lineHeight/letterSpacing/fontStyle/stroke/dropShadow); editor Properties panel exposes them. **(c) Persistence:** launcher `normalizeDoc` now preserves `space`/`align`/the new style fields on R2 round-trip (was silently dropping `background`+`align`). **(d) Validation:** live client-side content warnings in the editor (sprite/spine referencing a missing/unassigned asset) surfaced next to slot warnings; scene space/align controls added. `pnpm --filter engine-layout build` + `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN; code-reviewed (no blockers). **⏳ Owner verify online:** `lines` renders unchanged; a background placed in the editor is no longer offset; text wrap/stroke/shadow render. **Deferred:** font-registry validation (no project font list yet — TODO marked in client); background **spine** cover-fit in the editor's WebGL preview (2D sprite path is correct); responsive per-layoutType text style overrides.
  - **SHIPPED to `main` (2026-06-05):** PR #21 (self-wrap+text+validation, `b34d371`), PR #23 cross-type clobber guard (`3b2bad0`), PR #24 seed bind-anchors (`47c801d`).
  - **Cross-type clobber guard (PR #23):** loading a "Placed game layout"/blank scenes whose game type ≠ the project's own (e.g. Lines into a bookOf project) now warns specifically AND **suppresses autosave** until you explicitly Save (converts) or Discard (reload). Same-type loads keep the non-destructive preview. This was the bug that overwrote `borut/bookofborut`'s doc with Lines content.
  - **Seed bind-anchor contract (PR #24) — IMPORTANT, applies to every game seed (`seed-game-editor.mjs` + each game's `defaultLayout.ts`):** dynamic / full-bleed / event-gated scenery (background, free-spin counter/intro/outro, Win/Transition) MUST be **bind anchors in `space: 'canvas'`** so the registered coded component owns rendering + layout + show/hide; `canvas` space means `<LayoutScene>` adds no `MainContainer` (the component supplies its own — avoids double-wrap). The board frame is the one genuinely static piece → real sprite in `game` space. The OLD seed emitted free-spin/bg as STATIC sprite+text → they showed during base game (no gating) + rendered serif (a static Text node can't use the `gold` BITMAP font) + the bg drew at main-box coords (offset). Re-seeding with the fixed seed corrects a live game **without a rebuild** (the game renders the R2 doc; bind anchors render right on the deployed engine too — `space` is just ignored by the old engine).
  - **CONSUMER MIGRATION required when a game bumps to this engine:** the self-wrapping `<LayoutScene>` means a game's `Game.svelte` must DROP its manual `<MainContainer>` wrapper around a `game`-space `<LayoutScene>` (else double-scale). Done for `apps/lines` AND **Book of Borut** (its repo, commit `1a2eaf2`: submodule→`47c801d`, Game.svelte de-wrapped, `defaultLayout.ts` scenes tagged `space:'canvas'`, `I18nTest` gated behind `import.meta.env.DEV`). **The game on `games.invisiblewall.org` is a separately-published bundle — it does NOT auto-update on merge; needs rebuild (`build:engine`) + `publish-game-bundle.mjs` republish.** So: re-seed = fixes doc-driven bugs live now; rebuild+republish = drops `I18nTest` + adopts the new engine.
  - **Editor "see the game composed" — COMPLETE (PRs #25–31, on `main` 2026-06-05).** Coded `bind` overlays (Background/Win/Transition/FreeSpin*) are now shown, placed, movable + stable in the editor — the game still mounts the real component (it ignores all of this). **(1) Art preview (#25/#26):** editor-only `BaseNode.preview.art` (`{kind:'spine'|'sprite', assetKey, region?, fit:'cover'|'contain'}`) draws a stand-in for a coded anchor (2D region + WebGL spine). **(2) Catalog-driven, not per-item (#27):** `packages/engine-layout/src/lib/boundComponentCatalog.ts` — `BOUND_COMPONENT_DEFAULTS` maps a coded component NAME → its preview by CONVENTION asset name; `resolveAnchorPreviewArt(node,assets,regionIndex)` resolves explicit override → catalog → the project's own assets. So any game gets previews with zero per-item config (convention published at `docs/conventions/coded-component-assets.md`). The seed emits plain bind anchors — no baked preview. **(3) Board-relative placement / Level 1 (#28):** catalog `placement` (`cover|centre|boardCentre|boardLeft`) + `computeOverlayPlacement(placement,{main,board,art})`; the editor reads the board rect from the doc's `boardFrame` node (game-agnostic) and maps MAIN→screen like `<MainContainer>`, so previews land at (≈) the real in-game spot. **(4) Drag to reposition / Level 2 (#29):** overlay anchors are draggable; the drag writes `node.x/y`, which the game ALREADY applies via `LayoutNodeView`'s wrapping `<Container>` for a canvas-space bind — so it moves in-game with NO engine/component/seed change. Background (cover) stays fixed. **(5) Spine overlay zoom-lock (#30 no-op, #31 real fix):** the WebGL spine preview slid under zoom because the vendored `OrthoCamera` `up=(0,-1,0)` (cancels WebGL's y-flip) makes `lookAt`'s x-axis `(-1,0,0)` → mirrors X. Fixed in `EditorSpineLayer` per-skeleton bake: `skeleton.x = clientW - (world*zoom+panX)`, negate `scaleX`; Y untouched. **Deferred:** resolution-independent (fractional) drag offset; true per-anchor in-game positioning for components that self-position (currently the bind offset is additive). Owner-confirmed working online.
- **Component Editor (`/components`) — UX batch A SHIPPED to `main` (2026-06-08, `editor:` commit).** Owner found the standalone Component Editor confusing (a polluted "library" of duplicate empty shells, clicking shows nothing, unclear buttons, no way back to the editor). Fixed: **(a) single-component feel** — the component picker shows ONLY in the home state; opening a component switches the left panel to the Asset library, so the tool visibly edits one component at a time. **(b) Delete** — new `deleteComponent` storage fn + `DELETE /api/editor/component` + per-row trash control (confirm), so accumulated shells can be purged (there was NO delete before). **(c) No more duplicate generation** — the editor's "Edit as component" (`editContainerAsComponent`) is now idempotent by name (overwrites the existing project component instead of minting a new id each click — the root cause of the duplicate Reel grid/Balance/Win rows). **(d) Navigation** — a `← Editor` back-link in the Component Editor topbar + the editor refreshes its component list when its tab regains focus. Also documented the required `R2_*` vars in `apps/launcher-api/.env.example` (a local launcher 500s without them). ⏳ Owner-verify live. **NEXT — Batch B ✅ SCOPED (owner-chosen 2026-06-08, NOT yet built) → see `invisible-editor.md` §13.** Owner chose the ambitious reading: **value-as-param** — HUD Balance/Win/Bet become ONE `HudReadout` component instanced 3× (the data source is a param, not coded identity), with **per-project** author-set defaults set in the Component Editor + per-placement overrides in the scene Editor. Key finding: this is **build step 6 of §8.8** (the param/value-binding layer — the explicit `ComponentInstance.svelte:53` "STATIC ONLY" seam); steps 1–5 (static componentInstance expansion + storage + reuse) are DONE and §12 already proves the appearance half on the bound HUD path, so Batch B = value feed + per-project defaults store + Component-Editor param surface + reconciling the two component systems (the Balance/Win shells in `/components` are dead artifacts). Phased B1 → B2 → B3 → B4. **B1+B2 ✅ SHIPPED to `main` 2026-06-08 (`b26bc3b`, `engine:`):** `BaseNode.paramBindings` + Svelte-free `resolveComponentParams`/`resolveBoundValue` (`componentParams.ts`) + `componentParamsContext.ts`; `ComponentInstance` threads resolved params + subscribes to a registered value SOURCE (`registerComponentValues.ts`, keyed by `balance`/`win`/`bet` — any component with a `source` param binds), exposing a reactive `value`; `ParamReadoutText.svelte` formats + counts up via `svelte/motion` `Tween` (no GSAP); `LayoutNodeView` text branch routes a numeric bind through it. Proof = `apps/lines` story renders ONE `HudReadout` def as TWO instances (balance tracks, win counts up). PARITY: no `paramBindings` + no registered source ⇒ byte-identical. **B3 ✅ SHIPPED 2026-06-08 (`editor:`):** per-project defaults store (`editor/<project>/component-defaults/<id>.json`, `componentDefaultsStorage.ts` + `GET/POST /api/editor/component-defaults`), Component-Editor "Defaults (this project)" controls (typed per param kind, debounced persist), and a **non-empty canvas preview** — `EditorCanvas`'s 2D text draw is now param-aware (reuses `resolveBoundValue`; parity when `componentParams` empty). Precedence wired: `def.param.default ◁ projectDefault` (instance override is B4). **⏳ Owner verify live.** **Gap to demonstrate B3/B4:** the existing `/components` entries are EMPTY shells (no params/text node) → preview still blank + "declares no params" hint; needs a real parametric `HudReadout` def seeded (the Component-Editor UI can't yet author `paramBindings` on a node) — natural B4 prep. **B4 ✅ SCOPED (owner-chose HYBRID 2026-06-08) → see `invisible-editor.md` §14.** Architecture finding: the HUD bottom bar is a **bespoke `LayoutEditable` renderer** decoupled from the engine path (3 separate coded `Label{Balance,Win,Bet}` snippets hardcoded to `stateBet.*`; not componentInstances, no value registry). HYBRID = `LayoutEditable` keeps its positioning but **mounts a `<ComponentInstance>`** of ONE shared `HudReadout` def for the 3 readouts (fed by `registerComponentValues({balance,win,bet})`), so B1/B2/B3 become the HUD's foundation + the Component-Editor-owns-it model holds (rejected: full engine rewrite = too risky; §12 `source`-prop = orphans B1/B2/B3). Phased B4.1 (def + registration — also makes B3 demonstrable) → B4.2 (value sources) → B4.3 (LayoutEditable mounts ComponentInstance) → B4.4 (convert 3 nodes + parity flip, retire coded `Label*`) → B4.5 (deliver per-project defaults to the standalone game — lean: launcher bakes defaults into served def `param.default`) → B4.6 (Borut mirror + republish). Parity-gated. **B4.1+B4.2 ✅ SHIPPED (`11f09df`):** `HUD_READOUT_DEF` built-in (`builtinComponents.ts`, surfaced to the Component Editor via a `componentStorage` built-in fallback → makes B3 demonstrable) + `apps/lines` registers it & the `balance`/`win`/`bet` value sources (`valueSource` runes adapter). **B4.3 ✅ SHIPPED (`8dc1476`):** `LayoutEditable` mounts a `componentInstance` HUD node via `<ComponentInstance space="standard">` at the same `hudPos()` Container — engine layout context confirmed live in the HUD tree. **B4.4 ✅ SHIPPED (`5143fd1`, owner-chose MOUNT path §14.3):** new coded `HudReadout.svelte` reuses `UiLabel` (keeps localized caption via the same `i18nDerived` selectors, font, per-source CURRENCY formatting, `svelte/motion` count-up, `LabelBet` tap-to-menu); `HUD_READOUT_DEF.root` is now a single `bind: HudReadout`; `hudBarScene({ readouts: true })` (opt-in, `apps/lines` only) emits `componentInstance(hudReadout)` for balance/win/bet; `LayoutEditable` `mounted()` gate stops the coded-snippet double-render; `Label*`/`UiLabel` KEPT for revert. **Reversible** (drop `{ readouts: true }`). **Borut untouched** (zero scene-set diff, gen-scene-sets check passed). **✅ VERIFIED LIVE (Claude, lines dev server 2026-06-08):** the `apps/lines` base-game HUD renders "Balance / $0.00" through the `HudReadout`→`UiLabel` mount — correct caption/font/currency, no console errors, no Svelte warnings (fixed one `state_referenced_locally` in `HudReadout`, `afa45b5`). - **Layout-doc bake (freeze doc + custom component defs at build): ✅ CAPABILITY BUILT (2026-06-09).** Closes the deploy gap behind the per-instance-graphics feature. (Background: a game's first load is NOT slowed by the data-driven layout — Svelte compiles at BUILD time; runtime just walks the ~12 KB JSON doc mounting already-compiled components, assets dominate first-paint, and component-library size is O(0) for games that don't reference a def. The gaps were deployment, not rendering: custom defs lived only in R2 / weren't registered at game runtime, and the doc was fetched live.) **Built (design: `docs/design/live-assets.md` → "Layout-doc bake"):** (1) `engine-layout` `collectComponentIds(nodes)` (pure walker, exported). (2) `GET /api/editor/doc?…&components=1` now also returns `componentDefs` = the transitive closure of referenced defs, resolved via `loadComponent` (built-in→shared→project precedence, so an edited `button` shadows the coded one) — opt-in so the runtime boot fetch stays lean. (3) `apps/launcher-api/scripts/bake-editor-doc.mjs` (sibling to `pull-project-assets.mjs`, fetch-only, `--optional`/`--dry-run`) writes `{ doc, componentDefaults, componentDefs }` to a game's `src/baked-editor-bundle.json`. (4) `apps/lines` wired as reference: `editor-scenes.ts` imports the bundle; a non-null `doc` flips to the **baked path** — `registerBakedComponents()` (after the built-in `registerComponents`, so baked defs shadow built-ins) + `loadEditorScenes()` returns the baked doc, skipping the live fetch. Self-describing (no env flag); checked-in placeholder has `doc: null` → un-baked repos keep fetching live, byte-identical parity. (5) `new-game.mjs` scaffolds a `bake:doc` script. **Builds GREEN:** `engine-layout` + `launcher-api` + `apps/lines` (`vite build`). **⏳ Remaining (owner / per-game):** wire `bake:doc` into a real shipped game's `build` (Book of Borut — its own repo, copy the `apps/lines` `editor-scenes.ts`/`Game.svelte` wiring + the placeholder JSON), run a live bake (needs `EDITOR_DOC_SECRET` + an authored doc), and verify a customized-Button background renders in the shipped bundle. Not browser-verified (endpoint is auth/token-gated). Same family as `gotcha_game_build_stale_engine_dist`.
- **Editor-art export — doc-referenced art reaches the build automatically ✅ CODE BUILT (2026-06-10).** Root cause of "sprite placed in the editor doesn't show in the published game": the editor renders sprites straight from R2 atlas MANIFESTS (`SpriteNode.assetKey` = manifest key), but a game resolves textures only from `assets.ts`-declared sheets — nothing exported doc-referenced atlases to `deploy/`, so they rendered as `Texture.EMPTY`. Built (design: `docs/design/live-assets.md` → "Editor-art export"): (1) `$lib/server/editorArtExport.ts` — walks doc + referenced defs (sprite manifest keys, image-param region names located among project atlases, standalone page images), writes per-atlas TexturePacker json-hash sheets (frames keyed by the editor REGION NAMES) + page copies + `index.json` under `deploy/editor-art/`, prunes stale, idempotent. (2) `POST /api/editor/export-art` (same `EDITOR_DOC_SECRET` gate as the doc endpoint). (3) `bake-editor-doc.mjs` triggers the export + embeds the index as `bundle.editorArt` (dry-run skips — it writes to R2). (4) `apps/lines`: `editor-scenes.ts#bakedEditorArtAssets()` → asset entries (`sprites` per sheet; `sprite` keyed by full assetKey for page drops); `stateApp.ts` spreads into `createApp`. Builds GREEN (launcher-api + lines). ⚠ Build-order per game: `bake:doc` BEFORE `pull:assets` (export writes deploy/, pull mirrors it). ⏳ Owner verify: republish Book of Borut from the desktop launcher → editor-placed sprites + instance image params render.
- **Editor-art region-name collisions — newly added sheet shadowed by an older one ✅ FIXED (2026-06-12, `engine:`).** Symptom (Book of Borut): a new sheet `atlas_manifest_S_StaticElements3D.json` "didn't reach the game" even though it deployed + registered fine (verified on disk + in the baked bundle). Root cause was NOT the pipeline: the engine loads every spritesheet into ONE flat `loadedAssets` map keyed by **bare frame name** (`assetLoad.ts` `sprites` → `rawAsset.textures`; `AssetsLoader` `reduce` = last-loaded-wins) and a sprite resolves by that bare name (`LayoutNodeView` `spriteKey = region ?? assetKey`). The 3D sheet is a remake of `S_StaticElements` that **reuses region names** (`T_Background`, `T_UI_Slots`, `T_UI_Bar`, `T_UI_Widget`); both are still doc-referenced, so both load, and the 2D sheet (registered last) overwrote the 3D textures — only `T_Background_Board` (unique to 3D) showed new art. Order-dependent → intermittent. **Fix (scope each editor-art sheet by its manifest):** (1) pixi-svelte `Asset.namespace` — a `sprites` asset now registers each frame BOTH bare and under `<namespace><frame>` (`assetLoad.ts`); `<Sprite>` gained `fallbackKey` (scoped key first, bare fallback). (2) engine-layout `editorArtKey.ts` (`isManifestAssetKey`/`editorArtNamespace`/`editorArtTextureKey`, exported) — `LayoutNodeView` resolves a manifest-backed sprite by `<assetKey>::<region>`, falling back to bare region (parity for game-bundled sheets + un-updated games). (3) per-game `editor-scenes.ts#bakedEditorArtAssets()` sets `namespace: editorArtNamespace(sheet.key)` (done in `apps/lines`; **each shipped game's copy must add the same one line** — fallbackKey means an un-updated game degrades to today's behaviour, no break). (4) guardrail: `editorArtExport.ts` now reports cross-sheet name `collisions` in `index.json` and `bake-editor-doc.mjs` prints a loud `⚠ region "X" exists in N sheets` so a stale superseded sheet is visible. Builds GREEN (pixi-svelte, engine-layout, apps/lines svelte-check 0 errors, launcher-api). ⏳ Per shipped game: bump the engine submodule, add the `namespace:` line to its `editor-scenes.ts`, rebuild + republish.
- **Scene Editor — composition-seeding reconciliation (§19), parity-safe core ✅ CODE BUILT (2026-06-12).** Owner report: the editor's "＋ Load a game scene…" picker is confusing (two unrelated groups) and the template concept "seems broken / behind Book of Borut." Root cause (design `invisible-editor.md` §19): a game's scenes were encoded TWICE — the rich **reference layout** (real composed game) vs the empty **`GameTemplate`** skeleton — and they drifted; new projects scaffolded from the EMPTY one while real games (Borut) were seeded from the RICH one, so "what you see on open" depended on which path created the project. **Owner direction (3 decisions):** reconcile concepts first; "template" should mean "pick a game KIND → get the correct screens + engine pieces for it"; fresh-project art = **ENGINE PIECES ONLY** (no artist art). **Model:** ONE source of truth per kind = the reference layout (slot-tagged); two COMPUTED projections — **import** (filled, as-is) + **scaffold** (`engineOwnedOnly()` = engine-owned nodes only). The empty-`GameTemplate` _seed_ path + the `skel:` menu group are retired; Template-MODE authoring stays for defining a brand-new kind. **Built (parity-safe, additive):** (1) `engine-layout` `engineOwnedOnly(doc)` (`src/lib/engineOwnedOnly.ts`, exported) — keeps any node with `bind`, `reelGrid`, `componentInstance`, + whole HUD scenes (`isHudScene`); drops plain artist `sprite`/`spine`/`text`; recurses plain containers (keep iff a child survives); every scene still emitted; input never mutated. Structural rule (no def lookup) — see §19.4 as-built note. Esbuild assertion harness `scripts/test-engine-owned-only.mjs` (22 assertions pass: basegame frame dropped → 0 nodes, bind/FS-counter/HUD retained). (2) launcher `projectScaffold.ts` seeds new projects from `engineOwnedOnly(getReferenceLayout(gameType)).scenes`, falling back to `seedScenesFromTemplate` when a kind has no reference layout; `objectExists` guard untouched (existing projects unaffected). (3) `/editor` `+page.svelte` dropdown collapsed: **New game from kind** (`scaffold:<type>` → `adoptScenes(engineOwnedOnly(ref), type)`) + **Import composed reference** (`ref:<type>`, filled), both driven off `listReferenceLayouts()`; `skel:` group removed; `loadChosen()` now sync; clobber-guard intact. Builds GREEN (`engine-layout`, `launcher-api`); code-reviewed (no blockers; one doc↔code wording nit resolved in §19.4). (4) **bookOf added to "New game from kind"** — the scaffold group is now sourced from `listFullSceneSets()` (lines + bookOf) via `getFullSceneSet`→`engineOwnedOnly`; `engineOwnedOnly(bookofReferenceLayout())` DROPS the two frame sprites (no `bind`) so §19.6's frame-atlas render problem doesn't arise, while keeping the reel grid + Win/Transition/FreeSpin/Loading/Background anchors + FS-counter instance + HUD. "Import composed reference" stays lines-only (filled bookOf import deferred, §19.6). `projectScaffold.ts` also switched `getReferenceLayout`→`getFullSceneSet` (covers a future bookOf project). Builds GREEN; test passes. **⏳ Owner verify online:** the picker now offers New lines + New bookOf (engine pieces) + Import Lines; a NEW project opens with the reel screen + HUD + anchors, no art; "New game from kind" into an existing project prompts the replace-confirm (`adoptScenes`) + stays a preview until an edit (non-destructive); existing projects + Borut unchanged. **Deferred (§19.8):** `game_type` column on projects (so "pick a kind" persists for non-lines — owner-applied migration, no `DATABASE_URL` for Claude — until then all new projects scaffold as lines via the `projectGameType` stub); `ways`/`cluster`/`scatter` scene sets (only lines+bookOf exist); project-aware _filled_ bookOf import (its board frame is a project-atlas FRAME, §19.6).
- **Scene Editor — per-project `game_type` (§19.8 unblock) + auto-migrate on boot ✅ CODE BUILT (2026-06-13).** Records which game KIND a project is so the editor scaffold / template resolution use the real kind instead of the `projectGameType` `'lines'` stub. **(a) Schema:** new nullable `projects.game_type` (migration `0010_dear_justice.sql` = `ALTER TABLE "projects" ADD COLUMN "game_type" text;`, additive). `projectGameType(key)` now reads + validates it (falls back to `'lines'`; transitional try/catch for the deploy window); `createProject` takes an optional kind; new `setProjectGameType`. Shared `GAME_KINDS`/`GameKind`/`isGameKind` in `roles.ts` (mirrors the editor `GAME_TYPES`). **(b) Admin UI:** a kind `<select>` on create-project + a per-project inline kind editor (so existing projects, e.g. `borut/bookofborut`, can be set to `bookOf`). **(c) Auto-migrate on boot (owner-chosen):** the launcher now applies pending Drizzle migrations ITSELF at server startup before serving — SvelteKit `init` hook → `runMigrations()` (`db/migrate.ts`, `postgres-js` programmatic migrator over the committed `drizzle/` folder). Fail-soft (logs + swallows; never crashes boot), idempotent/transactional. So pushing a migration + its schema-dependent code in ONE deploy is safe now and for all future migrations — **no manual `db:migrate` step** (it still works out-of-band). See `docs/INFRA.md` "Auto-migrate on boot". Builds GREEN. **INCIDENT (2026-06-13, RESOLVED):** the first auto-migrate boot DID fail — prod's `__drizzle_migrations` was empty (prod was provisioned via `db:push`), so the migrator replayed from `0000` → `relation "sessions" already exists` → aborted → `0010` never applied → authed routes 500'd. Fixed by BASELINING prod (NOT `db:migrate`, which replays the same): ran `ALTER TABLE "projects" ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS "game_type" text;` + inserted one `__drizzle_migrations` row at `created_at=1781337176046` (0010's `when`), via a one-off node script over the prod proxy URL in `apps/launcher-api/.env`. Site restored (`/`→303, `/login`→200); prod baselined through 0010 → future migrations apply cleanly. See `docs/INFRA.md` "Auto-migrate on boot" (migrator compares by `created_at` threshold, not per-hash). **⏳ Owner:** set `bookofborut`/`hotfruits` → bookOf (etc.) in `/admin` so their editor scaffold resolves to the right kind (all project kinds currently null → fall back to lines).

- **Unified Tool Bar — Phase 1 SHIPPED (2026-06-12, `launcher:`).** One consistent top bar across every tool: the Invisible emblem (→ launcher home), the current tool's name, and a horizontal switcher of every OTHER **online** tool the signed-in user can see (icon + text, icon-only when tight). Refinement vs the original design: every switcher link routes back through the launcher origin (`ORIGIN + /editor` etc.) instead of baking each Python sibling's secret — the launcher re-gates + forwards the secret/active project, so secrets stay server-side. **Phase 3 (the twin):** a vanilla-JS `.iw-toolbar` that reads `?home`/`?tools` and renders the emblem + brand + switcher (icon map mirrors `roles.ts` `TOOL_ICONS` by id) now lives in **(a) the Spine viewer** `static/spine/view.html` (canonical twin — VERIFIED live in preview: 8 buttons, correct order, brand `#7ee0c0`, current tool excluded, icon-only @1100px), **(b) Atlas Maker** `services/atlas-tool/ui_server.py` (`IW_TOOLBAR` const injected via a `{iw_toolbar}` slot to dodge the `.format()` brace trap; `py_compile` + 66 KB render-smoke GREEN), **(c) Sheet Maker** `services/sheet-tool/ui.html` (UTF-8/no-NUL OK). NOTE: editor panel widths moved to their own localStorage key (`iw-editor-panels:<project>`) — previously-saved widths in the old ui blob are ignored once (one-time reset to defaults).

Button = same blank root (author drops their own art) pre-declared with the `action` param (`ENGINE_ACTION_CATALOG` options) + category `ui`; a placed instance picks its action from the dropdown and the §18.4 engine hit surface makes the whole art clickable — no coded part, no manual param wiring. Owner-chosen direction: buttons stay COMPONENTS (not a scene-Library element / new node kind), reusing instancing, action feed, editor-art export and the bake.
- **§18.7 editor↔game HUD text fidelity ✅ FIXED (2026-06-10), launcher-only.** HUD readout caption/value font + vertical alignment differed between editor and game. Editor-side fixes: (1) added the games' Typekit kit (`use.typekit.net/aba0ebl.css`) to launcher `app.html` so the editor previews proxima-nova instead of a sans-serif fallback (+ `document.fonts.ready` redraw in EditorCanvas); (2) `drawHudChip`'s `style:'text'` chip now matches the coded `HudCaption`/`HudValue` — resolved fontFamily/fontSize, normal weight, top-anchored at the node origin (was hardcoded `600 30px sans-serif`, vertically centred). Coded parts remain an editor approximation; true WYSIWYG = author caption/value as textBox/text nodes (§18). No game republish. Design §18.7.
- **§18.6 ghost coded HUD buttons after replacing with custom components ✅ FIXED (2026-06-10).** Custom button components placed on the HUD bar left the old coded spin/turbo/± buttons rendering as duplicates: `LayoutEditable`'s `mounted(id)` guard only suppressed the coded snippet when an instance had the RESERVED id, but from-scratch/placed instances get random ids. Now also suppressed when a placed instance covers the snippet's ACTION (buttons) or SOURCE (readouts), id-independent. Parity-safe (seeds carry reserved ids → same result). Design §18.6.
- **§18.5 free author art on HUD scenes ✅ FIXED (2026-06-10).** A plain sprite/text/container dropped on a HUD scene (hudBar/hudCorners) showed in the editor but not the game: HUD scenes render through the bespoke `LayoutEditable` (not `<LayoutScene>`), which only positioned coded snippets by id + rendered `componentInstance` nodes — any other node was dropped. `LayoutEditable` now also renders every "free" node (non-reserved-id, non-componentInstance) via the engine `<LayoutNodeView>` in the scene's space (bar = standard inside MainContainer, corners = canvas), before the coded snippets. Parity-safe (live seed has none). No special component needed. Design §18.5.
- **§18.4 default hit surface for art-only components ✅ FIXED + VERIFIED (2026-06-10, engine `d87bb13`, Borut `2e50713`).** The owner's custom button (Component-Editor def: sprite + text, NO coded `ButtonFrame` part) published as a static image — all button interactivity lived in the coded part. `<ComponentInstance>` now wraps an action-bound, bind-less def in an interactive Container (whole art = hit area, pointer/not-allowed cursor, press → onpress unless disabled); defs WITH bind parts keep the coded part as sole press owner (no double-fire, parity). Verified in Borut dev against the real baked doc: cursors + bet +/− fires. ⏳ Owner: republish Borut from the desktop launcher to ship it.
- **§18 Text Box + universal text localization ✅ CODE BUILT (2026-06-10), engine+lines verified in browser.** ONE reusable `textBox` built-in (text param = literal OR localization key; optional `source` param feeds live engine values — strings render as text, numbers via the count-up readout path), engine text resolver (`registerTextResolver` + `LayoutNodeView` hook, parity when unregistered), `ValueSource` widened to `number|string`, and the Localization tool finally DELIVERS to games: `GET /api/localization/strings` (token-gated, reviewed-only) → baked into the bundle → merged into the Lingui catalog at boot. See `docs/design/invisible-editor.md` §18. ⏳ Remaining: Borut mirror, HUD-corner migration to textBox (needs `gameName`/`clock` string sources), editor key-picker.
- **Sheet Maker ↔ Atlas Maker round-trip — UNIFIED ✅ CODE BUILT (2026-06-10, `626d799`).** Symptom: a sheet packed in the Sheet Maker loaded in the Atlas Maker but "didn't match". Root cause: the Sheet Maker writes packed output to `<client>/<project>/sheets/<sheet>/…` (page PNG, `.atlas`, TP json) + loose inputs to `…/sheet_src/<sheet>/…`, but the Atlas Maker only ever read `input/…`, bridged by a fragile copy `_ingest_self_contained()` (sheets/ → input/refs/) that silently failed (empty `r2_prefix` at export, unticked atlas/TP boxes, already-rewritten paths, once-per-process cache after a container cache-clear). **Fix (Atlas-Maker-side only, no sheet-tool change):** resolve/hydrate/browse `sheets/` + `sheet_src/` natively and DELETE the copy. `cloud_paths.py`: both are lazy subtrees (pulled per-manifest, not eagerly — `sheet_src/` can be large). `batch_atlas.py`: `STAGING_ROOT` proxy + `_staging_rel()` (strips the `<client>/<project>/` R2 prefix — STAGING_ROOT is already project-rooted, so EVERY `STAGING_ROOT / x` must pass through it or you double the prefix); `_hydrate_from_r2_by_name` gains an exact-`r2_key` fetch + a basename scan over sheets/sheet_src; `atlas_file_path`/`source_image_candidates` add STAGING_ROOT candidates + read `source_image_path`. `ui_server.py`: `_resolve_region_ref` routes sheet refs through by-key hydrate; `_fsbrowse` re-rooted at STAGING_ROOT with an allowed-roots whitelist (input/sheets/sheet_src/atlas; legacy `refs/…` field values mapped to `input/…` so the picker opens at the field's folder instead of snapping to root); removed `_ingest_self_contained`, `_fetch_r2_into_input`, `_ingested_manifests` + the `load_manifest` guard. Three manifest shapes still resolve: (a) self-contained `source_image_path`=`sheets/…`, (b) legacy bare-basename, (c) already-ingested `refs/atlas/…`. Closes the same family as B27. `py_compile` clean, code-reviewed (no blockers). ⏳ Owner: deploy auto-runs from `main` (hydration change needs a container restart); smoke-test on Railway — pack a sheet with `.atlas` on → ↻ Refresh from R2 in the Atlas Maker → open the manifest → page + region boxes align; watch logs for `[atlas] lazy-pull sheets/ …`. (HTTP 403s behind the auth gate → verify via Railway logs / in-app, not curl.)
- **Atlas Maker — bulk "Refs → generated" seed ✅ CODE BUILT (2026-06-10, `6dc30da`).** The per-card "USE THIS IMAGE" action (`/userefimg`, bind a region's `shape_ref`/`style_ref` as its atlas tile via `output_override`, verbatim/no-AI) now has a bulk form so you can build/preview an atlas straight from the refs placed in the Sheet editor without running generation. (1) Toolbar button **"⤵ Refs → generated"** (`/userefall` → `_seed_refs_into_outputs`) fills every EMPTY region slot from its ref. (2) **Automatic** on activating a sheet-derived manifest (`export_prefix` truthy) — hooked in `_saveconfig` (the dropdown-activation handler) + the `_handle_deeplink` re-activate branch, so the dropdown-load round-trip seeds too, not just the "Open in Atlas Maker" deeplink (which already seeds from the page slice on fresh import). **Only-empty for both paths** (owner rule): `_region_has_output` skips a region that already has a committed override or a generated variant — and it `ensure_lazy("batch/")`-hydrates the variant pile first so a just-switched project's variants aren't misread as absent and clobbered. Sizing is safe (compose runs every override through `fit_to_region`). Reuses `output_override`/`_mirror`/`_drop_fx_snapshot`; one `save_manifest` per bulk op. `py_compile` clean, code-reviewed (hydration overwrite-risk caught + fixed, no blockers). ⏳ Owner: after redeploy, smoke-test — load a sheet-derived manifest from the Settings dropdown → empty cells auto-fill from refs; the toolbar button fills any remaining empties; existing generations are untouched.
- **Scene Editor mis-cropped re-packed atlases — stale page pick ✅ FIXED (2026-06-10, `7fd7cda`).** Symptom: after Sheet Maker re-pack → Atlas Maker compose+deploy, the editor cut every region wrong ("as if the .atlas is still the old one"). Root cause (verified against live R2, borut/bookofborut S_StaticElements): `findDeployedPage` stem-matched ANY image under `deploy/` — **including the editor's own `deploy/editor-art/` bake output** — ranked ".webp then largest", so a stale 855KB editor-art webp (baked at 10:22, before the re-pack) beat the fresh 840KB deployed page (14:22) by 14KB; new manifest rects then sliced old pixels. The editor never reads the `.atlas` (geometry = manifest `regions[]` only) and the Atlas Maker's compose only reaches R2 via Deploy — so the page pick is the whole game. Fix: shared `$lib/server/deployedPage.ts::pickDeployedPage()` — excludes `deploy/editor-art/` (circular as a display source), ranks lastModified desc → newest ~10s batch → prefer .webp → size desc; used by BOTH `editorRegions.findDeployedPage` and `spine.resolveSpinePageKeys` (had the identical bug). `listAllObjects` now returns `{key,size,lastModified}`. Also fixed: `RegionThumb`'s module-level page cache ignored "↻ Reload art" (sidebar thumbnails stayed stale all session) — now version-busted + cleared by `refreshAssets`. Build GREEN, reviewed (no blockers). ⏳ Owner: after Railway redeploys the launcher, Reload art / refresh the editor — regions should align. Note for later: a re-pack that changes geometry needs a manifest whose `regions[]` match the deployed page (true today: Sheet Maker writes both together, Atlas Maker composes per the same `.atlas`).
- **Editor: bad image-param bindings + advisory sprite slots ✅ (2026-06-10, `79526b4` + `3be2f94`).** (1) The borut `hudreadout` def bound BOTH sprite image fields (`assetKey` + `region`) to one `bg_image` param, shoving a region NAME into `assetKey` → the canvas fetched `/api/editor/regions?sheet=T_UI_Widget_Big` (403 from the scope guard) and placeholder'd forever. Def fixed directly in R2 (`editor/bookofborut/components/hudreadout.json`, stray `assetKey` binding removed); canvas hardened (non-key assetKeys skip the fetch; failed/missing sets fall through to the cross-atlas name index; Reload art clears the failed-key memo); the two image bind dropdowns in EditorProperties now clear each other when pointed at the same param. (2) Owner decision: **graphical (sprite) template slots are advisory, never `required`** — `boardFrame`/`boardFrameEdge` de-required in BOTH `lines` + `bookof` templates (an unfilled sprite slot renders nothing in-game; authors may replace slot art with free nodes). Mount slots (reelGrid etc.) untouched — they anchor engine-owned components and the in-scene anchors must not be deleted. No authored R2 template overrides exist, so the built-ins are live.
- **Atlas Maker CRT splash phrases restored ✅ (2026-06-10, `c023d7d`).** The MIN_SPLASH_MS refactor (removing the double-render probe) made the WORK phrase pool unreachable and froze the splash at a blinking cursor while `location.replace` waited on the cold `_index()` render. Now the splash background-fetches the real UI while the typewriter loops phrases, then document-swaps it in (`history.replaceState` keeps the `fast=1` reload contract; single server render kept; fetch failure → FATAL screen). Verified: py_compile + `node --check` on the exact served script. **Splash SHARED (2026-06-11, `32e6b6f`):** extracted to `iw_common.splash.splash_html(tool_name, phrases)`; Sheet Maker now serves it for `/` BEFORE `_resolve_context()` (cold visits previously blanked the tab while the eager `sheets/` hydration ran) with a packing-flavored pool; atlas behavior unchanged. Audit of other surfaces: Spine Viewer (static R2) + launcher pages (SvelteKit incremental) don't need it.
- **Atlas Maker stale-staging on manifest activation ✅ FIXED (2026-06-11, `091711f`).** Symptom: a Sheet Maker re-export (new sprite added; Save = full export to fixed R2 keys) never showed in the Atlas Maker. Three layers: the manifest dropdown lists/loads once-per-process staging copies (hydrate guard); `atlas_file_path` returned the stale staged `.atlas` and `merge_atlas_regions` (.atlas-authoritative) silently dropped the manifest-only region; and the activation auto-seed loaded the STALE staging manifest + mirrored it back to R2 — overwriting the fresh export (data loss; introduced with the refs→generated auto-seed). Fix: `_refresh_manifest_from_r2()` exact-key re-pulls manifest + bound sheets/ `.atlas` on every real manifest switch (dropdown + deeplink); `_saveconfig` saves the previous manifest only when an edit touched a manifest-backed field (`manifest_dirty`); `all_regions` banners+logs manifest regions missing from the bound `.atlas` instead of vanishing. ⚠ Owner: after the redeploy, re-Save the affected sheet once in the Sheet Maker (in case a stale manifest was already pushed back), then re-pick the manifest in the Atlas Maker.
- **Sheet Maker exports mis-routed to manifests/ ✅ FIXED (2026-06-11, `4551617`).** Follow-up to the stale-staging fix: the persistent "1 manifest region(s) missing from .atlas" banner was REAL — opening a sheet via its `atlas_manifest_*.json` set `loadedDir` = the manifest's parent dir (`source_dir`), Save reused it as `dest_dir`, and `_dest_output_dir` accepted anything under the staging root → the fresh page/.atlas/TP json landed at `manifests/<sheet>.*` in R2 while the manifest's hardcoded back-refs pointed at the stale `sheets/<sheet>/` files (verified live: 24-region `.atlas` at `manifests/S_StaticElements.atlas`, 23-region at the canonical key). Fix: `_dest_output_dir` confines custom destinations to the `sheets/` subtree; `api_export` derives `export_prefix`/back-refs from the ACTUAL output dir. ⚠ NOTE: commit `4551617` also unintentionally carried the owner's concurrent in-flight session-persistence work (`api_session`/`load_session` + api_state hook — additive, compiles, harmless if unused; owner aware). Strays `manifests/S_StaticElements.{png,atlas,json}` left in R2 — delete when convenient. Owner: after redeploy, re-Save the sheet once → re-pick the manifest in the Atlas Maker.
- **"Clear local cache" → TRUE reset of the active project (2026-06-11, CODE BUILT, not yet committed).** Symptom: a file deleted from R2 (launcher FTP browser / a separate container) lingered in a tool and kept showing/reading. Root cause: tools mirror R2 into a per-(client,project) staging subtree, but `pull_prefix` only adds/overwrites (never prunes) and listings come from the local staging glob — so deletions never propagate; the old "Clear local cache" only rmtree'd the ACTIVE project's regenerable subtrees (atlas `batch/`+`deploy/`, sheet `sheet_src/`), KEEPING manifests/atlas/input (atlas) and manifests/sheets/config (sheet), so it could never drop a deleted manifest/atlas/sheet for the project you were in. Fix: the active project now rmtrees the WHOLE per-project subtree (same whole-tree delete the inactive branch already did) then `hydrate(..., force=True)` to rebuild from R2 — manifests/config (+ sheet_session.json, sheets/) pull synchronously, input/atlas (atlas) background, batch/deploy/sheet_src lazily; lazy guards cleared. Net: active staging EXACTLY matches R2; anything deleted from R2 is gone. Guarded so rmtree only ever hits `STAGING_BASE/<client>/<project>`, never STAGING_BASE or a sibling. Sheet WIP `sheet_session.json` lives under the project subtree + mirrors to R2, so it drops then re-pulls from R2 (source of truth) — WIP intact. UI relabeled **↺ Reset from R2** with confirm "Reset this project from the cloud? Re-downloads everything from R2 and DROPS any local files that were deleted from the cloud. Unsaved local work is lost." Files: `services/atlas-tool/ui_server.py::_clearcache` + the embedded clearCache JS/button; `services/sheet-tool/sheet_server.py::api_clearcache` + `services/sheet-tool/ui.html`. `py_compile` clean. ⏳ Owner: redeploy (push to main) then smoke-test — delete a manifest from R2 via the launcher FTP browser → Reset from R2 in the tool → the dropdown no longer lists it.
- **Launcher v1.0.8 — Build & publish auto-shares project SETUP (2026-06-12, RELEASED).** Symptom: a "new system" project (`Projects/<client>/<key>`) failed Build & publish with `ERR_PNPM_NO_IMPORTER_MANIFEST_FOUND` — pnpm ran in an EMPTY folder. Root cause: the portal launcher-profile for `bookofborut` had no `repo` block, so Sync had nothing to `git clone` and just made an empty dir; the profile lacked the repo because the launcher only captured `repo.url` from the LOCAL git remote at the manual "Publish setup" click, and a freshly-synced folder isn't a checkout (chicken-and-egg). Fix (`C:\Invisible Wall SL\ComfyUI\Invisible_Launcher.py`): **Build & publish now also POSTs the machine-independent profile** (`repo.url`/`branch` from the build folder's real git remote + the `game.publish` block) to `/api/launcher/projects` — so every other launcher gets new/updated games on **↻ Sync from cloud** (auto-clone + build), no manual step. New `_project_setup_payload()` helper (cloud key doubles as portal project key); warns if no git remote was found. Built + uploaded via `py scripts/build-launcher-exe.py --upload`; `latest.json` = v1.0.8 (running launchers self-update). New repo script `apps/launcher-api/scripts/seed-project-profile.mjs` = headless/bootstrap way to register or repair a profile. **v1.0.9 (2026-06-12):** `build_game` now DEFAULTS the build command (`pnpm install && pnpm build`) and the standard game-build env (`PUBLIC_RGS_TRANSPORT=play4fun`; `PUBLIC_RGS_GAME=book` for book games) instead of erroring on an empty `build_cmd` — a project needs only key + name + protocol; explicit `publish.build_cmd`/`env` still override. **v1.0.10 (2026-06-12):** `build_game` preflights the JS toolchain (`_ensure_build_toolchain`) — a missing `pnpm`/Node now yields an actionable message (install Node ≥ 22.16.0 → `corepack enable` → restart launcher) instead of the raw shell "'pnpm' is not recognized…"; auto-runs `corepack enable && corepack prepare pnpm@10.5.0 --activate` when Node is present but pnpm isn't. **v1.0.11 (2026-06-12):** when Node is missing entirely, `install_node_portable()` now **auto-downloads a portable Node.js LTS** (newest ≥ 22.16.0; resolved from nodejs.org `index.json`) into `CONFIG_DIR/_tools/node`, activates it on the process PATH (`_activate_managed_node`) and enables pnpm via Corepack — no admin, no system PATH change, **no launcher restart** (the build subprocess inherits the patched env). Building stays a dev/owner action but is now fully hands-off on a bare machine. **v1.0.12 (2026-06-12):** `clone_project_repo` now calls `_ensure_git()` — when Git is missing it **auto-installs portable Git** (MinGit, from git-for-windows releases) into `CONFIG_DIR/_tools/git` and activates it on PATH, so Sync can clone the project repo on a machine that has neither Node nor Git. This fixes the "fetched 0" + empty `Projects/<client>/<key>` folder → `ERR_PNPM_NO_PKG_MANIFEST` symptom on bare machines. Also: the `bookofborut` portal profile was completed via a direct Postgres write (repo url was already there from the main-PC auto-share; build_cmd/env were blank). **v1.0.13 (2026-06-12):** Sync records per-project clone outcomes (cloned / no repo URL / already cloned / FAILED+reason) into a summary dialog and `%TEMP%/iw_sync.log` — a "fetched 0" is now explainable instead of the reason flashing past in the closing modal. **v1.0.14 (2026-06-12) — the actual root-cause fix:** the v1.0.13 report revealed `Book of Borut: FAILED — <lambda>() takes 1 positional argument but 2 were given`. The Sync click passed a one-arg progress callback (`lambda m:`) but the new Git/Node portable-installers call `progress(msg, frac)` (two-arg, like the build/tunnel paths) — so the moment Git auto-install started it threw, silently giving "fetched 0". Fix: Sync's callback is now `lambda m, frac=None:`. Portable Git now installs and the repo clones on a bare machine. **v1.0.15 (2026-06-12) — zero-login private-repo clone:** the game repos are PRIVATE, so a fresh machine got a GitHub login prompt on clone. New portal endpoint `GET /api/launcher/git-credentials` (bearer-auth, no role gate like `/projects`) serves a read-only GitHub token from `GIT_CLONE_TOKEN`; the launcher fetches it during Sync and clones via a command-line `url.<tokened>.insteadOf` rewrite that covers submodules and is never written to the repo (token scrubbed from errors). 404 when `GIT_CLONE_TOKEN` is unset → launcher falls back to interactive auth. **⚠ Owner one-time setup:** create a fine-grained read-only PAT (Contents: read on the org's game repos, incl. `Invisible-Engine` submodule) and set `GIT_CLONE_TOKEN` on the launcher Railway service. **v1.0.16 (2026-06-12):** the "Portal project key" (`publish.project`) is no longer required — `register_game` defaults it to `_project_key`/cloud key, and `_project_profile` always stamps `publish.project` into the shared profile so it propagates on Sync. Self-inflicted regression: the direct DB write that completed the `bookofborut` profile (v1.0.15 step) dropped the original `project` field → next Sync cleared the local field → `register_game` hard-errored "missing project key". DB profile re-fixed (project=bookofborut). ⏳ Owner: (1) **smoke-test the FROZEN exe** — run it, do one Build & publish (boto3-in-onefile DataNotFoundError only surfaces frozen); (2) one-time bootstrap for `bookofborut` (+`hotfruits`): run the seed OR Build & publish once from the real checkout, then Sync on other machines. Ensure the game repo's `main` is pushed (others clone `origin/main`).
- **Invisible Storybook engine reference PUBLISHED (2026-06-11):** 207 files / 73.7 MB at `_shared/storybook/engine/` via `publish-storybook.mjs --shared` — `/storybook` now has its first entry; Borut's storybook still to publish (needs `.storybook/` config or build-elsewhere + `--dir`).
- **sheet-tool project-file + B19:** rename/Save/Save As + blank/dup name guards, browser-tested live.
- **B12 project switch:** browser test an actual project switch + cross-project ref picking via `/fsbrowse`.
- **Spine Viewer (B21):** active-project propagation + named empty-state message live (data: run `r2-sync-spines.mjs` for projects meant to have spines — none synced for non-hotfruits yet).
- **Launcher beauty pass (B22):** authed home page not browser-tested.
- **Threaded-race concurrent smoke (fix #2):** two concurrent `?project=` requests for different projects → each writes only its own R2 prefix.
- **iw_common deploys (fixes #3/#7):** verify atlas-tool/sheet-tool/atlas-backend build + run after the Root-Directory flip.

**Invisible Launcher — canonical release + fresh-machine checklist (B31–B37):**

1. **Release the desktop launcher:** in the **`invisible-launcher`** repo, bump `LAUNCHER_VERSION` in `Invisible_Launcher.py` + commit/push, then `py build_and_publish.py` (builds from the repo's own copy, publishes the exe + `latest.json` to R2; running launchers self-update). Creds come from the saved launcher config or `R2_*` env. ⚠️ **Smoke-test the FROZEN exe, not source** — boto3-in-onefile can fail only when frozen (`DataNotFoundError`); after a build, run the exe and click a **Publish** button once. (The former engine-side `scripts/build-launcher-exe.py` was removed 2026-06-14 — it built from the `ComfyUI` clone, which drifted behind `main` and once re-shipped a stale version.)
2. **Seed the cloud (owner, main PC, once / when assets change):** in the launcher → **Publish to cloud** → enter **R2 credentials…** once → **Publish tunnel creds** + **Publish models** + **Publish nodes**. (CLI equivalents for the first two: `node apps/launcher-api/scripts/seed-tunnel-bundle.mjs`, `py scripts/seed-comfyui-models.py`, both with `R2_*` env.)
3. **Fresh machine (hands-off):** Install/Update ComfyUI → **Sync models** + **Sync custom nodes** (sign in → downloads + pip-installs node deps) → **Start tunnel** (auto-installs cloudflared, sign in → fetches creds) → **restart ComfyUI** → generate.

**Open backlog (B-items):**

- **B23 — launcher CLIENT/PROJECT selector broken** ✅ DONE (2026-05-31). Rebuilt the two-step selector in `(app)/+page.svelte` (no schema change — client stays derivable). The four root causes are gone: (A) the re-seeding `$effect` that clobbered the user's pick on every `invalidateAll` now re-seeds ONLY when the server's active project actually changes (gated by a `syncedActive` sentinel) — never mid-edit; (B) **CLIENT is now a pure local filter** — `onClientChange` only re-filters the PROJECT list (keeps the project selected if it belongs to the chosen client, else blanks it), it no longer auto-submits `?/setProject`; (C) committing happens ONLY on an explicit PROJECT change (`onProjectChange` submits on a real change); a blank state shows a disabled "— choose a project —" placeholder so a cross-client browse forces an explicit pick instead of silently snapping; (D) dropped the `await tick()` papering (and the now-unused `tick` import + `activeProject` derived) since the options no longer change out from under the submit. Server `?/setProject` (validates `canAccessProject`) + the layout's accessible-list fallback to `cloud` are unchanged — that fallback is now benign (client=Unassigned, project=cloud, stable) rather than triggering a reshuffle. `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN. ⏳ Not browser-tested live (authed page) — verify: pick a project → it sticks across reloads; switch CLIENT → only the project list refilters (active unchanged until you pick); pick a project under the new client → commits + sticks.
- **B24 — restore back/forward navigation between tools (+ back to launcher)** ✅ DONE (2026-05-31). Both launcher redirects (`(app)/atlas/+page.server.ts`, `(app)/sheet/+page.server.ts`) now append `home=<ENV.ORIGIN>` + a `sibling=<full other-tool URL>` (the sibling carries its OWN `k`+`client`+`project`, URL-encoded) — **but only when the user actually has the sibling tool** (`tools.some(t => t.id === …)`), so the link can't bypass the role gate (the tools' own gate is just the shared secret). Sheet Maker (`services/sheet-tool/ui.html`): added an "Atlas Maker →" anchor (`#siblinglink`, hidden until `init()` reads `?sibling=`); the `← Launcher` back-link already read `?home=`. Atlas Maker (`services/atlas-tool/ui_server.py` `_index` header): added `← Launcher` (`#homelink`) + "Sheet Maker →" (`#siblinglink`) anchors (hidden by default) + a small JS reader of `?home=`/`?sibling=` (back-link falls back to `LAUNCHER_URL` env, default `app.invisiblewall.org`); `LAUNCHER_URL` added to `atlas-tool/.env.example`. The "Atlas Maker linked ✓" status pill is kept separate (it's honest config status, not a link). **Verified:** `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN; atlas `py -m py_compile` OK **and** a real `_index()` render-smoke (44 KB HTML, no `.format()` brace error) confirms both links + the JS readers are present; sheet `ui.html` UTF-8/no-NUL. ⏳ Not browser-tested live (needs the launcher→tool redirect + cross-origin hop). NOTE: the sibling link's client/project are baked at redirect time — switching project _inside_ a tool makes the sibling link point at the prior project until you return via the launcher (acceptable; the launcher is the source of truth for the active project).
- **B25 extraction (the real payoff)** ⏳ pending: progressively extract genuinely-shared UI into `packages/components-*` (start `<R2Browser>` from `ftpScope.ts`+`api/files`, `<DataTable>` for admin/localization) + the launcher gate/scope consolidation. Keep `docs/ui-inventory.md` updated. (`reuse-check` skill + `docs/ui-inventory.md` infra is ✅ DONE.)
- **B26 — tool output/staging not reachable from the FTP Browser** ⏳ TODO (needs investigation): sheet-tool output (e.g. `/data/sheet-tool/invisible_wall/test1/output`) + atlas-tool outputs aren't visible in `/files`. **Key distinction:** `/data/<tool>/...` is the tool's **container-local staging**; the FTP Browser only browses **R2** (`<tool>/<client>/<project>/` via `ftpScope.allowedPrefixes`). So an output is FTP-visible only if **mirrored to R2** under `sheet_maker|atlas_maker/<client>/<project>/output/`.
