Likely root: outputs aren't (fully) written through to R2 (atlas-tool's raw `output/` is intentionally not mirrored; sheet-tool may only `_mirror` some paths) → they live only on container disk. **Decision:** (1) write outputs through to R2 so the existing FTP browser shows them (preferred, no new UI) vs (2) add a "browse tool staging" mode for the `/data` disk (heavier, ephemeral). Confirm what's actually missing from R2 first. Files: `lib/server/ftpScope.ts`, `api/files/list`, `services/{atlas-tool,sheet-tool}` mirror/`push_dir` logic.
- **B27 — Atlas Maker can't generate: Sheet-authored manifest's `atlas.atlas_file` is a raw Windows path, not an R2 key** ✅ DONE (`f04fb26`, 2026-05-31). **Root cause = the geometry resolver, fixed.** `batch_atlas.atlas_file_path()` treated a Windows path (`C:\…\symbols.atlas`) as relative — `Path(ref).is_absolute()` is `False` on Linux — and joined it onto `INPUT_DIR` verbatim → `/data/.../input/C:/.../symbols.atlas` (the exact error); its `p.name` fallbacks were dead because POSIX `Path.name` of a backslash string is the whole string. Fix: normalise separators, derive the true basename (`Path(ref.replace('\\','/').lstrip('/')).name`), try `INPUT_DIR/refs/atlas/<name>` (where B10 Upload .atlas + the B14 ingest write) + bare-name candidates — the same robustness `source_image_candidates()` already had for the page image; a genuine container-absolute path that exists still wins; the not-found fallback now reports `refs/atlas/<name>` so the message names a real, fixable location. Also tightened `ui_server._ingest_self_contained._looks_like_r2_key` to reject Windows drive paths (`^[A-Za-z]:/`) so ingest no longer wastes an R2 fetch on a local path. **Verified:** `py -m py_compile` clean (both files) + a pathlib unit-check proving the Windows path now resolves to `refs/atlas/symbols.atlas` instead of `input/C:/…`. **Scope note (honest):** part (a) "sheet side always emit `atlas_file` as an R2 key" was NOT needed — B14 already emits the R2 key on cloud libGDX export; the broken manifests were authored OFFLINE (no R2 prefix, so no key derivable), and the resolver fix (b) is exactly what rescues those. ⏳ Not live-tested: load such a manifest → Upload .atlas (or rely on ingest) → generate. The `unassigned/cloud` vs real-project context point (cf. B23) is separate and unchanged.
- **B29 — seed Borut/HotFruits into a real launcher project** 🚧 in progress (2026-06-01). Created the **Borut** client + **hotfruits** + **book_of_borut** projects via `/admin` (underscore key for Book of Borut to dodge the Python `_safe_proj_name` hyphen-rewrite — see note below); the launcher now hands the atlas tool `?client=borut&project=hotfruits` (verified live: the tool's SESSION bar reads `hotfruits`, not `unassigned/cloud`). To populate the empty project, **fixed `services/atlas-tool/seed_r2.py`** (it predated `<client>/<project>` isolation — hardcoded the wrong `atlas_maker/cloud/<proj>` prefix). It now: (1) targets `atlas_maker/<ATLAS_CLIENT>/<ATLAS_PROJECT>` (defaults `borut`/`hotfruits`), keeping a valid slug key VERBATIM; (2) **localizes atlas-bound manifests** — a manifest authored offline carries a Windows path in `atlas.atlas_file`/`source_image` (the B27 error case); the seed now copies the referenced `.atlas` + its page image from the game repo into `input/refs/atlas/` and rewrites the manifest to the relative `refs/atlas/<name>` key the cloud resolver expects; (3) also drops each tool-folder `.atlas` into `input/refs/atlas/` so manifests with a bare relative `atlas_file` (symbols2/3/transition → `symbols2.atlas`) resolve too. **Verified:** `py -m py_compile` clean + a DRY-RUN against the real local manifests proving symbols/anticipation/buy*button/loader/mmBG get their Windows paths rewritten and the real `.atlas`+PNG bytes read off disk (e.g. `symbols.atlas` 2937 B + `symbols.png` 1.06 MB), while symbolsStatic/symbols2/3/transition are correctly left as-is. ⏳ NOT yet run against live R2 / not Create-Atlas-tested: run `py seed_r2.py` (R2 creds in env) on the box with the local tool, restart atlas-tool (or ↻ Refresh from R2), then Create Atlas on `symbols`. **Slug bug noted (not yet fixed):** the launcher allows hyphens in keys but the Python tools run keys through `_safe_proj_name` (→ `*`), so a hyphenated launcher key (`book-of-borut`) would diverge from the tool's path (`book_of_borut`); using underscore keys sidesteps it. Worth a follow-up fix in `services/\_shared/iw_common/context.py`+ both`cloud_paths.py`.

- **B30 — launcher ComfyUI card pointed at the wrong ComfyUI** ✅ DONE (2026-06-01). The `comfyui` local-tool card (`apps/launcher-api/src/lib/roles.ts`) linked download → `comfy.org/download` (ComfyUI **Desktop**), a different product from the `ComfyUI_windows_portable` build the pipeline actually uses (tunnel `comfy.invisiblewall.org → localhost:8188`, started by the desktop `Invisible_Launcher.py` which hardcodes `…\ComfyUI_windows_portable\ComfyUI`). Repointed download → `github.com/comfyanonymous/ComfyUI/releases/latest` (the portable `.7z`) + reworded the steps (portable not Desktop; extract with 7-Zip; save the extracted ComfyUI folder path). The install-path **recording** already works (saved per-user to the `toolInstalls` table via `?/saveInstallPath`, retrievable with `getInstallPaths(userId)`) — no new code; it's available to surface as an env value later. `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN. **Open follow-up — auto-install:** the **web** launcher can't install to a user's disk (browser sandbox); the natural place for an automated download+extract+register-path is the **desktop `Invisible_Launcher.py`** (already manages the portable ComfyUI). Today new users still install manually. Also worth wiring: have the desktop launcher read the recorded per-user path instead of hardcoding it.

- **B31 — ComfyUI auto-install + Invisible Launcher as the distributed desktop app** 🚧 mostly done (2026-06-01, follow-up to B30). Replaces the manual ComfyUI install with a one-click flow and turns the desktop launcher into a downloadable `.exe`. **(a) Desktop `Invisible_Launcher.py`** (outside the repo, at `C:\Invisible Wall SL\ComfyUI\`): added `install_comfyui()` + `latest_comfyui_asset()`/`_select_portable_asset()` — resolves the latest **Windows portable** build from the GitHub releases API, downloads it (urllib, stdlib, with % progress + reuses an already-downloaded archive), extracts with the **real 7-Zip engine** (`_ensure_7z()` prefers an installed `7z.exe`, else auto-downloads the standalone `7zr.exe` from 7-zip.org), backs up any existing install to `…_portable.bak`, and writes `comfyui_dir`/`python_exe` into config. **NOTE:** py7zr was tried first but **cannot decode the BCJ2 filter** the ComfyUI portable `.7z` uses ("BCJ2 filter is not supported by py7zr") — hence the 7-Zip CLI. Added a **ComfyUI control card** on the Launcher tab (status + **Install / Update ComfyUI** + **Start**, both on worker threads via `_ui_after`). Made `LOGO_FILE` frozen-aware (`_MEIPASS`) so the bundled exe finds the emblem. **Verified:** `py -m py_compile` clean + live `latest_comfyui_asset()` resolves `ComfyUI_windows_portable_nvidia.7z` (2.01 GB); full download/extract NOT run. **(b) Web launcher** (`apps/launcher-api`, via launcher-studio agent): removed the `comfyui` tool entirely; added a `invisibleLauncher` **local** tool (rocket icon, `download: '/download/launcher'`); new auth-gated route `src/routes/(app)/download/launcher/+server.ts` **streams** the exe from R2 key `tools/invisible-launcher/Invisible_Launcher.exe` (presign skipped — `@aws-sdk/s3-request-presigner` isn't a dep; streams via existing `getObjectBytes`, 404 until uploaded). `TOOL_DOC_SLUG` updated (`invisibleLauncher → invisible-launcher`). `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN. **(c) Build pipeline:** `scripts/build-launcher-exe.py` — PyInstaller `--onefile --noconsole`, renders an `.ico` from the emblem, `--collect-all customtkinter` + `--collect-submodules py7zr`, optional `--upload` to R2 (boto3). NOTE: drop the `#!/usr/bin/env python` shebang in repo Python scripts — the Windows `py` launcher honors it and delegates to the broken Store `python` stub. **(d) Docs:** new `docs/tools/invisible-launcher.md`; `docs/tools/comfyui.md` updated to the auto-install flow. The live launcher was committed + pushed to `Invisible-Wall-SL/invisible-launcher` (`5d8a1e4`). **⏳ OPEN (owner):** (1) **upload the `.exe` to R2** — owner runs `py scripts/build-launcher-exe.py --upload` with `R2_*` env set; until then `/download/launcher` 404s; (2) **live-test** the exe on a clean machine: download from the portal → Install / Update ComfyUI → Start → Start tunnel; (3) consider streaming vs presigned for the ~big exe (Node loads it fully into memory per download — fine for a few internal users); (4) optional: have the desktop launcher read the per-user recorded install path (B30) instead of the fixed `C:\Invisible Wall SL\ComfyUI` location.

- **B32 — Invisible Launcher self-update** ✅ DONE (2026-06-01, follow-up to B31). The desktop launcher can now update itself in place. **(a) Web (`apps/launcher-api`, launcher-studio agent):** two **open** routes (outside the `(app)` auth gate, since the desktop app has no portal session) — `GET /api/launcher/latest` (returns the R2 manifest `tools/invisible-launcher/latest.json`) and `GET /api/launcher/download` (streams the exe). The portal card's `download` was repointed to `/api/launcher/download` and the old authed `(app)/download/launcher` route deleted (one path now). Build GREEN. **(b) Desktop launcher:** `LAUNCHER_VERSION` constant + a **Check for updates** button in the header. `check_launcher_update()` polls `/api/launcher/latest`; `is_newer()` compares dotted versions via tuple (so 1.2.10 > 1.2.9). If newer + frozen, `download_launcher_update()` pulls the new exe to `%TEMP%` and verifies sha256, then the **swap-on-restart** dance: `apply_update_and_restart()` relaunches the new exe with `--apply-update <oldpath>` and `os._exit(0)`s to release the lock; the new exe's `_run_apply_update()` copies itself over the old path (retrying while locked) and relaunches. From source (not frozen) it tells the user to `git pull`. **(c) Build script** `scripts/build-launcher-exe.py`: reads `LAUNCHER_VERSION` from source, and on `--upload` publishes `latest.json` (`{version, size, sha256, notes}`) alongside the exe; added `--notes`. **Verified:** both .py compile clean; version-compare unit-checked (1.0.1>1.0.0 True, 1.0.0>1.0.0 False, v0.9>1.0.0 False, v1.2.10→(1,2,10)); exe rebuilt 31 MB; web build GREEN. **Release flow:** bump `LAUNCHER_VERSION` → `py scripts/build-launcher-exe.py --upload`. ✅ Live-tested end-to-end (owner, 2026-06-01): version bump → build/upload → in-place self-update swap.

- **B33 — fresh-ComfyUI-install hardening + install progress bar** ✅ DONE (2026-06-01, follow-up to B31). After the BCJ2 fix, a fresh portable install couldn't START: (1) the launcher starts ComfyUI with `--base-directory …\Shared`, which makes ComfyUI resolve `custom_nodes` under `Shared\custom_nodes` — the prestartup `os.listdir()` crashed with FileNotFoundError because the seed list omitted it. Fixed `_build_comfyui_bat` to also seed `custom_nodes` + `models`. (2) `--enable-manager` needs ComfyUI-Manager's deps; `install_comfyui` now runs the portable python to `pip install -r ComfyUI/manager_requirements.txt` post-extract (non-fatal). **Progress UX:** the install `progress` callback now passes `(msg, frac)`; the ComfyUI card shows a `CTkProgressBar` (determinate % during download, indeterminate while extracting/installing), shown during install and hidden after. **Verified:** `py -m py_compile` clean + introspection confirms the seeds, the bar helpers, and the `(msg, frac)` signature; exe rebuilt. ✅ Live-tested: fresh install → Start on a clean machine (owner, 2026-06-01). **Overwrite/reinstall fix (same day):** renaming `ComfyUI_windows_portable` → `.bak` failed with WinError 5 (Access denied) because a leftover ComfyUI console holds the folder as its cwd. Dropped the rename entirely — `install_comfyui` now stops ComfyUI (`_free_port(8188)`) and extracts **over the folder in place** (`7z x … -y -aoa`), which sidesteps the directory-lock; the error message now tells the user to close ComfyUI windows + retry.

- **B34 — Atlas geometry "not found in R2" (B27 follow-up: R2 auto-hydrate + thread-context fix)** ✅ DONE (2026-06-01). The B27 resolver (`f04fb26`) correctly collapsed a Windows `atlas.atlas_file` to its basename but only probed **local staging** — so a legacy/offline-authored manifest whose `.atlas` lives only in R2 still errored ("Atlas geometry not found in R2: C:\…\symbols.atlas"). Two root causes fixed: **(1) reader never looked in R2** and **(2) the run resolved under `unassigned/cloud`, not `borut/hotfruits`** (the context point B27 flagged as "separate and unchanged"). Changes: **(a)** `batch_atlas.py` — new `_hydrate_from_r2_by_name()` pulls geometry/page files from the active project's R2 tree by basename (the two `seed_r2.py` locations: `{prefix}/input/refs/atlas/<name>` + `{prefix}/manifests/<name>`) into staging before the not-found fallback; wired into `atlas_file_path()` + `source_image_candidates()` (list-return contract preserved); module-level `import storage`. `slice_atlas.py` inherits it via the shared helpers. **(b)** `ui_server.py` — `/render` + `/createatlas` spawn **worker threads**, and (client, project) context is **thread-local**, so workers fell back to env defaults; now ctx is captured on the request thread and re-applied via `project_paths.set_context(*ctx)` inside `run_render`/`run_compose` (slice/upload already ran synchronously, so were fine). **(c)** `atlas_writers.build_manifest` — `atlas.source_image` is reduced to basename so a local OS/Windows path can never enter a new manifest (`source_image_path` R2 key stays the resolvable field). **Verified:** `py -m py_compile` clean (5 files) + code-review clean (no blocking/should-fix). The manual **Upload .atlas** (B10) flow is untouched as a fallback; auto-hydrate only fires when nothing is on disk. ✅ Live-tested (owner, 2026-06-01): manifest under a real project → Create Atlas / slice with no manual upload. **Deploy note:** restart the Railway service after deploy — staging only hydrates net-new R2 objects on demand via this path.

- **B35 — automate ComfyUI-tunnel setup on a fresh machine** ✅ DONE (2026-06-01, live-tested by owner). On a new box the tunnel couldn't start because (1) `cloudflared.exe` wasn't installed and (2) `~/.cloudflared/{config.yml,<id>.json,cert.pem}` (the tunnel credentials) were absent. The desktop **Invisible Launcher** now closes both gaps; the credentials are fetched from the portal (owner login), never bundled in the exe. **(a) Web (`apps/launcher-api`, launcher-studio agent):** `POST /api/launcher/login` (OPEN — desktop has no session; JSON `{email,password}` → **short-lived 10-min** session bearer token; reuses `verifyCredentials`+`createSession`; generic 401, `no-store`) and `GET /api/launcher/tunnel-bundle` (`Authorization: Bearer`, `validateSession` + **role `admin` only**, returns the bundle JSON from R2 key `tools/invisible-launcher/cloudflared-bundle.json`; 401/403/404; `no-store`, never logs secrets). Seed script `scripts/seed-tunnel-bundle.mjs` (owner runs once with `R2_*` env — reads the 3 local files, prints only byte counts, PUTs `{tunnel_id,config_yml,credentials_json,cert_pem}`). Build GREEN. **(b) Desktop `Invisible_Launcher.py`** (outside repo, `C:\Invisible Wall SL\ComfyUI\`): `cloudflared_exe()` resolver (system → PATH → launcher-managed `_tools/cloudflared.exe`); `install_cloudflared()` downloads the official standalone exe (no admin/MSI) with % progress; `portal_login()`/`fetch_tunnel_bundle()`/`write_tunnel_bundle()` (writes the 3 files, **repoints `credentials-file:` to the local path** — injects one if the seeded config lacks it — so it can't inherit the authoring box's home dir); refactored `start_cloudflared()`+`_build_cloudflared_bat(exe)`; `_start_tunnel()` now does ensure-install → login modal (`_prompt_portal_login`) → fetch → write → start on a worker thread (Tk-safe via `_ui_after`). The login token is used immediately + discarded (not persisted). **Code-reviewed:** safe-to-ship verdict; the two should-fixes (short-TTL token, credentials-file no-op) are applied. `py -m py_compile` clean. **✅ OWNER STEPS done (2026-06-01):** bundle seeded, exe shipped, and live-tested on a clean machine (Start tunnel → installs cloudflared → sign-in → fetches creds → tunnel up). **Follow-up (security, ✅ DONE in B38):** the open `/api/launcher/login` had no rate-limiting (at parity with the existing web `/login`, which also had none) — a shared per-IP/email throttle now covers BOTH.

- **B36 — automate ComfyUI model-file provisioning on a fresh machine** ✅ DONE (2026-06-01, live-tested by owner). Completes the fresh-machine story (B31–B33 ComfyUI binary, B35 tunnel creds): a new box's `Shared\Models` is empty → ComfyUI sees no checkpoints/LoRAs → generation preflight fails ("(none installed)"). Models are now mirrored to R2 and pulled by the launcher. **(a) Web (`apps/launcher-api`, launcher-studio agent):** `GET /api/launcher/models-manifest` (`Authorization: Bearer` + role `admin`; reads R2 `tools/invisible-launcher/models-manifest.json`; for each model validates `key` is under `base_prefix/` then adds a **6-h presigned R2 GET url**; returns manifest + `expires_in`; 401/403/404; `no-store`, never logs). Added `presignGet(key, ttl)` to `r2.ts` + dep `@aws-sdk/s3-request-presigner@3.1057.0`. **(b) Seed script `scripts/seed-comfyui-models.py`** (owner, boto3, `R2_*` env): walks `Shared\Models`, uploads each model to R2 `comfyui-models/<relpath>` (multipart; **idempotent** — skips files already in R2 at same size, reuses sha from prior manifest to avoid re-hashing tens of GB), writes the manifest. **(c) Desktop `Invisible_Launcher.py`** (outside repo, v**1.0.2**): `fetch_models_manifest()` + `sync_models()` + `_download_resumable()` (HTTP-Range resume to `.part`, restart on 200-not-206, sha256 verify, atomic replace, **legible message on expired presigned URL** — `.part` kept so re-click resumes); a "ComfyUI Models" card with **Sync models** button + progress bar; reuses the B35 login modal. Downloads go **R2→client directly** (presigned) so the multi-GB data never transits Railway. **Code-reviewed:** safe-to-ship; should-fix #1 (legible expiry error) applied. `py -m py_compile` clean (launcher + seed); `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN. **✅ OWNER STEPS done (2026-06-01):** models seeded, exe shipped, and Sync verified on a fresh machine (Sync models → downloads → restart ComfyUI → generate). Note: `pnpm-lock.yaml` in this commit also carries incidental dedupe churn beyond the one dep add.

- **B37 — "Publish to cloud" button (owner seeds R2 from the launcher UI)** ✅ DONE (2026-06-01, follow-up to B35/B36; live-tested by owner). The fetch side (download from R2) was automated; publishing (uploading THIS machine's local assets INTO R2) still meant running the two `scripts/seed-*` CLIs. Now the desktop launcher does it from the UI. **(a) Desktop `Invisible_Launcher.py`** (outside repo, v**1.0.3**): a "Publish to cloud · owner only" card with **R2 credentials…** (modal → saved to local `comfyui_manager_config.json`, secret fields masked; endpoint+bucket pre-filled non-secret defaults), **Publish tunnel creds**, **Publish models** buttons + status/progress. New fns `r2_creds()`/`_r2_client()` (boto3), `publish_tunnel_bundle()` (mirrors `seed-tunnel-bundle.mjs`), `publish_models()` (mirrors `seed-comfyui-models.py` — walk `Shared\Models`, skip-by-size, sha-reuse, multipart `upload_file`, write manifest). R2 **write** creds are entered by the owner (env-or-config), never bundled in the exe. **(b) `scripts/build-launcher-exe.py`** (in repo): bundles boto3 into the frozen exe (`--collect-all boto3 botocore` — the data files frozen boto3 needs, not just submodules). **Code-reviewed:** the one blocking bug (dead `_set_r2_credentials` → `_prompt_r2_credentials`) + should-fixes (boto3 collect flag, stale 404 text now points to the Publish card, progress final tick) all applied. `py -m py_compile` clean (launcher + build script). **✅ Frozen-exe smoke-tested (owner, 2026-06-01)** — boto3 works frozen; v1.0.3 shipped, R2 creds entered, and Publish tunnel creds + Publish models run from the main box (replaces the CLI seed steps). **Drift risk (reviewer, STILL OPEN):** the manifest/bundle JSON shapes now have THREE definitions — launcher `publish_*`, the `scripts/seed-*`, and the TS endpoint — with no shared schema; keep them in lockstep if `version`/fields change.

- **B38 — brute-force throttle on the password-login surfaces** ✅ DONE (2026-06-01, follow-up to B35). Both password-login surfaces — the open `POST /api/launcher/login` (desktop tunnel-setup, B35) and the web `/login` form action — accepted unlimited email+password guesses. Added a **shared** throttle used by both so behavior is identical. **(a) `src/lib/server/loginThrottle.ts`** — counts failures independently per **client IP** and per **target email**; a lockout on EITHER scope blocks the request. Policy is **asymmetric** by design: **IP** = 10 free attempts, then exponential backoff (1 min doubling, capped at **1 h**), 1 h decay; **email** = 5 free attempts, backoff (30 s doubling) capped at **15 min**, 30 min decay. The short email cap deliberately bounds the **account-lockout DoS** (a remote attacker who knows an email — possibly the owner's — but not the password could otherwise freeze that login): worst case is a brief, self-healing delay, not a sustained denial. Residual accepted risk: repeated short delays on a known email can't be fully eliminated without CAPTCHA/PoW (out of scope). A scope decays to zero when quiet; a successful login clears both. `checkLoginThrottle()` runs BEFORE the scrypt verify (returns `{blocked, retryAfterSeconds}`); `recordLoginFailure()` after a bad verify; `recordLoginSuccess()` after a good one. Every DB call **fails open** (logs + allows) so a DB hiccup can't lock the whole team out. **(b) Storage = Postgres `login_attempts` table** (`key` PK = `ip:<addr>`|`email:<addr>`, `failures`, `locked_until`, `updated_at`) via Drizzle — migration `drizzle/0006_blushing_snowbird.sql`. Chosen over an in-memory map because the launcher restarts on every Railway deploy and may scale horizontally (in-memory would silently reset/shard the counter). Tradeoffs (both acceptable at this scale): a read-modify-write under high concurrency can lose an increment (only ever _under_-counts, never over-locks); and rows are deleted only on success, so never-succeeding scanner IPs accumulate slowly — the decay logic ignores their stale counts, so it's housekeeping, not correctness (prune rows older than the decay window if the table grows). **(c) Both endpoints** now take `getClientAddress()` and reject with **429** + generic message (`Retry-After` header on the JSON endpoint). The JSON login still returns `{token, expiresAt, role}` on success — desktop contract unchanged. **⚠️ Requires Railway env `ADDRESS_HEADER=x-forwarded-for` + `XFF_DEPTH=1`** so `getClientAddress()` (adapter-node) returns the real client IP behind Railway's proxy — without them the per-IP bucket collapses to one global counter (per-email bucket still works). See `docs/INFRA.md`. **Build GREEN** (`pnpm --filter launcher-api build`). **✅ SHIPPED + LIVE-VERIFIED (2026-06-01):** committed `ab9c9df`, deployed; owner set `ADDRESS_HEADER`/`XFF_DEPTH`; the `login_attempts` table is created in prod with the correct shape and all existing tables/PKs verified intact. Probed live `POST /api/launcher/login` with a throwaway email — 5 free attempts return 401, then 429 + a counting-down `Retry-After`, exactly as designed. **⚠️ Migration gotcha (for next time):** `db:push` against prod errors part-way with `42P16 column "id" is in a primary key` — there's a **pre-existing schema drift** (the live DB differs from `schema.ts` such that push wants to drop/recreate a PK constraint). It created `login_attempts` first (additive, succeeded) then refused the destructive ALTER, so prod is intact — but **don't re-run `db:push`** (it retries that destructive op every time). Hand-apply the generated SQL or use `db:migrate` for future schema changes until the drift is investigated and fixed.

- **B38 — custom-node provisioning on a fresh machine (file-sync)** 🚧 code done (2026-06-01, follow-up to B36/B37). A fresh ComfyUI has no custom nodes → workflows die on a missing node (`RMBG`, `CannyEdgePreprocessor`, IP-Adapter…). The needed packs are **Comfy-registry installs, not git repos** (folders w/ `pyproject` version, no remote/commit), and **cm-cli is fragile headless** (needs `COMFYUI_PATH`+network, was unresponsive in test, confused by the `--base-directory Shared` split) — so a Manager-snapshot restore was rejected in favour of **file-sync** (the proven models pattern). **(a) Web (`apps/launcher-api`, launcher-studio agent):** `GET /api/launcher/comfyui-nodes` (Bearer + role `admin`; reads R2 `tools/invisible-launcher/comfyui-nodes.json`; presigns each node's `zip_key` (6 h); 401/403/404; `no-store`). Factored a shared `presignManifestEntries(entries, keyField, basePrefix, ttl)` helper in `r2.ts` now used by BOTH this and models-manifest (models contract unchanged). **(b) Desktop `Invisible_Launcher.py`** (outside repo, v**1.0.4**): `publish_custom_nodes()` zips each pack under `Shared\Custom_Nodes` (excl `ComfyUI-Manager`/`.git`/`__pycache__`/`.github`/`ckpts`) → R2 `comfyui-nodes/<name>.zip` + manifest; `sync_custom_nodes()` downloads (presigned, resumable) → `extractall` → pip-installs each `requirements.txt` with the portable Python (**surfaces nodes whose deps fail** so a half-installed pack isn't silently reported OK); "ComfyUI Custom Nodes" card (**Sync custom nodes**) + **Publish nodes** button on the publish card. **Code-reviewed:** safe-to-ship; both should-fixes (surface pip failures; exclude `ckpts` weight cache from zips) + the zip-slip trust note applied. Compiles under Python 3.9; `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN. **⏳ OWNER:** ship v1.0.4; on the main box **Publish nodes**; on a fresh machine **Sync custom nodes** → restart ComfyUI. **Note:** node-internal weight caches (`ckpts`) are excluded — nodes re-download those on first use (or seed them via Models if they live under `Shared\Models`).

- **B39 — Invisible Test Server (run the games from any machine)** 🚧 code done + locally verified (2026-06-01). The launcher's Games section only ever _linked_ to a game URL; nothing hosted the games, so they couldn't be launched. Built `services/test-server/` — one small Node service that (1) serves each game's built bundle under `GET /<gameKey>/` and (2) hosts a per-game **mock Play4Fun RGS** under `/api/<gameKey>/rgs/engine`, so a game has a backend to spin against (fake money, no real spend). **One server serves ALL games.** Bundles + a `test_server/games.json` manifest (gameKey → `lines`|`book` protocol + name) live in **R2** (`test_server/<gameKey>/…`); the service **hydrates from R2 on boot** (and on secret-gated `POST /refresh`) — same R2-is-source-of-truth model as the other tools. A local-dev mode (`TEST_SERVER_LOCAL=<dir>`) hydrates from disk (no R2 creds) — **not** used on Railway. **Reused, not duplicated:** the two existing mock servers (`scripts/mock-rgs-server.mjs`, `…-book.mjs`) were refactored into a `createMockRgs(opts)` factory (per-instance session store + RNG; path-suffix routing so the handler works mounted under `/api/<gameKey>`); their standalone CLIs are preserved behind an `isMainModule` guard. Publish flow: `apps/launcher-api/scripts/publish-game-bundle.mjs <gameKey> <buildDir> [--protocol] [--name]` (R2 write creds) uploads the whole `build/` tree + updates the manifest. Games are registered in `/admin → Games` with a launch URL `https://games.invisiblewall.org/<gameKey>/?sessionID=demo&rgs_url=games.invisiblewall.org/api/<gameKey>&lang=en&currency=USD&device=desktop` (launcher appends `&project=`). Key build fact: the games' static build emits **relative** asset paths (SvelteKit runtime base = `new URL('.')`), so subpath serving needs no rebuild. **Verified locally (owner machine, 2026-06-01):** built `lines` (play4fun), served via the test server in local mode — `/healthz` OK, bundle + nested `_app/assets` files serve (200), full mock round (heartbeat→bet→play→collect, balance accounting) correct via curl, and **the game booted + rendered in a real browser tab and its auth heartbeat reached the mock through `/api/hotfruits/rgs/engine` with zero console errors**. ⏳ NOT yet done: get a visible spin to render on the production static bundle (the stale `lines` demo showed boot+connect but the reels didn't appear past the intro — likely a game-build nuance, re-check with a fresh build of the actual target game). **⏳ OWNER:** (1) create the Railway service (Root Dir = repo root, Dockerfile Path = `services/test-server/Dockerfile`; envs `R2_*`, optional `TEST_SERVER_SECRET`); (2) DNS `games.invisiblewall.org` → the service; (3) build + `publish-game-bundle.mjs` for Hot Fruits (and Book of Borut from its own repo, `--protocol book`); (4) add the `/admin → Games` rows. Docs: `docs/tools/test-server.md`.

- **B40 — "Build & publish game" one-click in the desktop launcher** 🚧 code done (2026-06-01, follow-up to B39). Turns the manual 3-step publish (build → `publish-game-bundle.mjs` → add `/admin` row) into a single button. **(a) Web (`apps/launcher-api`):** new open route `POST /api/launcher/register-game` (Bearer → `validateSession` + role `admin`; body `{key,name,url}`; validates key via `isValidGameKey` + `url` is https; **upserts** the `games` row via `gameExists`/`setGameUrl`/`renameGame`/`createGame`; `no-store`, never logs) — same bearer pattern as `comfyui-nodes`. No schema change. `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN. **(b) Desktop `Invisible_Launcher.py`** (external repo, v**1.0.5**): extended a project's `game` with an optional `publish` block (`key`, `name`, `protocol` lines|book, `build_cwd`, `build_cmd`, `build_out`, `env`); added module fns `build_game` (runs `pnpm` via subprocess, captures output, raises with tail on failure / missing pnpm), `publish_game` (uploads `build/` → R2 `test_server/<key>/` + MERGES `games.json`), `refresh_test_server` (best-effort `POST /refresh`), `register_game` (calls the web endpoint with a portal token), reusing `r2_creds`/`_r2_client`/`_resolve_service_cwd`/`portal_login`/`_prompt_portal_login`. UI: a "Cloud publish" section in `ProjectDialog` (the editable registry) + a **☁ Build & publish** button on the Projects toolbar that runs build→publish→refresh→register on a worker thread behind a progress modal. `py -3.9 -m py_compile` clean. **(c) Manifest contract** documented once in `docs/tools/test-server.md` (shape `{games:{<key>:{protocol,name,updatedAt}}}`, merge-not-clobber) + referenced from both producers. **⏳ OWNER:** (1) merge + deploy the web endpoint to `main`; (2) ship launcher v1.0.5 (`py scripts/build-launcher-exe.py --upload`, smoke-test the FROZEN exe — boto3 frozen quirk); (3) fill a project's Cloud-publish fields + click Build & publish; verify the game lands on the test server AND a row appears in `/admin → Games`. Note: the build half needs Node+pnpm+the game repo (owner/dev boxes only).

- **B41 — structured diagnostics ("what / why / how to fix") for the Atlas Maker** 🚧 code done + validated (2026-06-02). Replaces the unstructured feedback (raw subprocess stdout dumped into `<pre id="log">`; bare one-line toast strings) with explain-only diagnostic cards. Motivated by the recurring confusion where Render does "nothing" — e.g. selecting only `output_override` regions (clover/clover_glow/clover_shadow) prints "Nothing to generate (all selected regions use a user image)" with no guidance. **(a) Shared framework `services/_shared/iw_common/diagnostics.py`** (stdlib-only): `diag(code, catalog, **ctx)` → `{code,severity,title,explain,fix}` (never raises — unknown code / missing placeholder → safe generic dict); `canonical(d)` (uniform `glyph title / why / → Fix:` string); `emit(d)` prints the canonical line **plus** a machine-readable `@@DIAG@@<json>` line; `parse_diag_line()`. Importable via the same `iw_common` PYTHONPATH shim as `storage`/`cloud_paths`. **(b) Atlas catalog `services/atlas-tool/diag_catalog.py`** — owner-facing copy for 24 codes across missing-file / wrong-setting / external-service (ComfyUI auth+payment hint text folded in from the old batch_atlas inline strings). **(c) `batch_atlas.py`** — converted the failure/skip prints + ComfyUI raises (auth/payment/node/timeout — emit then raise, non-zero exit preserved) to `emit(diag(...))`. **Latent bug fixed:** the generation override-skip partition counted a region as "uses my image" on `output_override` key truthiness ALONE, never checking the file exists — so a stale key (file deleted) blocked a region from ever generating. Now `override_image_path(region) is not None` gates the skip; a missing-file override emits `OUTPUT_OVERRIDE_FILE_MISSING` (warn) and falls through to normal generation. `ALL_REGIONS_OVERRIDDEN` is emitted exactly once at the partition (the final no-jobs guard no longer re-emits it after the locked filter — that was a false-positive for the mixed override+locked case). **(d) `ui_server.py`** — `_render_state` gains a `diagnostics` list (reset per run); `_run_cmd` pulls `@@DIAG@@` lines out of the stream into it (stripped from the visible log; `/progress` serializes it for free); sync-handler failure strings routed through `_diag()` (catalog wording) — no dispatch-contract change. UI: a `#diags` panel + `renderDiagnostics`/`flashDiag` JS (card text via `textContent` — XSS-safe) + `.diag-error/.warn/.info` CSS, all inside the `PAGE.format()` string with **braces doubled**. **Validated:** `py -m py_compile` clean (4 files); real `PAGE.format()` render-smoke (balanced braces, `#diags`/`renderDiagnostics`/`flashDiag` present); unit checks (unknown code + missing placeholder don't raise, `parse_diag_line` round-trips); **code-reviewed** (no blockers; both should-fix/nit applied). Scope decision: framework is shared; only the Atlas Maker is wired this pass — **Sheet Maker (`services/sheet-tool`) still uses bare-string feedback and can adopt the same framework + its own catalog later.** **FX-aware extension (same session):** the generic "all regions use your own image" dead-end was the owner hitting an FX-layer misunderstanding — `<base>_glow`/`_shadow`/`_shine` (also `_blur`/`_zoom`) regions are **derived locally from their base** (PIL, no AI, no ref) and carry an `output_override` once built, so the AI pass skips them _by design_. Added a canonical, dependency-free classifier `shine.fx_layer_info(name)` (+ `FX_SUFFIX_MODE` map) — the **shared FX-naming primitive** intended to also back future _automated FX_ (e.g. auto-rebuild FX layers when their base regenerates). `batch_atlas.py` now splits the skipped/overridden set into **FX layers vs genuine user images**: FX layers emit a new `FX_LAYERS_SKIPPED` (info) card explaining they're base-derived and giving two paths (regenerate base → ⚙ build the FX → Create Atlas; OR switch the layer to AI mode → Render); `ALL_REGIONS_OVERRIDDEN` now fires only when _every_ skipped region is a genuine user image. Catalog is now 25 codes. Validated: `py -m py_compile` clean (5 files); `fx_layer_info`/`diag` unit checks + a 3-bucket partition trace (all-FX / mixed / all-user-image) pass. ⏳ NOT yet committed/deployed or browser-tested live.

- **B42 — automated FX rebuild** 🚧 code done + validated (2026-06-02), `services/atlas-tool/ui_server.py` only. Builds on B41's `shine.fx_layer_info`: when a base region's image changes, its FX layers (`<base>_glow`/`_shadow`/`_shine`/`_blur`/`_zoom`) auto-rebuild so they stay in sync — **(a)** after a Render completes, for layers whose base was just rendered; **(b)** before Create Atlas, for ALL FX layers — instead of the user clicking ⚙ per layer. **One shared code path (house rule):** extracted the FX build core out of `Handler._fxbuild` into three module-level fns — `fx_source(m, name)` (resolution order unchanged; `Handler._fx_source` is now a thin delegate), `build_fx_region(m, name, mode=None, payload=None) -> (ok, msg)` (snapshot-freeze + param-merge/clamp + make\_* dispatch + save PNG + set region fields, verbatim from the old `_fxbuild`; differences: operates on the passed `m`, defaults `mode` to the region's stored mode, **now `_mirror`s the useroutput PNG to R2** (was a persistence bug — manual builds never mirrored either), and does NOT `save_manifest` — caller saves), and `rebuild_fx_layers(m, base_names=None) -> list[str]` (batch driver). `Handler._fxbuild` is now a thin wrapper (validate mode → `build_fx_region` → `save_manifest` if ok → return msg) — **manual build behaviour is byte-for-byte preserved** (same success/failure `_diag` strings) and **always runs regardless of the toggle**. **Selection/order/toggle:** `rebuild_fx_layers` reads `config['auto_fx_rebuild']` via `batch_atlas._truthy(..., default=True)` so a MISSING key = ON; candidates = regions where `fx_layer_info(name)` truthy AND base region exists AND stored `mode` in `shine.FX_PRESETS` (a *configured\* FX layer), filtered to `base_names` when given (render) or all (compose); ordered ascending by FX-suffix depth so `clover_glow` rebuilds before a nested `clover_glow_shadow`; each build wrapped in try/except (one failure never aborts the rest). **Hooks:** `_run_cmd` gained `post_hook=`/`pre_note=` — `run_render` passes a `post_hook` that runs `rebuild_fx_layers(m, base_names=set(names))` + saves + surfaces "Auto-rebuilt N FX layer(s)…" in the render log (client `refreshCards()` then shows updated tiles); `run_compose` runs `rebuild_fx_layers(m, base_names=None)` BEFORE building the cmd (best-effort try/except, never blocks compose) and passes the summary as `pre_note`. **Global setting:** added `("auto_fx_rebuild", "Auto-rebuild FX layers on render/compose", "text")` to `CONFIG_FIELDS` + `"auto_fx_rebuild": "on"` to `DEFAULT_CONFIG`; `_control_html` renders it as an on/off `<select>` (missing/blank → ON). `colour` mode has no suffix (recolour-in-place) so `fx_layer_info` never matches it — correctly excluded from auto-rebuild. **Validated:** `py -m py_compile ui_server.py batch_atlas.py shine.py` clean; offline render-smoke of the real `_index()`→`PAGE.format()` path (102 KB HTML, new Settings label + `data-cfg="auto_fx_rebuild"` present, no brace error); unit harness proving candidate selection picks `{clover_glow, clover_shadow}` for `base_names={'clover'}` (excludes `apple`, orphan-base layers, and non-preset-mode layers), returns `[]` when toggle off, defaults ON on missing key, and orders nested layers by depth. ⏳ NOT yet committed/deployed or browser-tested live.

- **B43 — Invisible Blueprints (shareable ComfyUI workflows)** 🚧 **ALL 8 PHASES CODE-COMPLETE + COMMITTED (2026-06-15 reconciliation); only owner-side LIVE verifies + the phase-7 docs tail remain.** Backbone phases 2-3 + Atlas Maker UI loop phases 5-6 + publish-gating half of 7 (`f00ffd5`, 2026-06-06); exposed params phase 8 (`a3bf684`, committed 2026-06-14); model auto-download phases 1+4 (`dc3f647`, committed 2026-06-14, LIVE verify owed). **Phase 5 (pick & generate) code-audited end-to-end 2026-06-15** — the picker is the `Pipeline` `<select>` (Built-in + Blueprints optgroups) in the `⚙ Global settings` panel + each card's `⚙` advanced popup; global selection → `atlas_config.json cfg["pipeline"]`, per-region override → `region["pipeline"]`; `region_pipeline()` → `run_region` dispatch → `build_workflow_blueprint`. Confirmed sound (the design-doc checklist was just never ticked; now corrected). NB `pipeline` is NOT in `PER_ATLAS_KEYS` — an earlier doc draft mis-stated that. Phase 7 fully closed 2026-06-15 (gating `f00ffd5` + launcher `blueprintPublish`/`toolScope.includeBlueprints` + the ComfyUI-Manager prerequisite note now in `docs/INFRA.md` "ComfyUI tunnel"). **Only remaining work = owner-side LIVE verifies** (needs the local GPU/tunnel): live generate THROUGH a blueprint id; live catalog-model auto-install+reboot; an exposed-param override reaching the KSampler. Designed 2026-06-04. Upload/share ComfyUI "blueprints" (a saved generation network) in the pipeline so everyone can see + use them, with the models a blueprint needs **auto-downloaded into the local ComfyUI** (via ComfyUI-Manager's install API over the existing tunnel), and a **blueprint picker in the Atlas Maker** so "generate" runs the chosen network. Full plan in **`docs/design/invisible-blueprints.md`**. Locked decisions: model install = ComfyUI-Manager API; authoring = export ComfyUI **API-format** JSON + a **binding step** (map roles→node IDs, since today's graphs are hardcoded Python with fixed node IDs); scope = one **global `_shared/blueprints/`** library visible to everyone (mirrors `_shared/spines/`). 7-phase build plan; **phase 1 is a spike** to pin the ComfyUI-Manager download/reboot endpoints (the one external unknown + the only cloud→local-filesystem boundary crossing).
- **B44 — games no longer land "global" on publish (project scope required)** ✅ DONE + shipped (2026-06-06). Bug: the GAMES section showed games from EVERY project under any client/project selection (e.g. both Book of Borut and Hotfruits appeared under Borut/HotFruits). Root cause was DATA, not the filter — `listGamesForProject` correctly returns `projectKey = NULL` (global) OR `= active`, but the auto-publish path (`POST /api/launcher/register-game`, called by the desktop launcher's ☁ Build & publish) treated a missing `project` as global, so every published game landed `NULL`. Nothing is hardcoded for `book_of_borut`. **Fix — three parts, all code done:** **(a) Web (`apps/launcher-api/src/routes/api/launcher/register-game/+server.ts`):** `project` is now REQUIRED — missing → 400 `Missing project`, unknown → 400 `Unknown project`; a global game is only creatable deliberately via `/admin → Games`. **(b) `/admin → Games` Create form (`admin/+page.svelte`):** the project `<select>` now defaults to the currently-active project (`data.activeProjectKey`) instead of "Global", so manual creation can't silently drift global either; the explicit "Global (all projects)" option is still selectable on purpose. `pnpm --filter launcher-api build` GREEN. **(c) Desktop `Invisible_Launcher.py` (external repo, bumped v1.0.5 → v1.0.6):** added a "Portal project key" field to a project's Cloud-publish block (`pub_project_var` + UI row + saved as `publish.project`); `register_game` now sends `{key,name,url,project}` and refuses to publish with a clear message if the field is empty; the HTTP error handler surfaces the endpoint's 400 reason (e.g. unknown project → hints at the underscore/hyphen slug gotcha). `py -m py_compile` clean. **CLOSED OUT (2026-06-06):** (1) ✅ web change committed + pushed to `main` (`8b5fb6f`), auto-deployed by Railway; (2) ✅ owner scoped the live `hotfruits`/`bookofborut` rows via `/admin → Games → Save scope` — symptom cleared; (3) ✅ launcher **v1.0.6** built + uploaded to R2 (`tools/invisible-launcher/Invisible_Launcher.exe` + `latest.json`, sha256 `8eeea28e15d5…`) and committed to the `invisible-launcher` repo (`3e0e12d`). Remaining per-game step is routine: fill the new "Portal project key" on each game's Cloud-publish settings before the next ☁ Build & publish.

  - **Backbone shipped (phases 2-3, BUILT-IN pipelines only) — `services/atlas-tool/`, code in working tree (NOT committed):** the data-driven pipeline path is in place and a 4th pipeline is now **two dropped files, zero Python change**. **(a) Loader `blueprints.py`** — hydrates the global `_shared/blueprints/` R2 prefix into staging (once per process, incremental, best-effort like the rest of cloud_paths), parses + validates `blueprint.json` (required roles `positive`/`seed`/`output` mapped), exposes `list_blueprints()` / `get_blueprint(id) -> {graph, bindings, meta}` (+ `validate_against_graph` for uploads). Self-contained, no secrets. **(b) Generic runner `batch_atlas.build_workflow_blueprint(region, style, blueprint) -> (wf, output_node_id)`** — deep-copies the API-format graph and applies the bindings (§2): positive/negative/seed via the SAME `_resolve_text` the sdxl branch uses, width/height, the `style_ref`/`shape_ref` LoadImage `image` fields (so the existing `_upload_workflow_refs` uploads them unchanged + `shape_ref` is normalized), and the output SaveImage `filename_prefix` = the project prefix. **(c) Dispatch + output generalization in `run_region`** — when `region_pipeline(region)` ∉ `{sdxl,flux,gpt_image}` AND a blueprint with that id exists, route to the generic runner; the hardcoded SaveImage node read `"17"` is generalized to `bindings.output.node` (defaults `"17"` for the built-in Python builders). The proven sdxl/flux/gpt path stays the default and behaves identically when no blueprint is selected — ZERO regression. **(d) Seeded 3 built-ins** as reference blueprints + templates under `blueprints_src/{sdxl,flux,gpt_image}/` (tracked source; API-format `workflow.json` + `blueprint.json` with correct bindings, derived faithfully from the builders / the old `flux_pipeline.blueprint.json`); `seed_blueprints.py` mirrors them to R2 `_shared/blueprints/`. The 3 stray UI-format `*.workflow.json` exports were folded into the dirs as `workflow.ui.json` (human authoring source, not uploaded); the tracked `flux_pipeline.blueprint.json` was deleted (folded into `blueprints_src/flux/`). **(e) README** `blueprints_src/README.md` documents the "drop `<id>/workflow.json` (API format) + `<id>/blueprint.json` (bindings)" flow + the bindings schema. **Validated:** `py -m py_compile *.py` clean; a throwaway self-test loaded the seeded `sdxl` blueprint, ran `build_workflow_blueprint` with a sample region, and asserted the prompt landed in node 10, seed in node 13, the style_ref filename on the LoadImage, output node = `"17"`, save_prefix = `<C>/<P>/batch/h1` (prefix resolved LIVE via the thread-local context). **Owner-side verify owed:** live ComfyUI generation through a blueprint (needs the local GPU/tunnel; gpt_image also needs `COMFY_ORG_API_KEY` + the `Images to RGB` node) — NOT testable from here. **Owner steps to activate:** `py seed_blueprints.py` (R2 creds in env) then RESTART atlas-tool so it hydrates. **Deferred at backbone time, NOW DONE (UI loop, 2026-06-06):** phase 5/6 (pick + upload UI) and the tool half of phase 7 (publish gating) — see the "UI loop" note below. **Still deferred:** only phase 1/4 = ComfyUI-Manager model auto-download spike (the `models[]` in `blueprint.json` is read-only metadata today; an uploaded blueprint's models must already be installed in the local ComfyUI). The phase 5/6 UI loop + the phase-7 publish gating (tool + launcher) all landed 2026-06-06 — see the "UI loop" + "Publish-gating" notes below.

  - **UI loop shipped (`services/atlas-tool/`, committed `f00ffd5`; launcher side `bc98bb9`+`9c9ef0f`) — 2026-06-06:** **(1) Pipeline picker lists blueprints** — `_pipeline_options_html(current)` renders the `pipeline` `<select>` as two `<optgroup>`s ("Built-in" = sdxl/flux/gpt_image, "Blueprints" = every shared blueprint whose id ISN'T one of the three keywords, so the built-in reference blueprints aren't double-listed and a built-in keyword keeps the proven Python path per `run_region` dispatch). Current value always kept selectable even if its blueprint vanished. Saves through the existing `data-cfg="pipeline"` → `/saveconfig` path (no save change). **(2) `/refresh` re-hydrates blueprints** — `_refresh()` now also calls `blueprints.hydrate(force=True)` (best-effort) so a seeded/uploaded blueprint appears after the existing "↻ Refresh from R2" button, no restart. **(3) In-browser upload + bind (`/uploadblueprint`)** — a "🧩 Blueprints" settings panel lists the library + (when allowed) a "＋ New blueprint" button opening a modal: pick an API-format `workflow.json`, parse in-browser, candidate-filter nodes per role by `class_type`/inputs (CLIPTextEncode→positive/negative; `seed`/`noise_seed` input→seed; width/height inputs→size; LoadImage→refs; SaveImage→output), enter name/description/base, POST. Server `Handler._uploadblueprint(payload)` slugs the name via `project_paths.r2_slug` → `<id>`, refuses built-in ids (sdxl/flux/gpt_image), warns on overwrite without `overwrite:true`, validates the graph is API-format JSON with required roles (positive/seed/output) bound to existing nodes (`blueprints.validate_against_graph`), writes `blueprint.json` (version/id/name/description/author/createdAt/base/bindings/`models:[]`; author = `IW_USER_NAME` env else "uploaded") + `workflow.json` to BOTH `BLUEPRINTS_STAGING/<id>/` AND R2 `_shared/blueprints/<id>/` directly via `storage.put` (NOT the per-route project push — blueprints are outside the project tree, like `seed_blueprints.py`), then `blueprints.hydrate(force=True)`. Returns a readable string on every path (never a 500). **(4) Per-blueprint options** — when the selected pipeline is a blueprint, `pipeVisible(g,p)` hides the built-in model `<select>`s (the sdxl/flux/gpt_image data-pipe groups) while keeping the universal + generic "both" generation fields (size/padding) editable, and the per-region Advanced ref fields (`style_ref`/`shape_ref`) show only when the active blueprint binds that role (`BP_BOUND_ROLES` map injected from `list_blueprints`+`get_blueprint`). **Publish-gating (phase-7 tool+launcher half) DONE + HARDENED (2026-06-06, post-review):** gated by KNOWLEDGE OF A SECRET, not a forgeable flag. The first review build used a bare `bp=1`, but every atlas user already holds the read secret `?k=`, so a constant flag gated nothing — anyone could self-publish/overwrite shared blueprints. Now a shared **`ATLAS_BLUEPRINT_SECRET`** (env on BOTH launcher + atlas-tool): the launcher (`atlas/+page.server.ts`) appends **`bp=<ATLAS_BLUEPRINT_SECRET>`** to the `/atlas` redirect ONLY when the user holds `blueprintPublish` AND the secret is set; the tool's `_resolve_publish()` requires param/cookie to EQUAL the secret (sets cookie `atlas_bp=<secret>`, same attrs as `atlas_tool`). When the secret is UNSET it falls back to dev-only (`not ATLAS_TOOL_SECRET` = local-open, or the explicit `ATLAS_BLUEPRINT_PUBLISH` env) — **fail safe: publishing is OFF in a deployed tool until the secret is set on both services.** `/uploadblueprint` also caps the body (~4 MB) + returns a readable error (never 500) on a malformed envelope. Refuses with a clear message when `can_publish` is false; the "＋ New blueprint" UI is hidden when false. **OWNER STEP ✅ DONE (2026-06-06):** `ATLAS_BLUEPRINT_SECRET` set as a Railway shared variable on BOTH launcher-api + atlas-tool, so publishing is live in prod (grant `blueprintPublish` per role in `/admin → Roles` to authorize publishers). **Launcher side also landed (phase 7):** `roles.ts` `blueprintPublish` capability (admin ON, else OFF; grant/revoke in `/admin` Roles matrix), `toolScope.includeBlueprints` read-allow for `_shared/blueprints/`. **Validated:** `py -m py_compile *.py` clean; `PAGE.format()` render-smoke (template parses, `BP_BOUND_ROLES`/"New blueprint" present, no brace error); a self-test of `_uploadblueprint` (offline, `storage.put` stubbed) asserting a valid graph+bindings with `can_publish=True` writes both files + `get_blueprint(id)` then loads it, `can_publish=False` refuses, built-in id refused, duplicate-without-overwrite warns, bad-node binding rejected with the node id, `overwrite=True` succeeds; plus `_pipeline_options_html` dedupe of built-ins from the Blueprints optgroup. **Owner-side verify owed:** the rendered HTML in a real browser + live ComfyUI generation through an uploaded blueprint (local GPU/tunnel) — not testable here.

  - **Phase 8 — blueprint exposed params / "general settings" (`services/atlas-tool/`, code in working tree, NOT committed) — 2026-06-14:** strictly-ADDITIVE follow-on so a blueprint can expose its OWN tunable knobs (steps/cfg/sampler/denoise/any node input) beyond the fixed semantic roles. A blueprint declares an OPTIONAL `params[]` array; each param carries a baked DEFAULT (the "general setting"), renders as an editable control in the Settings panel when that blueprint is the active pipeline, and the generic runner sets its effective value (per-manifest override else default) onto the bound node input. **Schema** (each entry): `{key, label, type (int|float|text|bool|select), default, min?, max?, step?, options?, node, field, group?}`. **(a) `blueprints.py`** — `_validate_params` parses/normalizes `params[]` (defaults `[]`); `validate_against_graph` checks each param's `node`/`field` EXISTS in the graph; rejects a key colliding with a binding role / another param key, and a param targeting a (node, field) already driven by a binding (no double-drive). `get_blueprint`/`list_blueprints` surface `params`. **(b) `batch_atlas.build_workflow_blueprint(region, style, blueprint, overrides=None)`** — after applying bindings, sets each param's effective value (override-if-present-else-default, coerced by `_coerce_param` to the declared type) onto `wf[node].inputs[field]` via `_set_node_input`; `run_region` threads the module global `BP_PARAM_OVERRIDES`, resolved in `main()` from `manifest.settings.bpParams[<activeBlueprint>]`. A blueprint with NO params, and the built-in Python paths, are byte-identical (overrides empty → defaults only → graph's baked values untouched). **(c) Authoring UI (`ui_server.py`)** — the "＋ New blueprint" modal grows an "Exposed settings" step (`addBpParam`/`collectBpParams`): pick a node + scalar/enum input filtered to inputs NOT already bound as a role, choose type/label/default/bounds/options; `_uploadblueprint` validates + persists `params[]` into `blueprint.json` (staging + R2). **(d) Generate UI** — `renderBpParams(pipe)` renders the active blueprint's params as number/text/checkbox/select controls in a new "🎛 Blueprint settings" panel (re-rendered on pipeline change); `saveBpParams` posts `{bpParams:{<id>:{...}}}` through `/saveconfig`, which MERGES into `settings.bpParams[<id>]` **namespaced by blueprint id** so switching blueprints never cross-contaminates; `BP_PARAMS`/`BP_PARAM_VALUES` injected from `list_blueprints`+`get_blueprint`. **(e) Seed** — `blueprints_src/sdxl/blueprint.json` now carries `steps`/`cfg`/`sampler_name` params (bound to KSampler node 13, which bindings don't drive) as a worked example; `blueprints_src/README.md` documents the `params[]` schema. **Validated:** `py -m py_compile *.py` clean; `PAGE.format()` render-smoke (22 keys, all supplied by the format call, panel + `renderBpParams`/`saveBpParams`/`bpParamsGrid` markup present, no brace error); offline self-test (`storage`-free) — defaults land on the right node inputs with no overrides + bindings still applied, an override wins and is type-coerced, a blank override falls back to default, a NO-params blueprint is unchanged even when overrides are passed, and validation rejects a bad param node/field, a key colliding with a role, a double-drive, and a duplicate key; the seeded sdxl blueprint validates against its own graph. **Owner-side verify owed:** seed (`py seed_blueprints.py`) + restart, then a live generate THROUGH the `sdxl` blueprint id with a changed `steps`/`cfg`/`sampler_name` to confirm the override reaches the KSampler in the remote ComfyUI graph (needs the local GPU/tunnel — not testable here). **Robustness follow-up (working tree, NOT committed, 2026-06-14):** bad/out-of-range override values now fall back gracefully instead of opaquely failing the remote prompt — new `batch_atlas._effective_param_value` drops an uncoercible override to the param `default` (and `_coerce_param` now RAISES `_CoerceError` instead of passing the raw string through), clamps numerics into `min`/`max`, and falls back a `select` value outside `options`; if even the default is unusable the node input is left at the graph's baked value (`_UNSET`). The bool generate-time control gained a blank "— default —" option so a saved bool can be unset like the other types (the `/saveconfig` blank-drop path already handles it). Re-validated: `py -m py_compile *.py` clean, `PAGE.format()` render-smoke (blank option present), 17/17 offline assertions (bad int→default, clamp min/max, bad select→default, valid override wins, no-override→default, no-params blueprint byte-identical).

  - **Track 2 — model auto-download (phases 1 + 4, ComfyUI-Manager) (`services/atlas-tool/`, code in working tree, NOT committed) — 2026-06-14:** the "prepare models" step that makes the local ComfyUI HAVE a blueprint's declared `models[]` before its graph is submitted. **Spike (phase 1) result:** the installed ComfyUI-Manager's API is **queue-based** and read from source (`glob/manager_server.py`) — `POST /manager/queue/install_model` (body IS the model dict; 200=QUEUED; **400 unless the model matches Manager's curated `model-list.json` by _(save_path, base, filename)_** — the catalog-only/whitelist constraint; 403 if security level > middle), `POST /manager/queue/start` (200 / 201-if-running), `GET /manager/queue/status` (poll `is_processing`+`in_progress_count`), `POST /manager/reboot` (`os.execv` → **connection DROP = expected success**, then poll `/system_stats`). All over the existing tunnel + CF headers + `InvisibleAtlas` UA. **Implementation (phase 4):** `blueprint_models.py` (`prepare_blueprint_models(...)` — pure, HTTP injected so it's offline-testable; returns `{ready, installed, still_missing:[{filename,save_path,base,url,reason}]}`, NEVER raises) + `batch_atlas.py` (`prepare_blueprint_models_for_run` binds the stdlib `/manager/*` twins `_manager_post`/`_manager_get` + the `_model_installed`/`_MODEL_FIELD_NODES` `/object_info` check; `_prepare_blueprint_models_or_fail` wired into `main()` AFTER `preflight_models`, runs ONLY for blueprint pipelines with a non-empty `models[]`, and FAILS the run with a readable checklist — mirroring `preflight_models` — if a required model stays missing). Flow: skip already-installed → partition missing into installable (full catalog keys) vs not → queue installable → start → poll → reboot ONCE (batched) → poll `/system_stats` → re-check → checklist anything still missing. **Kill-switch `BLUEPRINT_AUTO_INSTALL_MODELS`** (env, default ENABLED): when off, NO install/reboot — just the checklist (safer, since reboot interrupts in-flight ComfyUI). **Schema alignment:** `blueprints._validate_models` accepts the aligned `models[]` shape (`url`/`save_path`/`base`/`filename` — catalog keys, OPTIONAL → gate auto-install vs checklist — plus `field` for the installed-check, optional `sha256`/`size`); the legacy `{dir, source}` shape still loads (mapped to `save_path`/`url`). Seeded `blueprints_src/sdxl/blueprint.json` models updated to the aligned shape; `blueprints_src/README.md` documents the schema + the catalog-only constraint + the kill-switch. **Fail-safe:** Manager-absent (404 on `/manager/*`), ComfyUI unreachable, per-model 400/403, download timeout, model-still-invisible-after-reboot all degrade to a readable manual checklist — no crash. **Validated:** `py -m py_compile *.py` clean; offline self-test (mocked Manager HTTP, deleted after) — 21 assertions across 8 scenarios (correct install body for a catalog model; non-catalog model → checklist with no POST; per-model 400+403 caught without aborting others; start→poll→reboot→system_stats→recheck fires when queued; already-installed skipped no-POST; Manager-absent 404 → checklist no crash; kill-switch off → checklist no install/reboot; still-missing required model → not-ready failure). **Owner-side LIVE verify owed (needs the local GPU/tunnel + a catalog model):** select a blueprint whose `models[]` includes a real catalog model that ISN'T installed, generate, and confirm the prepare step QUEUES it, downloads (status polled), reboots ComfyUI (connection drops as expected), and the model reappears in `/object_info` so generation proceeds — then a no-op fast path on the second run. **Catalog-only boundary:** only models in Manager's curated `model-list.json` auto-install; custom Civitai/gated-HF URLs that aren't catalogued (or are missing catalog keys) fall to the manual checklist by design. **Owner step to activate:** re-seed (`py seed_blueprints.py`) + restart atlas-tool if blueprint schema changed; set Manager security level to "middle" or below. **Phase 7 ONBOARDING note still owed:** local ComfyUI needs ComfyUI-Manager (≤ middle security) for auto-install.

**`.atlas` geometry — Part B (data migration), PENDING owner + R2 creds:** existing manifests still point `atlas.atlas_file`/`atlas.source_image` at local Windows paths; the real `.atlas` + page bitmaps live in `apps/<game>/static/assets/spines/<group>/` (pages often `.webp` while some manifests name `.png` — fix the mismatch on repoint). Migration = upload each `.atlas` + page to R2 `atlas_maker/unassigned/cloud/input/refs/atlas/<name>`, repoint manifest fields to `refs/atlas/<name>.<ext>`, re-upload manifests, restart atlas-tool. Easiest path: per region card, **Upload .atlas (B10)** auto-does it. (`fix_manifest_paths.py` still targets the STALE `atlas_maker/cloud/<proj>/` prefix — needs `atlas_maker/unassigned/cloud/`.) Needs `R2_*` env + the engine repo. (Part A code ✅ done.)

### Key lessons

Full list (+ the 2026-05-29 session lessons) in `docs/history.md#key-lessons`. The operationally-critical few:

- **Always `git push`** after committing — Railway can't deploy local-only commits.
- **Railway staged vars** — adding a var only stages it; click "Apply changes / Deploy". Prefer a **code default** for non-secret config so deploys don't depend on the dashboard.
- **Cloudflare blocks `Python-urllib` UA with 403** — always send a custom User-Agent to ComfyUI.
- **Windows trailing-dot folders** — Windows silently strips a trailing `.` (`symbols.` → `symbols` locally but NOT on Linux/R2); normalize path segments in R2 manifests. Watch on any Windows-sourced data.
- **The desktop `Invisible_Launcher.py` runs from source under Python 3.9** (VS `Python39_64`) — so **no PEP 604 `X | None`** or other 3.10+ runtime syntax in annotations (the built exe uses a newer Python and hides it). The file now carries `from __future__ import annotations` (lazy annotations) to neutralize this; keep it.
- **Game deploy `ERR_PNPM_OUTDATED_LOCKFILE` = engine-submodule drift, not a "left-behind" lockfile.** Standalone game repos (Book of Borut, …) have a single root `pnpm-lock.yaml` that flattens `engine/packages/*` as workspace members (`pnpm-workspace.yaml`). The desktop launcher's `build_game` runs `pnpm install && pnpm build` with `CI=1` → **frozen-lockfile**. If a machine's `engine/` submodule has drifted off the committed pin, that frozen install hard-fails — even though the repo's lockfile is correct and pushed. **Root cause of the drift:** the launcher's `update_existing_clone` does `git pull --ff-only` then `git submodule update`, BUT the build writes _tracked_ files (`src/game/baked-editor-bundle.json` via `bake:doc`, `static/assets/**` via `pull:assets`), so after the first build the ff-pull is refused and the **submodule update is early-returned/skipped**, leaving `engine/` stale vs the committed pin/lockfile. **Fix shipped (zero-action, `077af80`):** `GET /api/launcher/projects` now `normalizeLauncherProfile()`s every served game build-cmd to prefix `git submodule update --init --recursive`, forcing the submodule to the superproject's committed pin right before the frozen install — reaches **every** launcher on next "Sync from cloud", no exe rebuild / re-seed / migration. Seed default also updated (`2130224`). **Two standing rules:** (1) bump the engine submodule + regenerate + commit the root lockfile in the **same commit** (mirror `bookofborut@43dd1f2`); (2) generated tracked artifacts (baked bundle, pulled assets) are what poison the ff-pull — durable follow-up is to fix the launcher's `update_existing_clone` to re-pin the submodule even when the pull doesn't fast-forward (and/or stop tracking those generated files). Immediate unblock on a stuck machine: `git submodule update --init --recursive` in the game folder, then retry. **Sync re-pin now also hardened in the launcher source (`update_project_repo` always runs the submodule update even when the ff-pull is refused) — ships in launcher v1.0.18+.**
- **Editor font params: detect by NATURE, not only by the `style.fontFamily` binding-walk.** The scene-editor instance-param editor (+ component default editor) rendered font params as a free-text box for a coded component (the HUD Readout), even though `a384b8d` had added font dropdowns. Root cause: detection scanned ONLY for a text node binding `style.fontFamily`→param. But the engine's canonical font param is `{ key: 'fontFamily', kind: 'font' }` (`boundComponentCatalog.ts`), and `ComponentParam.kind` has no `'font'` — so a coded component's font is stored as a plain `string` with NO `style.fontFamily` binding → missed → text box. Fixed with a shared pure helper `fontParamKeysOf(def)` in `engine-layout/componentParams.ts` (binding-walk **OR** key===`fontFamily` **OR** label===`font`), used by both editors; instance dropdown also now shows the component default in its inherit option. Diagnosed by reading the REAL saved defs from R2 (the launcher `.env` creds), not by guessing. **Separate data bug still open:** two `bookofborut` "Button" defs have ORPHAN bindings (text binds `style.fontFamily`→`textFont` but `textFont`/`textSize`/`textColour` are MISSING from `params`, so those controls never render) — `exposeTextParams`' re-entry early-returns when a binding exists without re-creating a missing param; needs a self-heal.
- **"Build & publish failed: The read operation timed out" = a portal POST read timeout, not the build.** The publish flow's `register_game` urllib POST caught only `HTTPError`, so a socket read timeout (an `OSError`/`socket.timeout`, raised when Railway is slow to respond on a contributor's slower link) propagated raw and aborted the whole publish — **after** the R2 bundle had already uploaded. A boto3 timeout would instead read "Read timeout on endpoint URL", so the bare "The read operation timed out" fingerprints the raw urllib read. **Fixed in launcher v1.0.19 (`invisible-launcher@a0c6a10`):** `register_game` retries the small POST 3× on transient `URLError`/`OSError` (timeout 30s→45s) and, if it still fails, reports the upload succeeded + to just re-run; `_r2_client` now uses `botocore Config(connect_timeout=30, read_timeout=300, standard retries ×6, tcp_keepalive)` so multi-file uploads survive a slow link. **This fix lives in the exe — unlike the server-side lockfile fix it needs a launcher republish (`build-launcher-exe.py --upload`) so the other machine auto-updates.** Immediate unblock without updating: just click Build & publish again (the upload is incremental; the timeout was transient).

---

Full done-work changelog: `docs/history.md`.
